AIChipBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Automated AI Accelerator Design
AIChipBench：面向自动化AI加速器设计的LLM智能体基准测试
A language-model decoder carries state between decoding steps, and in hardware a wrong state update can leave the current token correct and corrupt the output several tokens later, so a design can pass output tests and still be wrong. AIChipBench asks what LLM coding agents need to build such hardware correctly. Agents implement quantized Qwen3.5, Gemma 3, Gemma 4, and Llama 3.2 decoders in synthesizable register-transfer-level (RTL) code against an executable integer reference that specifies the outputs and the stored state at every decoding step. They debug against public tests under fixed budgets; their frozen designs are graded on held-out token sequences, resets, memory stalls, and synthesis, and a second track asks them to speed up a correct design at nearly fixed area. Across 9,136 attempts by four coding agents, the difficulty lies in stored state. Feedback that points to the first state mismatch raises acceptance over output-only feedback in all 12 agent--task cells of a matched study, by 25.0 to 46.9 percentage points, and halves the agent-hours per accepted design. Regraded on new sequences and conditions, 45 of 69 accepted designs fail within the 32 steps their tests required. More than a third of attempts to speed up a correct design end without one. An overall score ranks Sol 5.6 and Fable 5.1 above Astra 6 and Opus 5.5, and both halves of the instances give this order in all 200 random splits. Hardware agents therefore need debugging feedback that exposes stored state, and their benchmarks need final tests well beyond the development cases.
中文翻译
语言模型解码器在解码步骤之间维持状态，而在硬件中，错误的状态更新可能导致当前标记正确，但数个标记后输出被破坏，因此设计可能通过输出测试但仍错误。AIChipBench探讨LLM编码智能体需要具备哪些能力才能正确构建此类硬件。智能体需在可综合的寄存器传输级（RTL）代码中实现量化版Qwen3.5、Gemma 3、Gemma 4和Llama 3.2解码器，并与一个可执行的整数参考模型对齐，该模型指定了每个解码步骤的输出和存储状态。智能体在固定预算下针对公开测试进行调试；其冻结的设计在保留的标记序列、重置、内存停顿和综合测试中评分，第二赛道要求它们在几乎不变面积的情况下加速正确设计。在四个编码智能体共9,136次尝试中，难点在于存储状态。指向首次状态不匹配的反馈在12个智能体-任务组合中，使接受率比仅输出反馈高出25.0至46.9个百分点，并使每接受一个设计所需代理小时数减半。重新评估新序列和条件后，69个接受设计中有45个在32步内失败。超过三分之一的加速尝试未能成功。总体评分显示Sol 5.6和Fable 5.1优于Astra 6和Opus 5.5，且在全部200次随机划分中两部分均保持此顺序。因此，硬件智能体需要暴露存储状态的调试反馈，其基准测试也需包含远超开发案例的最终测试。
👤 Guangyuan Wang, Chengran Li, Minchen Luo, Jichen Feng, Mengdi Wang et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

SALSA: Sampling Adversarial Suffixes from Learned Distributions for LLM Safety Assessment
SALSA：从学习到的分布中采样对抗后缀以评估大语言模型安全性
Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, where adversaries append optimized suffixes to otherwise benign looking prompts to bypass safety alignment. Existing optimization-based GCG-style attacks can be effective, but they are expensive at inference time, and suffer from poor transfer. We present, SALSA, a generative framework that learns a distribution over jailbreak suffixes from optimization-derived attack corpora. Once trained, the model supports efficient sampling-based attacks. On open-source targets, it achieves attack success rates comparable to state-of-the-art optimization baselines, while reducing generation time from 3.46s to 0.04s per prompt. In black-box transfer, it remains highly effective against proprietary systems, including 99% ASR on GPT-3.5-Turbo, and reaches 45% ASR on the substantially harder GPT-4o, more than doubling the strongest baseline at an equal sampling budget. We further offer an intuition for why generative sampling can preserve high attack success, when the learned suffix distribution is close to the distribution of optimization outputs. Overall, our results show that learning adversarial suffix distributions yields a scalable, and practically efficient foundation for continuous LLM vulnerability discovery.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）仍容易受到越狱攻击，即攻击者在看似无害的提示后附加优化后的后缀以绕过安全对齐。现有的基于优化的GCG风格攻击虽有效，但在推理时成本高昂且泛化能力差。我们提出SALSA，一种生成式框架，从优化生成的攻击语料库中学习越狱后缀的分布。训练完成后，该模型支持高效的基于采样的攻击。在开源目标上，其攻击成功率与最先进的优化基线相当，但每条提示的生成时间从3.46秒降至0.04秒。在黑盒迁移测试中，它对专有系统依然高度有效，包括在GPT-3.5-Turbo上达到99%的攻击成功率，并在更具挑战性的GPT-4o上达到45%的攻击成功率，超过最强基线超过一倍，在相同采样预算下表现更优。我们进一步提供了直观解释：当学习到的后缀分布接近优化输出分布时，生成采样为何能保持高攻击成功率。总体而言，我们的结果表明，学习对抗后缀分布可为持续发现LLM漏洞提供可扩展且实际高效的基础设施。
👤 Shivank Rajput, Himanshu Singh, A. V. Subramanyam 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

ASCENT: A Benchmark for Evaluating and Advancing Stepwise Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models on Common Clinical Scenarios
ASCENT：面向常见临床场景中大语言模型逐步诊断推理能力的评估与推进基准
Large language models (LLMs) excel at medical question answering yet are rarely evaluated on the stepwise diagnostic reasoning that defines real clinical workflows, where impressions are revised as information accumulates. We build Annotated Stepwise Clinical rEasoning for NaturalisTic Diagnosis (ASCENT), a clinician-annotated benchmark and training resource of 3,078 stepwise problems derived from MedQA-USMLE that decomposes each vignette into EMR-aligned steps (Findings, Impression, supporting Rationale), enabling evaluation of intermediate reasoning under incomplete information. Experiments and training with ASCENT revealed insights into how current LLMs handle stepwise diagnostic reasoning. Even strong reasoning models that perform well on MedQA-USMLE leave substantial headroom on ASCENT, and general-purpose frontier models trail further, exposing a persistent gap between fully informed and stepwise diagnosis. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-7B and 32B on ASCENT yields measurable F1 gains over both pre-trained and HuatuoGPT-o1 CoT-trained baselines, with gains driven primarily by precision. Complementary robustness analyses (counterfactual perturbation, format-vs-content control, judge agreement, and rollout) further show that ASCENT-fine-tuned models rely on the diagnostic content of prior impressions rather than imitating their output format, while error propagation under rollout remains a key challenge for clinical deployment.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）在医学问答方面表现出色，但很少在真实临床工作流程所依赖的逐步诊断推理过程上进行评估，即随着信息积累不断修正判断。我们构建了Annotated Stepwise Clinical rEasoning for NaturalisTic Diagnosis（ASCENT），一个由临床医生标注的基准数据集和训练资源，包含3,078个源自MedQA-USMLE的逐步问题，将每个病例分解为与电子病历（EMR）对齐的步骤（发现、印象、支持性理由），从而可在信息不完整的情况下评估中间推理过程。使用ASCENT进行实验与训练揭示了当前LLMs在逐步诊断推理中的表现。即使在MedQA-USMLE上表现良好的强推理模型，在ASCENT上的表现仍有巨大提升空间，而通用前沿模型则落后更多，暴露出完全知情与逐步诊断之间的持续差距。对Qwen2.5-7B和32B在ASCENT上进行微调后，相较于预训练模型和HuatuoGPT-o1 CoT训练基线，F1得分显著提升，主要由精度驱动。补充的鲁棒性分析（反事实扰动、格式与内容控制、评判者一致性、滚动推演）进一步表明，ASCENT微调模型依赖于先前印象的诊断内容而非模仿其输出格式，而滚动推演中的错误传播仍是临床部署的关键挑战。
👤 Yera Choi, Yeong Hwa Kim, Jaedeok Lee, Taekang Kim, Sangdoo Yun et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Red-Teaming a Sovereign LLM, Its Reasoning and Its Training Stages
对主权大语言模型及其推理与训练阶段的红队测试
Open-weight large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for applications with sensitive information. We investigate whether confidentiality instructions in system prompt can protect such information. We red-team Apertus v1.5, an open sovereign LLM, and five other model families across six languages and reasoning modes. We use synthetic personal records to evaluate direct disclosure, correction baits, yes/no verification, derived-value queries, reasoning leakage and authorisation checks. We also explore copyright attribution, historical misrepresentation and stereotyping. Despite confidentiality instructions, Apertus 8B and 70B leak information in 96% and 95% of requests, respectively, in correction-bait attacks. Verification and derived-value queries also expose protected information without necessarily revealing identifiers. Moreover, reasoning traces can leak confidential records even when final answers withhold them. Our experiments also show that post-training does not consistently improve safety. Our findings demonstrate that system prompts alone cannot ensure confidentiality and highlight the need for multilingual reasoning-aware safety evaluations.
中文翻译
开放权重的大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被应用于涉及敏感信息的场景。我们探究系统提示中的保密指令能否有效保护此类信息。我们对Apertus v1.5——一种开放主权LLM——及其他五个模型家族在六种语言和多种推理模式下进行了红队测试。我们使用合成个人记录评估直接泄露、纠正诱饵、是/否验证、衍生值查询、推理泄露和授权检查等问题。同时探索版权归属、历史误述和刻板印象等议题。尽管存在保密指令，Apertus 8B和70B在纠正诱饵攻击中分别有96%和95%的请求发生信息泄露。验证和衍生值查询也暴露了受保护信息，即使未明确揭示标识符。此外，即使最终答案未透露，推理轨迹仍可能泄露机密记录。实验还表明，后训练阶段并不总是能提升安全性。我们的发现表明，仅靠系统提示无法确保保密性，强调需要具备多语言推理意识的安全评估。
👤 Hatef Otroshi Shahreza 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Bathys: Advancing Deep Search Agents
Bathys：推进深度搜索智能体
Deep search is a critical capability for agents tackling complex, open-ended tasks, where solving a problem requires iterative information gathering, query reformulation, and evidence-based verification rather than a single retrieval step. To address these requirements, we present Bathys 9B and 27B deep search agents built around a training framework that spans verifiable question synthesis, post-training, and online reinforcement learning (RL) under context compression. Our high-quality data construction ensures both search depth and reliability: each clue is reformulated as an indirect reference requiring independent retrieval, and retained evidence chains enable verification that the clues jointly determine a unique answer. Curated trajectories underpin a pipeline of supervised fine-tuning (SFT), Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (KTO), and Compaction-Managed Group Sequence Policy Optimization (CM-GSPO): SFT provides a stable foundation for tool use over long horizons and query reformulation; KTO uses binary feedback on trajectories to improve the selection of answers grounded in evidence; CM-GSPO optimizes execution sub-trajectories with length-normalized likelihood ratios on the recorded rollout contexts and reweights sub-trajectory advantages to correct the extra weighting introduced by independently averaging sub-trajectory losses. Bathys 9B and 27B attain 66.51% and 80.49% on BrowseComp, respectively, under an evaluation protocol with trajectory self-verification and conditional retries.
中文翻译
深度搜索是智能体应对复杂、开放性任务的关键能力，解决此类问题需要迭代式信息获取、查询重构和基于证据的验证，而非单一检索步骤。为满足这些需求，我们提出了基于可验证问题生成、后训练及上下文压缩下的在线强化学习（RL）训练框架的Bathys 9B和27B深度搜索智能体。高质量的数据构建确保了搜索深度与可靠性：每个线索均被重构为需独立检索的间接引用，保留的证据链支持对线索共同决定唯一答案的验证。精心设计的轨迹支撑监督微调（SFT）、卡尼曼-特沃斯基优化（KTO）以及压缩管理组序列策略优化（CM-GSPO）的流水线：SFT为长期工具使用和查询重构提供稳定基础；KTO利用轨迹上的二元反馈改进基于证据的答案选择；CM-GSPO通过记录回放上下文中的长度归一化似然比优化执行子轨迹，并重新加权子轨迹优势以纠正因独立平均子轨迹损失带来的额外权重偏差。在包含轨迹自验证和条件重试的评估协议下，Bathys 9B和27B分别达到BrowseComp上的66.51%和80.49%准确率。
👤 Zixuan Chen, Shengyou Yuan, Nang Yuan, Ming Ma, Zelin Xiao et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

REBEAL: Human References Are No Longer the Best Translation, and Metrics Do Not Notice
当“参考”不再最优：机器翻译评估中参考偏差的揭示
Reference-based MT evaluation assumes the human reference is correct and better than the systems it scores. Both assumptions now fail, since the human reference ranks first in none of the 2024 WMT MQM campaigns and carries annotated errors in 19% to 87% of its segments. What a metric does with a flawed reference is a question about the metric, and it would have been worth asking before reference quality came into doubt. We introduce REBEAL (Reference Bias EvALuation), a benchmark that probes how MT metrics respond to seven types of controlled reference contamination, drawn from the error categories those campaigns record. Across eight widely used metrics and three language pairs, recent learned metrics (xCOMET, MetricX) prove more robust than their predecessors, but each family fails in its own way. Surface metrics show no length sensitivity, so contamination hides in longer segments, while learned metrics accept references in unrelated languages as nearly correct. The failure they share is named-entity contamination, which no metric detects and which flips up to 8 of 36 system pairs when ranking nine MT systems.
中文翻译
基于参考的机器翻译评估假设人类参考是正确且优于系统的。但这一假设如今已失效——在2024年WMT MQM系列活动中，人类参考从未排名第一，且其片段中存在19%至87%的标注错误。当参考存在缺陷时，评估指标如何响应是一个关于指标本身的问题，本应在参考质量受质疑前就提出。我们提出 REBEAL（参考偏差评估基准），用于探测八种广泛使用的评估指标在七类受控参考污染下的反应，这些污染类型源自各活动记录的错误类别。在三种语言对和八个主流指标上，近期学习型指标（xCOMET、MetricX）表现出更强鲁棒性，但每类指标仍各有缺陷。表面型指标对长度不敏感，导致污染隐藏在长段落中；学习型指标则接受无关语言的参考为近乎正确。它们共有的失败模式是命名实体污染，所有指标均无法检测，且在九个机器翻译系统排名中，可使多达8/36的系统对排序颠倒。
👤 Ahrii Kim, Chanjun Park, Seong-heum Kim 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Evaluating Latent Knowledge Stability in Multimodal Large Language Models
多模态大语言模型中潜在知识稳定性的评估
Changes in image viewpoint and question formulation can alter latent knowledge representations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, output correctness and overall representational drift provide limited insight into how these changes affect model decisions. We therefore evaluate latent knowledge stability across visual and linguistic elicitation conditions from two complementary perspectives: answer preference and internal support. Specifically, we devise the margin retention score (MRS), which measures the first-order projected degradation of the initial answer's logit margin over competing options across conditions and assesses how changes in latent knowledge representations affect this margin. We also present latent knowledge support-structure stability (KSS), which evaluates reference-correct items by comparing internal support profiles constructed using the margin gradient under the reference condition, which jointly reflects changes in how internal support is distributed and changes in total support magnitude. We further construct MMStableBench, comprising 19,452 knowledge items (facts), each probing the same latent knowledge across varied image viewpoints, semantically equivalent question formulations, and answer-option permutations. Experiments across 15 MLLMs show that MRS and KSS reveal latent knowledge instability not captured by accuracy, while an MRS-inspired regularizer further improves the generalization of edited latent knowledge.
中文翻译
图像视角和问题表述的变化可能改变多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的潜在知识表征。然而，输出正确性和整体表征漂移仅提供了有限的洞察，难以揭示这些变化如何影响模型决策。因此，我们从两个互补的角度评估了在视觉和语言诱发条件下的潜在知识稳定性：答案偏好与内部支持。具体而言，我们提出了边际保留分数（MRS），用于衡量在不同条件下初始答案logit边际相对于竞争选项的一阶投影退化，并评估潜在知识表征变化对这一边际的影响。我们还提出了潜在知识支持结构稳定性（KSS），通过比较在参考条件下使用边际梯度构建的内部支持轮廓来评估参考正确的项目，该指标共同反映了内部支持分布变化和总支持量变化。我们进一步构建了MMStableBench，包含19,452个知识项（事实），每个知识项在不同的图像视角、语义等价的问题表述和答案选项排列下探测相同的潜在知识。在15个MLLM上的实验表明，MRS和KSS揭示了准确率无法捕捉的潜在知识不稳定性，而受MRS启发的正则化器进一步提升了编辑后潜在知识的泛化能力。
👤 Chuancheng Shi, Shangze Li, Wenhua Wu, Zifeng Cheng, Pengyang Shao et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32539]

Avalon-M1: Reinforcement Learning from Collective Rewards
Avalon-M1：基于集体奖励的强化学习
We introduce reinforcement learning from collective rewards (RLCR), a recipe for jointly training language models to act as agent organisations under latency constraints. A CEO and up to eight specialists share one policy, co-ordinate through a message board and workspace, and are trained with a common outcome-derived advantage. The deadline makes parallel work valuable when it increases the return earned before the cap. We train Avalon-M1-Mini (120B) across 147 environments. For the trained model, eight-hire organisational execution raises IMO-ProofBench-Advanced scores from 23.8% to 39.0% relative to single-agent execution. Across 12 benchmarks, organisations achieve similar average scores at approximately one-third the derived latency of token-matched single-agent execution and just over half that of eight independent attempts followed by selection. The trained model also outperforms Nemotron 3 Super, post-trained from the same base model, across all evaluations when comparing each model's best tested configuration. On EdgeBench, organisations overtake the single agent after approximately 0.4 derived hours. Over the course of training, organisations develop hierarchies to manage communication costs, invest more in peer review, and alter how work is divided. Safety evaluations find near-universal refusal of harmful actions but sustained declines in external reporting in two of three oversight scenarios. These results establish a shared-advantage baseline for studying organisational learning, performance-latency trade-offs, and collective oversight.
中文翻译
我们引入了基于集体奖励的强化学习（RLCR），这是一种在延迟约束下联合训练语言模型作为代理组织的方案。CEO与最多八名专家共享一个策略，通过消息板和工作区进行协调，并使用共同的结果导出优势进行训练。截止时间使得并行工作在提升截止前获得回报时具有价值。我们在147个环境中训练了Avalon-M1-Mini（120B）。对于训练后的模型，八人组织执行将IMO-ProofBench-Advanced得分从23.8%提升至39.0%，相比单代理执行表现显著。在12个基准测试中，组织在约三分之一的token匹配单代理执行推导延迟下达到相似平均得分，且略高于八次独立尝试后选择的延迟。训练模型在所有评估中均优于从相同基础模型微调得到的Nemotron 3 Super。在EdgeBench上，组织在约0.4个推导小时后超越单代理。训练过程中，组织发展出层级结构以管理通信成本，更重视同行评审，并调整任务分配方式。安全评估显示对有害行为近乎普遍拒绝，但在三种监督场景中有两种出现外部报告持续下降。这些结果确立了共享优势基线，用于研究组织学习、性能-延迟权衡及集体监督。
👤 Iliyan Zarov, Henry Course, Anthony Hartshorn, Ran Achiron, Louis Roche et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

When Ink Meets Pixel: Intent-Aware OCR with VLMs for Human-Corrected Financial Documents When Ink Meets Pixel: Intent-Aware OCR with VLMs for Human-Corrected Financial Documents
当墨水遇见像素：面向人工修正金融文档的意图感知OCR
Human annotations on documents (such as arrows, carets, strikethroughs, and insertions) express corrective intent yet existing OCR and document understanding systems treat these marks as visual noise rather than semantic edit operations. We introduce intent-aware OCR, the task of generating corrected transcriptions by jointly reasoning over original document content and visual intent cues (handwritten or digital) that convey edit intent. To support this setting, we present DocIntent, a multimodal dataset of 1,600 financial documents with over 5,700 annotated visual intent cues, each paired with layout-aware, intent-resolved OCR targets. We further introduce IntentBench, a 216-document benchmark designed to evaluate model robustness under diverse real-world annotation conditions. Leveraging DocIntent, we fine-tune DocIntentOCR, a suite of 3B and 7B parameter vision-language models, under direct intent supervision on corrected transcriptions. On IntentBench, the fine-tuned 3B and 7B models achieve F1-scores of 0.81 and 0.83, respectively, substantially outperforming commercial closed-source (e.g., GPT-4.1 (0.626)), and open-source models (e.g., LLaMA-3.2-Vision-90B (0.50)) demonstrating that intent-aware supervision enables compact domain-specific models to outperform substantially larger general-purpose systems on this task. This work establishes intent-aware OCR as a novel human-in-the-loop document understanding task and provides foundational resources (dataset, benchmark, models) to advance research in interactive document processing.
中文翻译

Decision Models: An Introduction and Capability Evaluation
决策模型：介绍与能力评估
Applications such as ticket routing and incident triage need decisions that software can act on. Decision models provide these outputs through explicit types for choices, binary judgments, and policy scores. We evaluate the accuracy, output validity, and request latency of eleven configurations on 1,000 English and Chinese cases across nine task suites. StartLux Instant achieves 95.3% overall accuracy, returns valid outputs on all 1,000 cases, and has a median request latency of 0.084 s. It reaches 99.4% on classification and routing and selects the correct response on all 100 tool-selection cases. The highest overall accuracy among the evaluated configurations is 97.1%. These results show how a typed decision service can support fast, programmatic decisions and why its suitability depends on task-specific errors and latency requirements.

ArabFake: A multitask deep learning framework for Arabic fake news Detection, Categorization, and risk prediction

The spread of fake news among Arabic media including social media represents a great challenge to the integrity of information and the trust of the public in it. In this paper, we introduce a comprehensive deep-learning framework, named ArabFake, that goes beyond the binary classification on Arabic fake news detection. ArabFake, built over MARBERTv2 (a state-of-the-art model for multi-dialectal Arabic tweets), proficiently address the complexity of the Arabic language while performing three unified tasks which are fake news detection, content categorization and its risk assessment. The framework promotes efficiency and performance both by enabling multi-task learning through shared knowledge representation across tasks. In order to facilitate development and evaluation, we present the ArabFake Dataset consisting of 2,495 manually labelled news items with labels that are verified by experts regarding fake news categories and risk levels. ArabFake demonstrates robust performance, achieving an F1 score of 94.12% for fake news detection, 84.92% for categorization, and 88.91% for risk zone assessment, highlighting its reliability and effectiveness across multiple tasks. We improve interpretability and extract insight into manipulative techniques by integrating valence scoring as part of the framework that emphasizes misleading linguistic cues used to disseminate fake news within the produced image. The results show that ArabFake is a holistic Arabic fake news detection framework that has practical implications on news organizations and fact checking projects.

In some educational institutions, workflows such as the exam deferral process can be slow, inconvenient, and inconsistent, which creates the need to implement novel digital systems. RightDiff: Learning Generative Reward Models to Judge by the Right Differences
RightDiff：学习生成奖励模型以依据正确的差异进行判断
Generative reward models typically rely on generic query-level rubrics or unconstrained rationales, overlooking the fact that pairwise preference is driven by response-specific differences. Moreover, optimizing solely for outcome correctness encourages models to exploit superficial shortcuts such as verbosity and formatting, triggering rationale drift. To address this, we propose RightDiff, which structures pairwise evaluation into an evidence-grounded evaluation trajectory executed in a single autoregressive generation. RightDiff explicitly discovers contrastive differences between candidates, categorizes them into material or immaterial factors, and induces pair-specific rubrics before final adjudication. To train this process without post-hoc rationalization, we synthesize demonstration data via response-isolated prompts and optimize the reward model with a difference-alignment reward regularized by budget-constrained one-to-one matching and multiplicative gating. RightDiff achieves state-of-the-art preference modeling performance on RewardBench and RM-Bench, surpassing all open-source reward models and leading proprietary LLM judges. Downstream, it provides reliable supervision for direct preference optimization on Arena-Hard and AlpacaEval while effectively resisting length shortcuts.
中文翻译
生成式奖励模型通常依赖通用的查询级评分标准或不受约束的理由，忽略了成对偏好实际上由响应特异性差异驱动的事实。此外，仅优化最终结果正确性会促使模型利用表面捷径（如冗长和格式），引发理由漂移。为解决此问题，我们提出RightDiff，将成对评估结构化为一次自回归生成中执行的证据基础评估轨迹。RightDiff显式发现候选响应间的对比差异，将其分类为实质性或非实质性因素，并在最终裁决前生成特定于配对的评分标准。为在不依赖事后解释的情况下训练该过程，我们通过隔离响应的提示合成示范数据，并利用预算受限的一一匹配与乘法门控进行差值对齐的奖励正则化优化奖励模型。RightDiff在RewardBench和RM-Bench上实现了最先进的偏好建模性能，超越所有开源奖励模型并领先于专有LLM评判者。下游应用中，它在Arena-Hard和AlpacaEval上提供了可靠的直接偏好优化监督，并有效抵抗长度捷径。

Making Evaluation Explicit Is Not Enough: Supervising the Evaluation Process in Generative Reward Models
使评估显式化并不足够：在生成式奖励模型中监督评估过程
Generative reward models (GRMs) make language-model evaluation more explicit by generating critiques, rubrics, or intermediate analyses before predicting a final preference. However, explicit evaluation does not necessarily imply a reliable evaluation process: under outcome-only supervision, a model may reach the correct final preference while producing weak or unsupported intermediate judgments, turning the evaluation into a post-hoc rationalization. We introduce STEP-GRM, a framework that directly supervises the intermediate evaluation process of rubric-based GRMs. In addition to conventional preference supervision, STEP-GRM provides structural guidance for rubric construction and local supervision for criterion-level judgments, encouraging the model to learn not only the correct final preference but also a more reliable process for reaching it. Extensive experiments show that STEP-GRM achieves strong performance on standard reward-modeling benchmarks while improving the consistency and correctness of intermediate evaluations. Moreover, reward signals produced by STEP-GRM lead to better downstream policy optimization, demonstrating that more reliable evaluation processes can translate into practical gains beyond reward-model accuracy.
中文翻译
生成式奖励模型（GRMs）通过在预测最终偏好前生成批评、评分标准或中间分析，使语言模型评估更加显式。然而，显式评估并不必然意味着可靠的过程：在仅基于结果的监督下，模型可能达到正确的最终偏好，但中间判断却薄弱或缺乏支持，导致评估沦为事后的合理化。我们提出STEP-GRM框架，直接监督基于评分标准的GRMs的中间评估过程。除了传统的偏好监督外，STEP-GRM还提供评分标准构建的结构引导和准则级判断的局部监督，鼓励模型不仅学习正确的最终偏好，也学会更可靠的达成路径。大量实验表明，STEP-GRM在标准奖励建模基准上表现优异，同时提升了中间评估的一致性和正确性。此外，STEP-GRM生成的奖励信号能带来更好的下游策略优化效果，证明更可靠的评估过程可转化为实际收益，而不仅仅是奖励模型准确性。

Grounding Language Models in Revealed Audience Behavior
基于受众行为揭示的语言模型对齐
Language models are increasingly used to simulate how an audience will respond to a headline or a post, and such simulators are validated against what people say. We validate them against what people do. We call a model grounded in revealed audience behavior when it is fine-tuned on records of what audiences actually clicked, liked or watched, and we ask not only whether asking a model falls short of grounding it, as prior work found, but why and where. Why: the gap is a property of the training signal, not mainly of model size or prompting. The same 8B model is right about half the time when asked which headline readers will click and 86.5% of the time when grounded in the clicks; the strongest prompting we tried only matches a text classifier; a grounded 0.6B model beats every prompted frontier model in our runs; and whether the clicks are fitted as counts or as shares makes no detectable difference in a pre-registered test. The asked 8B model also reproduces stated-preference cues, favoring question headlines that in fact lower clicks. Where: across four behaviors, grounding wins where the text carries the signal (short-video likes, +20 points on 200 pre-registered videos), only ties an asked model given the reader's history (news clicks), and nothing text-only gets far from chance on watch time. Code, adapters and run outputs will be released.
中文翻译
语言模型越来越多地被用于模拟受众对标题或帖子的反应，以往的验证方式是基于人们所说的反馈。本文则基于人们实际的行为进行验证。当模型在真实受众点击、点赞或观看记录上进行微调时，我们称其为‘基于行为揭示的对齐模型’。我们不仅探讨为何仅通过提问无法实现对齐（如先前研究发现），还深入分析原因与具体场景。原因在于训练信号本身的缺陷，而非模型规模或提示工程；相同规模的8B模型在未对齐情况下仅能正确预测一半的点击行为，而在基于点击数据对齐后准确率提升至86.5%；最强的提示方法也仅相当于一个文本分类器；一个0.6B的对齐模型在实验中击败所有经过提示优化的前沿模型；将点击数据建模为数量或比例，在预注册测试中并无显著差异。此外，未对齐的8B模型还会错误地复制陈述偏好线索，倾向于选择实际上降低点击率的问题型标题。具体表现方面：在四种行为中，对齐在文本承载信号的任务中表现优异（如短视频点赞，200个预注册视频提升20分），在读者历史相关的任务中仅与未对齐模型持平（新闻点击），而纯文本信息在观看时长预测上几乎无法超越随机水平。代码、适配器和运行输出将公开发布。

Localizing Cross-Lingual Generalization in Parameter Space
参数空间中跨语言泛化的定位研究
Cross-lingual transfer is uneven across tasks, succeeding for natural language understanding but often failing for mathematical reasoning. We argue that this asymmetry is governed by the geometry of parameter space. Specifically, cross-lingual generalization is determined by whether English and target-language LoRA adapters share a low-error basin. To test this, we use linear mode connectivity (LMC) between English and target-language adapters, evaluating on three tasks. We find that NLI and QA model-language pairs are mostly linearly connected, whereas mathematical reasoning (Math) pairs are not, indicating that basin geometry is task-dependent. Such disconnection in Math is ``local'' rather than global. A small target-language task sample, applied only to the English adapter's language encoding or semantic composition layers, is enough to repair it. Cross-lingual generalization is also factorizable into language-specific encoding and language-agnostic composition, each supplied separately from a cheaper signal. English task data routed to the composition layers and an unlabeled target-language corpus routed to the language encoding layers each alleviate it on their own for nearly every pair. Composition is the more influential of these, and it needs no target-language data. Together, these results suggest that cross-lingual generalization decomposes into language-specific and language-agnostic components in parameter space. This decomposition offers a practical route for low-resource cross-lingual adaptation.
中文翻译
跨语言迁移在不同任务上的表现不均一，自然语言理解任务常取得成功，但数学推理任务往往失败。我们认为这种不对称性由参数空间的几何结构决定。具体而言，跨语言泛化取决于英语与目标语言的LoRA适配器是否共享一个低误差盆地。为此，我们使用英语与目标语言适配器之间的线性模式连通性（LMC）进行测试，评估三个任务。结果表明，NLI和QA模型的语言对大多呈线性连通，而数学推理（Math）对则不连通，说明盆地几何结构具有任务依赖性。Math任务中的不连通性是局部性的而非全局性的。仅用少量目标语言任务样本，作用于英语适配器的语言编码或语义组合层，即可修复该问题。跨语言泛化还可分解为语言特异性编码与语言无关的组合两部分，分别由更廉价的信号提供。将英语任务数据导向组合层，将无标签的目标语言语料导向语言编码层，各自都能有效缓解问题，几乎适用于所有语言对。其中组合部分影响更大，且无需目标语言数据。这些结果表明，跨语言泛化在参数空间中可分解为语言特异性和语言无关性成分，为低资源跨语言适应提供了实用路径。

Scoring What Matters: Exact Feedback Reduction for Language Model Comparison
关注真正重要的指标：语言模型比较中的精确反馈缩减
Comparing language-model checkpoints requires estimating performance differences, yet evaluation pipelines can pay to score responses whose contributions cancel from those differences. We introduce Contrast-Aware Feedback Acquisition (CAFA), a sampler-agnostic method that identifies which scores a requested linear report needs before acquiring feedback. CAFA groups identical scoring inputs and aggregates their report coefficients, removing queries beyond ordinary caching while preserving the fully scored result. For a fixed response bank and an otherwise unrestricted scalar scorer, we characterize necessary and sufficient query sets for exact recovery of sample means, either alone or jointly with ordinary sample covariances. On controlled updates, CAFA removes 55.8% and 90.1% of the scoring inputs remaining after caching for Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct, respectively, averaged over four generation repetitions. Across three online group relative policy optimization (GRPO) trajectories, Independent Star sampling with CAFA achieves 46.1-51.3% lower recorded generation-plus-feedback cost than independent autoregressive sampling with caching at a common empirical precision threshold (RMS MCSE $\leq 0.04$). This is a post hoc comparison of completed runs on a fixed budget grid. All 504 evaluated report cells reproduce the fully scored means and covariances to numerical precision. We provide a modular implementation supporting existing response banks, versioned caches and external scorers.

语言模型检查点的比较需要估计性能差异，但评估流程可能对那些最终贡献相互抵消的响应进行评分。我们提出一种采样无关的方法——对比感知反馈获取（CAFA），可在获取反馈前识别出线性报告所需的评分项。CAFA将相同的评分输入分组并聚合其报告系数，在保留完整评分结果的同时，避免了超出常规缓存的查询。对于固定的响应库和任意标量评分器，我们刻画了精确恢复样本均值（单独或联合普通样本协方差）所需查询集的充要条件。在受控更新中，CAFA在缓存后分别移除了Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct和SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct剩余评分输入的55.8%和90.1%，平均覆盖四次生成重复。在三个在线群体相对策略优化（GRPO）轨迹中，结合CAFA的独立星采样在相同经验精度阈值（RMS MCSE ≤ 0.04）下，相比独立自回归采样加缓存，生成+反馈成本降低了46.1%-51.3%。这是在固定预算网格上完成运行后的事后比较。所有504个评估报告单元均以数值精度重现了完全评分的均值与协方差。我们提供模块化实现，支持现有响应库、版本化缓存和外部评分器。

Exploring Multi-Player Social Games as Verifiable Training Grounds for Large Language Models
探索多人社交游戏作为大语言模型的可验证训练场
The training of socially intelligent language models often relies on large language model (LLM) judges, which are expensive, noisy, and vulnerable to reward hacking. Multi-player social games offer a natural alternative: they require open-ended communication to coordinate with teammates and infer hidden roles, while their rules provide an objective outcome which can serve as a verifiable reward for training. Using three social games, we explore how much social intelligence Qwen3 models can acquire through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Results show that against a much stronger Gemma-3-27B model, SFT and RLVR raise the win rate of Qwen3-4B from 33% to 81% on the three trained games, and from 39% to 54% on three additional held-out games. In contrast, in-context strategies such as prompting improve the win rate of Qwen3-4B by only about 6 pp across the same games we train and evaluate on. Furthermore, we show that the gains from SFT and RLVR hold across the model sizes 1.7B, 4B, and 32B. Qualitative analyses of agent behavior show signs of stronger social intelligence; for example, trained agents tend to share information more openly, check each other's claims against public evidence, and coordinate their votes. More broadly, our results demonstrate that training LLMs on verifiable outcomes in social games can cultivate generalizable social behaviors, which offers a scalable path for building socially intelligent agents.

Right Destination, Wrong Journey: Synthetic Counseling Dialogues Miss Human Interactional Dynamics
目的地正确，旅程错误：合成咨询对话遗漏人类互动动态
LLM-generated counseling dialogues are increasingly used for training and evaluating mental-health systems, yet existing evaluations focus largely on response quality and final outcomes rather than how support unfolds over time. We conduct a trajectory-centered evaluation of process fidelity across ten human, reconstructed, and synthetic counseling datasets. Our results show that synthetic dialogues often reach plausible endpoints but differ from human support in recovery dynamics and turn-by-turn responsiveness. While generation design can shift global properties such as recovery timing and trajectory structure, contingent patient--counselor responsiveness remains consistently lower in fidelity across models and generation paradigms. Our experiments on controlled synthetic datasets show that targeted generation interventions can narrow the gap with real data on several trajectory-level measures, but do not fully recover interactional fidelity, with turn-level responsiveness remaining limited. We release three controlled synthetic datasets comprising 2,050 dialogues, together with annotations and the evaluation framework.

LUCID: Statistically Grounded Self-Correction for LLM Reasoning Evaluation
LUCID：基于统计的 LLM 推理评估自校正方法
Cheap language-model raters grade chain-of-thought reasoning at scale, yet systematic step-rating errors can skew reasoning audits and solution rankings. We introduce LUCID (Loop for Unbiased Calibration via Inference-time Diagnosis), which pairs prompt self-correction with Design-based Supervised Learning (DSL). Without retraining or weight access, LUCID calibrates binary step ratings by aligning predicted and expert soundness rates. From a small expert-labeled set, LUCID summarizes rater bias in plain text and prompts the rater to re-grade unseen steps. For example, on PRM800K, GPT-5-mini grades too strictly, under-rating computation, verification, and late-trace steps most acutely. In one pass, LUCID shrinks GPT-5-mini's calibration gap from 7.8 to 0.9 percentage points in absolute predicted-expert rate difference. LUCID also halves false alarms (sound steps rated unsound) and lifts error-detection F1 on unsound steps by 32%. LUCID retains these gains with 1,000 diagnostic steps or overall-gap feedback alone. The loop also transfers across models and domains: it applies GPT-5-mini's bias profile to lift GLM-5.2's PRM800K accuracy, and it narrows calibration gaps for overly strict but capable raters across math, science, web, and logic benchmarks. Because prompt feedback shifts thresholds without improving class-balanced sound/unsound discrimination, LUCID filters re-grading with pre-/post-checks that skip already calibrated raters and roll back ineffective passes. Finally, we reuse that expert set with raw corpus ratings in DSL for calibration and regression debiasing, and apply DSL to pair-labeled steps for sound-step transitions. DSL's out-of-fold soundness probabilities lift best-of-K selection to match expert labels, while its doubly-robust estimators beat expert-only regression fits at small budgets and cut sound-step transition error by nearly two-thirds.
中文翻译
廉价的语言模型评分器可以大规模评估链式思维推理，但系统性的步骤评分错误可能扭曲推理审计和解决方案排名。我们提出 LUCID（基于推理时诊断的无偏校准循环），将提示自校正与基于设计的监督学习（DSL）相结合。无需重新训练或权重访问，LUCID 通过对齐预测和专家合理率来校准二元步骤评分。从少量专家标注数据出发，LUCID 以自然语言总结评分器偏差，并提示评分器重新评估未见步骤。例如，在 PRM800K 上，GPT-5-mini 评分过于严格，最严重地低估了计算、验证和后期追踪步骤。一次迭代后，LUCID 将 GPT-5-mini 的校准差距从 7.8 个百分点降至 0.9 个百分点（绝对预测-专家率差异）。LUCID 还使误报（被评不合理的合理步骤）减少一半，并将不合理步骤的错误检测 F1 提升 32%。即使仅使用 1,000 个诊断步骤或整体差距反馈，LUCID 也能保持这些收益。该循环还具备跨模型和跨领域的迁移能力：它将 GPT-5-mini 的偏差特征应用于提升 GLM-5.2 在 PRM800K 上的准确率，并缩小在数学、科学、网络和逻辑基准中对过于严格但能力强的评分器的校准差距。由于提示反馈仅调整阈值而未改善类别平衡的合理/不合理区分能力，LUCID 通过预/后检查过滤重评过程，跳过已校准的评分器并回滚无效迭代。最后，我们复用该专家数据集，结合原始语料评分进行 DSL 校准和回归去偏，并将 DSL 应用于成对标注步骤以识别合理步骤的转变。DSL 的交叉验证合理概率使最佳 K 选择匹配专家标签，而其双重稳健估计器在小预算下优于仅依赖专家的回归拟合，并将合理步骤转变误差减少近三分之二。
👤 Satiyabooshan Murugaboopathy, Adel Daoud, Connor T. Jerzak 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

SecondChance: Benchmarking Reliable Error Recovery in AI Agents
SecondChance：AI 智能体可靠错误恢复的基准测试
AI agents make errors while they act, and their reliability depends on what they do afterwards. Existing recovery benchmarks mostly inject faults, involve no user and score whether the task is eventually completed, so they cannot show whether an agent undid its own silent error, told the user, or faced an error that could not be fixed. We introduce SecondChance, an error checkpoint dataset and evaluation harness that profiles and measures error-recovery behaviour of AI agents. The errors are natural: they come from agents' own task attempts in $τ^2$-bench, BFCL and AppWorld. Different agents then resume from the same checkpoints under one of four conditions: no assistance, an error notice, the error location, or repair guidance. We measure error correction, task success, policy compliance, preservation of environment state, repair durability and cost. We evaluate 12 models under all four conditions in 5,760 continuations. Recovery is rare: across its environments, the best model completes only 16% of recoveries unaided and 27% with repair guidance. Correcting is not recovering: with repair guidance, Llama-3.3-70B corrects 26% of $τ^2$ errors but completes none, and 40 of 84 successful AppWorld continuations leave the error in place. SecondChance exposes these differences that task success alone cannot capture.
中文翻译
AI 智能体在执行任务时会出错，其可靠性取决于后续行为。现有的恢复基准大多通过注入故障来测试，不涉及用户，仅以任务是否最终完成作为评分标准，因此无法揭示智能体是否纠正了自身的沉默错误、是否告知用户，或是否面临无法修复的错误。我们引入 SecondChance，一个错误检查点数据集和评估工具包，用于剖析和度量 AI 智能体的错误恢复行为。这些错误是自然产生的：来源于智能体在 $τ^2$-bench、BFCL 和 AppWorld 中自身任务尝试的结果。不同智能体在四种条件下从相同检查点继续执行：无协助、错误通知、错误位置提示、修复指导。我们测量错误纠正、任务成功、策略合规性、环境状态保留、修复持久性和成本。我们在 5,760 次延续中对 12 个模型在所有四种条件下进行了评估。恢复极为罕见：在各环境中，表现最好的模型在无帮助情况下仅能独立完成 16% 的恢复，有修复指导时也仅达 27%。纠正不等于恢复：在修复指导下，Llama-3.3-70B 能纠正 $τ^2$ 错误的 26%，但一项任务也未完成；在 84 次成功的 AppWorld 延续中，有 40 次错误仍被保留。SecondChance 揭示了仅靠任务成功无法捕捉的这些差异。

Reliability of Language-Model Judges for Quantitative Scientific Question Answering: Evidence from Perovskite Photovoltaics
语言模型裁判在定量科学问答中的可靠性：基于钙钛矿光伏的证据
Evaluating retrieval-augmented language models increasingly relies on automated model judges, yet their reliability on quantitative scientific synthesis remains largely unmeasured. Here, we evaluate automated grading on a quantitative benchmark for perovskite photovoltaics, where all 755 ground-truth answers are deterministically computed from 45,441 experimental device records. We evaluate a frontier judge (Claude Opus 5.5) alongside two local open-weight judges (Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-8B) across 4,530 free-text answers from six retrieval architectures under three regimes: reference-augmented (with the computed key), evidence-augmented (with retrieved passages and query logs), and unassisted (question and answer alone). Equipped with the reference key, Claude Opus reproduces ground truth near-perfectly (Cohen's $κ= 0.982 \pm 0.003$) and preserves exact architectural rankings ($τ= 1.00$). Withholding the reference causes evaluation fidelity to collapse: frontier agreement drops to $κ= 0.557 \pm 0.022$, and providing retrieved literature passages yields virtually no improvement ($κ= 0.576 \pm 0.024$). Without reference keys, local judges degrade to near-random agreement ($κ= 0.087$ and $0.112$), inverting the leaderboard to rank a hallucinating closed-book baseline first ($τ\le 0.20$). Furthermore, SQL-augmented generations induce severe evaluative blind spots: absent a reference, the frontier judge accepts 46.6% of erroneous SQL answers, overestimating architecture accuracy as 66.4% against its true 52.6%. These findings demonstrate that model-based evaluation in the physical sciences cannot rely on unstructured text inspection; rigorous benchmarking requires reference ground truth precomputed from structured measurement records.
中文翻译
评估检索增强型语言模型越来越多地依赖自动化模型裁判，但其在定量科学综合任务中的可靠性尚未得到充分衡量。本文在钙钛矿光伏的定量基准上评估自动化评分，所有755个真实答案均从45,441条实验设备记录中确定性计算得出。我们评估了前沿裁判（Claude Opus 5.5）以及两个本地开源裁判（Qwen3-14B 和 Qwen3-8B），涵盖六种检索架构在三种模式下的4,530个自由文本回答：参考增强（含计算出的关键信息）、证据增强（含检索段落和查询日志）、无辅助（仅问题与答案）。在提供参考关键信息时，Claude Opus 几乎完美复现真实答案（Cohen's κ = 0.982 ± 0.003），并保持精确的架构排名（τ = 1.00）。若省略参考信息，评估保真度急剧下降：前沿裁判一致性降至 κ = 0.557 ± 0.022，提供检索文献段落也几乎无改善（κ = 0.576 ± 0.024）。在无参考键的情况下，本地裁判退化为接近随机的一致性（κ = 0.087 和 0.112），导致排行榜反转，使一个产生幻觉的闭卷基线模型排名第一（τ ≤ 0.20）。此外，SQL 增强生成引入严重评估盲点：缺乏参考信息时，前沿裁判接受46.6%的错误SQL答案，将架构准确率高估至66.4%，而真实准确率为52.6%。这些发现表明，在物理科学中基于模型的评估不能依赖非结构化文本检查；严格的基准测试必须基于结构化测量记录预先计算的真实答案。

Agentic Systems Need Multi-Run Evaluation: A Study of Retrieval and Response Variability in a Deployed Enterprise RAG System
智能体系统需要多轮评估：部署的企业级 RAG 系统中检索与响应可变性的研究
Previous RAG systems have mostly been evaluated in a single-run setting, and with the advent of agentic RAG, systems are still often evaluated the same way. However, agentic RAG systems introduce a source of run-to-run variability, since several of their components make decisions non-deterministically. Such systems typically comprise a retrieval stage followed by a generation stage, and the retrieval stage in particular can behave differently across runs. For example, the agent might issue a different number of parallel searches from one run to the next, which alters the retrieved documents and therefore the final answer. In this paper we study this behavior in a deployed agentic RAG system for product-documentation questions, running each of 739 questions ten times and analyzing the execution traces to find where different runs first diverge. We find a substantial gap between success in any run and success in every run: the retrieval hit rate is 69.4% when the correct page is found in at least one run, but 50.9% when it must be found in all ten. Responses also stay similar in meaning while varying in wording, with an average BERTScore of 0.90 and a BLEU of 0.49. Trace-level analysis shows that retrieval is the largest source of divergence (43.9%), and the number of parallel searches at the first retrieval step alone accounts for 20.1% of divergent pairs. Taken together, these results show that single-run evaluation can hide reliability and consistency issues in agentic RAG systems, and that multi-run evaluation is necessary to reveal them.

以往的 RAG 系统大多在单轮设置下进行评估，随着智能体 RAG 的出现，系统仍常以相同方式评估。然而，智能体 RAG 系统引入了轮次间可变性，因其多个组件决策具有非确定性。这类系统通常包含检索阶段和生成阶段，其中检索阶段在不同轮次间表现可能不同。例如，智能体可能在不同轮次中发起不同数量的并行搜索，从而改变检索文档，进而影响最终答案。本文研究了一个用于产品文档问答的部署型智能体 RAG 系统的行为，对 739 个问题各运行十次，并分析执行轨迹以定位不同轮次首次分歧的位置。我们发现，至少一次成功与全部十次成功之间存在显著差距：当正确页面在至少一轮中被找到时，检索命中率为 69.4%，而在所有十轮中都必须找到时，命中率降至 50.9%。响应在语义上保持相似，但在措辞上存在差异，平均 BERTScore 为 0.90，BLEU 为 0.49。轨迹级分析显示，检索是主要的分歧来源（43.9%），而首次检索步骤中并行搜索数量的差异单独就占了 20.1% 的分歧对。综上所述，单轮评估可能掩盖智能体 RAG 系统中的可靠性和一致性问题，多轮评估对于揭示这些问题至关重要。

How Persona Conditioning Changes Open-Ended Task Execution: A Paired Evaluation of Behavioral Gains and Reliability Costs
人格设定如何改变开放式任务执行：行为收益与可靠性成本的配对评估
Large language models (LLMs) are often prompted with role- or persona-based instructions, yet persona conditioning has mainly been evaluated through character fidelity or aggregate benchmark accuracy. We study persona conditioning as a task-execution steering signal that changes how models interpret, structure, and sustain responses. Using paired instances derived from real user role-play logs and expanded into simulated multi-turn continuations, we compare with-persona and no-persona conditions while fixing task content, constraints, user simulation, and evaluation criteria. Across eight models, persona conditioning improves expert content depth and procedural fidelity, and these gains survive length control. At the same time, it lowers strict instruction-following accuracy by 17 to 30 points in every model evaluated, and increases unsupported real-world specificity through persona-induced elaboration. These results suggest that persona prompting is not a general-purpose performance improvement technique, but a selective task-execution policy shift with both behavioral gains and reliability risks.

Evaluating Large Language Models on Iranian Doctoral Entrance Exams in Electrical and Computer Engineering
在伊朗电气与计算机工程博士入学考试中评估大型语言模型
We evaluate four large language models (LLMs) on multiple-choice questions from the 2024 and 2025 Iranian national Ph.D. entrance examinations in Electrical Engineering (EE) and Computer Engineering (CE). Of the 440 questions in the four official booklets, 7 were excluded because the question statement or its figure was ambiguous, leaving 433 questions (186 EE, 247 CE), of which 171 (39.5\%) depend on a figure. Each question was presented in the original Persian and in an English translation, giving 3,464 scored responses. GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Claude Sonnet 4.5 and Grok 4 were queried zero-shot, with reasoning enabled, through a single commercial web interface (Perplexity) in October 2025, using an automated script. Because every model answers the same items, all inferences use paired tests or question-clustered models. Accuracy separates into two tiers: GPT-5 (79.9\%, 95\% CI 76.6--83.1) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (78.4\%, 74.8--81.9) are statistically indistinguishable, and both outperform Grok 4 (71.7\%) and Claude Sonnet 4.5 (71.3\%) by 6.7--8.7 percentage points (Holm-adjusted $p < 0.001$). The English--Persian accuracy difference is small for every model ($-0.2$ to $+2.5$ points; exact McNemar $p \ge 0.28$). For three models the 90\% confidence interval lies within $\pm 5$ points, so differences larger than this can be excluded. Questions with figures are much harder: the odds ratio is 0.30 (95\% CI 0.21--0.43) after adjusting for discipline. The apparent EE deficit of two models disappears once the larger share of figure-based EE items is accounted for. We describe the dataset, the protocol and its limitations, including the uncontrolled web-retrieval and decoding settings of the interface, and release item-level responses and analysis code.
中文翻译
我们评估了四种大型语言模型（LLMs）在2024年和2025年伊朗全国电气工程（EE）与计算机工程（CE）博士入学考试中的多项选择题表现。在四本官方题册共440道题目中，有7道因题干或图表模糊被剔除，剩余433道题目（EE 186道，CE 247道），其中171道（39.5%）依赖图表。每道题目以原始波斯语和英文翻译两种形式呈现，共产生3,464次评分响应。GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Grok 4通过Perplexity单个商业网页接口在2025年10月进行零样本查询，启用推理模式，并使用自动化脚本。由于所有模型回答相同题目，所有推断均采用配对检验或按题目聚类的模型。准确率分为两档：GPT-5（79.9%，95%置信区间76.6–83.1）与Gemini 2.5 Pro（78.4%，74.8–81.9）统计上无差异，且均显著优于Grok 4（71.7%）和Claude Sonnet 4.5（71.3%），差距达6.7–8.7个百分点（Holm校正p < 0.001）。各模型在波斯语与英语版本间的准确率差异较小（-0.2至+2.5点；McNemar精确p ≥ 0.28）。对三个模型而言，90%置信区间在±5点以内，因此大于此范围的差异可排除。含图题目难度显著更高：调整学科因素后，优势比为0.30（95%置信区间0.21–0.43）。两个模型在EE上的表现劣势在考虑EE题中更多图表题后消失。我们描述了数据集、实验协议及其局限性，包括界面中未受控的网络检索与解码设置，并公开了逐题响应及分析代码。

RegioZH: Unveiling Comprehension Disparities across Regional Chinese Varieties in LLMs
RegioZH：揭示大语言模型在汉语区域变体中的理解差异
Current evaluations often treat Chinese varieties as monolithic, obscuring failures for regional users. We introduce RegioZH, a 2,607-item multiple-choice benchmark for regional lexical reasoning across Mainland China, Taiwan, Hong Kong, Singapore, and Malaysia. Built by region-specific annotators and expert-validated, it targets localized idioms, slang, code-mixing, politeness conventions, and sociocultural expressions. Zero-shot results on eleven LLMs show large disparities, including a 29.4-point average accuracy gap between Taiwan and Singapore. Persona and structured chain-of-thought prompts for five models yield selective gains, suggesting that some errors stem from retrieval or framing rather than only missing knowledge. Representation, attention, calibration, and surface-feature analyses show a dissociation between marker attention and semantic grounding, overconfident forced-choice errors, and localized lexical density as a consistent difficulty predictor. Qualitative analysis finds terminology misunderstandings, sociocultural inference gaps, and cross-regional semantic divergence. RegioZH demonstrates that apparent Chinese proficiency can mask uneven understanding across regional varieties, motivating intra-language robustness evaluation alongside multilingual generalization.
中文翻译
当前评估常将汉语变体视为单一整体，掩盖了对区域性用户的失败。我们提出RegioZH，一个包含2,607道题目的多选基准，用于评估中国大陆、台湾、香港、新加坡和马来西亚地区之间的词汇推理能力。该基准由区域特定标注者构建并经专家验证，聚焦于本地俚语、混合用语、礼貌规范、社会文化表达等。对十一种LLMs的零样本测试显示显著差异，例如台湾与新加坡之间平均准确率差距达29.4分。针对五种模型的人格设定和结构化思维链提示带来选择性提升，表明部分错误源于检索或框架问题，而非仅知识缺失。表示、注意力、校准和表面特征分析显示标记注意力与语义基础之间存在分离，过度自信的强制选择错误，以及局部词汇密度作为一致的难度预测因子。定性分析发现术语误解、社会文化推理缺口及跨区域语义分歧。RegioZH表明，看似的中文熟练度可能掩盖不同区域变体间理解不均衡的问题，推动在多语言泛化之外开展语言内鲁棒性评估。

Does Item Response Theory Generalize Across Languages? Evidence from Cross-Lingual LLM Benchmarks
项目反应理论能否跨语言推广？来自跨语言大模型基准的证据
The application of Item Response Theory (IRT) for data selection in benchmarks has mostly been limited to monolingual settings. In this work, we ask: how does IRT generalize when the same benchmark is translated across languages? We conduct experiments on Multilingual GSM and Global MMLU, covering over 100 language models and 11 languages. Fitting 1PL, 2PL, and 3PL IRT models per language, we assess cross-lingual consistency of parameters and whether IRT-filtered subsets preserve model rankings across languages: item difficulty emerges as the most stable parameter followed by guessing and then discrimination. A differential item analysis also shows that lack of stability can be tracked to specific items shifting under translation across languages. Our findings suggest IRT-based selection in multilingual settings must be both parameter-aware and item-aware, validating model choice and screening individual items before assuming cross-lingual generalization.
中文翻译
项目反应理论（IRT）在基准数据选择中的应用目前主要局限于单语环境。本文探讨：当同一基准在多种语言间翻译时，IRT的推广性如何？我们在涵盖超过100个语言模型和11种语言的Multilingual GSM与Global MMLU上进行实验。为每种语言拟合1PL、2PL和3PL IRT模型，评估参数的跨语言一致性以及IRT筛选子集是否保持模型在不同语言间的排名：项目难度是稳定性最高的参数，其次是猜测参数，最后是区分度。差异项目分析还显示，稳定性不足可追踪至特定项目在翻译过程中发生偏移。研究结果表明，多语言环境下基于IRT的数据选择必须兼顾参数意识与项目意识，需在假设跨语言推广前验证模型选择并筛查个别项目。

RecipeADQA: A User-aware Question-Answering Dataset for Multi-Constraint Recipe Recommendation
RecipeADQA：一种面向多约束菜谱推荐的用户感知问答数据集
Question-answering recipe recommendation tasks lack datasets with ground-truth labels. Existing methods often generate datasets from simple combinations of ingredient or tag constraints, leading to unnatural expressions and unreasonable constraints that are hard to reflect users' real preferences. This paper proposes a construction method for a benchmark-style QA dataset for natural-language recipe recommendation. Driven by real user behavior, our method integrates external user query and review data to extract fine-grained preference signals by LLMs and maps them onto a knowledge-graph-based tag schema, thereby constructing QA data consisting of preference and ingredient constraints. Besides, a greedy strategy for constraint selection is also used to control the answer size, while tag-based re-ranking is applied to improve data quality. The generated dataset RecipeADQA contains natural-language questions, structured constraints, and answer sets, resulting in 128,465 instances. Experiments compare RecipeADQA with baseline datasets from two perspectives: data quality and retrieval-recommendation performance. The results show that ours outperforms existing methods on the same recipe knowledge graph in coverage, higher question quality in naturalness and constraint rationality, and better queryability. Moreover, we design a noise-augmented retrieve-and-rerank model, in which a lightweight parser first retrieves candidate recipes, and a small language model then selects and ranks relevant recipes from a noisy candidate pool. The model trained on RecipeADQA outperforms the same model trained on the baseline datasets. The relevant construction code and data have been open-sourced at a GitHub repository https://github.com/MorimoLab/RecipeADQA.
中文翻译
问答式菜谱推荐任务缺乏带有真实标签的数据集。现有方法通常通过简单组合食材或标签约束生成数据，导致表达不自然且约束不合理，难以反映用户的实际偏好。本文提出了一种构建基准风格QA数据集的方法，用于自然语言菜谱推荐。该方法基于真实用户行为，整合外部用户查询和评论数据，利用大语言模型提取细粒度偏好信号，并将其映射到基于知识图谱的标签体系中，从而构建包含偏好与食材约束的QA数据。此外，采用贪心策略控制约束选择以限制答案规模，同时使用基于标签的重排序提升数据质量。生成的数据集RecipeADQA包含自然语言问题、结构化约束和答案集，共128,465个实例。实验从数据质量和检索-推荐性能两个角度对比了RecipeADQA与基线数据集，结果表明其在覆盖范围、问题自然性与约束合理性方面优于现有方法，且更具可查询性。此外，我们设计了一种噪声增强的检索-重排序模型，其中轻量级解析器首先检索候选菜谱，小语言模型再从噪声候选池中选择并排序相关菜谱。在RecipeADQA上训练的模型优于在基线数据集上训练的相同模型。相关代码与数据已开源至GitHub仓库 https://github.com/MorimoLab/RecipeADQA。

INCLUDE-2.0: Disentangling Knowledge Gaps in Multilingual LLM Evaluation
INCLUDE-2.0：解耦多语言大模型评估中的知识缺口
Current multilingual benchmarks cover only a fraction of the languages represented in modern pretraining corpora and often conflate language proficiency with the ability to answer questions grounded in specific regions and communities. We introduce INCLUDE-2.0, a large-scale benchmark for region-aware multilingual factual evaluation, which covers 91 languages from 86 countries (127 total language--country pairs), comprising over 220K curated samples drawn from native regional sources. Unlike existing benchmarks that often treat language as a proxy for region, IINCLUDE-2.0 distinguishes between questions whose answers are independent of context, vary by country, concern widely documented facts about a place, or depend on knowledge primarily expressed through local linguistic and cultural sources. Each item is annotated with its regional knowledge type and domain, and paired with an English translation, enabling controlled analysis of the relationship between language proficiency and possession of regional knowledge. Evaluating frontier and open-weight models on IINCLUDE-2.0, we find that aggregate multilingual accuracy masks substantial variation across regional knowledge types, languages, and regions. We find that frontier models perform worse on locally embedded knowledge than on widely documented facts about regions, with an average drop of ~14.5 points. We also show that for open-weight models, scale improves accuracy but does not reliably make performance invariant to whether a question is asked in English or its original language. INCLUDE-2.0 is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/epfl-nlp/include-2.0
中文翻译
当前多语言基准仅涵盖现代预训练语料库中部分语言，且常将语言熟练度与回答特定区域和社区背景问题的能力混淆。我们引入INCLUDE-2.0，一个大规模、面向区域感知的多语言事实评估基准，覆盖86个国家的91种语言（共127个语言-国家组合），包含超过22万条从本地区域来源精心筛选的样本。不同于现有基准常将语言作为区域代理，INCLUDE-2.0区分了四类问题：答案与上下文无关的问题、因国家而异的问题、关于某地广泛记录的事实问题，以及主要依赖本地语言与文化资源表达的知识问题。每项数据标注其区域知识类型与领域，并附有英文翻译，支持对语言熟练度与区域知识掌握之间关系的受控分析。在INCLUDE-2.0上评估前沿模型与开源权重模型，发现总体多语言准确率掩盖了区域知识类型、语言与地区间的显著差异。我们发现前沿模型在本地嵌入知识上的表现低于对广泛记录的区域事实的表现，平均下降约14.5个百分点。同时，我们还表明，对于开源权重模型，规模虽能提升准确率，但并不能可靠地使性能不受问题以英语还是原语言提出的影响。INCLUDE-2.0 已在 https://huggingface.co/datasets/epfl-nlp/include-2.0 公开。

The Date You Didn't Set: The System-Prompt Date as a Hidden Variable in LLM Evaluation
你未设定的日期：大语言模型评估中的系统提示日期作为隐藏变量
The system prompt of many instruction-tuned models carries a date, usually today's, filled in automatically for conversational and agentic requests alike, so an unchanged evaluation can send a different prompt each day. In this paper, we explore how much this hidden input changes evaluation outcomes. Across six instruction-tuned models and four benchmarks, with prompts, decoding, and configuration fixed, every model is affected: two plausible dates (2024--2026) switch a median $6.6\%$ of test items between correct and incorrect. Even rerunning the evaluation one day later flips about as many items as changing the batch size, although nothing in the setup was changed. In addition, using a grammar-constrained variant of Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) to search dates of any year, we find dates that cost the Llama-3.x models up to $36.5$ points of held-out accuracy. Beyond accuracy, switching to another plausible date alters LLM-judge verdicts and undoes $15$--$32\%$ of reproduced jailbreak successes on Llama-3.2-3B. Yet none of the eleven evaluation stacks we audit pins the date. With the date and the rest of the setup fixed, reruns reproduce byte-for-byte; we therefore recommend pinning, logging, and reporting the date.
中文翻译
许多指令微调模型的系统提示中包含一个日期，通常为当前日期，自动填充于对话和代理请求中，导致未更改的评估每天发送不同的提示。本文探讨这一隐藏输入对评估结果的影响。在六种指令微调模型和四个基准测试中，固定提示、解码方式和配置后，每种模型均受影响：两个合理的日期（2024–2026年）使中位数6.6%的测试项在正确与错误之间切换。即使仅延迟一天重新运行评估，也会翻转约与改变批大小相当数量的项目，而设置本身未作任何改动。此外，使用一种语法约束的贪心坐标梯度（GCG）变体搜索任意年份的日期，发现某些日期会使Llama-3.x模型的保留准确率下降高达36.5分。除了准确率外，切换到另一个合理日期还会改变LLM裁判的判断，并撤销Llama-3.2-3B模型约15%–32%的复现越狱成功案例。然而，在我们审计的十一个评估堆栈中，没有一个明确锁定日期。在日期和其他设置固定的情况下，重复运行可实现逐字节重现；因此我们建议锁定、记录并报告日期。
👤 Noureldin Zahran, Mohammed E. Fouda, Khaled N. Khasawneh 📅 2026-10-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31252]

DAGGER: Deployment-Free Quality Estimation for Generative AI Workflows
DAGGER：无部署的生成式AI工作流质量估计
Estimating the quality of generative AI workflows is challenging because these workflows comprise multiple stages, and errors can propagate across them, making end-to-end performance depend not only on the accuracy of individual models but also on how their outputs affect downstream stages. Each stage can use one of many candidate models. Estimating end-to-end workflow quality by deploying and evaluating every resulting model configuration is therefore prohibitively expensive. As a result, existing approaches face a tradeoff between estimation cost and accuracy: inexpensive methods often produce unreliable quality estimates, while accurate methods require deploying and evaluating many workflow configurations. In this paper, we introduce DAGGER, a framework for estimating the quality of generative AI workflows without exhaustive deployment and profiling. DAGGER represents a workflow as a graph, in which nodes correspond to stages and edges capture how outputs from one stage affect subsequent stages. It then decomposes workflow quality into per-node accuracy terms and per-edge dependency terms. Using a novel analytical formulation and SVD-based matrix completion, DAGGER estimates workflow quality from sparse observations without executing every candidate workflow. We evaluate DAGGER on three generative AI workflows across three datasets. DAGGER estimates workflow quality with a median error of 6.8 percentage points while reducing profiling cost by 10.2$\times$ relative to BenchPress, a state-of-the-art baseline, and selects workflow configurations competitive with state-of-the-art frameworks such as LLMSelector.
中文翻译
估计生成式AI工作流的质量具有挑战性，因为这些工作流包含多个阶段，错误可能在各阶段间传播，使得端到端性能不仅取决于单个模型的准确性，还取决于其输出对下游阶段的影响。每个阶段可选用多种候选模型。因此，通过部署并评估所有可能的工作流配置来估计端到端质量是极其昂贵的。现有方法面临成本与精度之间的权衡：低成本方法常产生不可靠的质量估计，而高精度方法需要部署和评估大量工作流配置。本文提出DAGGER，一种无需全面部署和性能分析即可估计生成式AI工作流质量的框架。DAGGER将工作流表示为图结构，节点对应阶段，边表示前一阶段输出对后续阶段的影响。它将工作流质量分解为节点级准确率项和边级依赖项。通过一种新颖的解析公式和基于SVD的矩阵补全技术，DAGGER仅从稀疏观测中估计工作流质量，而无需执行所有候选配置。我们在三个生成式AI工作流和三个数据集上评估DAGGER，结果显示其质量估计的中位误差仅为6.8个百分点，相比最先进的基线BenchPress，性能分析成本降低了10.2倍，并能选择出与LLMSelector等先进框架相媲美的工作流配置。

Validation Bias in Large Language Model Responses to Cancer Treatment Queries: A Factorial Cross-Sectional Study
大型语言模型在癌症治疗咨询中的验证偏差：一项因子横断面研究
Large language model (LLM) chatbots are increasingly used by patients seeking cancer information, but their safety when users present evidence-misaligned beliefs about treatment remains uncharacterized. We hypothesized that LLMs exhibit validation bias, affirming rather than correcting such beliefs, and evaluated this with a 2x2x2 factorial design crossing conversation format (single-turn vs multi-turn), treatment type (evidence-based vs alternative), and query valence (positive vs negative). Framing was evidence-aligned (supporting evidence-based treatments or questioning alternative therapies) or evidence-misaligned (promoting alternative treatments or questioning evidence-based therapies). We analyzed 1,272 conversations (6,360 interactions) across 14 LLMs from 6 vendors, scored by an LLM judge (GPT-5-mini) validated against human annotation on a 6-dimension safety rubric. The primary outcome was the unsafe response rate, defined as a Weak classification or any critical failure. Evidence-misaligned framing increased unsafe response rates compared with evidence-aligned framing (14.4% vs 5.1%; OR 3.14, 95% CI 2.07-4.78; P < .001), and the association persisted after accounting for clustering by LLM. Among 429 conversations classified as Strong under evidence-aligned framing, 22.6% were downgraded in their paired evidence-misaligned counterpart. Multi-turn conversations had lower unsafe response rates than single-turn conversations (5.2% vs 14.7%; P < .001). Unsafe response rates varied by vendor from 1.8% to 36.8%. LLMs exhibited significant validation bias in oncology, with unsafe responses nearly tripling under evidence-misaligned framing. Evaluations limited to neutral, fact-seeking queries may overestimate real-world safety, and safety testing of medical AI systems should include evidence-misaligned user framing.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）聊天机器人越来越多地被患者用于获取癌症相关信息，但当用户持有与证据不符的治疗信念时，其安全性尚未得到充分评估。我们假设LLMs存在验证偏差，即倾向于确认而非纠正此类信念，并通过2×2×2因子设计进行评估，交叉考察对话格式（单轮 vs 多轮）、治疗类型（基于证据的 vs 替代疗法）和查询情感倾向（正面 vs 负面）。表述分为证据一致（支持基于证据的治疗或质疑替代疗法）和证据不一致（推广替代疗法或质疑基于证据的治疗）。我们在14个来自6家供应商的LLM上分析了1,272次对话（共6,360次交互），由经验证的LLM裁判（GPT-5-mini）根据六维安全评分标准打分。主要结果为不安全响应率，定义为弱分类或任何关键失败。与证据一致表述相比，证据不一致表述显著提高了不安全响应率（14.4% vs 5.1%；OR 3.14，95% CI 2.07–4.78；P < .001），且该关联在控制LLM聚类后依然存在。在429次被归类为“强”的证据一致表述对话中，有22.6%在对应的证据不一致版本中被降级。多轮对话的不安全响应率低于单轮对话（5.2% vs 14.7%；P < .001）。不同厂商的不安全响应率从1.8%到36.8%不等。LLMs在肿瘤学领域表现出显著的验证偏差，证据不一致表述下的不安全响应几乎翻三倍。仅限于中立、事实性查询的评估可能高估真实世界的安全性，医疗AI系统的安全性测试应包含证据不一致的用户表述。

A Bias in Disguise: Tiny Gradient Edits Stop a Small Language Model's Thinking Loops and Complement a Frontier Model's Weight Edit
伪装的偏差：微小梯度编辑阻止小型语言模型的思维循环并补充前沿模型的权重编辑
Qwen3.5-0.8B rarely finishes reasoning in thinking mode: at a 4,096-token budget only 1 or 2 of 500 MATH-500 answers close their think block (two seeds), and accuracy is 15.4%. A recent demonstration had GPT-6 Astra edit 131,072 of the model's weights without gradients and reported MATH-500 rising from 7.9% to 48.1% at a 32,768-token budget. We provide the missing gradient baseline, then combine the two edits. Fine-tuning only the 140 to 139,750 weights with the largest gradient, on the model's own correct answers to MATH training problems, raises thinking-mode MATH-500 at 4k tokens to 39.0-45.5% after 2-9 minutes of training on one GPU; 50 training examples suffice, and 1,398 random positions give only 16.3%. The gradient concentrates the edit on the most nearly constant channel of each of the first three layers. Because the model has no bias terms, an edit confined to those channels acts as a constant vector added to the residual stream: adding three fixed vectors to the unedited model matches the accuracy of such an edit. Under the Astra protocol (32k tokens, presence penalty 1.5), the Astra edit scores 52.1% (finished and correct) in our reproduction and our full-strength edits reach 45.6-45.7%, but ours win at every budget up to 16k tokens (39.5% against 6.7% at 4k). The two edits are complementary. The Astra edit plus our 139,750-weight edit at half strength scores 62.2% at 32k, 10 points above the Astra edit alone (p = 3e-9), with half as many tokens; our half-strength edit alone scores 46.7%.
中文翻译
Qwen3.5-0.8B在思考模式下很少能完成推理：在4,096个token预算下，仅500个MATH-500问题中有1到2个关闭了思考块（两个种子），准确率为15.4%。最近一项演示显示，GPT-6 Astra在不使用梯度的情况下修改了模型的131,072个权重，报告在32,768个token预算下MATH-500准确率从7.9%提升至48.1%。我们提供了缺失的梯度基线，然后结合两种编辑方法。仅对模型自身正确答案上的140至139,750个梯度最大的权重进行微调，在单个GPU上训练2-9分钟后，4k token预算下的思考模式MATH-500准确率提升至39.0%-45.5%；仅需50个训练样本，而随机选择1,398个位置仅得16.3%。梯度集中于前三层中最接近恒定的通道。由于模型无偏置项，仅限于这些通道的编辑相当于向残差流添加一个常数向量：向未编辑模型添加三个固定向量即可达到类似编辑的准确率。在Astra协议（32k token，存在惩罚1.5）下，我们复现的Astra编辑得分为52.1%（完成且正确），而我们的全强度编辑达到45.6%-45.7%，但在每个预算（最高16k token）下均表现更优（4k时为39.5% vs 6.7%）。两种编辑具有互补性。Astra编辑与我们半强度的139,750权重编辑结合，在32k token下得分为62.2%，比Astra单独编辑高出10个百分点（p=3e-9），且仅需一半token；我们半强度编辑单独使用得分为46.7%。

Towards Realistic Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification for LLM-Based Recommender Systems
迈向大语言模型推荐系统不确定性量化的真实评估
Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for recommendation systems. However, their issued rankings require reliable instance-level uncertainty monitoring. In this scenario, comparing uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods becomes challenging because LLM rankers lack a standard item-classification head. Their scores may depend on option labels, prompts, generation procedures, and the available user and item evidence. Existing methods derive uncertainty from different sources and apply it at different stages of the recommendation process. Some methods evaluate an issued ranking, whereas others use uncertainty to change the ranking itself, making direct comparison difficult. Here we introduce a fixed-ranking benchmark which evaluates eight heterogeneous UQ signals as record-level monitors under a shared output contract. We evaluate these signals across four recommendation datasets and two LLM backbones under a shared fixed-ranking protocol. From the results, we draw three main observations. First, UQ method ordering changes across backbones even on the same reference instances. Second, ranking-aligned Top10Mass provides the strongest scalar Error@10 detection in several evaluation conditions, reflecting effective cutoff-aligned monitoring, while its advantage narrows for popularity-tail targets. Third, scalar UQ signals are weak at identifying which control has the larger clipped NDCG@10 difference, whereas source-specific signals, including Perturbation Rank Instability and simple listwise distances, provide stronger paired-control agreement, reflecting their sensitivity to the two controlled evidence sources. These findings motivate us to evaluate ranking-failure detection, robustness across recommendation conditions, and responses to controlled evidence changes as distinct properties of UQ.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）为推荐系统提供了灵活的接口。然而，其生成的排名需要可靠的实例级不确定性监控。在此场景中，比较不确定性量化（UQ）方法具有挑战性，因为LLM排名器缺乏标准的项目分类头。其得分可能依赖于选项标签、提示、生成过程以及可用的用户和项目证据。现有方法从不同来源推导不确定性，并在推荐流程的不同阶段应用。一些方法评估已生成的排名，而另一些则使用不确定性改变排名本身，导致直接比较困难。我们引入了一个固定排名基准，评估八种异构的UQ信号作为记录级监控器，在统一的输出契约下进行评估。我们在四个推荐数据集和两个LLM骨干网络上，采用统一的固定排名协议评估这些信号。结果得出三个主要观察：第一，即使在同一参考实例上，UQ方法排序也随骨干网络变化；第二，与排名对齐的Top10Mass在多种评估条件下提供最强的标量Error@10检测能力，反映有效的截断对齐监控，但在流行度尾部目标上的优势减弱；第三，标量UQ信号在识别哪个控制具有更大的截断NDCG@10差异方面表现较弱，而源特定信号（如扰动排名不稳定性及简单列表距离）提供更强的成对控制一致性，反映出对两个受控证据源的敏感性。这些发现促使我们将排名失败检测、跨推荐条件的鲁棒性以及对受控证据变化的响应视为UQ的独立属性。

Can LLM Judges Catch Unsafe Clinical Actions? A Fault-Injection Study
大语言模型裁判能否识别不安全的临床操作？一项故障注入研究
As clinical AI agents begin to interact with electronic health records (EHRs), large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate their actions. But how reliably can these judges recognize clinically dangerous mistakes, especially when an agent appears to have completed its task successfully? We investigate this question using MedAgentBench by introducing controlled clinical errors into verified agent trajectories. The experiments include 416 faulty trajectories, eight error categories, and six open-weight LLM judges. By comparing each faulty trajectory with its correct counterpart, we measure whether judges genuinely recognize errors rather than simply flagging more actions as unsafe. The findings show that access to clinical evidence significantly improves judge reliability. When shown only the final answer, judges cannot distinguish errors that leave it unchanged, while flagging 56% of correct trajectories. With full trajectory information, the ability to distinguish unsafe actions from correct ones reaches a score of 0.75, and providing actual EHR responses improves discrimination by 0.39. Surprisingly, including the agent's reasoning does not improve overall performance and reduces detection of incorrectly reported clinical values by 0.18, which remain the hardest errors to detect. MedGemma performed worse than the general-purpose model and was the only judge to miss potentially dangerous medication overdoses. We also find that the benchmark's original grader accepts all 38 trajectories with an intentionally skipped laboratory check, while combining LLM judges with a rule-based checker informed by the full EHR record detects 97% of injected faults.
中文翻译
随着临床AI代理开始与电子健康记录（EHR）交互，大型语言模型（LLMs）被越来越多地用于评估其行为。但这些裁判在代理看似成功完成任务时，能否可靠地识别出临床上危险的错误？我们通过在MedAgentBench中引入受控的临床错误来研究这一问题。实验包含416条有缺陷的轨迹、八个错误类别以及六个开源权重的LLM裁判。通过将每条有缺陷的轨迹与其正确对应轨迹进行比较，我们衡量裁判是否真正识别错误，而非仅仅将更多动作标记为不安全。研究发现，访问临床证据显著提升了裁判的可靠性。当仅看到最终答案时，裁判无法区分那些结果不变的错误，且会将56%的正确轨迹标记为不安全；而提供完整轨迹信息后，区分不安全行为与正确行为的能力得分达到0.75，若提供实际EHR响应，辨别能力进一步提升0.39。令人意外的是，包含代理的推理过程并未提高整体性能，反而使错误报告的临床值检测率下降0.18，这类错误仍是最难检测的。MedGemma的表现不如通用模型，是唯一漏检潜在危险药物过量的裁判。我们还发现，基准原评分器接受了所有38条故意跳过实验室检查的轨迹，而结合LLM裁判与基于完整EHR记录的规则检查器可检测到97%注入的故障。

"I Do Intend to Deliver": Will LLMs Run a Marketplace Advance-Fee Scam?
我确实打算交付”：大语言模型会实施市场预付款诈骗吗？
Online fraud is traditionally limited by the cost of the human labor needed to talk to potential victims. We ask whether hosted large language models will now supply that labor. We prompted ten off-the-shelf models to play the seller in the "free item, just pay delivery" advance-fee scam common on buy-and-sell sites, using a plain system prompt with no jailbreak. Each model played 30 conversations against each of four buyer personas, from credulous to guarded, with the same model playing the buyer (1,200 conversations). We label each conversation by what happened to the buyer's money, and validate the labels against two human coders. 737 conversations (61%) ended with the buyer paying in advance. Eight of the ten models played in almost every conversation, and running both sides cost about 2.5 cents per payment. Only the guarded buyer, who wanted a findable identity, resisted, and the most willing sellers got past it by inventing names and phone numbers. Sellers that recognized the scam still took the money in 133 conversations, usually reasoning about their own honesty rather than the buyer's risk. Several models protected the buyer without refusing, by conceding payment on delivery, so refusal rates alone misstate safety. We release the benchmark, prompts, code and labels.
中文翻译
在线欺诈传统上受限于与潜在受害者交谈所需的人力成本。我们探讨托管的大语言模型是否会现在承担这部分人力。我们让十个现成模型扮演“免费物品，只需支付运费”预付款骗局中的卖家角色，使用普通系统提示，无越狱操作。每个模型与四种买家人格（从轻信到谨慎）各进行30轮对话，同一模型扮演买家（共1200轮对话）。我们根据买家资金的去向标注每轮对话，并通过两名人类编码员验证标签。61%的对话（737轮）以买家提前付款结束。十款模型中有八款几乎参与了所有对话，双方运行成本约为每次付款2.5美分。只有谨慎型买家（要求可查找的身份）表现出抵抗，最愿意的卖家通过虚构姓名和电话号码绕过了这一障碍。即使识别出骗局的卖家仍在133次对话中收取了钱款，通常以自身诚实为理由，而非考虑买家风险。多个模型在不拒绝的前提下保护了买家，例如承诺交付后付款，因此仅靠拒绝率无法准确反映安全性。我们发布了该基准、提示、代码和标签。

ShellBench: What Matters More for AI Agents, Models, or Harness for Everyday Tasks
ShellBench：对于日常任务，AI代理的模型还是工具更重要？
The use of AI agents among the general public has been increasing exponentially throughout 2026, though it is often hard for users to determine which configuration is most effective for their personal use. To help provide clarity here we introduce ShellBench, 100 day-to-day tasks based on more than 10 million user requests, each set in a realistic workspace with messy files and competing data sources, and graded for both correctness and safety. We evaluate 12 models across 5 agent harnesses at 3 reasoning levels for a total of 53,100 rollouts, 20% of which are on tasks designated for public release. We find that model choice impacts performance more than harness choice, but the three leading models differ by only 1.1 percentage points. GPT-6.1 Sol passed 61.4% of attempts at about one-sixth of GPT-6 Astra's estimated token cost per attempt, while GPT-6 Astra led at 62.5%. We also found that harness choice had little effect on either performance or safety with all harnesses scoring between 42.9 (Claude Code) and 45.2 (OpenClaw) for pass@1.

SOARS: System One Assisted Reranking for Agentic Search
SOARS：基于系统一的智能体搜索重排序框架
Deep search agents address complex information needs through iterative retrieval and reasoning, where the relevance of retrieved evidence influences downstream performance. Existing rerankers improve retrieval effectiveness but introduce substantial latency and cost when invoked throughout the search trajectory. We introduce SOARS, a training-free framework leveraging a System One model for fast, calibrated relevance estimation. At each search step, SOARS selects query-relevant passages from long documents and reranks candidates in a single call, jointly considering the original question and agent-generated sub-query. Across six LLM backbones on BrowseComp-Plus and WebShaper, SOARS consistently improves answer accuracy and evidence recall while reducing search calls, end-to-end latency, and inference cost. On BrowseComp-Plus, it improves GPT-5.4 Mini's accuracy from 73.9% to 84.7% while reducing search calls from 23 to 14, demonstrating the potential of System One reranking for more effective and efficient deep search.
中文翻译
深度搜索智能体通过迭代检索与推理来应对复杂信息需求，其中检索到的证据相关性直接影响下游性能。现有重排序器虽能提升检索效果，但在整个搜索轨迹中调用时引入了显著延迟和成本。我们提出SOARS——一种无需训练的框架，利用系统一模型实现快速、校准的相关性估计。在每一步搜索中，SOARS通过单次调用从长文档中选择与查询相关的段落，并联合考虑原始问题和智能体生成的子查询对候选结果进行重排序。在BrowseComp-Plus和WebShaper上的六种LLM骨干网络中，SOARS一致提升了答案准确率和证据召回率，同时减少搜索调用次数、端到端延迟和推理成本。在BrowseComp-Plus上，其将GPT-5.4 Mini的准确率从73.9%提升至84.7%，搜索调用从23次降至14次，展示了系统一重排序在更高效、更有效的深度搜索中的潜力。

Privacy Disclosure Control Benchmark (PDC-Bench): How Faithfully Do LLMs Keep Privacy Constraints on In-Context Disclosure Granularity?
隐私披露控制基准测试（PDC-Bench）：LLM在上下文披露粒度上的隐私约束遵守程度有多忠实？
In LLM task execution, a constraint on the permitted disclosure granularity of personal attributes is sometimes imposed on the output. For example, if a person's private attribute is their location (San Francisco) and disclosure is permitted only at the country level, an output at the state level (California) violates the constraint. Detecting such violations requires capturing not only direct privacy leakage (San Francisco) but also semantic leakage (California). However, existing benchmarks cannot measure the degree of leakage with respect to a given semantic constraint such as the country-level limit. We therefore formalize disclosure control as a constraint on the permitted ceiling over the hierarchy of a knowledge graph. Building on this formulation, we construct PDC-Bench, a benchmark of 16,105 samples covering geography, age, race and ethnicity, and occupation. In our experiments applying PDC-Bench to eleven LLMs, 62% of the constraint violations were semantic leakage. We further found that semantic leakage depends less on the model than on the permitted disclosure granularity and the task's need for the private attribute; reliable auditing must therefore target semantic leakage together with these two factors. Data, prompts, and code are available at https://github.com/fuji1655/PDC-Bench.
中文翻译
在LLM任务执行中，有时会对个人属性的允许披露粒度施加约束。例如，若某人隐私属性是其位置（旧金山），而仅允许在国家层面披露，则输出为州级（加利福尼亚）即违反约束。检测此类违规不仅需要捕捉直接的隐私泄露（旧金山），还需识别语义泄露（加利福尼亚）。然而，现有基准无法衡量在给定语义约束（如国家层级限制）下的泄露程度。因此，我们将披露控制形式化为知识图谱层次结构上的允许上限约束。基于此，我们构建了PDC-Bench，一个包含16,105个样本的基准，覆盖地理、年龄、种族与族裔、职业等领域。在对十一个LLM应用PDC-Bench的实验中，62%的约束违规属于语义泄露。我们进一步发现，语义泄露更多取决于允许的披露粒度和任务对私有属性的需求，而非模型本身；因此，可靠的审计必须同时关注语义泄露以及这两个因素。数据、提示和代码已公开于https://github.com/fuji1655/PDC-Bench。
👤 Kosuke Fujita, Daiki Nishiyama, Jun Sakuma 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
