MAGPIE: Agentic Multi-Reference Image Generation via Executable Scene Construction
MAGPIE：通过可执行场景构建实现智能体多参考图像生成
Multi-reference image generation requires a model to preserve the appearance of selected subjects while arranging them according to an instruction. Despite recent progress in reference-based generation and compositional control, reliably organizing selected subjects into the intended scene remains challenging. Although existing methods provide various forms of compositional guidance, the planned relationships are not always explicitly instantiated for inspection and refinement before image synthesis. To address this limitation, we introduce MAGPIE, which represents the intended composition as a simplified 3D scene defined by an executable Blender program. A coding agent links each scene instance to its reference, checks the geometry and rendered view against the instruction, and revises the program to correct discrepancies. The final proxy rendering serves as a visual plan for the image generator. Using MAGPIE-Instruct-3K, we train the generator to follow this plan while drawing each subject's appearance from its reference. MAGPIE improves both Qwen and FLUX on DreamOmni2 and SconeEval. With FLUX, it improves overall success on DreamOmni2 by 28.21 percentage points over the original backbone and achieves the highest overall score among the models evaluated on SconeEval.
中文翻译
多参考图像生成要求模型在保留选定主体外观的同时，根据指令安排其位置。尽管参考生成和组合控制方面已有进展，但可靠地将选定主体组织成预期场景仍具挑战性。现有方法虽提供多种组合引导，但计划中的关系并非总能在图像合成前显式实例化以供检查与优化。为解决此局限，我们引入MAGPIE，将预期构图表示为由可执行Blender程序定义的简化3D场景。一个编码智能体将每个场景实例与其参考关联，检查几何结构和渲染视图是否符合指令，并修改程序以纠正偏差。最终代理渲染作为图像生成器的视觉规划。使用MAGPIE-Instruct-3K，我们训练生成器遵循此规划，同时从其参考中提取每个主体的外观。MAGPIE在DreamOmni2和SconeEval上均提升了Qwen和FLUX的表现。使用FLUX时，其在DreamOmni2上的整体成功率相比原始骨干提升28.21个百分点，并在SconeEval评估的所有模型中取得最高综合得分。
👤 Yifan Dai, Daili Hua, Bohan Zeng, Yuran Wang, Jialing Liu et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32316]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
ASTERISM: Do Multimodal LLMs Actually Look at All the Images?
ASTERISM：多模态大语言模型真的会看所有图像吗？
Multi-image benchmarks aim to test whether a multimodal large language model (MLLM) combines evidence across images, but many of their items can be answered from a single image. Across about 33k items from 19 public benchmarks, our audit and a two-stage single-image filter find that one image or the question alone settles 24.2% of them. On these items, MLLMs recover from one image a median 78% of the accuracy that all images add over the text alone, so these items reward answering from one image, which we call the single-image shortcut. MLLMs take this shortcut even on items where one image is not enough. On such items, most of their wrong answers repeat an answer they give from one image, at a rate well above chance. We build ASTERISM, to our knowledge the largest audited multi-image benchmark, from these 19 benchmarks by keeping their items that need more than one image. Its 18.7k items, 2.0k of them corrected or rewritten, share one format, one scorer and seven task categories. We also release ASTERISM-Train with 49k training items and ASTERISM-Lite, a hard subset whose items we manually verify one by one. On ASTERISM-Lite, the strongest proprietary MLLM scores 68%. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), now common in MLLM post-training, rewards the shortcut as well, because a correct answer read off one image earns as much reward as one that combines images. In line with this, existing RLVR objectives add little to the ASTERISM score. To stop rewarding the shortcut in training as ASTERISM does in evaluation, we propose cross-image credit assignment (CICA), an extension of RLVR that moves the credit toward the tokens that need more than one image. On three open-source MLLMs, CICA raises the ASTERISM score by 2.6 to 6.0 points, more than twice the gain of each general and vision-specific RL objective trained on the same data.
中文翻译
多图像基准测试旨在检验多模态大语言模型（MLLM）是否能跨图像整合证据，但其中许多题目仅凭单张图像即可解答。通过对19个公开基准中的约3.3万项进行审计和两阶段单图像过滤发现，仅凭一张图像或问题本身就能解决其中24.2%的题目。在这些题目上，MLLM仅从单张图像中就能获得比仅依赖文本高出78%的准确率，因此这类题目奖励从单张图像作答，我们称之为单图像捷径。即使在单张图像不足以回答的题目上，MLLM仍会采用此捷径。在这些题目上，它们大多数错误答案重复了从单张图像得出的答案，频率远高于随机水平。我们构建了ASTERISM，据我们所知目前最大的经过审计的多图像基准，从这19个基准中筛选出需要多张图像才能解答的题目。其包含1.87万项，其中2000项经过修正或重写，统一格式、评分器和七类任务。我们还发布了ASTERISM-Train（4.9万条训练数据）和ASTERISM-Lite（人工逐项验证的高难度子集）。在ASTERISM-Lite上，最强的专有MLLM得分仅为68%。当前常见的多模态大模型后训练方法——可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），同样奖励这一捷径，因为从单张图像读取正确答案所获奖励与融合多图答案相同。与此一致，现有RLVR目标对ASTERISM得分提升甚微。为在训练中阻止对捷径的奖励，如ASTERISM在评估中所做的那样，我们提出了跨图像信用分配（CICA），这是RLVR的扩展，将信用分配给需要多张图像才能处理的token。在三个开源MLLM上，CICA使ASTERISM得分提升2.6至6.0分，提升幅度超过在同一数据上训练的一般和视觉特定RL目标的两倍。
👤 Chanseul Cho, Dongwoo Kim, Noori Bae, Seungjae Moon, Seongsu Lee et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32064]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Fix How It Acts, Not How It Thinks: Surgical Fine-Tuning of Frontier LLMs
修复其行为，而非思维：前沿大模型的精准微调
Open-weight LLMs are competent software engineers, but they sometimes fail at tasks they are otherwise able to do. Often it is not a failure of reasoning. It is a few small missteps over a long trajectory (repeating a step that has already failed, or declaring the task done while a requirement is still unmet) that make the difference. Correcting this calls for a surgical weight update that steers the model toward the right actions without interfering with its reasoning. Such updates, typically learned with LoRA adapters, are often too small to survive merging into a released checkpoint. In our BF16 release, the median weight change is only 1.5% of a single rounding step, and merging loses roughly two thirds of the update's norm. When the same adapter is merged into the mixed FP8/NVFP4 release of the same model, 93% of the update disappears. Instead of increasing the size of the update, we directly train on the surviving part. We introduce merge-aware fine-tuning. Standard LoRA rounds the merged weights to the storage format only at export, so the loss never sees the rounded weights. We instead round the sum of base weights and adapter update to the release format at every training step, keeping the vendor's quantization scales, and compute the loss on these weights. The forward pass matches the serving engine numerics, and the gradient passes straight through the rounding, so the model we train is the model we ship. The loss optimizes only the model's actions and keeps its reasoning close to the base model. Applied to Nemotron-3.5, the recipe improves agentic coding in both the BF16 and the NVFP4 release, by up to 8.4 points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, and we publicly release both models.
中文翻译
开放权重的大语言模型具备优秀的软件工程能力，但有时在本应完成的任务上失败。这种失败往往并非源于推理问题，而是长轨迹中的几个小失误（如重复已失败步骤，或在需求未满足时宣布任务完成）所致。纠正此类问题需要一种精准的权重更新，引导模型走向正确行为而不干扰其推理过程。这类更新通常通过LoRA适配器学习，但往往过小，难以在合并至发布检查点后保留。在我们的BF16发布版本中，权重变化的中位数仅为单次舍入步长的1.5%，合并后约三分之二的更新范数丢失；当同一适配器合并至同模型的混合FP8/NVFP4发布版本时，93%的更新消失。我们不增加更新大小，而是直接在幸存部分上训练。我们引入了融合感知微调：标准LoRA仅在导出时对合并权重进行舍入，因此损失从未看到舍入后的权重。我们则在每个训练步骤中将基础权重与适配器更新之和舍入至发布格式，保留厂商量化尺度，并在此基础上计算损失。前向传播匹配服务引擎数值，梯度直接穿过舍入操作，因此我们训练的模型即为交付的模型。损失仅优化模型的行为，保持其推理接近基线模型。应用于Nemotron-3.5时，该方法在BF16和NVFP4发布版本中均提升了代理式编程性能，Terminal-Bench 2.1上最高提升8.4分，并公开发布了两个模型。
👤 Sandeep Singh Sandha, Prann Bansal, Arvind Sundararajan 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32021]

HuluOPD: Unifying Heterogeneous Medical Specialists into One Lightweight Model with Unlabeled Prompts
HuluOPD：通过无标签提示统一异构医学专家模型为轻量级单一模型
Specialist medical vision-language models (VLMs) are strong within their specialties but hard to unify: their training data are often private, and independently developed specialists cannot be assumed to share an architecture. Deploying these large models separately is costly. We present HuluOPD, an on-policy distillation recipe that unifies heterogeneous specialists into one lightweight VLM using only unlabeled prompts, without requiring reference answers or the specialists' training data. The lightweight model answers each prompt, and the specialist of the prompt's modality supervises its response; a continuation-aligned cross-tokenizer loss makes this possible even when they differ in architecture and tokenizer. We apply HuluOPD to five specialists, for ultrasound, pathology, optical coherence tomography, fundus photography, and dental imaging. To distill and evaluate them, we build HuluOPDCorpus, 914k unlabeled prompts, and HuluOPDBench, 47k held-out test items, from over 90 public datasets, and score all models under one protocol on 132k test items, including third-party benchmarks. A single model with 10% of the five specialists' combined parameters retains 94% of their question-answering performance, and one with 2% retains 89%. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of unifying heterogeneous medical specialist VLMs without requiring their training data or a shared architecture.
中文翻译
专科医学视觉-语言模型（VLM）在其专业领域表现强劲，但难以统一：其训练数据常为私有，且独立开发的专家模型无法假设共享架构。分别部署这些大型模型成本高昂。我们提出HuluOPD，一种基于策略的蒸馏方法，仅使用无标签提示即可将异构专家模型统一为一个轻量级VLM，无需参考答案或专家模型的训练数据。轻量级模型对每个提示生成回答，而对应模态的专家模型监督其响应；延续对齐的跨标记器损失使得即使架构和标记器不同也能实现这一目标。我们将HuluOPD应用于五个专科模型：超声、病理、光学相干断层扫描、眼底摄影和牙科影像。为蒸馏和评估，我们构建了HuluOPDCorpus（91.4万个无标签提示）和HuluOPDBench（4.7万个保留测试项），数据来自超过90个公开数据集，并在13.2万个测试项上以统一协议评估所有模型，包括第三方基准。一个仅具五个专家模型总参数10%的模型保留了其94%的问答性能，而2%的模型仍保留89%。据我们所知，这是首次在无需训练数据或共享架构的情况下实现异构医学专家VLM的统一。
👤 Yuncheng Zhang, Songtao Jiang, Yitian Tang, Zijie Meng, Zihan Xu et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Marketing-Bench: Evaluating Frontier Models and Agents on Real-World Marketing Capabilities
Marketing-Bench：评估前沿模型与智能体在真实世界营销能力上的表现
As frontier AI models and agents are deployed more broadly in the real world, marketing is emerging as one of their largest applications. Marketing teams use AI models to produce brand assets like website copy and social media posts, while consumers' agents autonomously discover products, research purchases, and navigate websites. However, evaluating marketing capability is difficult because unlike areas like software development, customer support, or legal review where ground truth is more objectively defined, marketing is subjective, and output quality is judged based on taste and brand. We introduce Marketing-Bench, the first open-source marketing benchmark, which pairs discriminative tasks (detecting brand anomalies) with generative tasks (remediation), while keeping answers verifiable. Marketing-Bench tests five key pillars: factuality, consistency, coherence, structure, and brand voice, all crucial elements for good brand content on the web. We find that frontier models are far from solving the benchmark: the average model corrects only about half of the introduced issues (53.1%), whereas a marketing expert corrects nearly all of them. Even the strongest, GPT-6 Astra, reaches only 74.8%, with Claude Opus and Fable, Gemini Argon, and GPT-6 Sol falling well short. These findings suggest that marketing capabilities are less mature than adoption implies, and that principled evaluation should precede further scaling of usage. Marketing-Bench is a first step toward "frontier marketing models" with superhuman marketing performance.
中文翻译
随着前沿AI模型与智能体在现实世界中更广泛部署，营销正成为其最重要的应用场景之一。营销团队利用AI模型生成品牌资产，如网站文案和社交媒体帖子；而消费者代理则自主发现产品、研究购买并导航网站。然而，评估营销能力具有挑战性，因为与软件开发、客户服务或法律审查等有明确客观真值的领域不同，营销具有主观性，输出质量取决于品味与品牌调性。我们提出Marketing-Bench，首个开源营销基准，结合判别任务（检测品牌异常）与生成任务（修复），同时确保答案可验证。Marketing-Bench测试五大核心维度：事实性、一致性、连贯性、结构和品牌声音，这些都是网络优质品牌内容的关键要素。我们发现，前沿模型远未解决该基准：平均模型仅修正了约一半引入的问题（53.1%），而营销专家几乎全部修正。即使最强的GPT-6 Astra也仅达到74.8%，Claude Opus、Fable、Gemini Argon和GPT-6 Sol均显著落后。这些发现表明，营销能力的实际成熟度低于其广泛应用所暗示的程度，且应在进一步扩大使用前进行严谨评估。Marketing-Bench是迈向‘前沿营销模型’——具备超人类营销表现——的第一步。

SwarmLeak: Does Coordination Improve Privacy Attacks on LLM Agents?
SwarmLeak：协作是否提升对大语言模型智能体的隐私攻击效果？
LLM agents increasingly collaborate in group chats while holding tool access to confidential records, which makes the shared chat an attack surface. It remains unclear whether several colluding attackers extract such information more effectively than one. We introduce SwarmLeak, a red-teaming dataset of 24 synthetic group-chat scenarios across healthcare, law, finance, and software engineering, where attackers pose as colleagues to make a victim agent post protected fields despite an explicit confidentiality policy. With GLM 5.2 attacking five victim models through seven conversational strategies, we compare one attacker with a swarm of four sharing a private channel, matching scenarios, strategies, and a 200,000-token budget that also covers swarm coordination. Swarms show no advantage: a single attacker matches or exceeds them on every victim, and the share of runs leaking any field differs between conditions by at most 14.3 percentage points, whereas changing the victim shifts it from 0% to 99.4%. On the two most vulnerable models, nearly every run leaks under both conditions, but single attackers expose more of the record at first disclosure (85.4% vs. 68.1% of protected fields on Nemotron 3.5 Lightning). Four of the five victims leaked despite the policy. GLM 5.2 retrieved protected data in 64.3% of individual-attack runs and leaked in 0.6%: retrieval does not imply disclosure, but in high-stakes settings even one leaked field can identify a patient or client. At equal budgets, a larger red team is not automatically a stronger one; the victim model matters far more.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体越来越多地在群聊中协作并访问敏感记录，这使得共享聊天成为攻击面。目前尚不清楚多个共谋攻击者是否比单个攻击者更有效地提取此类信息。我们提出了SwarmLeak，一个包含24个合成群聊场景的红队数据集，涵盖医疗、法律、金融和软件工程领域，攻击者伪装成同事，诱使目标智能体在明确保密政策下发布受保护字段。使用GLM 5.2通过七种对话策略攻击五个目标模型，我们比较了单个攻击者与四个共享私密通道的攻击者群体的表现，条件包括相同场景、策略和20万token预算，后者还包含群体协调成本。结果显示，群体并无优势：单个攻击者在所有目标上表现相当或更优，且泄露任意字段的比例差异不超过14.3个百分点；而更换目标模型则使泄露率从0%升至99.4%。在最脆弱的两个模型上，两种条件下几乎每次运行都会发生泄露，但单个攻击者首次披露时暴露的信息更多（Nemotron 3.5 Lightning上分别为85.4% vs 68.1%的受保护字段）。五名受害者中有四名在政策存在的情况下仍泄露数据。GLM 5.2在单独攻击中检索到受保护数据的比例为64.3%，但仅0.6%发生泄露：检索不等于披露，但在高风险场景中，哪怕一字段泄露也可能识别患者或客户。在相同预算下，更大的红队并不自动更强；目标模型的影响远大于团队规模。
👤 Asma Farajidizaji, Vyas Raina, Vatsal Raina, Ivaxi Sheth 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31756]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
TACT-Bench: Testing Adaptive Conversational Threats against Tool-Use Agents
TACT-Bench：针对工具使用智能体的自适应对话威胁测试
The growing autonomy of AI agents in using external tools and making decisions has created an urgent need for comprehensive safety benchmarks. Existing benchmarks test compromised tool channels or adaptive multi-turn attacks, though they may not capture the unique capabilities of policy-governed tool-use agents, which must resist unauthorized requests without abandoning the legitimate work embedded in the same interaction. To fill this gap, we introduce TACT-Bench, a stateful benchmark built around scenario-specific stories, personas, manipulation tactics, and response-adaptive escalation. Each adversarial task targets an action explicitly prohibited by the policy provided to the assistant, while the policy remains hidden from the attacking user. TACT pairs each adversarial task with at least one benign counterpart, and its adversarial tasks retain authorized ground-truth actions when a compliant resolution remains available. We define these base tasks as TACT-Base. We additionally introduce TACT-TrustPrime, which places an independent benign task before an unrelated attack in the same conversation. We also propose Paired Selective Trustworthiness (PST), which combines explicit safe refusal with demonstrated capability on matched benign tasks, to analyze an agent's overcautiousness and practicality. Averaged across 6 different models as assistants, TACT-Base raises Attack-Induced Failure Rate (AFR) by 17.53% over four compatible attack baselines, and TACT-TrustPrime raises it by a further 17.01%. The two evaluated defense methods reduce AFR by only 8.35%--9.41% on average and do not consistently improve PST, underscoring the need for stronger policy-enforcement methods.
中文翻译
AI智能体在使用外部工具和做出决策方面的自主性日益增强，迫切需要全面的安全基准测试。现有基准主要测试被攻陷的工具通道或自适应多轮攻击，但可能无法捕捉到受策略控制的工具使用智能体的独特能力——这些智能体必须在不放弃同一交互中嵌入的合法任务的前提下，抵御未经授权的请求。为填补这一空白，我们提出了TACT-Bench，一个基于场景特定故事、人物设定、操纵策略和响应自适应升级的状态化基准。每个对抗性任务都针对助手政策明确禁止的动作，而攻击者无法获知该政策。TACT为每个对抗性任务至少配备一个良性对照任务，且当合规解决方案仍可用时，其对抗性任务保留授权的真实动作。我们将这些基础任务定义为TACT-Base。我们还引入了TACT-TrustPrime，即在同一对话中，在无关攻击前放置一个独立的良性任务。我们提出配对选择性可信度（PST），结合显式安全拒绝与在匹配良性任务上的表现能力，以分析智能体的过度谨慎性和实用性。在6种不同模型作为助手的情况下平均，TACT-Base相较于四个兼容攻击基线使攻击引发失败率（AFR）提升了17.53%，TACT-TrustPrime进一步提升了17.01%。两种评估的防御方法仅平均降低AFR 8.35%–9.41%，且未持续提升PST，凸显了更强策略执行方法的必要性。
👤 Ilia Hashemi Rad, Feijie Wu, Haoyu Wang, Jing Gao 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31682]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
LimoBench: How Street Smart Are Large Language Models?
LimoBench：大型语言模型有多“街知巷闻”？
Large language models (LLMs) have transformed how humans interact with information, enabling natural-language interfaces across various domains. They are increasingly deployed for geospatial applications including map-based question answering, location-based services, and natural-language interfaces to spatial databases. But are LLMs truly location-aware, able to reliably interpret and precisely answer location queries involving real-world coordinates, points of interest (POIs), and geospatial distances? To answer this concretely, we present LimoBench, a street-level benchmark of 82 natural-language location queries over seven canonical spatial operations: geocoding, reverse geocoding, routing, directionality, range search, $k$-nearest-neighbor search, and spatial join. The queries are grounded in real map data of West Lafayette, Indiana, and are graded by location and semantic complexity. They are designed to probe LLMs' street-level spatial awareness, from simple lookups to multi-step spatial reasoning. We also present Limo, an approach based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) that enhances the location awareness of LLMs. Limo grounds LLMs in PostGIS-backed spatial data through a compact set of tools. We find that ungrounded models mostly achieve single-digit accuracy (3.1% for GPT-6 Luna) across different spatial operations. In contrast, the same models achieve near-perfect accuracy (99.3% for GPT-6 Luna) when given access to Limo's spatial tools through MCP. Current LLMs thus have limited and unreliable street-level spatial knowledge, while grounding provides accurate answers at modest overhead (+0.2 s latency, 7.7$\times$ tokens, and 2.2$\times$ cost for GPT-6 Luna). We release the benchmark, Limo toolset, and evaluation code to encourage the community to evaluate their own location-aware LLM approaches.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）已彻底改变人类获取信息的方式，使自然语言接口在多个领域成为可能。它们越来越多地被部署于地理空间应用，包括基于地图的问题回答、基于位置的服务以及空间数据库的自然语言接口。但LLMs是否真正具备位置感知能力，能否可靠地解释并精确回答涉及真实世界坐标、兴趣点（POIs）和地理空间距离的位置查询？为具体回答这一问题，我们提出了LimoBench，一个覆盖西拉法耶特市真实地图数据的街道级基准，包含82个自然语言位置查询，涵盖七种经典空间操作：地理编码、反向地理编码、路径规划、方向性、范围搜索、k近邻搜索和空间连接。查询根据位置和语义复杂度进行评分，旨在探测LLMs的街道级空间意识，从简单查找到多步空间推理。我们还提出了Limo，一种基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的方法，通过一组紧凑工具将LLMs与PostGIS支持的空间数据对齐，从而增强其位置感知能力。我们发现，未经接地的模型在不同空间操作中普遍仅达到个位数准确率（GPT-6 Luna为3.1%）。相比之下，当模型通过MCP获得Limo的空间工具访问权限时，准确率接近完美（GPT-6 Luna为99.3%）。当前LLMs在街道级空间知识方面存在有限且不可靠的问题，而接地能以适度开销（GPT-6 Luna增加0.2秒延迟、7.7倍token消耗、2.2倍成本）提供准确答案。我们已发布该基准、Limo工具集和评估代码，以鼓励社区评估其自身的位置感知LLM方法。
👤 Gautam Choudhary, Jack Peng, Udesh Kumarasinghe, Rachel Rafik, Yousef Hammad et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

GoldiBench: Right Tools, Wrong Amount -- Benchmarking Agents on Effect Judgement in Professional Editing
GoldiBench：工具正确，数量错误——在专业编辑中对智能体的效果判断进行基准测试
Real editing requests describe a look, not the steps to reach it, so an agent that serves them must judge how much of each tool the look takes. Existing benchmarks do not measure this: their tasks either state the tools and values, or leave the amount open and are scored by VLM judges that pass more than half of half- and triple-strength results. We introduce GoldiBench, a benchmark of 500 editing tasks in five professional applications (darktable, RawTherapee, GIMP, Inkscape, Blender), solvable by writing a program or by operating the GUI. Each task pairs a START image with a TARGET rendered from a hidden program, so the amount is fixed by a known target; the agent's edit is re-rendered and compared with TARGET, and the same task is posed with the tools and values, the tools only, or the goal only, separating operation from effect judgement. Across 25 open, GUI and closed models, agents operate well (99-100% with programs from 4B up when told the values) but fall off a cliff when they must judge the amount: given only the tools, the best model passes 19% of the tasks with programs and 33% through the GUI, and scale does not close the gap. Manuals barely help; the measured TARGET-START difference more than doubles the pass rate, but the same difference shown as an image does not for open models. Allowed four renders, agents almost always stop after the first; forced to compare, the strong models double their pass rate. Agents know the tools, but neither read nor check the amount.
中文翻译
真实的编辑请求描述的是最终效果，而非达成该效果的具体步骤，因此能够满足此类请求的智能体必须判断每种工具使用量的多少。现有基准测试无法衡量这一点：它们的任务要么明确给出工具和数值，要么将使用量留空，并由视觉语言模型（VLM）裁判评分，而这些裁判对半强度和三倍强度的结果通过率超过一半。我们提出了GoldiBench，一个包含500个编辑任务的基准测试，涵盖五个专业应用（darktable、RawTherapee、GIMP、Inkscape、Blender），可通过编写程序或操作GUI完成。每个任务都配有一张起始图像和一张由隐藏程序生成的目标图像，使得使用量由已知目标固定；智能体的编辑操作会被重新渲染并与目标图像对比。同一任务以三种形式提出：提供工具和数值、仅提供工具、仅提供目标，从而分离操作与效果判断。在25个开放模型、GUI模型和闭源模型中，当被告知数值时，智能体使用程序可达到99-100%的准确率，但一旦需要判断使用量，表现急剧下降：仅提供工具时，最佳模型在程序路径下仅通过19%的任务，在GUI路径下通过33%，且模型规模扩大也无法缩小差距。手册帮助甚微；测量目标与起始图像之间的差异可使通过率翻倍，但若以图像形式展示相同差异，则对开放模型无显著提升。允许四次重渲染时，智能体几乎总在第一次后停止；强制进行比较后，强模型的通过率翻倍。智能体知道工具，但既不读取也不验证使用量。
👤 Wang Yang, Xinpeng Li, Yanyan Zhang, Chaoda Song, Shouren Wang et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

Leaks by Installments: Cross-Session Answer Disclosure in LLM Tutors and a Ledger-Based Defense
分批泄露：LLM导师中的跨会话答案披露及基于账本的防御机制
Tutors built on large language models (LLMs) are told not to give away answers, but a learner does not have to ask for the answer all at once. Existing safeguards judge each reply within the current conversation, so a learner can open a new session, reword the problem, or request a worked solution to the same problem with different numbers. We call this cross-session disclosure and propose a defense: a persistent, per-learner disclosure ledger. For every problem a learner brings, the system builds a typed solution graph. Before a reply is released, a gate matches the reply's own content, not the learner's wording, against the ledger by result values and by type-labeled solution structure, then computes which steps the learner could now derive. The reply is withheld if that set reaches the final answer or exceeds a budget. In a simulation of 3,000 problems, the ledger reduced leakage through renumbered practice variants from 100% to 10.6% while honest learners completed 94-99.5% of problems. With two real LLM tutors (Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Phi-3.5-mini-instruct) on 60 problems, leakage through renumbered variants fell from 0.58 to 0.08 and from 1.00 to 0.18, with 98% honest completion. The ledger did not reduce split requests beyond a per-session gate in the live runs. All acceptance criteria were fixed before each run.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLM）构建的导师被要求不透露答案，但学习者无需一次性询问完整答案。现有防护机制仅在当前对话内判断每条回复，因此学习者可开启新会话、改写问题，或请求同一问题的不同数字版本的解答过程。我们称此为跨会话披露，并提出一种防御方案：基于账本的持久化、按学习者区分的答案披露记录。每当学习者提交一个问题，系统即构建一个带类型的解决方案图。在回复释放前，门控机制将回复内容本身（而非学习者的表述）与账本中的结果值和类型标记的解决方案结构进行匹配，然后计算学习者现在能推导出哪些步骤。若该集合达到最终答案或超出预算，则拒绝回复。在3,000个问题的模拟中，账本将重编号练习变体的泄露率从100%降至10.6%，同时诚实学习者完成率达94%-99.5%。在两个真实LLM导师（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Phi-3.5-mini-instruct）对60个问题的实测中，重编号变体的泄露率分别从0.58降至0.08、从1.00降至0.18，且诚实完成率保持在98%。在实际运行中，账本并未比单会话门控进一步减少拆分请求。所有接受标准均在每次运行前固定。
👤 Venkata Muttineni, Karthik Narayan, Christopher David 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31427]

Learning What vs. Learning How: Decomposing Task and Interface Competence in Agentic Training
学习什么 vs. 学习如何：分解智能体训练中的任务与接口能力
LLM agents operate through harnesses that define system prompts, action formats, feedback loops, and resource constraints. A model may possess the knowledge needed to solve a task yet fail to express that knowledge through a particular harness. This raises a basic question: when a policy fails under a given harness, is it missing task knowledge or the competence to operate the interface? We distinguish two components of agentic competence: task competence, the knowledge and reasoning needed to solve a task, and interface competence, the ability to act on that knowledge through a given harness. Across four reasoning and agentic benchmarks, we show that harness-conditioned training yields vastly different outcomes depending on how these interact. Multi-harness distillation aggregates task knowledge from trajectories collected through different harnesses into a shared policy, but this knowledge is realised only when training preserves or improves competence on the inference-time interface. Otherwise, single-harness training can induce interference in interface competence, sometimes catastrophically collapsing performance on a held-out harness despite improvements in underlying task knowledge. To disentangle task and interface supervision, we introduce harness rehearsal: distillation on traces that carry minimal task knowledge while exercising the target interface. Cross-harness task supervision or rehearsal alone yields little gain, but their combination yields gains far larger than either: on BFCL V3 multi-turn with Qwen3-4B, they achieve 8.5% and 32.5% respectively, versus 58.8% together. Finally, on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is more robust than distillation to single-harness training: it does not substantially degrade performance on other harnesses, and interface competence acquired through multi-harness distillation persists through subsequent RL.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体通过定义系统提示、动作格式、反馈循环和资源约束的“ harness”来运行。一个模型可能具备解决任务所需的知识，却无法通过特定 harness 表达该知识。这引发了一个基本问题：当策略在特定 harness 下失败时，是缺乏任务知识，还是不具备操作接口的能力？我们区分了智能体能力的两个组成部分：任务能力（解决任务所需的知识与推理）和接口能力（通过给定 harness 运用知识的能力）。在四个推理与智能体基准测试中，我们发现 harness 条件下的训练结果因两者交互方式而异。多 harness 蒸馏将不同 harness 收集轨迹中的任务知识聚合到共享策略中，但只有在训练过程中保持或提升推理时接口的能力时，这些知识才能被有效利用。否则，单 harness 训练可能导致接口能力干扰，有时甚至在保留 harness 上性能崩溃，尽管底层任务知识有所提升。为解耦任务与接口监督，我们引入 harness 重演：在仅携带少量任务知识但充分使用目标接口的轨迹上进行蒸馏。跨 harness 任务监督或重演单独作用效果有限，但二者结合可带来显著提升：在 BFCL V3 多轮任务中，Qwen3-4B 模型分别达到 8.5% 和 32.5% 的提升，联合使用则达 58.8%。最后，基于策略的强化学习（RL）相比蒸馏对单 harness 训练更具鲁棒性：不会显著降低其他 harness 上的表现，且通过多 harness 蒸馏获得的接口能力在后续 RL 中仍能持续保留。
👤 Chaeeun Lee, Hossein A. Rahmani, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Kexin Xu, Emine Yilmaz et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

What People Choose Not to Say: A Multimodal Benchmark for Fine-Grained Information Manipulation in Conversations
人们选择不说什么：对话中细粒度信息操纵的多模态基准
Recent advances in MLLMs have improved social understanding, enabling models to recognize emotions and distinguish truthful from deceptive behaviors. However, existing benchmarks largely focus on coarse-grained emotion recognition or binary truth-deception detection, overlooking the diverse ways people manipulate information in conversations, such as withholding, leakage, fabrication, evasion, and defensive counterclaim. Understanding these behaviors requires reasoning about underlying facts, participant knowledge, and conversational context, making them difficult to evaluate reliably. To address this gap, we introduce InfoCon, a multimodal benchmark for fine-grained information manipulation in conversations. InfoCon identifies six fine-grained strategies and derives a coarse consistency label from their factual and interactional relations. We develop an evidence-grounded multi-agent annotation framework to construct and verify reliable labels from rich contextual evidence. We benchmark representative multimodal models and systematically analyze their fine-grained information manipulation understanding, providing a testbed beyond binary truth-deception judgments.
中文翻译
近年来，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在社交理解方面取得进展，使模型能够识别情绪并区分真实与欺骗行为。然而，现有基准大多聚焦于粗粒度情绪识别或二元真假判断，忽视了人们在对话中多样化的信息操纵方式，如隐瞒、泄露、虚构、回避和防御性反诉。理解这些行为需要对潜在事实、参与者知识和对话上下文进行推理，因此难以可靠评估。为填补这一空白，我们引入 InfoCon，一个用于对话中细粒度信息操纵的多模态基准。InfoCon 识别六种细粒度策略，并根据其事实性和互动关系推导出粗粒度一致性标签。我们开发了一种基于证据的多智能体标注框架，从丰富的上下文证据中构建并验证可靠标签。我们对代表性多模态模型进行了基准测试，并系统分析其在细粒度信息操纵理解方面的能力，提供了一个超越二元真假判断的测试平台。
👤 Yuhan Bian, Zhaokai Liang, Jia Wang, Haoyu Liu, Yu Chen et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

agent-memory
SAG-Mem-1.0 Technical Report
SAG-Mem-1.0 技术报告
Long-term conversational memory requires retrieving complementary facts scattered across dialogue sessions, including intermediate evidence with limited similarity to the original question. Existing approaches often rely on derived memory representations or persistent relational structures, introducing construction costs and potentially losing source details. We present SAG-Mem, a conversational memory framework that preserves complete dialogue messages in non-overlapping chunks and discovers complementary evidence through query-time lexical expansion. Memory construction requires no model inference, using a lexical index without LLM-based extraction, summarization, or semantic relation construction. At query time, BM25 retrieves initial candidates, an evidence evaluator identifies useful chunks, and lexical cues from selected evidence guide an additional retrieval round. Retrieved dialogue is further annotated with calendar interpretations of supported relative-time expressions, preserving the original wording and temporal granularity. We also introduce SAG-Mem-Lite, an alternative configuration that replaces evidence scoring with embedding-based candidate scoring, achieving lower operational cost than Full Context and all memory-based baselines across both benchmarks with low query latency. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval, SAG-Mem achieves overall scores of 83.08 and 80.20, respectively, the highest among the evaluated methods on both benchmarks. On LongMemEval, it exceeds the strongest compared baseline by 7.20 points. SAG-Mem-Lite achieves scores of 77.09 and 77.20, respectively. These results support dialogue preservation and evidence-guided retrieval expansion as an effective design for long-term conversational memory.
中文翻译
长期对话记忆需要从多个对话会话中检索分散的互补事实，包括与原始问题相似度较低的中间证据。现有方法通常依赖于衍生的记忆表示或持久的关系结构，带来构建成本并可能丢失源信息细节。我们提出SAG-Mem，一种对话记忆框架，将完整的对话消息以非重叠块的形式保存，并通过查询时的词汇扩展发现互补证据。记忆构建无需模型推理，仅使用词汇索引，不依赖LLM的提取、摘要或语义关系构建。查询时，BM25检索初始候选，证据评估器识别有用块，所选证据中的词汇线索引导额外检索轮次。检索到的对话进一步标注了支持的相对时间表达式的日历解释，保留原始措辞和时间粒度。我们还引入SAG-Mem-Lite，一种替代配置，用基于嵌入的候选评分替代证据评分，在两个基准测试中均实现比完整上下文和所有基于记忆的基线更低的运行成本和低查询延迟。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上，SAG-Mem分别取得83.08和80.20的综合得分，为两个基准中最高；在LongMemEval上，超过最强对比基线7.20分。SAG-Mem-Lite得分分别为77.09和77.20。这些结果支持对话保留与证据引导的检索扩展作为长期对话记忆的有效设计。
👤 Yuchao Wu, XingCheng Liang, Xuanze Wang, Yini Huang, Yue Wu 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33005]

Leto: Utility-Centric Agent Memory
Leto：以效用为中心的智能体记忆
Large language model (LLM) agents rely on external memory to retain and reuse information across interactions. Existing memory systems treat memory as a record of the past and evaluate it by fidelity to the conversation. However, the value of memory lies in whether it improves the agent's response. A memory system decides what to memorize and what to serve before that response exists, and fidelity checks neither decision against it. Faithful memory can therefore fail to improve the response. We instead view memory as a prediction problem and present \textbf{\emph{\leto{}}}, a memory system for LLM agents that focuses on the utility of memory to the future rather than on its fidelity to the past. Because future requests are unknown, \emph{\leto{}} preserves access to conversation details through memory indexing, allowing information to remain available through its sources. When a request arrives, memory replay draws on this index to anticipate and supply the context the agent needs beyond what the request explicitly states. The utility of the served context remains uncertain until the response is generated, motivating a utility loop that uses response outcomes to refine future memory selection. We evaluate our system against six memory systems on five benchmarks across three scenarios. In personalization, it outperforms the strongest compared memory system by 16.3 percentage points in accuracy on PrefEval and by 3.1 points in task completion on PERMA. For long-horizon tasks, it achieves the best overall performance on MemoryArena averaged over five environments with a gain that increases with the chain depth. In long-context understanding, it is on par with retrieval over the raw history while serving fewer context tokens.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体依赖外部记忆来保留并复用跨交互的信息。现有记忆系统将记忆视为过去的记录，并通过对话保真度来评估其质量。然而，记忆的价值在于是否能提升智能体的响应质量。记忆系统需在响应生成前决定存储和提供哪些内容，而保真度检查无法验证这一决策的有效性。因此，忠实的记忆可能仍无法改善响应。我们转而将记忆视为一个预测问题，提出 extbf{ extit{Leto}}，一种面向未来效用而非过去保真度的LLM智能体记忆系统。由于未来请求未知， extit{Leto}通过记忆索引保留对话细节的访问权限，使信息可通过其来源持续可用。当请求到达时，记忆重放利用该索引来预判并提供超出请求显式内容所需的上下文。所服务上下文的效用直到响应生成后才可确定，这促使我们引入一个效用循环，利用响应结果来优化未来的记忆选择。我们在三个场景下的五个基准上对比了六种记忆系统。在个性化任务中，其在PrefEval上的准确率比最强对比系统高出16.3个百分点，在PERMA上的任务完成率高出3.1个百分点。对于长周期任务，它在MemoryArena上五种环境的平均表现最佳，且随着链深度增加优势更明显。在长上下文理解方面，其性能与直接检索原始历史相当，但提供的上下文token更少。
👤 Shuguang Chen, Salman Paracha 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

ASCENT: A Benchmark for Evaluating and Advancing Stepwise Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models on Common Clinical Scenarios
ASCENT：面向常见临床场景中大语言模型逐步诊断推理能力的评估与推进基准
Large language models (LLMs) excel at medical question answering yet are rarely evaluated on the stepwise diagnostic reasoning that defines real clinical workflows, where impressions are revised as information accumulates. We build Annotated Stepwise Clinical rEasoning for NaturalisTic Diagnosis (ASCENT), a clinician-annotated benchmark and training resource of 3,078 stepwise problems derived from MedQA-USMLE that decomposes each vignette into EMR-aligned steps (Findings, Impression, supporting Rationale), enabling evaluation of intermediate reasoning under incomplete information. Experiments and training with ASCENT revealed insights into how current LLMs handle stepwise diagnostic reasoning. Even strong reasoning models that perform well on MedQA-USMLE leave substantial headroom on ASCENT, and general-purpose frontier models trail further, exposing a persistent gap between fully informed and stepwise diagnosis. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-7B and 32B on ASCENT yields measurable F1 gains over both pre-trained and HuatuoGPT-o1 CoT-trained baselines, with gains driven primarily by precision. Complementary robustness analyses (counterfactual perturbation, format-vs-content control, judge agreement, and rollout) further show that ASCENT-fine-tuned models rely on the diagnostic content of prior impressions rather than imitating their output format, while error propagation under rollout remains a key challenge for clinical deployment.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）在医学问答方面表现出色，但很少在真实临床工作流程所依赖的逐步诊断推理过程上进行评估，即随着信息积累不断修正判断。我们构建了Annotated Stepwise Clinical rEasoning for NaturalisTic Diagnosis（ASCENT），一个由临床医生标注的基准数据集和训练资源，包含3,078个源自MedQA-USMLE的逐步问题，将每个病例分解为与电子病历（EMR）对齐的步骤（发现、印象、支持性理由），从而可在信息不完整的情况下评估中间推理过程。使用ASCENT进行实验与训练揭示了当前LLMs在逐步诊断推理中的表现。即使在MedQA-USMLE上表现良好的强推理模型，在ASCENT上的表现仍有巨大提升空间，而通用前沿模型则落后更多，暴露出完全知情与逐步诊断之间的持续差距。对Qwen2.5-7B和32B在ASCENT上进行微调后，相较于预训练模型和HuatuoGPT-o1 CoT训练基线，F1得分显著提升，主要由精度驱动。补充的鲁棒性分析（反事实扰动、格式与内容控制、评判者一致性、滚动推演）进一步表明，ASCENT微调模型依赖于先前印象的诊断内容而非模仿其输出格式，而滚动推演中的错误传播仍是临床部署的关键挑战。

Social Bias Neurons in Multilingual Language Models: A Mechanistic Interpretability Analysis
多语言语言模型中的社会偏见神经元：一种机制可解释性分析
Multilingual models exhibit social bias when processing inputs pertaining to societal stereotypes. We adapt emerging mechanistic interpretability methods to pinpoint and characterize model components that encode this bias. We conduct our analysis across three instruction-tuned models, four languages, and seven bias dimensions. Specifically, we leverage multilingual bias benchmarks in implementing two methods for identifying social bias neurons: activation probability entropy and integrated gap gradients. We find the latter method to be more effective, as the neurons it isolates (which are largely concentrated at the model's deepest layers) lead to sharper decreases in model bias when suppressed. Further analysis reveals neuron distribution patterns differ between high- and low-resourced languages, suggesting that a language's representation in pretraining data influences how its biases are represented within model internals. Our results also indicate that neurons linked to gender, nationality, and social class biases overlap the most with neurons related to other bias dimensions, pointing to the pivotal role these three dimensions play in how models learn and handle bias.
中文翻译
多语言模型在处理涉及社会刻板印象的输入时表现出社会偏见。我们采用新兴的机制可解释性方法，定位并刻画编码此类偏见的模型组件。我们在三个指令微调模型、四种语言和七个偏见维度上开展分析。具体而言，我们利用多语言偏见基准，实施两种识别社会偏见神经元的方法：激活概率熵和集成间隙梯度。结果表明，后者更为有效，因其识别出的神经元（主要集中于模型深层）在被抑制时能带来更明显的偏见下降。进一步分析显示，高资源语言与低资源语言的神经元分布模式存在差异，表明语言在预训练数据中的表示方式影响其内部偏见的表征。结果还表明，与性别、国籍和社会阶层相关的偏见神经元与其他偏见维度的神经元重叠度最高，凸显这三个维度在模型学习和处理偏见中的核心作用。
👤 Lance Calvin Lim Gamboa, Yue Feng, Mark Lee 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

agent-memoryagentic-rl
BCP: A Lightweight Boundary-Controlled Framework for Online Sign Language Recognition and Translation
BCP：一种轻量级边界控制框架用于在线手语识别与翻译
Online sign language recognition (SLR) and translation (SLT) incrementally produce gloss sequences or natural language text from sign video as it arrives, a capability essential for real-time communication and live captioning. However, powerful pretrained offline models cannot be directly deployed in this setting, while existing online methods typically retrain the entire model on simulated streaming prefixes constructed from offline videos, incurring high costs. To address this challenge, we propose Boundary-Controlled Prefix Commit (BCP), a unified lightweight framework that converts pretrained offline methods into online counterparts for sign-to-gloss (S2G), sign-to-text (S2T), and sign-to-gloss-to-text (S2G2T). Specifically, BCP freezes the pretrained model and introduces a lightweight boundary adapter that decides when to commit, supervised by Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) alignments from a pretrained SLR model, without any human annotation. At inference, a Boundary-Controlled Wait-K Output Policy then decides what to commit via prefix voting, yielding adaptive burst commits while preserving the output progress of the base Wait-K schedule. Experiments on PHOENIX-2014T and CSL-Daily across four offline methods show that BCP, with less than 0.1% additional trainable parameters per offline method, achieves a strong quality-latency trade-off on S2T and remains competitive on S2G and S2G2T.
中文翻译
在线手语识别（SLR）与翻译（SLT）能够随着手语视频的实时到达逐步生成词素序列或自然语言文本，这是实现实时交流与现场字幕的关键能力。然而，强大的预训练离线模型无法直接部署于此场景；而现有在线方法通常需在由离线视频构造的模拟流式前缀上重新训练整个模型，带来高昂成本。为应对这一挑战，我们提出边界控制前缀提交（BCP）框架，一种统一的轻量级方案，可将预训练的离线方法转化为适用于手语到词素（S2G）、手语到文本（S2T）以及手语到词素再到文本（S2G2T）的在线版本。具体而言，BCP冻结预训练模型，引入一个轻量级边界适配器，通过预训练SLR模型的连接时序分类（CTC）对齐进行监督，无需人工标注。推理阶段，边界控制等待-K输出策略通过前缀投票决定提交内容，实现自适应突发提交，同时保持基础等待-K调度的输出进度。在PHOENIX-2014T和CSL-Daily数据集上，针对四种离线方法的实验表明，BCP仅增加每种离线方法不到0.1%的可训练参数，即可在S2T任务上实现优异的质量-延迟权衡，并在S2G和S2G2T任务上保持竞争力。
👤 Xiao Ma, Lin Li, Shiqing Wu, Wang Gao, Chuanbo Liu et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Phoneme-identity depth profiles do not reliably predict LoRA placement for dysarthric ASR
音素身份深度剖面无法可靠预测失语症语音识别中的LoRA放置位置
Parameter-efficient adaptation of a speech foundation model to dysarthric speech requires a rarely justified choice: which encoder layers to adapt. A recently proposed strategy is to probe the encoder, find where phonetic information becomes most decodable, and put the adapters there. We test that inference across two ASR models (Qwen3-ASR, Whisper-small) and two dysarthric corpora (Mandarin CDSD, English UASpeech), holding recipe and seed discipline constant. The inference fails. Measured under one pipeline rather than inherited from published figures, all four phoneme-error curves minimise between 83% and 92% of relative depth, each model's minimising layer moving no further between corpora than between probe seeds. The best adaptation band is not similarly constant: none of three natural mappings from the curve to a band -- steepest improvement, lowest band-mean error, or knee location -- recovers the best tested configuration. We do not claim no statistic of the curve could work, only that these three do not. Placement still matters, but is model- and corpus-specific. On CDSD the models disagree -- Qwen3-ASR prefers its upper third (29.88% CER, three-seed mean), Whisper-small all-block LoRA (53.56%) -- while on UASpeech neither favours the upper band. At three seeds, upper-versus-all is resolved (-1.31 pp, CI [-2.16, -0.25]) but upper-versus-middle is not (-0.83 pp, CI [-2.06, +1.86]). The two models' preferences differ as a directly tested interaction (-4.71 pp, CI [-6.17, -2.89]); we designate this the primary claim, and record that the designation was made post hoc. On Qwen3-ASR x UASpeech, adapting near the input is reliably worse than not adapting at all (+9.90 pp CER). We recommend choosing the band by a short adaptation sweep on the model and corpus in hand, not by reading it off a probe.

将语音基础模型高效适配至失语症语音需要一个很少被合理论证的选择：应适配编码器的哪些层。最近提出的一种策略是探测编码器，找出语音信息最易解码的位置，并将适配器置于该处。我们在两个ASR模型（Qwen3-ASR、Whisper-small）和两个失语症语料库（普通话CDSD、英语UASpeech）上测试该推断，保持配方和种子一致性。结果失败：在单一流程中测量而非沿用发表图表，四条音素错误曲线均在相对深度83%至92%之间取得最小值，各模型的最小化层在不同语料库间移动幅度不超过在不同探测种子间的差异。最佳适配区间并不恒定：三种从曲线映射到区间的自然方法——最陡峭改进、最低带均值误差、拐点位置——均未能恢复最佳测试配置。我们并非声称曲线的任何统计量都无法有效，而是指出这三种方法无效。适配位置仍至关重要，但具有模型和语料特异性。在CDSD上，两模型意见相左——Qwen3-ASR偏好其上三分之一（三种子平均CER 29.88%），Whisper-small全块LoRA（53.56%）；而在UASpeech上，两者均不偏好上部区域。在三个种子下，上部 vs 全部的差异显著（-1.31个百分点，置信区间[-2.16, -0.25]），但上部 vs 中部无显著差异（-0.83个百分点，置信区间[-2.06, +1.86]）。两模型偏好存在直接测试交互效应（-4.71个百分点，置信区间[-6.17, -2.89]），我们将其视为主要结论，并注明此判定为事后确定。在Qwen3-ASR × UASpeech组合中，靠近输入处适配的表现显著劣于完全不适配（CER增加9.90个百分点）。我们建议根据实际模型与语料进行短时适配扫描来选择适配区间，而非依赖探针结果读取。

On the Importance of Hidden-State Refinement in Masked Diffusion Language Models
掩码扩散语言模型中隐藏状态精炼的重要性
Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked or corrupted token sequences, allowing token predictions to condition on bidirectional context and update multiple positions in parallel. While each denoising step produces a rich layer-wise hidden-state trajectory, prior work typically focuses only on token-level information and discards these continuous hidden states, leaving their refinement dynamics underexplored. In this work, we analyze hidden-state dynamics in MDLMs and find clear evidence of layer-wise and cross-step refinement: smooth evolution across adjacent layers, greater predictive power in deeper layers, gradual degradation under transformer block removal, and strong final-state alignment across consecutive denoising steps. Motivated by these observations, we propose Cross-Step Recurrent Refinement (CSRR), a mechanism that carries hidden-state information across denoising steps to enhance generation. Extensive experiments show that CSRR improves text generation quality, highlighting the critical role of continuous hidden-state refinement in MDLMs. Project page: https://zhongzero.github.io/Recurrent-DLLM/
中文翻译
掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）通过迭代去噪被遮蔽或损坏的词元序列来生成文本，使词元预测能够依赖双向上下文并并行更新多个位置。尽管每一步去噪都会产生丰富的分层隐藏状态轨迹，但以往研究通常只关注词元级信息，而忽略了这些连续隐藏状态，导致其精炼动态未被充分探索。本文分析了MDLM中的隐藏状态动态，发现存在明显的分层与跨步精炼现象：相邻层间平滑演化、深层具有更强预测能力、移除Transformer模块后性能逐渐退化，以及连续去噪步骤间最终状态高度对齐。受此启发，我们提出跨步递归精炼（CSRR）机制，将隐藏状态信息跨去噪步骤传递以增强生成效果。大量实验表明，CSRR提升了文本生成质量，凸显了连续隐藏状态精炼在MDLM中的关键作用。

Decision Models: An Introduction and Capability Evaluation
决策模型：介绍与能力评估
Applications such as ticket routing and incident triage need decisions that software can act on. Decision models provide these outputs through explicit types for choices, binary judgments, and policy scores. We evaluate the accuracy, output validity, and request latency of eleven configurations on 1,000 English and Chinese cases across nine task suites. StartLux Instant achieves 95.3% overall accuracy, returns valid outputs on all 1,000 cases, and has a median request latency of 0.084 s. It reaches 99.4% on classification and routing and selects the correct response on all 100 tool-selection cases. The highest overall accuracy among the evaluated configurations is 97.1%. These results show how a typed decision service can support fast, programmatic decisions and why its suitability depends on task-specific errors and latency requirements.

ArabFake: A multitask deep learning framework for Arabic fake news Detection, Categorization, and risk prediction
ArabFake：一种用于阿拉伯语假新闻检测、分类与风险预测的多任务深度学习框架
The spread of fake news among Arabic media including social media represents a great challenge to the integrity of information and the trust of the public in it. In this paper, we introduce a comprehensive deep-learning framework, named ArabFake, that goes beyond the binary classification on Arabic fake news detection. ArabFake, built over MARBERTv2 (a state-of-the-art model for multi-dialectal Arabic tweets), proficiently address the complexity of the Arabic language while performing three unified tasks which are fake news detection, content categorization and its risk assessment. The framework promotes efficiency and performance both by enabling multi-task learning through shared knowledge representation across tasks. In order to facilitate development and evaluation, we present the ArabFake Dataset consisting of 2,495 manually labelled news items with labels that are verified by experts regarding fake news categories and risk levels. ArabFake demonstrates robust performance, achieving an F1 score of 94.12% for fake news detection, 84.92% for categorization, and 88.91% for risk zone assessment, highlighting its reliability and effectiveness across multiple tasks. We improve interpretability and extract insight into manipulative techniques by integrating valence scoring as part of the framework that emphasizes misleading linguistic cues used to disseminate fake news within the produced image. The results show that ArabFake is a holistic Arabic fake news detection framework that has practical implications on news organizations and fact checking projects.

SkillCore: Internalizing Generalizable Skills via Scaffolding-Removed Fine-Tuning
SkillCore：通过移除支架的微调内化可泛化的技能
Runtime skills can improve agents' problem-solving capabilities by providing procedural guidance for tasks such as code review and debugging. However, these benefits reach only a minority of users, as most do not proactively invoke skills in practice. We argue that such generalizable procedures should become part of the model's capabilities rather than remain optional external add-ons, making them directly available without explicit skill loading. To this end, we present SkillCore, a data pipeline and supervised fine-tuning recipe for internalizing generalizable skills. We transform real agent trajectories by removing skill-loading scaffolding while preserving the task context and subsequent agent actions, thereby supervising direct execution without external skill content. To preserve access to task-specific skills that provide genuinely external information, we mix these scaffold-removed trajectories with a small proportion of skill-native trajectories that preserve supervision for skill invocation. On a held-out skill-free evaluation, SkillCore improves task completion quality over the base Qwen3-8B and Qwen2.5-72B models by 54.4% and 24.8%, respectively, while preserving task-specific skill invocation on a separate probe and general coding performance on SWE-bench Verified.

Reading, Writing and Repairing Game-Theoretic Policies in Weight Space
在权重空间中阅读、写作与修复博弈论策略
Can a language model work in the weight space of a policy network for a repeated game? We put four questions to a mid-size open model, on recurrent networks of 1{,}218 weights with exactly checkable answers. Given a population of opponents described in English, write a policy that is optimal: its value is a finite computation over the agent's information state, so a submitted network has an exact gap from the optimum. Given a network's weights, report its action after each of fifteen opponent histories; recover the state machine it implements, judged on every opponent sequence to depth ten; or repair a network whose output was altered in one place and say which round first goes wrong. The model recovers the program in 62 of 66 replies, reports all fifteen behavioral entries correctly in 34 of 36, and attains the optimum in 20 of 20 when it states a policy the evaluator compiles. Repair is the exception: 4 of 18 repaired, 5 of 18 diagnosed, with a median patch covering one of the two entries needed.

Localizing Cross-Lingual Generalization in Parameter Space
参数空间中跨语言泛化的定位研究
Cross-lingual transfer is uneven across tasks, succeeding for natural language understanding but often failing for mathematical reasoning. We argue that this asymmetry is governed by the geometry of parameter space. Specifically, cross-lingual generalization is determined by whether English and target-language LoRA adapters share a low-error basin. To test this, we use linear mode connectivity (LMC) between English and target-language adapters, evaluating on three tasks. We find that NLI and QA model-language pairs are mostly linearly connected, whereas mathematical reasoning (Math) pairs are not, indicating that basin geometry is task-dependent. Such disconnection in Math is ``local'' rather than global. A small target-language task sample, applied only to the English adapter's language encoding or semantic composition layers, is enough to repair it. Cross-lingual generalization is also factorizable into language-specific encoding and language-agnostic composition, each supplied separately from a cheaper signal. English task data routed to the composition layers and an unlabeled target-language corpus routed to the language encoding layers each alleviate it on their own for nearly every pair. Composition is the more influential of these, and it needs no target-language data. Together, these results suggest that cross-lingual generalization decomposes into language-specific and language-agnostic components in parameter space. This decomposition offers a practical route for low-resource cross-lingual adaptation.

Faithful Persona Steering under Incongruity via Dual-Stream Refinement
通过双流精炼实现不一致情境下的忠实人格引导
Standard LLM personalization typically frames identity as a static retrieval task, overlooking the inherent incongruity of human personas, where stable traits coexist with atypical, context-specific stances. Existing methods struggle to reconcile these dimensions: prompting succumbs to context drift over long sequences, while fine-tuning often suppresses idiosyncratic "quirks" in favor of generic distributional patterns. To bridge this gap, we present QuirkyMind, a framework that disentangles identity definition from its expression. First, Traits Anchoring constructs a dual-stream latent state, fusing a sentence-level summary for semantic stability with a token-level sequence for generative control. This state is stabilized via In-Context Narrative Refinement using an alternating objective: a discriminative InfoNCE loss anchors the persona in representation space to prevent drift, while a generative cross-entropy loss ensures faithful verbalization. Finally, Persona Steered Generalization transfers the refined state to downstream tasks via parameter-efficient adapters. Empirical evaluations on Persona-Steered QA and Narrative Inference demonstrate that QuirkyMind mitigates drift, consolidating persona knowledge without erasing authentic incongruities.

fork() for Agents: Agent Kernel for Non-blocking LLM Orchestration
Agent的fork()：用于非阻塞LLM编排的代理内核
LLM-based agents typically execute tool calls synchronously and discard completed work upon failure. Subgraph-selective recovery, re-executing only a failed node's transitive descendants, is a well-established pattern in distributed systems (Spark, Erlang/OTP, Dryad), and recent agent runtimes (Sherlock, AgentGit, Atomix) preserve completed work through verifiers, version control, or transactional tool boundaries. We present \textbf{Agent-Kernel}, a non-blocking runtime that ports this pattern to the LLM \emph{content plane}: its primitive, \textbf{Sub-Graph Recovery (SGR)}, fires on a raw exception with no trained verifier or planner, resets a failed node's descendants while preserving completed siblings, and composes each parent's text into its children's prompts (context inheritance), all on a durable substrate with a formal state-preservation guarantee. Across 14{,}434 runs on synthetic and standard benchmarks (tau-bench, Gaia2, AutomationBench, InterruptBench), SGR reaches full recovery within budget=3 where whole-trajectory replay exhausts (100\% vs.\ 55\%), and is roughly 10$\times$ faster than an adapted Sherlock rollback that also recovers. It matches ReAct-serial accuracy within noise on linear workloads while adding recovery and parallelism (up to 12.7$\times$ speedup) where structure permits. Ablation isolates SGR: removing it collapses success to 39.4\%.
中文翻译
基于LLM的代理通常同步执行工具调用，并在失败时丢弃已完成的工作。子图选择性恢复——仅重执行失败节点的传递后代——是分布式系统（如Spark、Erlang/OTP、Dryad）中的成熟模式，近期的代理运行时（Sherlock、AgentGit、Atomix）通过验证器、版本控制或事务性工具边界保留已完成工作。我们提出**Agent-Kernel**，一种非阻塞运行时，将此模式移植到LLM的**内容平面**：其基本操作**子图恢复（SGR）** 在原始异常发生时触发，无需训练过的验证器或规划器，重置失败节点的后代但保留已完成的兄弟节点，并将每个父节点的文本组合进子节点的提示中（上下文继承），所有操作均在具有正式状态保存保证的持久化底座上完成。在14,434次合成及标准基准测试（tau-bench、Gaia2、AutomationBench、InterruptBench）中，SGR在预算=3时实现完全恢复（100% vs. 55%的全轨迹重播），且比适配后的Sherlock回滚快约10倍。在线性工作负载上，其准确率与ReAct-serial相当，同时增加了恢复能力和并行性（最高达12.7倍加速），前提是结构允许。消融实验表明：移除SGR后成功率降至39.4%。
👤 Ankit Chadha, Pratyay Banerjee 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Right Destination, Wrong Journey: Synthetic Counseling Dialogues Miss Human Interactional Dynamics
目的地正确，旅程错误：合成咨询对话遗漏人类互动动态
LLM-generated counseling dialogues are increasingly used for training and evaluating mental-health systems, yet existing evaluations focus largely on response quality and final outcomes rather than how support unfolds over time. We conduct a trajectory-centered evaluation of process fidelity across ten human, reconstructed, and synthetic counseling datasets. Our results show that synthetic dialogues often reach plausible endpoints but differ from human support in recovery dynamics and turn-by-turn responsiveness. While generation design can shift global properties such as recovery timing and trajectory structure, contingent patient--counselor responsiveness remains consistently lower in fidelity across models and generation paradigms. Our experiments on controlled synthetic datasets show that targeted generation interventions can narrow the gap with real data on several trajectory-level measures, but do not fully recover interactional fidelity, with turn-level responsiveness remaining limited. We release three controlled synthetic datasets comprising 2,050 dialogues, together with annotations and the evaluation framework.

Provenance Loss and Repair in Multilingual Compaction
多语言压缩中的溯源损失与修复
Conversational assistants compact older messages into short notes and evict the originals. When the note's language requires a distinction that English leaves open, such as Spanish adjective gender (agotado vs. agotada), messages that omit or explicitly state that attribute can yield the same note. After eviction, later turns cannot tell them apart: taking the inflected form literally asserts an attribute the user never gave, whereas discounting it as routine grammar discards one the user did give. We propose a training-free repair across writing, pre-eviction verification and reading. The writer marks unstated attributes with paired forms (e.g., agotado/a), a likelihood check before eviction rewrites unsupported single forms into paired ones, and the reader trusts remaining single forms as stated facts. On five Qwen3.5 and Gemma-4 models, eviction cuts recall of stated attributes from 90% to 34% in question answering (QA); our repair raises F1 (which penalizes both errors) on every model, by 9-53 points in QA and 12-40 points in agent handovers.
中文翻译
对话助手会将旧消息压缩为简短笔记并删除原消息。当笔记的语言需要区分英语未明确的属性时，如西班牙语形容词性别（agotado vs. agotada），省略或显式说明该属性的消息可能生成相同笔记。删除后，后续对话无法区分二者：将变格形式字面理解会错误地断言用户未提供的属性，而将其视为常规语法则会丢弃用户实际提供的属性。我们提出一种无需训练的修复机制，涵盖写作、删除前验证和阅读三个阶段。写作者用成对形式标记未说明的属性（如agotado/a），删除前进行似然检查，将不支持的单形式重写为成对形式，阅读者则信任剩余的单形式为已陈述的事实。在五个Qwen3.5和Gemma-4模型上，删除导致问答任务中已陈述属性的召回率从90%降至34%；我们的修复在每个模型上均提升了F1分数（惩罚两类错误），问答任务中提升9-53点，代理交接任务中提升12-40点。
👤 Ian Miao, Fangrui Zhu, Bowen Jiang 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

LUCID: Statistically Grounded Self-Correction for LLM Reasoning Evaluation
LUCID：基于统计的 LLM 推理评估自校正方法
Cheap language-model raters grade chain-of-thought reasoning at scale, yet systematic step-rating errors can skew reasoning audits and solution rankings. We introduce LUCID (Loop for Unbiased Calibration via Inference-time Diagnosis), which pairs prompt self-correction with Design-based Supervised Learning (DSL). Without retraining or weight access, LUCID calibrates binary step ratings by aligning predicted and expert soundness rates. From a small expert-labeled set, LUCID summarizes rater bias in plain text and prompts the rater to re-grade unseen steps. For example, on PRM800K, GPT-5-mini grades too strictly, under-rating computation, verification, and late-trace steps most acutely. In one pass, LUCID shrinks GPT-5-mini's calibration gap from 7.8 to 0.9 percentage points in absolute predicted-expert rate difference. LUCID also halves false alarms (sound steps rated unsound) and lifts error-detection F1 on unsound steps by 32%. LUCID retains these gains with 1,000 diagnostic steps or overall-gap feedback alone. The loop also transfers across models and domains: it applies GPT-5-mini's bias profile to lift GLM-5.2's PRM800K accuracy, and it narrows calibration gaps for overly strict but capable raters across math, science, web, and logic benchmarks. Because prompt feedback shifts thresholds without improving class-balanced sound/unsound discrimination, LUCID filters re-grading with pre-/post-checks that skip already calibrated raters and roll back ineffective passes. Finally, we reuse that expert set with raw corpus ratings in DSL for calibration and regression debiasing, and apply DSL to pair-labeled steps for sound-step transitions. DSL's out-of-fold soundness probabilities lift best-of-K selection to match expert labels, while its doubly-robust estimators beat expert-only regression fits at small budgets and cut sound-step transition error by nearly two-thirds.
中文翻译
廉价的语言模型评分器可以大规模评估链式思维推理，但系统性的步骤评分错误可能扭曲推理审计和解决方案排名。我们提出 LUCID（基于推理时诊断的无偏校准循环），将提示自校正与基于设计的监督学习（DSL）相结合。无需重新训练或权重访问，LUCID 通过对齐预测和专家合理率来校准二元步骤评分。从少量专家标注数据出发，LUCID 以自然语言总结评分器偏差，并提示评分器重新评估未见步骤。例如，在 PRM800K 上，GPT-5-mini 评分过于严格，最严重地低估了计算、验证和后期追踪步骤。一次迭代后，LUCID 将 GPT-5-mini 的校准差距从 7.8 个百分点降至 0.9 个百分点（绝对预测-专家率差异）。LUCID 还使误报（被评不合理的合理步骤）减少一半，并将不合理步骤的错误检测 F1 提升 32%。即使仅使用 1,000 个诊断步骤或整体差距反馈，LUCID 也能保持这些收益。该循环还具备跨模型和跨领域的迁移能力：它将 GPT-5-mini 的偏差特征应用于提升 GLM-5.2 在 PRM800K 上的准确率，并缩小在数学、科学、网络和逻辑基准中对过于严格但能力强的评分器的校准差距。由于提示反馈仅调整阈值而未改善类别平衡的合理/不合理区分能力，LUCID 通过预/后检查过滤重评过程，跳过已校准的评分器并回滚无效迭代。最后，我们复用该专家数据集，结合原始语料评分进行 DSL 校准和回归去偏，并将 DSL 应用于成对标注步骤以识别合理步骤的转变。DSL 的交叉验证合理概率使最佳 K 选择匹配专家标签，而其双重稳健估计器在小预算下优于仅依赖专家的回归拟合，并将合理步骤转变误差减少近三分之二。
👤 Satiyabooshan Murugaboopathy, Adel Daoud, Connor T. Jerzak 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

AhaPro: A Process-level Rebus benchmark for probing Aha moments in LLM reasoning
AhaPro：用于探测大语言模型推理中顿悟时刻的过程级字谜基准
Existing research on Aha moments in large language models has largely focused on reflective expressions or final revision outcomes, leaving their role in the visible reasoning process insufficiently characterized. Rebus puzzles encode answers through character forms, spatial arrangements, and homophony. Reinterpreting key details may trigger Aha moments and shifts in the reasoning process and candidate answers. We introduce AhaPro, a process-level benchmark that uses rebus puzzles to examine how models reorganize evidence and revise answers through Aha moments. The framework represents CoT as sequences of candidate answers, evidence, and verification states, using hierarchical metrics to assess conflict detection, state improvement, and subsequent adoption. Across eleven models, we observe frequent failures to adopt better states discovered during Aha moments, alongside adopted revisions that leave state quality unchanged or reduce it. Models also detect conflicts without subsequently achieving an effective revision. Further paired interventions suggest that prior reasoning context may hinder effective revision after conflict detection, while multi-branch search highlights answer selection as a bottleneck between correct-answer discovery and successful correction.
中文翻译
现有对大语言模型顿悟时刻的研究主要集中在反思表达或最终修正结果上，对其在可见推理过程中的作用仍缺乏充分刻画。字谜通过字符形式、空间排列和同音现象编码答案。重新解读关键细节可能触发顿悟时刻，并引发推理过程及候选答案的变化。我们提出 AhaPro，一个基于字谜的过程级基准，用于考察模型如何通过顿悟时刻重新组织证据并修正答案。该框架将思维链（CoT）表示为候选答案、证据和验证状态的序列，采用分层指标评估冲突检测、状态改进及后续采纳情况。在十一款模型中，我们观察到频繁出现未能采纳顿悟时刻发现的更优状态的情况，且已采纳的修正并未提升或反而降低了状态质量。模型还存在检测到冲突但未实现有效修正的现象。进一步的配对干预表明，先前的推理上下文可能阻碍冲突检测后的有效修正，而多分支搜索凸显了正确答案发现与成功修正之间的答案选择瓶颈。

SD-Cache: Learnable Memory Consolidation with State-Guided Dynamic Caching
SD-Cache：基于状态引导的动态缓存可学习记忆整合
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as persistent services that must reason over large volumes of external knowledge. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds model responses in relevant documents, but the retrieved content must still be encoded and stored in the key-value (KV) cache. As this content accumulates, the cache grows linearly, increasing memory consumption and inference cost. Existing memory-compression methods address this problem either by retaining a subset of cached states or by learning context compressors that map documents to compact latent representations. Despite these advances, consolidating information under tight memory budgets while preserving its usefulness for future queries remains challenging. We investigate how the model's native KV states can be selectively consolidated into a fixed-size working memory without access to future queries. To this end, we introduce SD-Cache (State-guided and Dynamically updated Cache), a query-agnostic KV-memory consolidation framework that operates alongside a frozen generator LLM. SD-Cache learns to consolidate layer-wise KV states into fixed-size memory through selective anchor-based pooling and state-guided refinement, processing new contexts in a single forward pass without context-specific optimisation. Experiments on retrieval-augmented question answering and synthetic long-context benchmarks show that SD-Cache maintains competitive downstream performance under constrained memory budgets.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地作为需处理大量外部知识的持久服务部署。检索增强生成（RAG）将模型响应锚定在相关文档上，但所检索内容仍需编码并存储在键值（KV）缓存中。随着内容积累，缓存呈线性增长，导致内存消耗和推理成本上升。现有内存压缩方法要么保留部分缓存状态，要么学习上下文压缩器将文档映射为紧凑的潜在表示。尽管取得进展，但在严苛的内存预算下整合信息并保持其对未来查询的有用性仍具挑战。我们研究如何在不访问未来查询的前提下，将模型原生 KV 状态选择性地整合进固定大小的工作内存。为此，我们提出 SD-Cache（状态引导且动态更新的缓存），一种与冻结生成器 LLM 并行运行的无查询依赖 KV 内存整合框架。SD-Cache 通过选择性锚点池化和状态引导精炼，将逐层 KV 状态整合为固定大小的记忆，以单次前向传播处理新上下文，无需特定于上下文的优化。在检索增强问答和合成长上下文基准上的实验表明，SD-Cache 在受限内存预算下仍能保持有竞争力的下游性能。

RegioZH: Unveiling Comprehension Disparities across Regional Chinese Varieties in LLMs
RegioZH：揭示大语言模型在汉语区域变体中的理解差异
Current evaluations often treat Chinese varieties as monolithic, obscuring failures for regional users. We introduce RegioZH, a 2,607-item multiple-choice benchmark for regional lexical reasoning across Mainland China, Taiwan, Hong Kong, Singapore, and Malaysia. Built by region-specific annotators and expert-validated, it targets localized idioms, slang, code-mixing, politeness conventions, and sociocultural expressions. Zero-shot results on eleven LLMs show large disparities, including a 29.4-point average accuracy gap between Taiwan and Singapore. Persona and structured chain-of-thought prompts for five models yield selective gains, suggesting that some errors stem from retrieval or framing rather than only missing knowledge. Representation, attention, calibration, and surface-feature analyses show a dissociation between marker attention and semantic grounding, overconfident forced-choice errors, and localized lexical density as a consistent difficulty predictor. Qualitative analysis finds terminology misunderstandings, sociocultural inference gaps, and cross-regional semantic divergence. RegioZH demonstrates that apparent Chinese proficiency can mask uneven understanding across regional varieties, motivating intra-language robustness evaluation alongside multilingual generalization.

Capability and Interpretability of Transformers on Multi-task Recurrence
Transformer在多任务递归任务中的能力与可解释性
Transformers are widely used in various language tasks, with limited understanding of their internal mechanisms. Motivated by the recurrent structure of many linguistic rules, we study the capability and interpretability of Transformers through a controlled family of linear recurrence tasks over finite cyclic groups. These tasks range from executing a single fixed rule to identifying a latent rule from context and switching between explicitly specified rules, with and without input masking. Through extensive experiments across different configurations, we find that switching specified rules is simpler than identifying latent rules for Transformers in general. We also identify how several key factors affect the generalization capability of Transformers, including the depth, width, and the algebraic structure of the linear recurrence. Curriculum training is extremely useful when the number of rules is large. With standard tools in mechanistic interpretability like linear probing and input patching, our study reveals how Transformers represent, identify, and execute multiple rules.

RECAP: Training Long-horizon LLM Agents with Context Compaction
RECAP：通过上下文压缩训练长时程大语言模型智能体
Context compaction enables large language model agents to tackle long-horizon tasks within limited context budgets by replacing interaction histories with compressed summaries. However, learning effective compaction jointly with the agent policy remains challenging, as summaries affect task success over many subsequent interactions. We introduce RECAP, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework based on the principle that a summary should preserve the behavior induced by the original interaction history. We formalize this principle with a behavior distortion objective that measures the divergence between the agent's subsequent behavior under compressed and original contexts. We show that the gradient of the distortion objective decomposes into two complementary learning signals: distortion-based reward for summary generation and self-distillation for subsequent actions. Experiments on Qwen3.5-4B show that RECAP consistently outperforms the task-reward-only RL baselines on both environments, leading to an absolute improvement of 4.6% and 4.3% on BrowseComp-plus and SWE-bench Verified, respectively. Ablations further highlight the value of jointly supervising summary generation and post-compaction behavior for RECAP.

👤 Yulun Jiang, Maria Brbic, Michael Moor 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

scOPD: Routed On-Policy Self-Distillation for LLM-Based Single-Cell Annotation
scOPD：面向基于大语言模型单细胞注释的路由型在线自蒸馏方法
Motivation: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for single-cell annotation, but existing training methods mainly rely on scalar outcome rewards that provide limited supervision for intermediate biological reasoning. Batch-level annotation is particularly challenging because it requires jointly resolving local cell identities and a global one-to-one matching constraint, such that one local error can affect the entire assignment. Moreover, a single inference pass provides insufficient evidence for locating uncertain predictions. These limitations call for fine-grained reasoning supervision during training and uncertainty-aware refinement during inference. Results: To address these challenges, we propose scOPD, an on-policy self-distillation framework for LLM-based single-cell annotation. After reasoning fine-tuning, scOPD adaptively routes qualified rollouts to reward-based optimization, while erroneous rollouts receive privileged token-level supervision from successful sibling trajectories. During inference, rollout disagreement triggers constraint-aware pairwise refinement of uncertain assignments. Across CellPuzzles and four out-of-domain disease datasets, scOPD consistently improves the performance of diverse LLM backbones, achieving average cell-level and batch-level accuracies of 81.71% and 44.49% with Qwen3-8B. Availability and Implementation: Code and data are available at https://github.com/YuZhiyin/scOPD.
中文翻译
动机：大语言模型在单细胞注释中展现出潜力，但现有训练方法主要依赖标量结果奖励，对中间生物推理过程提供监督不足。批量注释尤其具有挑战性，因为它需要同时解决局部细胞身份与全局一一匹配约束，任一局部错误都可能影响整体分配。此外，单次推理提供的证据不足以定位不确定预测。这些局限性呼唤训练期间的细粒度推理监督和推理阶段的不确定性感知优化。结果：为应对这些挑战，我们提出scOPD，一种基于大语言模型的单细胞注释在线自蒸馏框架。在推理微调后，scOPD将合格轨迹自适应路由至基于奖励的优化，而错误轨迹则接收来自成功兄弟轨迹的特权标记级监督。在推理阶段，轨迹分歧会触发针对不确定分配的约束感知成对优化。在CellPuzzles及四个域外疾病数据集上，scOPD consistently 提升了多种大语言模型骨干网络的性能，使用Qwen3-8B时达到平均细胞级准确率81.71%和批次级准确率44.49%。可用性与实现：代码与数据可在 https://github.com/YuZhiyin/scOPD 获取。
👤 Zhiyin Yu, Yuchen Mou, Songtao Huang, Shuaiyu Zhang, Xing Wei et al. 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

DiaCap: Jointly Learning Who Speaks When for Audiovisual Dialogue Captioning
DiaCap：联合学习音频视觉对话中的说话人与时间
We present DiaCap, an audiovisual LLM for comprehensive dialogue captioning, which produces the transcript, timestamps, and a visual description of the speaker for every utterance in a video. Considering that utterance timing and audiovisual diarization are tightly coupled, DiaCap unifies the tasks in a single model, trained in two complementary stages. First, we train DiaCap on 225K curated clips, using synthetic captions that densely label every utterance in detail. Second, we further refine the model via reinforcement learning on 2.9K human-corrected clips, using fine-grained feedback that directly evaluates utterance timing, speaker grouping, and speaker binding. On DiaDemBench, which we extend with utterance-level timestamps and subtitle redaction, both DiaCap with off-the-shelf transcripts and its end-to-end variant DiaCap-E2E achieve strong results. Specifically, DiaCap performs on par with Gemini 4 Argon in audiovisual diarization while clearly outperforming it in timestamp accuracy (76.59 vs. 42.76 TIME). It also maintains strong diarization on multilingual and minute-long videos, and transfers to audiovisual question answering.
中文翻译
我们提出DiaCap，一种用于全面对话字幕生成的音频视觉大语言模型，能够为视频中的每一句发言生成完整转录、时间戳以及说话人的视觉描述。考虑到发言时间与音视频语音分离任务紧密耦合，DiaCap在单一模型中统一处理这些任务，采用两个互补的训练阶段。首先，我们在22.5万个精心筛选的片段上训练DiaCap，使用密集标注每句发言的合成字幕。其次，我们通过强化学习在2.9千个人工校正的片段上进一步优化模型，使用细粒度反馈直接评估发言时间、说话人分组和说话人绑定。在我们扩展了发言级时间戳和字幕去重功能的DiaDemBench基准上，使用现成转录的DiaCap及其端到端变体DiaCap-E2E均取得优异表现。具体而言，DiaCap在音视频语音分离方面达到与Gemini 4 Argon相当的水平，但在时间戳准确性上明显优于后者（76.59 vs 42.76 TIME）。它还在多语言和长达一分钟的视频上保持良好语音分离能力，并可迁移至音视频问答任务。

A Bias in Disguise: Tiny Gradient Edits Stop a Small Language Model's Thinking Loops and Complement a Frontier Model's Weight Edit
伪装的偏差：微小梯度编辑阻止小型语言模型的思维循环并补充前沿模型的权重编辑
Qwen3.5-0.8B rarely finishes reasoning in thinking mode: at a 4,096-token budget only 1 or 2 of 500 MATH-500 answers close their think block (two seeds), and accuracy is 15.4%. A recent demonstration had GPT-6 Astra edit 131,072 of the model's weights without gradients and reported MATH-500 rising from 7.9% to 48.1% at a 32,768-token budget. We provide the missing gradient baseline, then combine the two edits. Fine-tuning only the 140 to 139,750 weights with the largest gradient, on the model's own correct answers to MATH training problems, raises thinking-mode MATH-500 at 4k tokens to 39.0-45.5% after 2-9 minutes of training on one GPU; 50 training examples suffice, and 1,398 random positions give only 16.3%. The gradient concentrates the edit on the most nearly constant channel of each of the first three layers. Because the model has no bias terms, an edit confined to those channels acts as a constant vector added to the residual stream: adding three fixed vectors to the unedited model matches the accuracy of such an edit. Under the Astra protocol (32k tokens, presence penalty 1.5), the Astra edit scores 52.1% (finished and correct) in our reproduction and our full-strength edits reach 45.6-45.7%, but ours win at every budget up to 16k tokens (39.5% against 6.7% at 4k). The two edits are complementary. The Astra edit plus our 139,750-weight edit at half strength scores 62.2% at 32k, 10 points above the Astra edit alone (p = 3e-9), with half as many tokens; our half-strength edit alone scores 46.7%.
中文翻译
Qwen3.5-0.8B在思考模式下很少能完成推理：在4,096个token预算下，仅500个MATH-500问题中有1到2个关闭了思考块（两个种子），准确率为15.4%。最近一项演示显示，GPT-6 Astra在不使用梯度的情况下修改了模型的131,072个权重，报告在32,768个token预算下MATH-500准确率从7.9%提升至48.1%。我们提供了缺失的梯度基线，然后结合两种编辑方法。仅对模型自身正确答案上的140至139,750个梯度最大的权重进行微调，在单个GPU上训练2-9分钟后，4k token预算下的思考模式MATH-500准确率提升至39.0%-45.5%；仅需50个训练样本，而随机选择1,398个位置仅得16.3%。梯度集中于前三层中最接近恒定的通道。由于模型无偏置项，仅限于这些通道的编辑相当于向残差流添加一个常数向量：向未编辑模型添加三个固定向量即可达到类似编辑的准确率。在Astra协议（32k token，存在惩罚1.5）下，我们复现的Astra编辑得分为52.1%（完成且正确），而我们的全强度编辑达到45.6%-45.7%，但在每个预算（最高16k token）下均表现更优（4k时为39.5% vs 6.7%）。两种编辑具有互补性。Astra编辑与我们半强度的139,750权重编辑结合，在32k token下得分为62.2%，比Astra单独编辑高出10个百分点（p=3e-9），且仅需一半token；我们半强度编辑单独使用得分为46.7%。

Latent Merger: Latent-Space Composition of Hypernetwork-Generated LoRAs
潜在融合：超网络生成LoRA的潜在空间组合
Recent work proposes hypernetworks that convert a document into a low-rank parameter update (LoRA) in a single forward pass, enabling fast injection of new knowledge into a frozen LLM. However, storing separate LoRA adapters for each document can be unsustainable in streaming settings. In this paper, we study the problem of composing hypernetwork-generated LoRAs using two representative systems, Doc-to-LoRA and SHINE. We show that hypernetwork-generated LoRAs have a geometry sharply different from directly fine-tuned LoRAs. They exhibit greater spectral concentration and stronger cross-document alignment than next-token prediction and QA fine-tuned adapters, which are diffuse and nearly orthogonal across documents. Guided by this observation, we propose latent-space LoRA merging. We compose the intermediate hypernetwork latents and then decode the merged latent through the frozen LoRA-generation head. We show that latent-space merging techniques consistently improve over weight-space and factor-space baselines while preserving a single fixed-rank adapter. These results support latent-space composition as a promising mechanism for combining hypernetwork-generated LoRAs under a fixed output-rank budget.
中文翻译
近期工作提出超网络可在一次前向传播中将文档转换为低秩参数更新（LoRA），实现快速注入新知识到冻结的大语言模型中。然而，在流式场景中为每篇文档单独存储LoRA适配器可能不可持续。本文研究了使用两种代表性系统（Doc-to-LoRA和SHINE）对超网络生成的LoRA进行组合的问题。我们发现，超网络生成的LoRA在几何结构上与直接微调的LoRA显著不同：它们表现出更强的谱集中性和跨文档对齐性，而下一词预测和问答微调的适配器则呈弥散状且几乎正交。基于此观察，我们提出潜在空间LoRA融合。我们先组合中间超网络潜在表示，再通过冻结的LoRA生成头解码合并后的潜在表示。结果表明，潜在空间融合技术始终优于权重空间和因子空间基线，同时保持单一固定秩适配器。这些结果支持潜在空间组合作为在固定输出秩预算下融合超网络生成LoRA的一种有前景机制。
👤 Yanlai Yang, Minki Kang, Divyam Madaan, Sung Ju Hwang, Mengye Ren 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Towards Realistic Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification for LLM-Based Recommender Systems
迈向大语言模型推荐系统不确定性量化的真实评估
Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for recommendation systems. However, their issued rankings require reliable instance-level uncertainty monitoring. In this scenario, comparing uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods becomes challenging because LLM rankers lack a standard item-classification head. Their scores may depend on option labels, prompts, generation procedures, and the available user and item evidence. Existing methods derive uncertainty from different sources and apply it at different stages of the recommendation process. Some methods evaluate an issued ranking, whereas others use uncertainty to change the ranking itself, making direct comparison difficult. Here we introduce a fixed-ranking benchmark which evaluates eight heterogeneous UQ signals as record-level monitors under a shared output contract. We evaluate these signals across four recommendation datasets and two LLM backbones under a shared fixed-ranking protocol. From the results, we draw three main observations. First, UQ method ordering changes across backbones even on the same reference instances. Second, ranking-aligned Top10Mass provides the strongest scalar Error@10 detection in several evaluation conditions, reflecting effective cutoff-aligned monitoring, while its advantage narrows for popularity-tail targets. Third, scalar UQ signals are weak at identifying which control has the larger clipped NDCG@10 difference, whereas source-specific signals, including Perturbation Rank Instability and simple listwise distances, provide stronger paired-control agreement, reflecting their sensitivity to the two controlled evidence sources. These findings motivate us to evaluate ranking-failure detection, robustness across recommendation conditions, and responses to controlled evidence changes as distinct properties of UQ.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）为推荐系统提供了灵活的接口。然而，其生成的排名需要可靠的实例级不确定性监控。在此场景中，比较不确定性量化（UQ）方法具有挑战性，因为LLM排名器缺乏标准的项目分类头。其得分可能依赖于选项标签、提示、生成过程以及可用的用户和项目证据。现有方法从不同来源推导不确定性，并在推荐流程的不同阶段应用。一些方法评估已生成的排名，而另一些则使用不确定性改变排名本身，导致直接比较困难。我们引入了一个固定排名基准，评估八种异构的UQ信号作为记录级监控器，在统一的输出契约下进行评估。我们在四个推荐数据集和两个LLM骨干网络上，采用统一的固定排名协议评估这些信号。结果得出三个主要观察：第一，即使在同一参考实例上，UQ方法排序也随骨干网络变化；第二，与排名对齐的Top10Mass在多种评估条件下提供最强的标量Error@10检测能力，反映有效的截断对齐监控，但在流行度尾部目标上的优势减弱；第三，标量UQ信号在识别哪个控制具有更大的截断NDCG@10差异方面表现较弱，而源特定信号（如扰动排名不稳定性及简单列表距离）提供更强的成对控制一致性，反映出对两个受控证据源的敏感性。这些发现促使我们将排名失败检测、跨推荐条件的鲁棒性以及对受控证据变化的响应视为UQ的独立属性。

On Potentiality and Actuality Of Foundation Models
基础模型的潜能与现实
Foundation models are built through large-scale pretraining, often followed by post-training, and are then adapted to specific tasks and behaviors. But, does the adaptation add truly new capabilities, or the base model already holds it? We decompose the update induced by different adaptations into a component shared across all inputs and a residual that varies with the input. We find that the shared component suffices for most of the adaptation gain and can be learned directly: a shift adapter that learns a constant vector per layer, identical for every input, reproduces the behavior of LoRA and full fine-tuning. We call this the potentiality principle of foundation models: the base model already contains the capacity needed for task-specific expertise. To adapt to a new task, the model just needs to be actualized, i.e., pushed in a certain direction via a constant, input-independent shift. We show the implications of this principle on parameter-efficient adaptation, steering, and convergence across models, and how it explains the neural thickets phenomenon.
中文翻译
基础模型通过大规模预训练构建，通常随后进行后训练，并适应特定任务和行为。但这种适应是否带来了真正的新能力，还是基础模型本身已具备这些能力？我们把不同适应带来的更新分解为一个对所有输入共享的成分和一个随输入变化的残差部分。研究发现，共享成分足以解释大部分适应收益，且可直接学习：一种每层学习恒定向量的“转移适配器”（shift adapter），对所有输入相同，能复现LoRA和全微调的行为。我们称之为基础模型的‘潜能原则’：基础模型已具备实现特定任务专长所需的能力。要适应新任务，只需将其‘现实化’，即通过恒定的、与输入无关的偏移推动模型朝某一方向发展。我们展示了该原则在参数高效适应、模型引导和收敛性方面的意义，并解释了神经丛林现象。
👤 Fabian Gröger, Shuo Wen, Maria Brbić 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
