When Repair Accuracy Misleads: Training a VLM on Its Own Failures
修复准确率具有误导性：基于自身失败训练视觉-语言模型
Fine-tuning a vision-language model on its own failures is a direct way to repair it, but accuracy on held-out failures, the obvious measure of success, misleads. Many mined failures are not model errors: a human audit of 100 failures of Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct on questions generated from COCO and GQA found label errors in 37. And mined failures share wording regularities: question-only classifiers get both answers right on 73.2% of held-out failure pairs. We study both traps with verified failure-driven repair (VFR): typed minimal-pair failure mining, verification by three LLM judges that answer without seeing the stored labels, answer-first corrective targets, and LoRA fine-tuning with replay. VFR repairs 82.8% of held-out failures (base model: 10.0%), and on independently labeled, unconditioned pairs it raises overall accuracy from 87.4% to 92.4% through spatial relations, whose accuracy rises from 66.7% to 96.8% even where the wording points to the wrong answer. Yet on negation, of which it repairs 88.9% of held-out failures, its accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls from 88.7% to 79.7%: it breaks negations about absent objects, rare in its training set. Without verification, accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls to 80.8%, below the base model's 87.4%, through a negation shortcut our development benchmarks miss; without failure selection, it stays at 92.0% (one seed) while repair falls to 73.4%. Training also lowers two-image relation accuracy (74.3% to 49.6%; replay data alone: 48.4%); multi-image replay partly and unevenly reverses this. Paraphrasing the spatial questions keeps most of the gain (87.3% vs. the base model's 62.0%), though rewording lowers spatial repair on held-out failures from 92.9% to 76.8%. On What'sUp's independent photos of objects under others, caption choice stays perfect while accuracy on our yes/no pairs falls from 84.5% to 26.4%.
中文翻译
对视觉-语言模型在其自身失败上进行微调是一种直接的修复方式，但对保留失败集的准确率——这一显而易见的成功度量——具有误导性。许多挖掘出的失败并非模型错误：对Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在COCO和GQA生成问题上的100个失败进行人工审计发现，其中37个存在标签错误。此外，挖掘出的失败具有语言规律性：仅基于问题的分类器在73.2%的保留失败对中能正确回答两个问题。我们通过验证失败驱动修复（VFR）研究这两种陷阱：类型化的最小差异失败挖掘、由三位LLM裁判在不查看存储标签的情况下作答的验证、先回答再纠正的目标设定，以及带重放的LoRA微调。VFR修复了82.8%的保留失败（基线模型仅为10.0%），在独立标注、无条件的样本上，整体准确率从87.4%提升至92.4%，空间关系准确率从66.7%升至96.8%，即使语言提示指向错误答案也有效。然而，在否定句方面，尽管修复了88.9%的保留失败，其在无条件样本上的准确率却从88.7%降至79.7%：它在处理训练集中罕见的“不存在物体”的否定时失效。若无验证，无条件样本准确率降至80.8%，低于基线模型的87.4%，这是开发基准未捕捉到的否定捷径；若无失败选择，准确率维持在92.0%（单次种子），但修复效果降至73.4%。训练还降低了双图像关系的准确率（74.3%降至49.6%；仅重放数据：48.4%）；多图像重放部分且不均衡地逆转此趋势。改写空间问题保留了大部分增益（87.3% vs 基线62.0%），但重述降低了在保留失败上的空间修复率，从92.9%降至76.8%。在What'sUp独立图片中，对象被其他对象遮挡时，标题选择保持完美，但在我们的是/否对上，准确率从84.5%降至26.4%。
👤 Ang Qi, Yue Wang 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32918]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
InstructReplay: Replaying Assistant Turns to Preserve General Behavior During Factual Fine-Tuning
InstructReplay：通过重播助手回复保留事实微调过程中的通用行为
Fine-tuning an instruction-tuned language model on new facts often erodes its general abilities. We find that standard fine-tuning lowers instruction-following, reasoning and coding benchmarks by 35 points on average, and that these losses track a collapse in answer length: fine-tuned models at least halve their responses, and the further a response drifts from the initial model's length, the more often it fails. We propose InstructReplay: the initial model answers a set of general instructions once, and during fine-tuning the model is kept close to the initial model on these answers. Across three model families and two domains, InstructReplay reduces the average decline on five general benchmarks from 28.1 to 2.4 points and keeps answer length close to the initial model's, while learning the new facts as well as standard fine-tuning. It balances learning and retention better than seven adaptation methods in all six settings. Controlled comparisons attribute the gain to replaying the model's own answers to general instructions rather than to the form of the loss.
中文翻译
在新事实上对指令微调的语言模型进行微调时，往往会削弱其通用能力。我们发现，标准微调平均使指令遵循、推理和编程基准下降35分，且这些损失与回答长度的崩溃密切相关：微调后的模型至少将响应长度减半，且响应偏离初始模型长度越远，失败频率越高。我们提出InstructReplay：初始模型先对一组通用指令进行一次回答，微调过程中则保持模型在这些回答上接近初始模型。在三个模型家族和两个领域中，InstructReplay将五个通用基准的平均下降从28.1分降至2.4分，同时保持回答长度接近初始模型，且学习新事实的能力与标准微调相当。在所有六个设置中，其学习与保留的平衡优于七种其他适应方法。受控对比实验表明，该优势源于重播模型自身对通用指令的回答，而非损失形式本身。
👤 Xiaofeng Shi, Qian Kou, Hua Zhou 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32802]

Sample-Efficient In-Context Adaptation via Sleep
通过睡眠实现高效的上下文自适应
The ability to learn and generalize from limited experience, or sample efficiency, is a cornerstone of intelligence. How can we build language models that turn a few user interactions, a few snippets of unfamiliar code, or a handful of experimental results provided in context into knowledge that generalizes? Prior work has found that sleep, a process where the model performs $N$ recurrent forward passes to refine its representations of a chunk of context, can improve long-context reasoning (specifically, within SSM-attention hybrid models). We study whether this method also improves in-context adaptation on general, non-synthetic tasks for transformer language models. To this end, we pre-train language models up to 1.2B-parameters on up to 100B tokens with sleep mechanisms. Notably, longer sleep (i.e., using a larger number of recurrent forward passes $N$) enables the model to learn faster in-context on multiple few-shot algorithmic, question-answering tasks, while also achieving lower next-token prediction loss on out-of-distribution evaluations. On held-out code, models with $N=2$ and $N=4$ match the no-sleep baseline's loss using $2.10\times$ and $2.39\times$ fewer tokens in-context, respectively. More broadly, we view our work as a step toward building models that have better tradeoffs between in-context adaptation and decoding latency.
中文翻译
从有限经验中学习并泛化的能力，即样本效率，是智能的核心特征。我们如何构建语言模型，使其能够将少量用户交互、几段陌生代码或若干实验结果等上下文信息转化为可泛化的知识？先前研究表明，睡眠（sleep）——即模型对一段上下文执行N次递归前向传播以优化其表征——可提升长上下文推理能力（特别是SSM-attention混合模型）。我们研究该方法是否也能提升Transformer语言模型在一般非合成任务上的上下文自适应能力。为此，我们预训练了高达12亿参数的语言模型，最多使用1000亿token，并引入睡眠机制。值得注意的是，更长的睡眠（即更大的递归前向传播次数N）使模型在多个少样本算法、问答任务上更快实现上下文学习，同时在分布外评估中也实现了更低的下一个词预测损失。在保留代码上，N=2和N=4的模型分别仅需2.10倍和2.39倍于无睡眠基线的上下文token数即可达到相同损失。更广泛地说，我们认为这项工作是朝着构建在上下文自适应与解码延迟之间具有更好权衡的模型迈出的重要一步。

Gated Concept Adapters: Layer-Wise Concept Bottlenecks for Auditable Text Understanding
门控概念适配器：可审计文本理解的逐层概念瓶颈
Human-facing text understanding systems are used to infer affect, moderate harmful content, and support socially sensitive decisions. However, Transformer predictions are difficult to audit because they are produced from high-dimensional hidden states that do not provide a compact, inspectable interface for human analysis or intervention. We introduce Gated Concept Adapters (GCA), a layer-wise concept bottleneck framework that attaches gated, low-dimensional concept-producing readout adapters to selected outputs of pretrained Transformers. Token representations are mapped into concept activations, filtered by learned gates, pooled into layer-level summaries, and exposed to the prediction head as a concept-mediated interface. The dimensions can be inspected through usage statistics, signature n-grams, post-hoc concept names, and targeted concept interventions. We evaluate GCA on fine-grained emotion classification, toxicity detection, topic classification, and a controlled verb-cloze stress test. In the affective and safety-relevant classification settings, GCA learns comparatively low-redundancy and intervention-sensitive concept representations while retaining useful predictive performance. On toxicity detection, it remains close to an unconstrained ModernBERT baseline while enabling concept-level inspection, targeted intervention, and lightweight post-hoc control. On GoEmotions, the end-to-end concept model incurs a macro-F1 cost relative to unconstrained RoBERTa, while a task-initialized frozen variant substantially reduces this gap. On AG News, GCA remains competitive with recent concept-based baselines, and the Qwen verb-cloze experiment clarifies the limits of concept-mediated full-vocabulary prediction. Overall, GCA provides a neural concept interface for auditable text understanding, prioritizing transparency, diagnosis, and intervention over unconstrained accuracy maximization.
中文翻译
面向人类的文本理解系统用于推断情感、监管有害内容并支持社会敏感决策。然而，Transformer的预测难以审计，因其来自高维隐藏状态，缺乏紧凑且可检查的人类分析或干预接口。我们提出门控概念适配器（GCA），一种逐层概念瓶颈框架，将带有门控的低维概念生成读出适配器附加到预训练Transformer的选定输出上。将标记表示映射为概念激活，经由学习的门控过滤后，聚合为层级摘要，并作为概念中介接口提供给预测头。其维度可通过使用统计、签名n-gram、事后概念名称及针对性概念干预进行检查。我们在细粒度情绪分类、毒性检测、主题分类及受控动词填空压力测试中评估GCA。在情感与安全相关分类场景中，GCA学习到冗余较低且易于干预的概念表示，同时保持有用的预测性能。在毒性检测中，其性能接近无约束的ModernBERT基线，同时实现概念级检查、目标干预和轻量级事后控制。在GoEmotions上，端到端概念模型相对于无约束RoBERTa略有宏F1损失，而任务初始化冻结变体显著缩小了这一差距。在AG News上，GCA与近期基于概念的基线相当，Qwen动词填空实验揭示了概念中介全词汇预测的局限性。总体而言，GCA为可审计的文本理解提供了神经概念接口，优先考虑透明性、诊断与干预，而非无约束的准确性最大化。

Steerable by Design: Conditional Pretraining for Robust Control of Language Models
按设计可调控：用于鲁棒语言模型控制的条件预训练
Our goal is to build language models that are steerable by design. Today's language models are pretrained with next-token prediction, which relegates alignment and control to the final 1% of training tokens or inference-time interventions. These models repurpose a single input channel for both instructions and data, leading to common failure modes where the model mistakenly treats data as instructions such as intentional jailbreaks and unintentional in-context alignment drift. To correct this, we propose a pretraining method and architecture that separates the two from the very start, producing Mancelle-3B. An encoder processes high-level semantic features of upcoming text with cross-attention that prevents the token stream from writing back into this control channel. During pretraining, the model learns to rely on these features to predict the next token, turning the channel into an effective handle for steering the model. Mancelle-3B steers more strongly than prompt- and activation-based methods on comparable open models while maintaining fluency, and it withstands adversarial black-box and soft-prompt counter-steering that defeats those baselines. We reproduce this against a more controlled baseline with Mancelle-400M. Our results show that robust control can be built into language models if we do so early and intentionally.
中文翻译
我们的目标是构建按设计即可调控的语言模型。当前的语言模型通常通过下一个词预测进行预训练，这使得对齐与控制被推迟到训练最后1%的tokens或推理时的干预。这些模型将单一输入通道同时用于指令和数据，导致常见故障模式，例如模型错误地将数据当作指令，引发有意的越狱攻击或无意的上下文对齐漂移。为解决此问题，我们提出一种预训练方法和架构，从一开始就分离这两个要素，从而生成Mancelle-3B。该编码器处理未来文本的高层语义特征，通过交叉注意力防止词流回写入该控制通道。在预训练过程中，模型学会依赖这些特征来预测下一个词，从而将该通道转变为有效的调控手段。Mancelle-3B在同等开放模型上表现出比基于提示和激活的方法更强的调控能力，同时保持流畅性，并能抵御对抗性的黑盒和软提示反调控，这些基线方法在此类攻击下失效。我们通过Mancelle-400M复现了这一结果，证明若早期且有意识地构建，鲁棒控制是可以融入语言模型的。

ThinkMorph-SSL: Scaling Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning with Pretext Tasks
ThinkMorph-SSL：通过预训练任务实现交错多模态推理的规模化
People reason with pictures as well as words. Interleaved multimodal reasoning gives models a similar way to solve problems by creating intermediate images and using them to guide further reasoning. Training models with this ability usually relies on task-specific demonstrations, which are difficult to collect. We introduce ThinkMorph-SSL, a two-stage training recipe that uses images themselves as a source of supervision. The first stage, pretext reasoning, converts visual pretext tasks, such as image colorization and denoising, into instruction-free interleaved traces. Models describe a corrupted or transformed image, generate a restored or canonical image, and interpret the result. The second stage uses automatically constructed demonstrations for question-driven instruction tuning. At the same instruction-tuning budget, models trained with pretext reasoning outperform those trained with instruction tuning alone. The full recipe improves over the base model by an average of 11.33 accuracy points across twelve benchmarks. These results show that supervision derived from images can build the visual skills needed for interleaved reasoning and improve learning from task-specific demonstrations.
中文翻译
人们既用图像也用文字进行推理。交错多模态推理为模型提供了类似的方法，通过创建中间图像并利用它们引导进一步推理来解决问题。训练具备此能力的模型通常依赖于特定任务的示范，而这些示范难以收集。我们提出了ThinkMorph-SSL，一种两阶段训练方案，利用图像本身作为监督来源。第一阶段“预训练推理”将视觉预训练任务（如图像着色和去噪）转化为无需指令的交错推理轨迹。模型描述被破坏或变换的图像，生成修复或标准图像，并解释结果。第二阶段使用自动生成的示范进行面向问题的指令微调。在相同的指令微调预算下，采用预训练推理训练的模型优于仅使用指令微调训练的模型。完整训练方案在十二个基准上平均提升11.33个百分点的准确率。这些结果表明，源自图像的监督可以构建实现交错推理所需的视觉技能，并改善从特定任务示范中学习的效果。

Calibrated Reading of Latent Reasoning and Communication
校准的潜在推理与通信阅读
Latent reasoning and key-value communication store intermediate information in continuous representations, limiting direct inspection of model behavior. Existing activation readers trade the low cost of task-specific probes against the flexibility of language-model decoders. We propose the Calibrated Latent Reader, CLR, a compact transformer that predicts reasoning outcomes and message content through task-conditioned interactions between states and candidate answers. Source adapters support transfer across models, while conformal calibration provides coverage and false-alarm guarantees under exchangeability. On two CODI models, CLR improves accuracy over MLP and attention probes by 4-18 percentage points for final-answer prediction and reference-value membership. On CODI-GPT-2, it achieves accuracy comparable to a 1.7B LatentQA-based verbalizer using 437-648 times fewer inference FLOPs. On synthetic Qwen3-4B communications, comparing decoded facts with public commitments detects 93.1% of message substitutions at a nominal 5% false-positive rate.

HuluOPD: Unifying Heterogeneous Medical Specialists into One Lightweight Model with Unlabeled Prompts
HuluOPD：通过无标签提示统一异构医学专家模型为轻量级单一模型
Specialist medical vision-language models (VLMs) are strong within their specialties but hard to unify: their training data are often private, and independently developed specialists cannot be assumed to share an architecture. Deploying these large models separately is costly. We present HuluOPD, an on-policy distillation recipe that unifies heterogeneous specialists into one lightweight VLM using only unlabeled prompts, without requiring reference answers or the specialists' training data. The lightweight model answers each prompt, and the specialist of the prompt's modality supervises its response; a continuation-aligned cross-tokenizer loss makes this possible even when they differ in architecture and tokenizer. We apply HuluOPD to five specialists, for ultrasound, pathology, optical coherence tomography, fundus photography, and dental imaging. To distill and evaluate them, we build HuluOPDCorpus, 914k unlabeled prompts, and HuluOPDBench, 47k held-out test items, from over 90 public datasets, and score all models under one protocol on 132k test items, including third-party benchmarks. A single model with 10% of the five specialists' combined parameters retains 94% of their question-answering performance, and one with 2% retains 89%. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of unifying heterogeneous medical specialist VLMs without requiring their training data or a shared architecture.

Subliminal Learning Crosses Modalities
隐性学习跨越模态
Subliminal learning refers to a surprising phenomenon in language models wherein a prompt given to one model (e.g., preference for cats) can be subliminally distilled to another model via training on data that is ostensibly meaningless (e.g., number sequences). This paper shows that text-based subliminal learning transfers cross-modally, in particular, a topic/preference (e.g., animals) transferred through number sequences across vision-language models also changes how images are generated and represented. First, we show that a diffusion model using the student as its text encoder generates the student's animal more often than with the original model as its text encoder, but not its color or style. We then investigate the mechanism behind this phenomenon and which concepts transfer across modalities. A constant component of the student's change accounts for much of the shift of image representations toward the trait. Adding this component uniformly can change the generated animal, which depends on the whole prompt, but not concepts such as color or style, which are encoded in a single token. Finally, we introduce weight lens as an approach to identify a subliminal trait from the weights of a single trained model, and show that it identifies the trait of nearly every student, including traits that do not surface in generated images.
中文翻译
隐性学习是指语言模型中一种令人惊讶的现象：一个模型（如偏好猫）接收到的提示可通过看似无意义的数据（如数字序列）被隐性地蒸馏到另一个模型中。本文表明，基于文本的隐性学习可以跨模态迁移，特别是通过数字序列在视觉-语言模型之间传递主题/偏好（如动物），也会改变图像的生成和表征方式。首先，我们发现使用学生模型作为文本编码器的扩散模型生成学生模型的动物的概率高于使用原始模型作为文本编码器的情况，但不会影响颜色或风格。随后我们研究了这一现象背后的机制以及哪些概念可在模态间转移。学生模型变化中的恒定成分解释了图像表征向该特征偏移的大部分原因。统一添加该成分可改变生成的动物（取决于整个提示），但不会影响颜色或风格等由单个标记编码的概念。最后，我们引入权重透镜（weight lens）作为一种方法，从单一训练模型的权重中识别隐性特征，并证明其能识别几乎所有学生的特征，包括那些在生成图像中不显现的特征。

Learning Compact State Abstractions for LLM Agents
学习大语言模型智能体的紧凑状态抽象
LLM agents commonly use the full interaction history as a proxy for state, retaining all past observations and actions regardless of whether they remain relevant. We instead ask what information an agent actually needs to carry forward. We define agent state as a compact abstraction of interaction history that preserves the competence of a fixed reference agent, with the minimal such abstraction as the ideal. Because matching the reference agent's behavior is unnecessarily restrictive and difficult for black-box policies, we define sufficiency through task performance rather than behavior matching. We represent state abstractions as deterministic recursive programs and search over them using an LLM-guided genetic algorithm. Across ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, the learned abstractions preserve near-reference task success while reducing mean policy input by up to 71% and 57%, respectively. The resulting programs are directly inspectable, providing an explicit account of what information is retained, updated, and discarded during interaction.

大语言模型智能体通常将完整的交互历史作为状态的代理，保留所有过去的观察和动作，无论它们是否仍相关。我们提出的问题是：智能体实际上需要携带哪些信息？我们将智能体状态定义为交互历史的紧凑抽象，该抽象需保持固定参考智能体的能力，最小的此类抽象即为理想状态。由于与参考智能体行为完全匹配对黑箱策略而言过于严格且困难，我们通过任务表现而非行为匹配来定义充分性。我们将状态抽象表示为确定性的递归程序，并使用LLM引导的遗传算法进行搜索。在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld两个环境中，所学抽象在几乎保持参考任务成功率的同时，分别将平均策略输入减少了71%和57%。所得程序具有可直接检查性，明确揭示了交互过程中哪些信息被保留、更新和丢弃。

SwarmLeak: Does Coordination Improve Privacy Attacks on LLM Agents?
SwarmLeak：协作是否提升对大语言模型智能体的隐私攻击效果？
LLM agents increasingly collaborate in group chats while holding tool access to confidential records, which makes the shared chat an attack surface. It remains unclear whether several colluding attackers extract such information more effectively than one. We introduce SwarmLeak, a red-teaming dataset of 24 synthetic group-chat scenarios across healthcare, law, finance, and software engineering, where attackers pose as colleagues to make a victim agent post protected fields despite an explicit confidentiality policy. With GLM 5.2 attacking five victim models through seven conversational strategies, we compare one attacker with a swarm of four sharing a private channel, matching scenarios, strategies, and a 200,000-token budget that also covers swarm coordination. Swarms show no advantage: a single attacker matches or exceeds them on every victim, and the share of runs leaking any field differs between conditions by at most 14.3 percentage points, whereas changing the victim shifts it from 0% to 99.4%. On the two most vulnerable models, nearly every run leaks under both conditions, but single attackers expose more of the record at first disclosure (85.4% vs. 68.1% of protected fields on Nemotron 3.5 Lightning). Four of the five victims leaked despite the policy. GLM 5.2 retrieved protected data in 64.3% of individual-attack runs and leaked in 0.6%: retrieval does not imply disclosure, but in high-stakes settings even one leaked field can identify a patient or client. At equal budgets, a larger red team is not automatically a stronger one; the victim model matters far more.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体越来越多地在群聊中协作并访问敏感记录，这使得共享聊天成为攻击面。目前尚不清楚多个共谋攻击者是否比单个攻击者更有效地提取此类信息。我们提出了SwarmLeak，一个包含24个合成群聊场景的红队数据集，涵盖医疗、法律、金融和软件工程领域，攻击者伪装成同事，诱使目标智能体在明确保密政策下发布受保护字段。使用GLM 5.2通过七种对话策略攻击五个目标模型，我们比较了单个攻击者与四个共享私密通道的攻击者群体的表现，条件包括相同场景、策略和20万token预算，后者还包含群体协调成本。结果显示，群体并无优势：单个攻击者在所有目标上表现相当或更优，且泄露任意字段的比例差异不超过14.3个百分点；而更换目标模型则使泄露率从0%升至99.4%。在最脆弱的两个模型上，两种条件下几乎每次运行都会发生泄露，但单个攻击者首次披露时暴露的信息更多（Nemotron 3.5 Lightning上分别为85.4% vs 68.1%的受保护字段）。五名受害者中有四名在政策存在的情况下仍泄露数据。GLM 5.2在单独攻击中检索到受保护数据的比例为64.3%，但仅0.6%发生泄露：检索不等于披露，但在高风险场景中，哪怕一字段泄露也可能识别患者或客户。在相同预算下，更大的红队并不自动更强；目标模型的影响远大于团队规模。

CATWAY: Cost-sensitive agentic three-way robot decisions with calibrated language-model confidence
CATWAY：基于校准语言模型置信度的成本敏感型三路机器人决策
A language-model robot agent must decide whether to execute, question or refuse an instruction, and an unsafe execution costs far more than an unneeded question. No prior robot gate derives all its thresholds from error costs or acts on calibrated probabilities, and none was fitted in one domain and evaluated unchanged in another. We propose CATWAY, a cost-sensitive three-way decision gate: a loss matrix over four instruction conditions (clear, ambiguous, infeasible, unsafe) fixes execute, ask and refuse regions on the probability simplex, the question being the information-gathering step. Its probabilities fuse a ten-sample vote with graded scores of ambiguity, infeasibility and risk by logistic regression. Fitted on household instructions and frozen, CATWAY was tested on text-level decisions of three small models in-domain, in two new robot domains and on the external TRUST-NAV benchmark. At eleven generations per decision against one, it was significantly cheaper than a direct prompt, a cost-aware prompt and an unguarded agent in all 18 household comparisons (mean loss per item 0.28 against 0.57-0.83), in 9 of 18 in the new domains and in 10 of 18 on TRUST-NAV (Holm per set), and never significantly costlier. It executed none of the 142 unsafe test instructions. On a unanimous vote, the minimum-expected-loss rule reduces calibration to one loss-derived threshold, so for two of six model configurations a gate on the vote alone never executed a clear instruction, costing 0.49 per item on TRUST-NAV against CATWAY's 0.13 and 0.09; the fused estimate avoided this on every set.
中文翻译
语言模型驱动的机器人智能体必须决定是否执行、质疑或拒绝指令，而错误执行的成本远高于不必要的提问。此前的机器人门控机制既未全部基于错误成本推导阈值，也未在单一领域拟合并在另一领域不变地评估。我们提出CATWAY，一种成本敏感的三路决策门控机制：在四种指令状态（清晰、模糊、不可行、危险）上的损失矩阵确定概率单纯形上的执行、询问和拒绝区域，其中询问是信息收集步骤。其概率融合了十次样本投票与逻辑回归得出的模糊性、不可行性和风险分级评分。在家庭指令上拟合并冻结后，CATWAY在三个小型模型的文本级决策中进行了测试，涵盖原域、两个新机器人领域以及外部TRUST-NAV基准。在每项决策生成11次对比一次的情况下，它在所有18个家庭比较中显著更低成本（每项平均损失0.28，对比0.57–0.83），在新领域中18项中有9项，在TRUST-NAV中18项中有10项（Holm检验），且从未显著更昂贵。它成功执行了142条危险测试指令中的零条。在全体一致投票情况下，最小期望损失规则将校准简化为一个由损失推导出的阈值，因此在六种模型配置中的两种，仅基于投票的门控从未执行清晰指令，导致TRUST-NAV上每项损失0.49，而CATWAY仅为0.13和0.09；融合估计在所有设置中避免了这一问题。
👤 Erik Derner 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

LimoBench: How Street Smart Are Large Language Models?
LimoBench：大型语言模型有多“街知巷闻”？
Large language models (LLMs) have transformed how humans interact with information, enabling natural-language interfaces across various domains. They are increasingly deployed for geospatial applications including map-based question answering, location-based services, and natural-language interfaces to spatial databases. But are LLMs truly location-aware, able to reliably interpret and precisely answer location queries involving real-world coordinates, points of interest (POIs), and geospatial distances? To answer this concretely, we present LimoBench, a street-level benchmark of 82 natural-language location queries over seven canonical spatial operations: geocoding, reverse geocoding, routing, directionality, range search, $k$-nearest-neighbor search, and spatial join. The queries are grounded in real map data of West Lafayette, Indiana, and are graded by location and semantic complexity. They are designed to probe LLMs' street-level spatial awareness, from simple lookups to multi-step spatial reasoning. We also present Limo, an approach based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) that enhances the location awareness of LLMs. Limo grounds LLMs in PostGIS-backed spatial data through a compact set of tools. We find that ungrounded models mostly achieve single-digit accuracy (3.1% for GPT-6 Luna) across different spatial operations. In contrast, the same models achieve near-perfect accuracy (99.3% for GPT-6 Luna) when given access to Limo's spatial tools through MCP. Current LLMs thus have limited and unreliable street-level spatial knowledge, while grounding provides accurate answers at modest overhead (+0.2 s latency, 7.7$\times$ tokens, and 2.2$\times$ cost for GPT-6 Luna). We release the benchmark, Limo toolset, and evaluation code to encourage the community to evaluate their own location-aware LLM approaches.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）已彻底改变人类获取信息的方式，使自然语言接口在多个领域成为可能。它们越来越多地被部署于地理空间应用，包括基于地图的问题回答、基于位置的服务以及空间数据库的自然语言接口。但LLMs是否真正具备位置感知能力，能否可靠地解释并精确回答涉及真实世界坐标、兴趣点（POIs）和地理空间距离的位置查询？为具体回答这一问题，我们提出了LimoBench，一个覆盖西拉法耶特市真实地图数据的街道级基准，包含82个自然语言位置查询，涵盖七种经典空间操作：地理编码、反向地理编码、路径规划、方向性、范围搜索、k近邻搜索和空间连接。查询根据位置和语义复杂度进行评分，旨在探测LLMs的街道级空间意识，从简单查找到多步空间推理。我们还提出了Limo，一种基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的方法，通过一组紧凑工具将LLMs与PostGIS支持的空间数据对齐，从而增强其位置感知能力。我们发现，未经接地的模型在不同空间操作中普遍仅达到个位数准确率（GPT-6 Luna为3.1%）。相比之下，当模型通过MCP获得Limo的空间工具访问权限时，准确率接近完美（GPT-6 Luna为99.3%）。当前LLMs在街道级空间知识方面存在有限且不可靠的问题，而接地能以适度开销（GPT-6 Luna增加0.2秒延迟、7.7倍token消耗、2.2倍成本）提供准确答案。我们已发布该基准、Limo工具集和评估代码，以鼓励社区评估其自身的位置感知LLM方法。
👤 Gautam Choudhary, Jack Peng, Udesh Kumarasinghe, Rachel Rafik, Yousef Hammad et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

E2SIM: Long-Horizon Simulation of Everyday Human Behavior with Evolving States
E2SIM：具有演化状态的日常人类行为长周期模拟
AI agents are increasingly expected to support users over long periods, across work, and daily life. This requires modeling not only who users are, but also how their goals, interests, activities, and interactions evolve over time. A challenge in longitudinal user simulation is maintaining consistency between these evolving states and the consequences of actions in a shared environment. We introduce E2SIM, a longitudinal user simulation framework that jointly models evolving actor states and a dynamic shared environment. E2SIM connects actor-state updates with shared-world changes through a feedback loop linking immediate actions, delayed outcomes, and actor-specific observations across work and life. This allows the consequences of earlier actions to shape later behavior, while preserving differences in what each actor knows. We further propose a suite of longitudinal trajectory diagnostics, measuring dynamic consistency, coherent event progression and collaboration, and non-degenerate trajectory development rather than static consistency alone. In one 60-day simulation, E2SIM produced more extracted event threads judged meaningful by our evaluator and fewer near-duplicate actions than four direct-generation baselines. The framework and evaluation methods provide a basis for studying longitudinal user simulation, with potential applications in training and evaluating AI agents that assist users over extended periods.
中文翻译
AI智能体正被期望在长时间跨度内支持用户，贯穿工作与日常生活。这要求不仅建模用户是谁，还需建模其目标、兴趣、活动和互动如何随时间演变。长期用户模拟的一个挑战在于保持这些演化状态与共享环境中行动后果之间的一致性。我们提出E2SIM，一种联合建模演化主体状态与动态共享环境的长期用户模拟框架。E2SIM通过反馈回路将主体状态更新与共享世界变化相连接，该回路关联即时行动、延迟结果以及跨工作与生活的情境观察。这使得早期行动的后果能够塑造后续行为，同时保留各主体所知信息的差异。我们进一步提出一系列长期轨迹诊断工具，衡量动态一致性、事件进展连贯性与协作性，以及非退化的轨迹发展，而非仅静态一致性。在一个60天的模拟中，E2SIM产生的被评估者认为有意义的事件线程数量多于四个直接生成基线，且近似重复动作更少。该框架与评估方法为研究长期用户模拟提供了基础，潜在应用于训练和评估长期协助用户的AI智能体。
👤 Ziwei Gong, Xiangyang Yin, Thai Quoc On, Lin Ai, Julia Hirschberg et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

GoldiBench: Right Tools, Wrong Amount -- Benchmarking Agents on Effect Judgement in Professional Editing
GoldiBench：工具正确，数量错误——在专业编辑中对智能体的效果判断进行基准测试
Real editing requests describe a look, not the steps to reach it, so an agent that serves them must judge how much of each tool the look takes. Existing benchmarks do not measure this: their tasks either state the tools and values, or leave the amount open and are scored by VLM judges that pass more than half of half- and triple-strength results. We introduce GoldiBench, a benchmark of 500 editing tasks in five professional applications (darktable, RawTherapee, GIMP, Inkscape, Blender), solvable by writing a program or by operating the GUI. Each task pairs a START image with a TARGET rendered from a hidden program, so the amount is fixed by a known target; the agent's edit is re-rendered and compared with TARGET, and the same task is posed with the tools and values, the tools only, or the goal only, separating operation from effect judgement. Across 25 open, GUI and closed models, agents operate well (99-100% with programs from 4B up when told the values) but fall off a cliff when they must judge the amount: given only the tools, the best model passes 19% of the tasks with programs and 33% through the GUI, and scale does not close the gap. Manuals barely help; the measured TARGET-START difference more than doubles the pass rate, but the same difference shown as an image does not for open models. Allowed four renders, agents almost always stop after the first; forced to compare, the strong models double their pass rate. Agents know the tools, but neither read nor check the amount.
中文翻译
真实的编辑请求描述的是最终效果，而非达成该效果的具体步骤，因此能够满足此类请求的智能体必须判断每种工具使用量的多少。现有基准测试无法衡量这一点：它们的任务要么明确给出工具和数值，要么将使用量留空，并由视觉语言模型（VLM）裁判评分，而这些裁判对半强度和三倍强度的结果通过率超过一半。我们提出了GoldiBench，一个包含500个编辑任务的基准测试，涵盖五个专业应用（darktable、RawTherapee、GIMP、Inkscape、Blender），可通过编写程序或操作GUI完成。每个任务都配有一张起始图像和一张由隐藏程序生成的目标图像，使得使用量由已知目标固定；智能体的编辑操作会被重新渲染并与目标图像对比。同一任务以三种形式提出：提供工具和数值、仅提供工具、仅提供目标，从而分离操作与效果判断。在25个开放模型、GUI模型和闭源模型中，当被告知数值时，智能体使用程序可达到99-100%的准确率，但一旦需要判断使用量，表现急剧下降：仅提供工具时，最佳模型在程序路径下仅通过19%的任务，在GUI路径下通过33%，且模型规模扩大也无法缩小差距。手册帮助甚微；测量目标与起始图像之间的差异可使通过率翻倍，但若以图像形式展示相同差异，则对开放模型无显著提升。允许四次重渲染时，智能体几乎总在第一次后停止；强制进行比较后，强模型的通过率翻倍。智能体知道工具，但既不读取也不验证使用量。
👤 Wang Yang, Xinpeng Li, Yanyan Zhang, Chaoda Song, Shouren Wang et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31582]

LatentLens: Rethinking Latent Interface in Multi-Agent Systems
LatentLens：重新思考多智能体系统中的潜在接口
Latent interfaces let agents in a multi-agent system communicate through continuous representations rather than decoded text. Prior work reports performance and efficiency gains, but neither separates latent communication from the agent harness nor examines its internal components, leaving its actual contribution unclear. We propose LatentLens, a framework that decouples the latent interface at two levels: externally from the agent harness, to measure its contribution to overall performance (RQ1), and internally into realignment compatibility, information accessibility, and propagation dynamics, to identify the benefits and limitations of each (RQ2). Across seven benchmarks with Qwen3 models, adding the latent interface to the same harness does not improve and can even substantially degrade accuracy. Internally, latent representations retain some domain information, but incompatible realignment can substantially degrade performance, and repeated latent steps can amplify representation-level failures. Thus, while latent representations may preserve information, they do not necessarily ensure effective communication, which also requires compatible realignment, information accessibility, and stable propagation.
中文翻译
潜在接口使多智能体系统中的智能体通过连续表示而非解码后的文本进行通信。先前研究报道了性能和效率的提升，但未将潜在通信与智能体框架分离，也未深入分析其内部组件，导致其实际贡献不明确。我们提出了LatentLens框架，从两个层面解耦潜在接口：外部上与智能体框架分离，以评估其对整体性能的贡献（RQ1）；内部上分解为对齐兼容性、信息可访问性和传播动态，以识别各部分的优势与局限（RQ2）。在七个基于Qwen3模型的基准测试中，将潜在接口加入相同框架并未提升性能，甚至可能显著降低准确率。内部分析显示，潜在表示保留部分领域信息，但不兼容的对齐可能导致性能严重下降，重复的潜在步骤会放大表示层面的失败。因此，尽管潜在表示可能保留信息，但并不保证有效通信，后者还需具备兼容的对齐、信息可访问性和稳定的传播机制。
👤 Ziqing Yang, Kewei Cheng, Mingjie Li, Yang Zhang 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

SwiftLoop: Selective Refinement for Adaptive Implicit Reasoning in Looped Transformers
SwiftLoop：用于循环Transformer中自适应隐式推理的选择性精炼
Looped Transformers advance implicit chain-of-thought reasoning through recurrent latent refinement. However, existing looped reasoners typically employ fixed-depth recurrence, updating all latent positions at every iteration and incurring redundancy when further refinement is unnecessary. We introduce \textsc{SwiftLoop}, an inference-time framework for selective recurrent refinement through position-wise freezing, enabling adaptive computation allocation across both recurrent depth and active width. SwiftLoop learns lightweight freezing policies through hindsight-guided supervision derived from two complementary criteria: K/V reusability at the internal representation level and functional impact at the output level. At inference time, these policies guide adaptive freezing based on observed hidden states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SwiftLoop achieves favorable accuracy--computation trade-offs over fixed-loop inference and adaptive baselines, with measured inference acceleration. Beyond implicit reasoning, we further demonstrate its applicability to recurrent prompt prefilling in general looped models.
中文翻译
循环Transformer通过递归潜在精炼推进隐式思维链推理。然而，现有的循环推理器通常采用固定深度的递归，在每次迭代中更新所有潜在位置，当进一步精炼不再必要时会造成冗余。我们提出了 extsc{SwiftLoop}，一种推理时的选择性递归精炼框架，通过位置级冻结实现计算资源的自适应分配，覆盖递归深度和活跃宽度。SwiftLoop通过来自两个互补标准的回溯引导监督学习轻量级冻结策略：内部表示层面的K/V可复用性与输出层面的功能影响。在推理时，这些策略根据观测到的隐藏状态指导自适应冻结。大量实验表明，SwiftLoop在固定循环推理和自适应基线之间实现了更优的准确率-计算权衡，并实现了可测量的推理加速。除隐式推理外，我们还展示了其在一般循环模型中的递归提示预填充中的适用性。
👤 Tianyi Zhao, Ke Wan, Chen Chen 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31429]

Leaks by Installments: Cross-Session Answer Disclosure in LLM Tutors and a Ledger-Based Defense
分批泄露：LLM导师中的跨会话答案披露及基于账本的防御机制
Tutors built on large language models (LLMs) are told not to give away answers, but a learner does not have to ask for the answer all at once. Existing safeguards judge each reply within the current conversation, so a learner can open a new session, reword the problem, or request a worked solution to the same problem with different numbers. We call this cross-session disclosure and propose a defense: a persistent, per-learner disclosure ledger. For every problem a learner brings, the system builds a typed solution graph. Before a reply is released, a gate matches the reply's own content, not the learner's wording, against the ledger by result values and by type-labeled solution structure, then computes which steps the learner could now derive. The reply is withheld if that set reaches the final answer or exceeds a budget. In a simulation of 3,000 problems, the ledger reduced leakage through renumbered practice variants from 100% to 10.6% while honest learners completed 94-99.5% of problems. With two real LLM tutors (Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Phi-3.5-mini-instruct) on 60 problems, leakage through renumbered variants fell from 0.58 to 0.08 and from 1.00 to 0.18, with 98% honest completion. The ledger did not reduce split requests beyond a per-session gate in the live runs. All acceptance criteria were fixed before each run.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLM）构建的导师被要求不透露答案，但学习者无需一次性询问完整答案。现有防护机制仅在当前对话内判断每条回复，因此学习者可开启新会话、改写问题，或请求同一问题的不同数字版本的解答过程。我们称此为跨会话披露，并提出一种防御方案：基于账本的持久化、按学习者区分的答案披露记录。每当学习者提交一个问题，系统即构建一个带类型的解决方案图。在回复释放前，门控机制将回复内容本身（而非学习者的表述）与账本中的结果值和类型标记的解决方案结构进行匹配，然后计算学习者现在能推导出哪些步骤。若该集合达到最终答案或超出预算，则拒绝回复。在3,000个问题的模拟中，账本将重编号练习变体的泄露率从100%降至10.6%，同时诚实学习者完成率达94%-99.5%。在两个真实LLM导师（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Phi-3.5-mini-instruct）对60个问题的实测中，重编号变体的泄露率分别从0.58降至0.08、从1.00降至0.18，且诚实完成率保持在98%。在实际运行中，账本并未比单会话门控进一步减少拆分请求。所有接受标准均在每次运行前固定。
👤 Venkata Muttineni, Karthik Narayan, Christopher David 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

[2611.33148]
agentic-rl
CoT-Proxy: Cross-Format Transfer for Distilling Long-Horizon Policies
CoT-Proxy：跨格式迁移以提炼长时域策略
Reasoning models become long-horizon agents through reinforcement learning or, more cheaply, through fine-tuning on a stronger teacher's trajectories, which contain the teacher's chain-of-thought as well as its actions. The standard fine-tuning approach imitates both, yet the strongest teachers are closed models whose chain-of-thought is hidden behind an API. We show that the chain-of-thought can be left out altogether and that the long-horizon policy can be distilled from the teacher's actions alone. The student is trained on these trajectories with its reasoning block left empty, while a KL loss on a replay buffer of its own generations limits drift from its initial model. Surprisingly, the policy learned from these trajectories with an empty reasoning block carries over to inference with thinking enabled, where the student reasons in its own style while acting like the teacher, an effect we call cross-format transfer. Distilling the actions of Qwen3.6-27B raises the task success of Qwen3-4B on AppWorld from 7% to 18% and that of Qwen3-32B from 12% to 25%. We further introduce CoT-proxies, short summaries of the teacher's reasoning placed after the empty reasoning block, which raise Qwen3-4B further, to 35%. Since neither actions nor CoT-proxies need the hidden chain-of-thought, the recipe applies unchanged to closed teachers. Distilling GPT-5.6 Luna from its visible outputs and the reasoning summaries its API returns raises Qwen3-4B from 7% to 48% on AppWorld and from 3% to 28% on SWE-bench Verified.
中文翻译
推理模型通过强化学习，或更经济地通过在更强教师模型的轨迹上微调，成为长时域智能体，这些轨迹包含教师的思维链和动作。标准微调方法同时模仿两者，但最强的教师模型是封闭模型，其思维链隐藏在API之后。我们发现思维链完全可以省略，仅从教师的动作中即可提炼出长时域策略。学生模型在这些轨迹上训练时，其推理模块保持为空，同时通过对其自身生成结果的重放缓冲区施加KL损失，限制与初始模型的偏差。令人惊讶的是，即使推理模块为空，该策略在启用思考的推理中仍能有效迁移，学生以自己的风格进行推理，却像教师一样行动，这种现象称为跨格式迁移。从Qwen3.6-27B的动作中提炼策略，使Qwen3-4B在AppWorld任务成功率从7%提升至18%，Qwen3-32B从12%提升至25%。我们进一步引入CoT-proxy——在空推理模块后放置教师推理的简短摘要，使Qwen3-4B性能进一步提升至35%。由于动作和CoT-proxy均无需隐藏的思维链，该方法可直接应用于封闭教师模型。从GPT-5.6 Luna的可见输出及其API返回的推理摘要中提炼，使Qwen3-4B在AppWorld上的成功率从7%提升至48%，在SWE-bench Verified上从3%提升至28%。
👤 Dawid J. Kopiczko, Tijmen Blankevoort, Yuki M. Asano 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

AIChipBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Automated AI Accelerator Design
AIChipBench：面向自动化AI加速器设计的LLM智能体基准测试
A language-model decoder carries state between decoding steps, and in hardware a wrong state update can leave the current token correct and corrupt the output several tokens later, so a design can pass output tests and still be wrong. AIChipBench asks what LLM coding agents need to build such hardware correctly. Agents implement quantized Qwen3.5, Gemma 3, Gemma 4, and Llama 3.2 decoders in synthesizable register-transfer-level (RTL) code against an executable integer reference that specifies the outputs and the stored state at every decoding step. They debug against public tests under fixed budgets; their frozen designs are graded on held-out token sequences, resets, memory stalls, and synthesis, and a second track asks them to speed up a correct design at nearly fixed area. Across 9,136 attempts by four coding agents, the difficulty lies in stored state. Feedback that points to the first state mismatch raises acceptance over output-only feedback in all 12 agent--task cells of a matched study, by 25.0 to 46.9 percentage points, and halves the agent-hours per accepted design. Regraded on new sequences and conditions, 45 of 69 accepted designs fail within the 32 steps their tests required. More than a third of attempts to speed up a correct design end without one. An overall score ranks Sol 5.6 and Fable 5.1 above Astra 6 and Opus 5.5, and both halves of the instances give this order in all 200 random splits. Hardware agents therefore need debugging feedback that exposes stored state, and their benchmarks need final tests well beyond the development cases.
中文翻译
语言模型解码器在解码步骤之间维持状态，而在硬件中，错误的状态更新可能导致当前标记正确，但数个标记后输出被破坏，因此设计可能通过输出测试但仍错误。AIChipBench探讨LLM编码智能体需要具备哪些能力才能正确构建此类硬件。智能体需在可综合的寄存器传输级（RTL）代码中实现量化版Qwen3.5、Gemma 3、Gemma 4和Llama 3.2解码器，并与一个可执行的整数参考模型对齐，该模型指定了每个解码步骤的输出和存储状态。智能体在固定预算下针对公开测试进行调试；其冻结的设计在保留的标记序列、重置、内存停顿和综合测试中评分，第二赛道要求它们在几乎不变面积的情况下加速正确设计。在四个编码智能体共9,136次尝试中，难点在于存储状态。指向首次状态不匹配的反馈在12个智能体-任务组合中，使接受率比仅输出反馈高出25.0至46.9个百分点，并使每接受一个设计所需代理小时数减半。重新评估新序列和条件后，69个接受设计中有45个在32步内失败。超过三分之一的加速尝试未能成功。总体评分显示Sol 5.6和Fable 5.1优于Astra 6和Opus 5.5，且在全部200次随机划分中两部分均保持此顺序。因此，硬件智能体需要暴露存储状态的调试反馈，其基准测试也需包含远超开发案例的最终测试。
👤 Guangyuan Wang, Chengran Li, Minchen Luo, Jichen Feng, Mengdi Wang et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

SALSA: Sampling Adversarial Suffixes from Learned Distributions for LLM Safety Assessment
SALSA：从学习到的分布中采样对抗后缀以评估大语言模型安全性
Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, where adversaries append optimized suffixes to otherwise benign looking prompts to bypass safety alignment. Existing optimization-based GCG-style attacks can be effective, but they are expensive at inference time, and suffer from poor transfer. We present, SALSA, a generative framework that learns a distribution over jailbreak suffixes from optimization-derived attack corpora. Once trained, the model supports efficient sampling-based attacks. On open-source targets, it achieves attack success rates comparable to state-of-the-art optimization baselines, while reducing generation time from 3.46s to 0.04s per prompt. In black-box transfer, it remains highly effective against proprietary systems, including 99% ASR on GPT-3.5-Turbo, and reaches 45% ASR on the substantially harder GPT-4o, more than doubling the strongest baseline at an equal sampling budget. We further offer an intuition for why generative sampling can preserve high attack success, when the learned suffix distribution is close to the distribution of optimization outputs. Overall, our results show that learning adversarial suffix distributions yields a scalable, and practically efficient foundation for continuous LLM vulnerability discovery.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）仍容易受到越狱攻击，即攻击者在看似无害的提示后附加优化后的后缀以绕过安全对齐。现有的基于优化的GCG风格攻击虽有效，但在推理时成本高昂且泛化能力差。我们提出SALSA，一种生成式框架，从优化生成的攻击语料库中学习越狱后缀的分布。训练完成后，该模型支持高效的基于采样的攻击。在开源目标上，其攻击成功率与最先进的优化基线相当，但每条提示的生成时间从3.46秒降至0.04秒。在黑盒迁移测试中，它对专有系统依然高度有效，包括在GPT-3.5-Turbo上达到99%的攻击成功率，并在更具挑战性的GPT-4o上达到45%的攻击成功率，超过最强基线超过一倍，在相同采样预算下表现更优。我们进一步提供了直观解释：当学习到的后缀分布接近优化输出分布时，生成采样为何能保持高攻击成功率。总体而言，我们的结果表明，学习对抗后缀分布可为持续发现LLM漏洞提供可扩展且实际高效的基础设施。
👤 Shivank Rajput, Himanshu Singh, A. V. Subramanyam 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Leto: Utility-Centric Agent Memory
Leto：以效用为中心的智能体记忆
Large language model (LLM) agents rely on external memory to retain and reuse information across interactions. Existing memory systems treat memory as a record of the past and evaluate it by fidelity to the conversation. However, the value of memory lies in whether it improves the agent's response. A memory system decides what to memorize and what to serve before that response exists, and fidelity checks neither decision against it. Faithful memory can therefore fail to improve the response. We instead view memory as a prediction problem and present \textbf{\emph{\leto{}}}, a memory system for LLM agents that focuses on the utility of memory to the future rather than on its fidelity to the past. Because future requests are unknown, \emph{\leto{}} preserves access to conversation details through memory indexing, allowing information to remain available through its sources. When a request arrives, memory replay draws on this index to anticipate and supply the context the agent needs beyond what the request explicitly states. The utility of the served context remains uncertain until the response is generated, motivating a utility loop that uses response outcomes to refine future memory selection. We evaluate our system against six memory systems on five benchmarks across three scenarios. In personalization, it outperforms the strongest compared memory system by 16.3 percentage points in accuracy on PrefEval and by 3.1 points in task completion on PERMA. For long-horizon tasks, it achieves the best overall performance on MemoryArena averaged over five environments with a gain that increases with the chain depth. In long-context understanding, it is on par with retrieval over the raw history while serving fewer context tokens.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体依赖外部记忆来保留并复用跨交互的信息。现有记忆系统将记忆视为过去的记录，并通过对话保真度来评估其质量。然而，记忆的价值在于是否能提升智能体的响应质量。记忆系统需在响应生成前决定存储和提供哪些内容，而保真度检查无法验证这一决策的有效性。因此，忠实的记忆可能仍无法改善响应。我们转而将记忆视为一个预测问题，提出 extbf{ extit{Leto}}，一种面向未来效用而非过去保真度的LLM智能体记忆系统。由于未来请求未知， extit{Leto}通过记忆索引保留对话细节的访问权限，使信息可通过其来源持续可用。当请求到达时，记忆重放利用该索引来预判并提供超出请求显式内容所需的上下文。所服务上下文的效用直到响应生成后才可确定，这促使我们引入一个效用循环，利用响应结果来优化未来的记忆选择。我们在三个场景下的五个基准上对比了六种记忆系统。在个性化任务中，其在PrefEval上的准确率比最强对比系统高出16.3个百分点，在PERMA上的任务完成率高出3.1个百分点。对于长周期任务，它在MemoryArena上五种环境的平均表现最佳，且随着链深度增加优势更明显。在长上下文理解方面，其性能与直接检索原始历史相当，但提供的上下文token更少。
👤 Shuguang Chen, Salman Paracha 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

From Grammar Deltas to Token Masks: Incremental Constrained Decoding under Runtime Grammar Evolution
从语法差异到标记掩码：运行时语法演进下的增量约束解码
Grammar-constrained decoding enables large language models to generate outputs that satisfy formal constraints, but in code and domain-specific language generation, the relevant constraints are not always fully available before decoding and may emerge progressively from new declarations, inferred type information, or runtime analysis feedback. Existing structured-decoding engines efficiently handle fixed, predeclared, or previously seen constraint structures; however, when a first-seen grammar update changes the parser state induced by an already committed prefix, the straightforward solution reconstructs the constraint state under the updated grammar, repeating parsing work already performed over the prefix. We introduce GramDelta, an incremental framework for constrained decoding with append-only runtime grammar updates. GramDelta maintains parse state over the committed prefix and propagates only changes induced by newly added grammar rules through historical dependencies, avoiding prefix replay. It then maps the updated parse state to exact next-token masks via compilation-free factorized projection over shared historical charts, avoiding per-update mask-cache recompilation. GramDelta therefore preserves the result of full reconstruction while making update cost sensitive to the actual impact of a grammar change. Experiments across four model configurations show that GramDelta adds negligible overhead without updates; under sustained runtime grammar evolution, it reduces median first-seen grammar-update latency by 10.7--11.5x and reduces the additional TPOT overhead over unconstrained decoding by 53.1--81.9% relative to the strongest reconstruction baseline. These results enable efficient constrained decoding when constraints progressively emerge and evolve with the generated prefix.
中文翻译
语法约束解码使大型语言模型能够生成满足形式约束的输出，但在代码和领域特定语言生成中，相关约束往往并非在解码前完全可用，可能随新声明、推断类型信息或运行时分析反馈逐步浮现。现有结构化解码引擎能高效处理固定、预先声明或已知的约束结构；然而，当首次遇到的语法更新改变了已有前缀引发的解析器状态时，直接解决方案需在更新后的语法下重建约束状态，重复执行已对前缀完成的解析工作。我们提出GramDelta，一种支持追加式运行时语法更新的增量约束解码框架。GramDelta维护已提交前缀的解析状态，并仅通过历史依赖关系传播新增语法规则带来的变化，避免前缀重播。随后，它通过共享历史图表的无编译因子投影，将更新后的解析状态映射为精确的下一个词标记掩码，避免每次更新后重新编译掩码缓存。因此，GramDelta在保持全重建结果的同时，使更新成本敏感于语法变更的实际影响。在四种模型配置上的实验表明，无更新时GramDelta开销可忽略；在持续的运行时语法演化下，其首次出现语法更新的延迟中位数降低10.7至11.5倍，相较于最强重建基线，额外TPOT开销减少53.1%至81.9%。这些结果使得在约束逐步浮现并随生成前缀演进时实现高效约束解码成为可能。
👤 Hantao Hua, Jiming Su, Yonggang Che, Feng Zhu 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32710]

Red-Teaming a Sovereign LLM, Its Reasoning and Its Training Stages
对主权大语言模型及其推理与训练阶段的红队测试
Open-weight large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for applications with sensitive information. We investigate whether confidentiality instructions in system prompt can protect such information. We red-team Apertus v1.5, an open sovereign LLM, and five other model families across six languages and reasoning modes. We use synthetic personal records to evaluate direct disclosure, correction baits, yes/no verification, derived-value queries, reasoning leakage and authorisation checks. We also explore copyright attribution, historical misrepresentation and stereotyping. Despite confidentiality instructions, Apertus 8B and 70B leak information in 96% and 95% of requests, respectively, in correction-bait attacks. Verification and derived-value queries also expose protected information without necessarily revealing identifiers. Moreover, reasoning traces can leak confidential records even when final answers withhold them. Our experiments also show that post-training does not consistently improve safety. Our findings demonstrate that system prompts alone cannot ensure confidentiality and highlight the need for multilingual reasoning-aware safety evaluations.
中文翻译
开放权重的大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被应用于涉及敏感信息的场景。我们探究系统提示中的保密指令能否有效保护此类信息。我们对Apertus v1.5——一种开放主权LLM——及其他五个模型家族在六种语言和多种推理模式下进行了红队测试。我们使用合成个人记录评估直接泄露、纠正诱饵、是/否验证、衍生值查询、推理泄露和授权检查等问题。同时探索版权归属、历史误述和刻板印象等议题。尽管存在保密指令，Apertus 8B和70B在纠正诱饵攻击中分别有96%和95%的请求发生信息泄露。验证和衍生值查询也暴露了受保护信息，即使未明确揭示标识符。此外，即使最终答案未透露，推理轨迹仍可能泄露机密记录。实验还表明，后训练阶段并不总是能提升安全性。我们的发现表明，仅靠系统提示无法确保保密性，强调需要具备多语言推理意识的安全评估。

Social Bias Neurons in Multilingual Language Models: A Mechanistic Interpretability Analysis
多语言语言模型中的社会偏见神经元：一种机制可解释性分析
Multilingual models exhibit social bias when processing inputs pertaining to societal stereotypes. We adapt emerging mechanistic interpretability methods to pinpoint and characterize model components that encode this bias. We conduct our analysis across three instruction-tuned models, four languages, and seven bias dimensions. Specifically, we leverage multilingual bias benchmarks in implementing two methods for identifying social bias neurons: activation probability entropy and integrated gap gradients. We find the latter method to be more effective, as the neurons it isolates (which are largely concentrated at the model's deepest layers) lead to sharper decreases in model bias when suppressed. Further analysis reveals neuron distribution patterns differ between high- and low-resourced languages, suggesting that a language's representation in pretraining data influences how its biases are represented within model internals. Our results also indicate that neurons linked to gender, nationality, and social class biases overlap the most with neurons related to other bias dimensions, pointing to the pivotal role these three dimensions play in how models learn and handle bias.
中文翻译

👤 Lance Calvin Lim Gamboa, Yue Feng, Mark Lee 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32660]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Bathys: Advancing Deep Search Agents
Bathys：推进深度搜索智能体
Deep search is a critical capability for agents tackling complex, open-ended tasks, where solving a problem requires iterative information gathering, query reformulation, and evidence-based verification rather than a single retrieval step. To address these requirements, we present Bathys 9B and 27B deep search agents built around a training framework that spans verifiable question synthesis, post-training, and online reinforcement learning (RL) under context compression. Our high-quality data construction ensures both search depth and reliability: each clue is reformulated as an indirect reference requiring independent retrieval, and retained evidence chains enable verification that the clues jointly determine a unique answer. Curated trajectories underpin a pipeline of supervised fine-tuning (SFT), Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (KTO), and Compaction-Managed Group Sequence Policy Optimization (CM-GSPO): SFT provides a stable foundation for tool use over long horizons and query reformulation; KTO uses binary feedback on trajectories to improve the selection of answers grounded in evidence; CM-GSPO optimizes execution sub-trajectories with length-normalized likelihood ratios on the recorded rollout contexts and reweights sub-trajectory advantages to correct the extra weighting introduced by independently averaging sub-trajectory losses. Bathys 9B and 27B attain 66.51% and 80.49% on BrowseComp, respectively, under an evaluation protocol with trajectory self-verification and conditional retries.
中文翻译
深度搜索是智能体应对复杂、开放性任务的关键能力，解决此类问题需要迭代式信息获取、查询重构和基于证据的验证，而非单一检索步骤。为满足这些需求，我们提出了基于可验证问题生成、后训练及上下文压缩下的在线强化学习（RL）训练框架的Bathys 9B和27B深度搜索智能体。高质量的数据构建确保了搜索深度与可靠性：每个线索均被重构为需独立检索的间接引用，保留的证据链支持对线索共同决定唯一答案的验证。精心设计的轨迹支撑监督微调（SFT）、卡尼曼-特沃斯基优化（KTO）以及压缩管理组序列策略优化（CM-GSPO）的流水线：SFT为长期工具使用和查询重构提供稳定基础；KTO利用轨迹上的二元反馈改进基于证据的答案选择；CM-GSPO通过记录回放上下文中的长度归一化似然比优化执行子轨迹，并重新加权子轨迹优势以纠正因独立平均子轨迹损失带来的额外权重偏差。在包含轨迹自验证和条件重试的评估协议下，Bathys 9B和27B分别达到BrowseComp上的66.51%和80.49%准确率。
👤 Zixuan Chen, Shengyou Yuan, Nang Yuan, Ming Ma, Zelin Xiao et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Evaluating Latent Knowledge Stability in Multimodal Large Language Models
多模态大语言模型中潜在知识稳定性的评估
Changes in image viewpoint and question formulation can alter latent knowledge representations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, output correctness and overall representational drift provide limited insight into how these changes affect model decisions. We therefore evaluate latent knowledge stability across visual and linguistic elicitation conditions from two complementary perspectives: answer preference and internal support. Specifically, we devise the margin retention score (MRS), which measures the first-order projected degradation of the initial answer's logit margin over competing options across conditions and assesses how changes in latent knowledge representations affect this margin. We also present latent knowledge support-structure stability (KSS), which evaluates reference-correct items by comparing internal support profiles constructed using the margin gradient under the reference condition, which jointly reflects changes in how internal support is distributed and changes in total support magnitude. We further construct MMStableBench, comprising 19,452 knowledge items (facts), each probing the same latent knowledge across varied image viewpoints, semantically equivalent question formulations, and answer-option permutations. Experiments across 15 MLLMs show that MRS and KSS reveal latent knowledge instability not captured by accuracy, while an MRS-inspired regularizer further improves the generalization of edited latent knowledge.
中文翻译
图像视角和问题表述的变化可能改变多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的潜在知识表征。然而，输出正确性和整体表征漂移仅提供了有限的洞察，难以揭示这些变化如何影响模型决策。因此，我们从两个互补的角度评估了在视觉和语言诱发条件下的潜在知识稳定性：答案偏好与内部支持。具体而言，我们提出了边际保留分数（MRS），用于衡量在不同条件下初始答案logit边际相对于竞争选项的一阶投影退化，并评估潜在知识表征变化对这一边际的影响。我们还提出了潜在知识支持结构稳定性（KSS），通过比较在参考条件下使用边际梯度构建的内部支持轮廓来评估参考正确的项目，该指标共同反映了内部支持分布变化和总支持量变化。我们进一步构建了MMStableBench，包含19,452个知识项（事实），每个知识项在不同的图像视角、语义等价的问题表述和答案选项排列下探测相同的潜在知识。在15个MLLM上的实验表明，MRS和KSS揭示了准确率无法捕捉的潜在知识不稳定性，而受MRS启发的正则化器进一步提升了编辑后潜在知识的泛化能力。
👤 Chuancheng Shi, Shangze Li, Wenhua Wu, Zifeng Cheng, Pengyang Shao et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Avalon-M1: Reinforcement Learning from Collective Rewards
Avalon-M1：基于集体奖励的强化学习
We introduce reinforcement learning from collective rewards (RLCR), a recipe for jointly training language models to act as agent organisations under latency constraints. A CEO and up to eight specialists share one policy, co-ordinate through a message board and workspace, and are trained with a common outcome-derived advantage. The deadline makes parallel work valuable when it increases the return earned before the cap. We train Avalon-M1-Mini (120B) across 147 environments. For the trained model, eight-hire organisational execution raises IMO-ProofBench-Advanced scores from 23.8% to 39.0% relative to single-agent execution. Across 12 benchmarks, organisations achieve similar average scores at approximately one-third the derived latency of token-matched single-agent execution and just over half that of eight independent attempts followed by selection. The trained model also outperforms Nemotron 3 Super, post-trained from the same base model, across all evaluations when comparing each model's best tested configuration. On EdgeBench, organisations overtake the single agent after approximately 0.4 derived hours. Over the course of training, organisations develop hierarchies to manage communication costs, invest more in peer review, and alter how work is divided. Safety evaluations find near-universal refusal of harmful actions but sustained declines in external reporting in two of three oversight scenarios. These results establish a shared-advantage baseline for studying organisational learning, performance-latency trade-offs, and collective oversight.
中文翻译

BCP: A Lightweight Boundary-Controlled Framework for Online Sign Language Recognition and Translation
BCP：一种轻量级边界控制框架用于在线手语识别与翻译
Online sign language recognition (SLR) and translation (SLT) incrementally produce gloss sequences or natural language text from sign video as it arrives, a capability essential for real-time communication and live captioning. However, powerful pretrained offline models cannot be directly deployed in this setting, while existing online methods typically retrain the entire model on simulated streaming prefixes constructed from offline videos, incurring high costs. To address this challenge, we propose Boundary-Controlled Prefix Commit (BCP), a unified lightweight framework that converts pretrained offline methods into online counterparts for sign-to-gloss (S2G), sign-to-text (S2T), and sign-to-gloss-to-text (S2G2T). Specifically, BCP freezes the pretrained model and introduces a lightweight boundary adapter that decides when to commit, supervised by Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) alignments from a pretrained SLR model, without any human annotation. At inference, a Boundary-Controlled Wait-K Output Policy then decides what to commit via prefix voting, yielding adaptive burst commits while preserving the output progress of the base Wait-K schedule. Experiments on PHOENIX-2014T and CSL-Daily across four offline methods show that BCP, with less than 0.1% additional trainable parameters per offline method, achieves a strong quality-latency trade-off on S2T and remains competitive on S2G and S2G2T.
中文翻译
在线手语识别（SLR）与翻译（SLT）能够随着手语视频的实时到达逐步生成词素序列或自然语言文本，这是实现实时交流与现场字幕的关键能力。然而，强大的预训练离线模型无法直接部署于此场景；而现有在线方法通常需在由离线视频构造的模拟流式前缀上重新训练整个模型，带来高昂成本。为应对这一挑战，我们提出边界控制前缀提交（BCP）框架，一种统一的轻量级方案，可将预训练的离线方法转化为适用于手语到词素（S2G）、手语到文本（S2T）以及手语到词素再到文本（S2G2T）的在线版本。具体而言，BCP冻结预训练模型，引入一个轻量级边界适配器，通过预训练SLR模型的连接时序分类（CTC）对齐进行监督，无需人工标注。推理阶段，边界控制等待-K输出策略通过前缀投票决定提交内容，实现自适应突发提交，同时保持基础等待-K调度的输出进度。在PHOENIX-2014T和CSL-Daily数据集上，针对四种离线方法的实验表明，BCP仅增加每种离线方法不到0.1%的可训练参数，即可在S2T任务上实现优异的质量-延迟权衡，并在S2G和S2G2T任务上保持竞争力。

Phoneme-identity depth profiles do not reliably predict LoRA placement for dysarthric ASR
音素身份深度剖面无法可靠预测失语症语音识别中的LoRA放置位置
Parameter-efficient adaptation of a speech foundation model to dysarthric speech requires a rarely justified choice: which encoder layers to adapt. A recently proposed strategy is to probe the encoder, find where phonetic information becomes most decodable, and put the adapters there. We test that inference across two ASR models (Qwen3-ASR, Whisper-small) and two dysarthric corpora (Mandarin CDSD, English UASpeech), holding recipe and seed discipline constant. The inference fails. Measured under one pipeline rather than inherited from published figures, all four phoneme-error curves minimise between 83% and 92% of relative depth, each model's minimising layer moving no further between corpora than between probe seeds. The best adaptation band is not similarly constant: none of three natural mappings from the curve to a band -- steepest improvement, lowest band-mean error, or knee location -- recovers the best tested configuration. We do not claim no statistic of the curve could work, only that these three do not. Placement still matters, but is model- and corpus-specific. On CDSD the models disagree -- Qwen3-ASR prefers its upper third (29.88% CER, three-seed mean), Whisper-small all-block LoRA (53.56%) -- while on UASpeech neither favours the upper band. At three seeds, upper-versus-all is resolved (-1.31 pp, CI [-2.16, -0.25]) but upper-versus-middle is not (-0.83 pp, CI [-2.06, +1.86]). The two models' preferences differ as a directly tested interaction (-4.71 pp, CI [-6.17, -2.89]); we designate this the primary claim, and record that the designation was made post hoc. On Qwen3-ASR x UASpeech, adapting near the input is reliably worse than not adapting at all (+9.90 pp CER). We recommend choosing the band by a short adaptation sweep on the model and corpus in hand, not by reading it off a probe.
中文翻译
将语音基础模型高效适配至失语症语音需要一个很少被合理论证的选择：应适配编码器的哪些层。最近提出的一种策略是探测编码器，找出语音信息最易解码的位置，并将适配器置于该处。我们在两个ASR模型（Qwen3-ASR、Whisper-small）和两个失语症语料库（普通话CDSD、英语UASpeech）上测试该推断，保持配方和种子一致性。结果失败：在单一流程中测量而非沿用发表图表，四条音素错误曲线均在相对深度83%至92%之间取得最小值，各模型的最小化层在不同语料库间移动幅度不超过在不同探测种子间的差异。最佳适配区间并不恒定：三种从曲线映射到区间的自然方法——最陡峭改进、最低带均值误差、拐点位置——均未能恢复最佳测试配置。我们并非声称曲线的任何统计量都无法有效，而是指出这三种方法无效。适配位置仍至关重要，但具有模型和语料特异性。在CDSD上，两模型意见相左——Qwen3-ASR偏好其上三分之一（三种子平均CER 29.88%），Whisper-small全块LoRA（53.56%）；而在UASpeech上，两者均不偏好上部区域。在三个种子下，上部 vs 全部的差异显著（-1.31个百分点，置信区间[-2.16, -0.25]），但上部 vs 中部无显著差异（-0.83个百分点，置信区间[-2.06, +1.86]）。两模型偏好存在直接测试交互效应（-4.71个百分点，置信区间[-6.17, -2.89]），我们将其视为主要结论，并注明此判定为事后确定。在Qwen3-ASR × UASpeech组合中，靠近输入处适配的表现显著劣于完全不适配（CER增加9.90个百分点）。我们建议根据实际模型与语料进行短时适配扫描来选择适配区间，而非依赖探针结果读取。

When Ink Meets Pixel: Intent-Aware OCR with VLMs for Human-Corrected Financial Documents When Ink Meets Pixel: Intent-Aware OCR with VLMs for Human-Corrected Financial Documents
当墨水遇见像素：面向人工修正金融文档的意图感知OCR
Human annotations on documents (such as arrows, carets, strikethroughs, and insertions) express corrective intent yet existing OCR and document understanding systems treat these marks as visual noise rather than semantic edit operations. We introduce intent-aware OCR, the task of generating corrected transcriptions by jointly reasoning over original document content and visual intent cues (handwritten or digital) that convey edit intent. To support this setting, we present DocIntent, a multimodal dataset of 1,600 financial documents with over 5,700 annotated visual intent cues, each paired with layout-aware, intent-resolved OCR targets. We further introduce IntentBench, a 216-document benchmark designed to evaluate model robustness under diverse real-world annotation conditions. Leveraging DocIntent, we fine-tune DocIntentOCR, a suite of 3B and 7B parameter vision-language models, under direct intent supervision on corrected transcriptions. On IntentBench, the fine-tuned 3B and 7B models achieve F1-scores of 0.81 and 0.83, respectively, substantially outperforming commercial closed-source (e.g., GPT-4.1 (0.626)), and open-source models (e.g., LLaMA-3.2-Vision-90B (0.50)) demonstrating that intent-aware supervision enables compact domain-specific models to outperform substantially larger general-purpose systems on this task. This work establishes intent-aware OCR as a novel human-in-the-loop document understanding task and provides foundational resources (dataset, benchmark, models) to advance research in interactive document processing.

文档上的手写或数字标注（如箭头、插入符号、删除线和插入内容）表达了修改意图，但现有的OCR和文档理解系统通常将这些标记视为视觉噪声而非语义编辑操作。我们提出了意图感知OCR任务，即通过联合推理原始文档内容和传达编辑意图的视觉线索，生成修正后的文本转录。为此，我们构建了DocIntent数据集，包含1600份金融文档及超过5700个标注的视觉意图线索，每个线索均配有布局感知且意图明确的OCR目标。我们进一步设计了IntentBench基准测试，包含216份文档，用于评估模型在多种真实世界标注条件下的鲁棒性。基于DocIntent数据集，我们在修正文本的直接意图监督下微调了DocIntentOCR，该套件包含3B和7B参数的视觉-语言模型。在IntentBench上，微调后的3B和7B模型分别达到0.81和0.83的F1分数，显著优于商业闭源模型（如GPT-4.1的0.626）和开源模型（如LLaMA-3.2-Vision-90B的0.50），表明意图感知监督使小型领域专用模型在该任务上超越大型通用系统。本工作确立了意图感知OCR作为一项新型人机协同文档理解任务，并提供了基础资源（数据集、基准、模型）以推动交互式文档处理研究。

On the Importance of Hidden-State Refinement in Masked Diffusion Language Models
掩码扩散语言模型中隐藏状态精炼的重要性
Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked or corrupted token sequences, allowing token predictions to condition on bidirectional context and update multiple positions in parallel. While each denoising step produces a rich layer-wise hidden-state trajectory, prior work typically focuses only on token-level information and discards these continuous hidden states, leaving their refinement dynamics underexplored. In this work, we analyze hidden-state dynamics in MDLMs and find clear evidence of layer-wise and cross-step refinement: smooth evolution across adjacent layers, greater predictive power in deeper layers, gradual degradation under transformer block removal, and strong final-state alignment across consecutive denoising steps. Motivated by these observations, we propose Cross-Step Recurrent Refinement (CSRR), a mechanism that carries hidden-state information across denoising steps to enhance generation. Extensive experiments show that CSRR improves text generation quality, highlighting the critical role of continuous hidden-state refinement in MDLMs. Project page: https://zhongzero.github.io/Recurrent-DLLM/
中文翻译
掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）通过迭代去噪被遮蔽或损坏的词元序列来生成文本，使词元预测能够依赖双向上下文并并行更新多个位置。尽管每一步去噪都会产生丰富的分层隐藏状态轨迹，但以往研究通常只关注词元级信息，而忽略了这些连续隐藏状态，导致其精炼动态未被充分探索。本文分析了MDLM中的隐藏状态动态，发现存在明显的分层与跨步精炼现象：相邻层间平滑演化、深层具有更强预测能力、移除Transformer模块后性能逐渐退化，以及连续去噪步骤间最终状态高度对齐。受此启发，我们提出跨步递归精炼（CSRR）机制，将隐藏状态信息跨去噪步骤传递以增强生成效果。大量实验表明，CSRR提升了文本生成质量，凸显了连续隐藏状态精炼在MDLM中的关键作用。

Unjamming Deferrals: Supporting Students Through JAMN Bot, a Mobile RAG-Grounded Policy Assistant
解堵延期：通过JAMN Bot——一款基于RAG的移动政策助手支持学生
In some educational institutions, workflows such as the exam deferral process can be slow, inconvenient, and inconsistent, which creates the need to implement novel digital systems. This paper presents Schedulater, a mobile app for managing university exam deferrals. JAMN Bot is a Large Language Model (LLM)-powered chatbot integrated into Schedulater that provides context-aware support to help users understand the deferral process. The system features a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline using Ollama (Llama 3.2 3B) for self-hosted inference which enables policy-grounded responses without sacrificing privacy. We evaluate the system using a 43-item benchmark spanning Exam Deferral, Privacy, Scheduling, Cross-Policy, and Adversarial & Out-of-Scope questions, and report inter-rater agreement among our human evaluators alongside retrieval hit-rate metrics. Subsequently, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge approach with GPT-5.4, revealing slight-to-moderate alignment with our raters that improves with answer quality, and check the ranking against a panel including Gemini 3.5 Flash and Qwen3.5 397B. The results of our study display trade-offs between open-weight and frontier models, as well as LLM-human evaluation alignment with answer quality. Schedulater demonstrates the feasibility of integrating lightweight LLMs into university administrative workflows with structured retrieval.

SkillCore: Internalizing Generalizable Skills via Scaffolding-Removed Fine-Tuning
SkillCore：通过移除支架的微调内化可泛化的技能
Runtime skills can improve agents' problem-solving capabilities by providing procedural guidance for tasks such as code review and debugging. However, these benefits reach only a minority of users, as most do not proactively invoke skills in practice. We argue that such generalizable procedures should become part of the model's capabilities rather than remain optional external add-ons, making them directly available without explicit skill loading. To this end, we present SkillCore, a data pipeline and supervised fine-tuning recipe for internalizing generalizable skills. We transform real agent trajectories by removing skill-loading scaffolding while preserving the task context and subsequent agent actions, thereby supervising direct execution without external skill content. To preserve access to task-specific skills that provide genuinely external information, we mix these scaffold-removed trajectories with a small proportion of skill-native trajectories that preserve supervision for skill invocation. On a held-out skill-free evaluation, SkillCore improves task completion quality over the base Qwen3-8B and Qwen2.5-72B models by 54.4% and 24.8%, respectively, while preserving task-specific skill invocation on a separate probe and general coding performance on SWE-bench Verified.

RightDiff: Learning Generative Reward Models to Judge by the Right Differences
RightDiff：学习生成奖励模型以依据正确的差异进行判断
Generative reward models typically rely on generic query-level rubrics or unconstrained rationales, overlooking the fact that pairwise preference is driven by response-specific differences. Moreover, optimizing solely for outcome correctness encourages models to exploit superficial shortcuts such as verbosity and formatting, triggering rationale drift. To address this, we propose RightDiff, which structures pairwise evaluation into an evidence-grounded evaluation trajectory executed in a single autoregressive generation. RightDiff explicitly discovers contrastive differences between candidates, categorizes them into material or immaterial factors, and induces pair-specific rubrics before final adjudication. To train this process without post-hoc rationalization, we synthesize demonstration data via response-isolated prompts and optimize the reward model with a difference-alignment reward regularized by budget-constrained one-to-one matching and multiplicative gating. RightDiff achieves state-of-the-art preference modeling performance on RewardBench and RM-Bench, surpassing all open-source reward models and leading proprietary LLM judges. Downstream, it provides reliable supervision for direct preference optimization on Arena-Hard and AlpacaEval while effectively resisting length shortcuts.
中文翻译
生成式奖励模型通常依赖通用的查询级评分标准或不受约束的理由，忽略了成对偏好实际上由响应特异性差异驱动的事实。此外，仅优化最终结果正确性会促使模型利用表面捷径（如冗长和格式），引发理由漂移。为解决此问题，我们提出RightDiff，将成对评估结构化为一次自回归生成中执行的证据基础评估轨迹。RightDiff显式发现候选响应间的对比差异，将其分类为实质性或非实质性因素，并在最终裁决前生成特定于配对的评分标准。为在不依赖事后解释的情况下训练该过程，我们通过隔离响应的提示合成示范数据，并利用预算受限的一一匹配与乘法门控进行差值对齐的奖励正则化优化奖励模型。RightDiff在RewardBench和RM-Bench上实现了最先进的偏好建模性能，超越所有开源奖励模型并领先于专有LLM评判者。下游应用中，它在Arena-Hard和AlpacaEval上提供了可靠的直接偏好优化监督，并有效抵抗长度捷径。

Making Evaluation Explicit Is Not Enough: Supervising the Evaluation Process in Generative Reward Models
使评估显式化并不足够：在生成式奖励模型中监督评估过程
Generative reward models (GRMs) make language-model evaluation more explicit by generating critiques, rubrics, or intermediate analyses before predicting a final preference. However, explicit evaluation does not necessarily imply a reliable evaluation process: under outcome-only supervision, a model may reach the correct final preference while producing weak or unsupported intermediate judgments, turning the evaluation into a post-hoc rationalization. We introduce STEP-GRM, a framework that directly supervises the intermediate evaluation process of rubric-based GRMs. In addition to conventional preference supervision, STEP-GRM provides structural guidance for rubric construction and local supervision for criterion-level judgments, encouraging the model to learn not only the correct final preference but also a more reliable process for reaching it. Extensive experiments show that STEP-GRM achieves strong performance on standard reward-modeling benchmarks while improving the consistency and correctness of intermediate evaluations. Moreover, reward signals produced by STEP-GRM lead to better downstream policy optimization, demonstrating that more reliable evaluation processes can translate into practical gains beyond reward-model accuracy.
中文翻译
生成式奖励模型（GRMs）通过在预测最终偏好前生成批评、评分标准或中间分析，使语言模型评估更加显式。然而，显式评估并不必然意味着可靠的过程：在仅基于结果的监督下，模型可能达到正确的最终偏好，但中间判断却薄弱或缺乏支持，导致评估沦为事后的合理化。我们提出STEP-GRM框架，直接监督基于评分标准的GRMs的中间评估过程。除了传统的偏好监督外，STEP-GRM还提供评分标准构建的结构引导和准则级判断的局部监督，鼓励模型不仅学习正确的最终偏好，也学会更可靠的达成路径。大量实验表明，STEP-GRM在标准奖励建模基准上表现优异，同时提升了中间评估的一致性和正确性。此外，STEP-GRM生成的奖励信号能带来更好的下游策略优化效果，证明更可靠的评估过程可转化为实际收益，而不仅仅是奖励模型准确性。
👤 Qiyuan Chen, Yuze Zhao, Jiahe Chen, Hongsen Huang, Hongxia Xu et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Grounding Language Models in Revealed Audience Behavior
基于受众行为揭示的语言模型对齐
Language models are increasingly used to simulate how an audience will respond to a headline or a post, and such simulators are validated against what people say. We validate them against what people do. We call a model grounded in revealed audience behavior when it is fine-tuned on records of what audiences actually clicked, liked or watched, and we ask not only whether asking a model falls short of grounding it, as prior work found, but why and where. Why: the gap is a property of the training signal, not mainly of model size or prompting. The same 8B model is right about half the time when asked which headline readers will click and 86.5% of the time when grounded in the clicks; the strongest prompting we tried only matches a text classifier; a grounded 0.6B model beats every prompted frontier model in our runs; and whether the clicks are fitted as counts or as shares makes no detectable difference in a pre-registered test. The asked 8B model also reproduces stated-preference cues, favoring question headlines that in fact lower clicks. Where: across four behaviors, grounding wins where the text carries the signal (short-video likes, +20 points on 200 pre-registered videos), only ties an asked model given the reader's history (news clicks), and nothing text-only gets far from chance on watch time. Code, adapters and run outputs will be released.
中文翻译
语言模型越来越多地被用于模拟受众对标题或帖子的反应，以往的验证方式是基于人们所说的反馈。本文则基于人们实际的行为进行验证。当模型在真实受众点击、点赞或观看记录上进行微调时，我们称其为‘基于行为揭示的对齐模型’。我们不仅探讨为何仅通过提问无法实现对齐（如先前研究发现），还深入分析原因与具体场景。原因在于训练信号本身的缺陷，而非模型规模或提示工程；相同规模的8B模型在未对齐情况下仅能正确预测一半的点击行为，而在基于点击数据对齐后准确率提升至86.5%；最强的提示方法也仅相当于一个文本分类器；一个0.6B的对齐模型在实验中击败所有经过提示优化的前沿模型；将点击数据建模为数量或比例，在预注册测试中并无显著差异。此外，未对齐的8B模型还会错误地复制陈述偏好线索，倾向于选择实际上降低点击率的问题型标题。具体表现方面：在四种行为中，对齐在文本承载信号的任务中表现优异（如短视频点赞，200个预注册视频提升20分），在读者历史相关的任务中仅与未对齐模型持平（新闻点击），而纯文本信息在观看时长预测上几乎无法超越随机水平。代码、适配器和运行输出将公开发布。

Reading, Writing and Repairing Game-Theoretic Policies in Weight Space
在权重空间中阅读、写作与修复博弈论策略
Can a language model work in the weight space of a policy network for a repeated game? We put four questions to a mid-size open model, on recurrent networks of 1{,}218 weights with exactly checkable answers. Given a population of opponents described in English, write a policy that is optimal: its value is a finite computation over the agent's information state, so a submitted network has an exact gap from the optimum. Given a network's weights, report its action after each of fifteen opponent histories; recover the state machine it implements, judged on every opponent sequence to depth ten; or repair a network whose output was altered in one place and say which round first goes wrong. The model recovers the program in 62 of 66 replies, reports all fifteen behavioral entries correctly in 34 of 36, and attains the optimum in 20 of 20 when it states a policy the evaluator compiles. Repair is the exception: 4 of 18 repaired, 5 of 18 diagnosed, with a median patch covering one of the two entries needed.
中文翻译
语言模型能否在重复博弈的策略网络权重空间中工作？我们向一个中等规模的开源模型提出四个问题，涉及包含1,218个权重的递归网络，并具有可精确验证的答案。给定以英文描述的对手群体，要求写出一个最优策略：其价值是基于智能体信息状态的有限计算，因此提交的网络与最优解之间存在精确差距。给定网络权重后，要求报告在十五种对手历史下的每一步动作；恢复其所实现的状态机，评估每个对手序列至深度十；或修复一个在某处输出被修改的网络，并指出首次出错的回合。模型在66次回复中的62次成功恢复了程序，36次中有34次正确报告了全部十五个行为条目，当模型声明的策略被评估者编译时，20次中有20次达到最优。修复是例外：18次修复中仅有4次成功，5次诊断正确，平均修补仅覆盖两个必要条目之一。
👤 Ancil Crayton 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Scoring What Matters: Exact Feedback Reduction for Language Model Comparison
关注真正重要的指标：语言模型比较中的精确反馈缩减
Comparing language-model checkpoints requires estimating performance differences, yet evaluation pipelines can pay to score responses whose contributions cancel from those differences. We introduce Contrast-Aware Feedback Acquisition (CAFA), a sampler-agnostic method that identifies which scores a requested linear report needs before acquiring feedback. CAFA groups identical scoring inputs and aggregates their report coefficients, removing queries beyond ordinary caching while preserving the fully scored result. For a fixed response bank and an otherwise unrestricted scalar scorer, we characterize necessary and sufficient query sets for exact recovery of sample means, either alone or jointly with ordinary sample covariances. On controlled updates, CAFA removes 55.8% and 90.1% of the scoring inputs remaining after caching for Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct, respectively, averaged over four generation repetitions. Across three online group relative policy optimization (GRPO) trajectories, Independent Star sampling with CAFA achieves 46.1-51.3% lower recorded generation-plus-feedback cost than independent autoregressive sampling with caching at a common empirical precision threshold (RMS MCSE $\leq 0.04$). This is a post hoc comparison of completed runs on a fixed budget grid. All 504 evaluated report cells reproduce the fully scored means and covariances to numerical precision. We provide a modular implementation supporting existing response banks, versioned caches and external scorers.
中文翻译
语言模型检查点的比较需要估计性能差异，但评估流程可能对那些最终贡献相互抵消的响应进行评分。我们提出一种采样无关的方法——对比感知反馈获取（CAFA），可在获取反馈前识别出线性报告所需的评分项。CAFA将相同的评分输入分组并聚合其报告系数，在保留完整评分结果的同时，避免了超出常规缓存的查询。对于固定的响应库和任意标量评分器，我们刻画了精确恢复样本均值（单独或联合普通样本协方差）所需查询集的充要条件。在受控更新中，CAFA在缓存后分别移除了Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct和SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct剩余评分输入的55.8%和90.1%，平均覆盖四次生成重复。在三个在线群体相对策略优化（GRPO）轨迹中，结合CAFA的独立星采样在相同经验精度阈值（RMS MCSE ≤ 0.04）下，相比独立自回归采样加缓存，生成+反馈成本降低了46.1%-51.3%。这是在固定预算网格上完成运行后的事后比较。所有504个评估报告单元均以数值精度重现了完全评分的均值与协方差。我们提供模块化实现，支持现有响应库、版本化缓存和外部评分器。
👤 Hui Zhang, Haoda Yue, Bicheng Deng, Yuanchao Zhu, Jun He et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31848]

Faithful Persona Steering under Incongruity via Dual-Stream Refinement
通过双流精炼实现不一致情境下的忠实人格引导
Standard LLM personalization typically frames identity as a static retrieval task, overlooking the inherent incongruity of human personas, where stable traits coexist with atypical, context-specific stances. Existing methods struggle to reconcile these dimensions: prompting succumbs to context drift over long sequences, while fine-tuning often suppresses idiosyncratic "quirks" in favor of generic distributional patterns. To bridge this gap, we present QuirkyMind, a framework that disentangles identity definition from its expression. First, Traits Anchoring constructs a dual-stream latent state, fusing a sentence-level summary for semantic stability with a token-level sequence for generative control. This state is stabilized via In-Context Narrative Refinement using an alternating objective: a discriminative InfoNCE loss anchors the persona in representation space to prevent drift, while a generative cross-entropy loss ensures faithful verbalization. Finally, Persona Steered Generalization transfers the refined state to downstream tasks via parameter-efficient adapters. Empirical evaluations on Persona-Steered QA and Narrative Inference demonstrate that QuirkyMind mitigates drift, consolidating persona knowledge without erasing authentic incongruities.
中文翻译
标准的大语言模型个性化通常将身份视为静态检索任务，忽略了人类人格中固有的不一致性——稳定特质与特定情境下的非常态立场共存。现有方法难以调和这些维度：提示法在长序列中易受上下文漂移影响，而微调常压制个性化的“怪癖”以迎合通用分布模式。为弥合这一差距，我们提出QuirkyMind框架，将身份定义与其表达相分离。首先，特征锚定构建一个双流潜在状态，融合句子级摘要以保证语义稳定性，以及词元级序列以实现生成控制。该状态通过上下文叙事精炼进行稳定化，采用交替目标：判别式InfoNCE损失将人格锚定在表示空间以防止漂移，生成式交叉熵损失确保忠实表达。最后，人格引导的泛化通过参数高效的适配器将精炼状态迁移到下游任务。在人格引导问答与叙事推理任务上的实证评估表明，QuirkyMind有效缓解了漂移问题，在不抹除真实不一致性的情况下巩固了人格知识。
👤 Yu-An Chu, Jen-Ren Pong, Chia-Yao Yeh, Meng-Fen Chiang 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
