Exploring Multi-Player Social Games as Verifiable Training Grounds for Large Language Models
探索多人社交游戏作为大语言模型的可验证训练场
The training of socially intelligent language models often relies on large language model (LLM) judges, which are expensive, noisy, and vulnerable to reward hacking. Multi-player social games offer a natural alternative: they require open-ended communication to coordinate with teammates and infer hidden roles, while their rules provide an objective outcome which can serve as a verifiable reward for training. Using three social games, we explore how much social intelligence Qwen3 models can acquire through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Results show that against a much stronger Gemma-3-27B model, SFT and RLVR raise the win rate of Qwen3-4B from 33% to 81% on the three trained games, and from 39% to 54% on three additional held-out games. In contrast, in-context strategies such as prompting improve the win rate of Qwen3-4B by only about 6 pp across the same games we train and evaluate on. Furthermore, we show that the gains from SFT and RLVR hold across the model sizes 1.7B, 4B, and 32B. Qualitative analyses of agent behavior show signs of stronger social intelligence; for example, trained agents tend to share information more openly, check each other's claims against public evidence, and coordinate their votes. More broadly, our results demonstrate that training LLMs on verifiable outcomes in social games can cultivate generalizable social behaviors, which offers a scalable path for building socially intelligent agents.
中文翻译
社会智能语言模型的训练通常依赖于昂贵、嘈杂且易受奖励欺骗的大语言模型（LLM）裁判。多人社交游戏提供了一个自然的替代方案：它们需要开放式的交流以协调队友并推断隐藏角色，同时其规则提供了客观的结果，可作为训练的可验证奖励。我们使用三种社交游戏，研究了Qwen3模型通过监督微调（SFT）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能获得多少社会智能。结果表明，在与更强的Gemma-3-27B模型对抗时，SFT和RLVR将Qwen3-4B在三个训练游戏中的胜率从33%提升至81%，在三个额外保留的游戏上从39%提升至54%。相比之下，在上下文策略（如提示）下，Qwen3-4B的胜率仅提升了约6个百分点。此外，我们还发现SFT和RLVR带来的收益在1.7B、4B和32B不同规模的模型中均成立。对代理行为的定性分析显示其表现出更强的社会智能，例如训练后的代理更倾向于公开分享信息，根据公开证据核查彼此的陈述，并协调投票。更广泛地，我们的结果表明，在社交游戏中基于可验证结果训练LLM可以培养可泛化的社会行为，为构建社会智能代理提供了一条可扩展的路径。
👤 Keyu He, Michael Li, Jiarui Liu, Mingqian Zheng, Koichi Onoue et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31727]

fork() for Agents: Agent Kernel for Non-blocking LLM Orchestration
Agent的fork()：用于非阻塞LLM编排的代理内核
LLM-based agents typically execute tool calls synchronously and discard completed work upon failure. Subgraph-selective recovery, re-executing only a failed node's transitive descendants, is a well-established pattern in distributed systems (Spark, Erlang/OTP, Dryad), and recent agent runtimes (Sherlock, AgentGit, Atomix) preserve completed work through verifiers, version control, or transactional tool boundaries. We present \textbf{Agent-Kernel}, a non-blocking runtime that ports this pattern to the LLM \emph{content plane}: its primitive, \textbf{Sub-Graph Recovery (SGR)}, fires on a raw exception with no trained verifier or planner, resets a failed node's descendants while preserving completed siblings, and composes each parent's text into its children's prompts (context inheritance), all on a durable substrate with a formal state-preservation guarantee. Across 14{,}434 runs on synthetic and standard benchmarks (tau-bench, Gaia2, AutomationBench, InterruptBench), SGR reaches full recovery within budget=3 where whole-trajectory replay exhausts (100\% vs.\ 55\%), and is roughly 10$\times$ faster than an adapted Sherlock rollback that also recovers. It matches ReAct-serial accuracy within noise on linear workloads while adding recovery and parallelism (up to 12.7$\times$ speedup) where structure permits. Ablation isolates SGR: removing it collapses success to 39.4\%.
中文翻译
基于LLM的代理通常同步执行工具调用，并在失败时丢弃已完成的工作。子图选择性恢复——仅重执行失败节点的传递后代——是分布式系统（如Spark、Erlang/OTP、Dryad）中的成熟模式，近期的代理运行时（Sherlock、AgentGit、Atomix）通过验证器、版本控制或事务性工具边界保留已完成工作。我们提出**Agent-Kernel**，一种非阻塞运行时，将此模式移植到LLM的**内容平面**：其基本操作**子图恢复（SGR）** 在原始异常发生时触发，无需训练过的验证器或规划器，重置失败节点的后代但保留已完成的兄弟节点，并将每个父节点的文本组合进子节点的提示中（上下文继承），所有操作均在具有正式状态保存保证的持久化底座上完成。在14,434次合成及标准基准测试（tau-bench、Gaia2、AutomationBench、InterruptBench）中，SGR在预算=3时实现完全恢复（100% vs. 55%的全轨迹重播），且比适配后的Sherlock回滚快约10倍。在线性工作负载上，其准确率与ReAct-serial相当，同时增加了恢复能力和并行性（最高达12.7倍加速），前提是结构允许。消融实验表明：移除SGR后成功率降至39.4%。
👤 Ankit Chadha, Pratyay Banerjee 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Training decision models for simulating human respondents
用于模拟人类应答者的决策模型训练
Researchers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as synthetic respondents in market research, conjoint analysis, and randomized experiments. However, synthetic choice data from off-the-shelf generative LLMs bias downstream econometric inference. Neither proprietary models nor higher reasoning effort reliably reduces this bias. We investigate decision models (System One architectures) for silicon sampling. Using choice training, we update KEV model weights on historical respondent-level choice microdata by conditional maximum likelihood, before inference and without target-study data. Across three model scales, training on two discrete choice experiments lowers treatment-effect error by 22% to 89% on four held-out studies from new domains (vaccines, nutrition labels, consumer rights, and AI) and new response formats. A small choice-trained model outperforms a much larger off-the-shelf model on three of the four studies. Although training on single studies lowers cross-entropy, it can exacerbate treatment-effect error. Choice training thus complements post-inference calibration by providing a stronger starting point for estimating human preferences.
中文翻译
研究人员越来越多地使用大语言模型（LLMs）作为市场调研、联合分析和随机实验中的合成应答者。然而，来自现成生成式 LLM 的合成选择数据会 bias 下游计量推断。无论是专有模型还是更高的推理努力，都无法可靠地减少这种偏差。我们研究了用于硅基采样的决策模型（System One 架构）。通过选择训练，我们在没有目标研究数据的情况下，基于历史应答者级别的选择微观数据，使用条件最大似然更新 KEV 模型权重，然后进行推断。在三个模型规模下，对两个离散选择实验进行训练，使四个新领域（疫苗、营养标签、消费者权利和人工智能）及新响应格式的保留研究中处理效应误差降低了 22% 到 89%。一个经过选择训练的小模型在其中三个研究中表现优于一个大得多的现成模型。尽管在单个研究上训练可降低交叉熵，但可能加剧处理效应误差。因此，选择训练通过提供更强的起点，补充了推断后的校准。
👤 Davood Wadi, Yu Ma 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31586]

SecondChance: Benchmarking Reliable Error Recovery in AI Agents
SecondChance：AI 智能体可靠错误恢复的基准测试
AI agents make errors while they act, and their reliability depends on what they do afterwards. Existing recovery benchmarks mostly inject faults, involve no user and score whether the task is eventually completed, so they cannot show whether an agent undid its own silent error, told the user, or faced an error that could not be fixed. We introduce SecondChance, an error checkpoint dataset and evaluation harness that profiles and measures error-recovery behaviour of AI agents. The errors are natural: they come from agents' own task attempts in $τ^2$-bench, BFCL and AppWorld. Different agents then resume from the same checkpoints under one of four conditions: no assistance, an error notice, the error location, or repair guidance. We measure error correction, task success, policy compliance, preservation of environment state, repair durability and cost. We evaluate 12 models under all four conditions in 5,760 continuations. Recovery is rare: across its environments, the best model completes only 16% of recoveries unaided and 27% with repair guidance. Correcting is not recovering: with repair guidance, Llama-3.3-70B corrects 26% of $τ^2$ errors but completes none, and 40 of 84 successful AppWorld continuations leave the error in place. SecondChance exposes these differences that task success alone cannot capture.
中文翻译
AI 智能体在执行任务时会出错，其可靠性取决于后续行为。现有的恢复基准大多通过注入故障来测试，不涉及用户，仅以任务是否最终完成作为评分标准，因此无法揭示智能体是否纠正了自身的沉默错误、是否告知用户，或是否面临无法修复的错误。我们引入 SecondChance，一个错误检查点数据集和评估工具包，用于剖析和度量 AI 智能体的错误恢复行为。这些错误是自然产生的：来源于智能体在 $τ^2$-bench、BFCL 和 AppWorld 中自身任务尝试的结果。不同智能体在四种条件下从相同检查点继续执行：无协助、错误通知、错误位置提示、修复指导。我们测量错误纠正、任务成功、策略合规性、环境状态保留、修复持久性和成本。我们在 5,760 次延续中对 12 个模型在所有四种条件下进行了评估。恢复极为罕见：在各环境中，表现最好的模型在无帮助情况下仅能独立完成 16% 的恢复，有修复指导时也仅达 27%。纠正不等于恢复：在修复指导下，Llama-3.3-70B 能纠正 $τ^2$ 错误的 26%，但一项任务也未完成；在 84 次成功的 AppWorld 延续中，有 40 次错误仍被保留。SecondChance 揭示了仅靠任务成功无法捕捉的这些差异。
👤 Atahan Dokme, Adar Avsian, Larry Heck 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

Reliability of Language-Model Judges for Quantitative Scientific Question Answering: Evidence from Perovskite Photovoltaics
语言模型裁判在定量科学问答中的可靠性：基于钙钛矿光伏的证据
Evaluating retrieval-augmented language models increasingly relies on automated model judges, yet their reliability on quantitative scientific synthesis remains largely unmeasured. Here, we evaluate automated grading on a quantitative benchmark for perovskite photovoltaics, where all 755 ground-truth answers are deterministically computed from 45,441 experimental device records. We evaluate a frontier judge (Claude Opus 5.5) alongside two local open-weight judges (Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-8B) across 4,530 free-text answers from six retrieval architectures under three regimes: reference-augmented (with the computed key), evidence-augmented (with retrieved passages and query logs), and unassisted (question and answer alone). Equipped with the reference key, Claude Opus reproduces ground truth near-perfectly (Cohen's $κ= 0.982 \pm 0.003$) and preserves exact architectural rankings ($τ= 1.00$). Withholding the reference causes evaluation fidelity to collapse: frontier agreement drops to $κ= 0.557 \pm 0.022$, and providing retrieved literature passages yields virtually no improvement ($κ= 0.576 \pm 0.024$). Without reference keys, local judges degrade to near-random agreement ($κ= 0.087$ and $0.112$), inverting the leaderboard to rank a hallucinating closed-book baseline first ($τ\le 0.20$). Furthermore, SQL-augmented generations induce severe evaluative blind spots: absent a reference, the frontier judge accepts 46.6% of erroneous SQL answers, overestimating architecture accuracy as 66.4% against its true 52.6%. These findings demonstrate that model-based evaluation in the physical sciences cannot rely on unstructured text inspection; rigorous benchmarking requires reference ground truth precomputed from structured measurement records.
中文翻译
评估检索增强型语言模型越来越多地依赖自动化模型裁判，但其在定量科学综合任务中的可靠性尚未得到充分衡量。本文在钙钛矿光伏的定量基准上评估自动化评分，所有755个真实答案均从45,441条实验设备记录中确定性计算得出。我们评估了前沿裁判（Claude Opus 5.5）以及两个本地开源裁判（Qwen3-14B 和 Qwen3-8B），涵盖六种检索架构在三种模式下的4,530个自由文本回答：参考增强（含计算出的关键信息）、证据增强（含检索段落和查询日志）、无辅助（仅问题与答案）。在提供参考关键信息时，Claude Opus 几乎完美复现真实答案（Cohen's κ = 0.982 ± 0.003），并保持精确的架构排名（τ = 1.00）。若省略参考信息，评估保真度急剧下降：前沿裁判一致性降至 κ = 0.557 ± 0.022，提供检索文献段落也几乎无改善（κ = 0.576 ± 0.024）。在无参考键的情况下，本地裁判退化为接近随机的一致性（κ = 0.087 和 0.112），导致排行榜反转，使一个产生幻觉的闭卷基线模型排名第一（τ ≤ 0.20）。此外，SQL 增强生成引入严重评估盲点：缺乏参考信息时，前沿裁判接受46.6%的错误SQL答案，将架构准确率高估至66.4%，而真实准确率为52.6%。这些发现表明，在物理科学中基于模型的评估不能依赖非结构化文本检查；严格的基准测试必须基于结构化测量记录预先计算的真实答案。

AhaPro: A Process-level Rebus benchmark for probing Aha moments in LLM reasoning
AhaPro：用于探测大语言模型推理中顿悟时刻的过程级字谜基准
Existing research on Aha moments in large language models has largely focused on reflective expressions or final revision outcomes, leaving their role in the visible reasoning process insufficiently characterized. Rebus puzzles encode answers through character forms, spatial arrangements, and homophony. Reinterpreting key details may trigger Aha moments and shifts in the reasoning process and candidate answers. We introduce AhaPro, a process-level benchmark that uses rebus puzzles to examine how models reorganize evidence and revise answers through Aha moments. The framework represents CoT as sequences of candidate answers, evidence, and verification states, using hierarchical metrics to assess conflict detection, state improvement, and subsequent adoption. Across eleven models, we observe frequent failures to adopt better states discovered during Aha moments, alongside adopted revisions that leave state quality unchanged or reduce it. Models also detect conflicts without subsequently achieving an effective revision. Further paired interventions suggest that prior reasoning context may hinder effective revision after conflict detection, while multi-branch search highlights answer selection as a bottleneck between correct-answer discovery and successful correction.
中文翻译
现有对大语言模型顿悟时刻的研究主要集中在反思表达或最终修正结果上，对其在可见推理过程中的作用仍缺乏充分刻画。字谜通过字符形式、空间排列和同音现象编码答案。重新解读关键细节可能触发顿悟时刻，并引发推理过程及候选答案的变化。我们提出 AhaPro，一个基于字谜的过程级基准，用于考察模型如何通过顿悟时刻重新组织证据并修正答案。该框架将思维链（CoT）表示为候选答案、证据和验证状态的序列，采用分层指标评估冲突检测、状态改进及后续采纳情况。在十一款模型中，我们观察到频繁出现未能采纳顿悟时刻发现的更优状态的情况，且已采纳的修正并未提升或反而降低了状态质量。模型还存在检测到冲突但未实现有效修正的现象。进一步的配对干预表明，先前的推理上下文可能阻碍冲突检测后的有效修正，而多分支搜索凸显了正确答案发现与成功修正之间的答案选择瓶颈。

Agentic Systems Need Multi-Run Evaluation: A Study of Retrieval and Response Variability in a Deployed Enterprise RAG System
智能体系统需要多轮评估：部署的企业级 RAG 系统中检索与响应可变性的研究
Previous RAG systems have mostly been evaluated in a single-run setting, and with the advent of agentic RAG, systems are still often evaluated the same way. However, agentic RAG systems introduce a source of run-to-run variability, since several of their components make decisions non-deterministically. Such systems typically comprise a retrieval stage followed by a generation stage, and the retrieval stage in particular can behave differently across runs. For example, the agent might issue a different number of parallel searches from one run to the next, which alters the retrieved documents and therefore the final answer. In this paper we study this behavior in a deployed agentic RAG system for product-documentation questions, running each of 739 questions ten times and analyzing the execution traces to find where different runs first diverge. We find a substantial gap between success in any run and success in every run: the retrieval hit rate is 69.4% when the correct page is found in at least one run, but 50.9% when it must be found in all ten. Responses also stay similar in meaning while varying in wording, with an average BERTScore of 0.90 and a BLEU of 0.49. Trace-level analysis shows that retrieval is the largest source of divergence (43.9%), and the number of parallel searches at the first retrieval step alone accounts for 20.1% of divergent pairs. Taken together, these results show that single-run evaluation can hide reliability and consistency issues in agentic RAG systems, and that multi-run evaluation is necessary to reveal them.
中文翻译
以往的 RAG 系统大多在单轮设置下进行评估，随着智能体 RAG 的出现，系统仍常以相同方式评估。然而，智能体 RAG 系统引入了轮次间可变性，因其多个组件决策具有非确定性。这类系统通常包含检索阶段和生成阶段，其中检索阶段在不同轮次间表现可能不同。例如，智能体可能在不同轮次中发起不同数量的并行搜索，从而改变检索文档，进而影响最终答案。本文研究了一个用于产品文档问答的部署型智能体 RAG 系统的行为，对 739 个问题各运行十次，并分析执行轨迹以定位不同轮次首次分歧的位置。我们发现，至少一次成功与全部十次成功之间存在显著差距：当正确页面在至少一轮中被找到时，检索命中率为 69.4%，而在所有十轮中都必须找到时，命中率降至 50.9%。响应在语义上保持相似，但在措辞上存在差异，平均 BERTScore 为 0.90，BLEU 为 0.49。轨迹级分析显示，检索是主要的分歧来源（43.9%），而首次检索步骤中并行搜索数量的差异单独就占了 20.1% 的分歧对。综上所述，单轮评估可能掩盖智能体 RAG 系统中的可靠性和一致性问题，多轮评估对于揭示这些问题至关重要。

SD-Cache: Learnable Memory Consolidation with State-Guided Dynamic Caching
SD-Cache：基于状态引导的动态缓存可学习记忆整合
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as persistent services that must reason over large volumes of external knowledge. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds model responses in relevant documents, but the retrieved content must still be encoded and stored in the key-value (KV) cache. As this content accumulates, the cache grows linearly, increasing memory consumption and inference cost. Existing memory-compression methods address this problem either by retaining a subset of cached states or by learning context compressors that map documents to compact latent representations. Despite these advances, consolidating information under tight memory budgets while preserving its usefulness for future queries remains challenging. We investigate how the model's native KV states can be selectively consolidated into a fixed-size working memory without access to future queries. To this end, we introduce SD-Cache (State-guided and Dynamically updated Cache), a query-agnostic KV-memory consolidation framework that operates alongside a frozen generator LLM. SD-Cache learns to consolidate layer-wise KV states into fixed-size memory through selective anchor-based pooling and state-guided refinement, processing new contexts in a single forward pass without context-specific optimisation. Experiments on retrieval-augmented question answering and synthetic long-context benchmarks show that SD-Cache maintains competitive downstream performance under constrained memory budgets.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地作为需处理大量外部知识的持久服务部署。检索增强生成（RAG）将模型响应锚定在相关文档上，但所检索内容仍需编码并存储在键值（KV）缓存中。随着内容积累，缓存呈线性增长，导致内存消耗和推理成本上升。现有内存压缩方法要么保留部分缓存状态，要么学习上下文压缩器将文档映射为紧凑的潜在表示。尽管取得进展，但在严苛的内存预算下整合信息并保持其对未来查询的有用性仍具挑战。我们研究如何在不访问未来查询的前提下，将模型原生 KV 状态选择性地整合进固定大小的工作内存。为此，我们提出 SD-Cache（状态引导且动态更新的缓存），一种与冻结生成器 LLM 并行运行的无查询依赖 KV 内存整合框架。SD-Cache 通过选择性锚点池化和状态引导精炼，将逐层 KV 状态整合为固定大小的记忆，以单次前向传播处理新上下文，无需特定于上下文的优化。在检索增强问答和合成长上下文基准上的实验表明，SD-Cache 在受限内存预算下仍能保持有竞争力的下游性能。

How Persona Conditioning Changes Open-Ended Task Execution: A Paired Evaluation of Behavioral Gains and Reliability Costs
人格设定如何改变开放式任务执行：行为收益与可靠性成本的配对评估
Large language models (LLMs) are often prompted with role- or persona-based instructions, yet persona conditioning has mainly been evaluated through character fidelity or aggregate benchmark accuracy. We study persona conditioning as a task-execution steering signal that changes how models interpret, structure, and sustain responses. Using paired instances derived from real user role-play logs and expanded into simulated multi-turn continuations, we compare with-persona and no-persona conditions while fixing task content, constraints, user simulation, and evaluation criteria. Across eight models, persona conditioning improves expert content depth and procedural fidelity, and these gains survive length control. At the same time, it lowers strict instruction-following accuracy by 17 to 30 points in every model evaluated, and increases unsupported real-world specificity through persona-induced elaboration. These results suggest that persona prompting is not a general-purpose performance improvement technique, but a selective task-execution policy shift with both behavioral gains and reliability risks.

Evaluating Large Language Models on Iranian Doctoral Entrance Exams in Electrical and Computer Engineering
在伊朗电气与计算机工程博士入学考试中评估大型语言模型
We evaluate four large language models (LLMs) on multiple-choice questions from the 2024 and 2025 Iranian national Ph.D. entrance examinations in Electrical Engineering (EE) and Computer Engineering (CE). Of the 440 questions in the four official booklets, 7 were excluded because the question statement or its figure was ambiguous, leaving 433 questions (186 EE, 247 CE), of which 171 (39.5\%) depend on a figure. Each question was presented in the original Persian and in an English translation, giving 3,464 scored responses. GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Claude Sonnet 4.5 and Grok 4 were queried zero-shot, with reasoning enabled, through a single commercial web interface (Perplexity) in October 2025, using an automated script. Because every model answers the same items, all inferences use paired tests or question-clustered models. Accuracy separates into two tiers: GPT-5 (79.9\%, 95\% CI 76.6--83.1) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (78.4\%, 74.8--81.9) are statistically indistinguishable, and both outperform Grok 4 (71.7\%) and Claude Sonnet 4.5 (71.3\%) by 6.7--8.7 percentage points (Holm-adjusted $p < 0.001$). The English--Persian accuracy difference is small for every model ($-0.2$ to $+2.5$ points; exact McNemar $p \ge 0.28$). For three models the 90\% confidence interval lies within $\pm 5$ points, so differences larger than this can be excluded. Questions with figures are much harder: the odds ratio is 0.30 (95\% CI 0.21--0.43) after adjusting for discipline. The apparent EE deficit of two models disappears once the larger share of figure-based EE items is accounted for. We describe the dataset, the protocol and its limitations, including the uncontrolled web-retrieval and decoding settings of the interface, and release item-level responses and analysis code.
中文翻译
我们评估了四种大型语言模型（LLMs）在2024年和2025年伊朗全国电气工程（EE）与计算机工程（CE）博士入学考试中的多项选择题表现。在四本官方题册共440道题目中，有7道因题干或图表模糊被剔除，剩余433道题目（EE 186道，CE 247道），其中171道（39.5%）依赖图表。每道题目以原始波斯语和英文翻译两种形式呈现，共产生3,464次评分响应。GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Grok 4通过Perplexity单个商业网页接口在2025年10月进行零样本查询，启用推理模式，并使用自动化脚本。由于所有模型回答相同题目，所有推断均采用配对检验或按题目聚类的模型。准确率分为两档：GPT-5（79.9%，95%置信区间76.6–83.1）与Gemini 2.5 Pro（78.4%，74.8–81.9）统计上无差异，且均显著优于Grok 4（71.7%）和Claude Sonnet 4.5（71.3%），差距达6.7–8.7个百分点（Holm校正p < 0.001）。各模型在波斯语与英语版本间的准确率差异较小（-0.2至+2.5点；McNemar精确p ≥ 0.28）。对三个模型而言，90%置信区间在±5点以内，因此大于此范围的差异可排除。含图题目难度显著更高：调整学科因素后，优势比为0.30（95%置信区间0.21–0.43）。两个模型在EE上的表现劣势在考虑EE题中更多图表题后消失。我们描述了数据集、实验协议及其局限性，包括界面中未受控的网络检索与解码设置，并公开了逐题响应及分析代码。

Capability and Interpretability of Transformers on Multi-task Recurrence
Transformer在多任务递归任务中的能力与可解释性
Transformers are widely used in various language tasks, with limited understanding of their internal mechanisms. Motivated by the recurrent structure of many linguistic rules, we study the capability and interpretability of Transformers through a controlled family of linear recurrence tasks over finite cyclic groups. These tasks range from executing a single fixed rule to identifying a latent rule from context and switching between explicitly specified rules, with and without input masking. Through extensive experiments across different configurations, we find that switching specified rules is simpler than identifying latent rules for Transformers in general. We also identify how several key factors affect the generalization capability of Transformers, including the depth, width, and the algebraic structure of the linear recurrence. Curriculum training is extremely useful when the number of rules is large. With standard tools in mechanistic interpretability like linear probing and input patching, our study reveals how Transformers represent, identify, and execute multiple rules.
中文翻译

RecipeADQA: A User-aware Question-Answering Dataset for Multi-Constraint Recipe Recommendation
RecipeADQA：一种面向多约束菜谱推荐的用户感知问答数据集
Question-answering recipe recommendation tasks lack datasets with ground-truth labels. Existing methods often generate datasets from simple combinations of ingredient or tag constraints, leading to unnatural expressions and unreasonable constraints that are hard to reflect users' real preferences. This paper proposes a construction method for a benchmark-style QA dataset for natural-language recipe recommendation. Driven by real user behavior, our method integrates external user query and review data to extract fine-grained preference signals by LLMs and maps them onto a knowledge-graph-based tag schema, thereby constructing QA data consisting of preference and ingredient constraints. Besides, a greedy strategy for constraint selection is also used to control the answer size, while tag-based re-ranking is applied to improve data quality. The generated dataset RecipeADQA contains natural-language questions, structured constraints, and answer sets, resulting in 128,465 instances. Experiments compare RecipeADQA with baseline datasets from two perspectives: data quality and retrieval-recommendation performance. The results show that ours outperforms existing methods on the same recipe knowledge graph in coverage, higher question quality in naturalness and constraint rationality, and better queryability. Moreover, we design a noise-augmented retrieve-and-rerank model, in which a lightweight parser first retrieves candidate recipes, and a small language model then selects and ranks relevant recipes from a noisy candidate pool. The model trained on RecipeADQA outperforms the same model trained on the baseline datasets. The relevant construction code and data have been open-sourced at a GitHub repository https://github.com/MorimoLab/RecipeADQA.
中文翻译
问答式菜谱推荐任务缺乏带有真实标签的数据集。现有方法通常通过简单组合食材或标签约束生成数据，导致表达不自然且约束不合理，难以反映用户的实际偏好。本文提出了一种构建基准风格QA数据集的方法，用于自然语言菜谱推荐。该方法基于真实用户行为，整合外部用户查询和评论数据，利用大语言模型提取细粒度偏好信号，并将其映射到基于知识图谱的标签体系中，从而构建包含偏好与食材约束的QA数据。此外，采用贪心策略控制约束选择以限制答案规模，同时使用基于标签的重排序提升数据质量。生成的数据集RecipeADQA包含自然语言问题、结构化约束和答案集，共128,465个实例。实验从数据质量和检索-推荐性能两个角度对比了RecipeADQA与基线数据集，结果表明其在覆盖范围、问题自然性与约束合理性方面优于现有方法，且更具可查询性。此外，我们设计了一种噪声增强的检索-重排序模型，其中轻量级解析器首先检索候选菜谱，小语言模型再从噪声候选池中选择并排序相关菜谱。在RecipeADQA上训练的模型优于在基线数据集上训练的相同模型。相关代码与数据已开源至GitHub仓库 https://github.com/MorimoLab/RecipeADQA。
👤 Haotian Li, Shuai Jiang, Sayaka Kamei, Yasuhiko Morimoto 📅 2026-10-19 🔗 arXiv

RECAP: Training Long-horizon LLM Agents with Context Compaction
RECAP：通过上下文压缩训练长时程大语言模型智能体
Context compaction enables large language model agents to tackle long-horizon tasks within limited context budgets by replacing interaction histories with compressed summaries. However, learning effective compaction jointly with the agent policy remains challenging, as summaries affect task success over many subsequent interactions. We introduce RECAP, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework based on the principle that a summary should preserve the behavior induced by the original interaction history. We formalize this principle with a behavior distortion objective that measures the divergence between the agent's subsequent behavior under compressed and original contexts. We show that the gradient of the distortion objective decomposes into two complementary learning signals: distortion-based reward for summary generation and self-distillation for subsequent actions. Experiments on Qwen3.5-4B show that RECAP consistently outperforms the task-reward-only RL baselines on both environments, leading to an absolute improvement of 4.6% and 4.3% on BrowseComp-plus and SWE-bench Verified, respectively. Ablations further highlight the value of jointly supervising summary generation and post-compaction behavior for RECAP.

scOPD: Routed On-Policy Self-Distillation for LLM-Based Single-Cell Annotation
scOPD：面向基于大语言模型单细胞注释的路由型在线自蒸馏方法
Motivation: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for single-cell annotation, but existing training methods mainly rely on scalar outcome rewards that provide limited supervision for intermediate biological reasoning. Batch-level annotation is particularly challenging because it requires jointly resolving local cell identities and a global one-to-one matching constraint, such that one local error can affect the entire assignment. Moreover, a single inference pass provides insufficient evidence for locating uncertain predictions. These limitations call for fine-grained reasoning supervision during training and uncertainty-aware refinement during inference. Results: To address these challenges, we propose scOPD, an on-policy self-distillation framework for LLM-based single-cell annotation. After reasoning fine-tuning, scOPD adaptively routes qualified rollouts to reward-based optimization, while erroneous rollouts receive privileged token-level supervision from successful sibling trajectories. During inference, rollout disagreement triggers constraint-aware pairwise refinement of uncertain assignments. Across CellPuzzles and four out-of-domain disease datasets, scOPD consistently improves the performance of diverse LLM backbones, achieving average cell-level and batch-level accuracies of 81.71% and 44.49% with Qwen3-8B. Availability and Implementation: Code and data are available at https://github.com/YuZhiyin/scOPD.

Directly Maximizing On-policy Expected Accepted Length for Speculative Decoding
直接最大化在线策略期望接受长度以实现推测解码
Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a lightweight draft model to propose tokens for verification by a target model. Draft training commonly optimizes token-level distribution alignment, but this does not directly optimize the length of the accepted prefix: once a draft token is rejected, all subsequent draft tokens are discarded. We propose FreDraft, an on-policy training method that directly maximizes the expected accepted length of drafts sampled from the current draft model. This objective accounts for first-rejection truncation and induces prefix-aware credit assignment, weighting each token by its contribution to the acceptance of the current and subsequent tokens. To optimize the objective efficiently, we analytically marginalize token-level acceptance rate, using the target model's vocabulary distribution to reduce gradient variance. We further redistribute part of this distribution-level gradient into an explicit penalty for sampled tokens whose probabilities are overestimated by the draft model, while preserving the expected gradient. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and target models show that FreDraft improves accepted length and decoding speedup over strong baselines, achieving faster on-policy training convergence.

推测解码通过使用轻量级草稿模型提出待验证的token来加速大语言模型推理。草稿训练通常优化token级别的分布对齐，但这并未直接优化被接受前缀的长度：一旦某个草稿token被拒绝，后续所有草稿token均被丢弃。我们提出FreDraft，一种直接最大化当前草稿模型采样草稿的期望接受长度的在线策略训练方法。该目标考虑首次拒绝截断现象，并引入前缀感知的信用分配机制，按每个token对当前及后续token接受的贡献进行加权。为高效优化该目标，我们通过分析方式对token级接受率进行边缘化，利用目标模型的词汇分布降低梯度方差。我们进一步将部分该分布级梯度重新分配为对草稿模型高估概率样本的显式惩罚项，同时保持期望梯度不变。在多个基准和目标模型上的实验表明，FreDraft在被接受长度和解码加速方面优于强基线，实现了更快的在线策略训练收敛。

The Date You Didn't Set: The System-Prompt Date as a Hidden Variable in LLM Evaluation
你未设定的日期：大语言模型评估中的系统提示日期作为隐藏变量
The system prompt of many instruction-tuned models carries a date, usually today's, filled in automatically for conversational and agentic requests alike, so an unchanged evaluation can send a different prompt each day. In this paper, we explore how much this hidden input changes evaluation outcomes. Across six instruction-tuned models and four benchmarks, with prompts, decoding, and configuration fixed, every model is affected: two plausible dates (2024--2026) switch a median $6.6\%$ of test items between correct and incorrect. Even rerunning the evaluation one day later flips about as many items as changing the batch size, although nothing in the setup was changed. In addition, using a grammar-constrained variant of Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) to search dates of any year, we find dates that cost the Llama-3.x models up to $36.5$ points of held-out accuracy. Beyond accuracy, switching to another plausible date alters LLM-judge verdicts and undoes $15$--$32\%$ of reproduced jailbreak successes on Llama-3.2-3B. Yet none of the eleven evaluation stacks we audit pins the date. With the date and the rest of the setup fixed, reruns reproduce byte-for-byte; we therefore recommend pinning, logging, and reporting the date.

DAGGER: Deployment-Free Quality Estimation for Generative AI Workflows
DAGGER：无部署的生成式AI工作流质量估计
Estimating the quality of generative AI workflows is challenging because these workflows comprise multiple stages, and errors can propagate across them, making end-to-end performance depend not only on the accuracy of individual models but also on how their outputs affect downstream stages. Each stage can use one of many candidate models. Estimating end-to-end workflow quality by deploying and evaluating every resulting model configuration is therefore prohibitively expensive. As a result, existing approaches face a tradeoff between estimation cost and accuracy: inexpensive methods often produce unreliable quality estimates, while accurate methods require deploying and evaluating many workflow configurations. In this paper, we introduce DAGGER, a framework for estimating the quality of generative AI workflows without exhaustive deployment and profiling. DAGGER represents a workflow as a graph, in which nodes correspond to stages and edges capture how outputs from one stage affect subsequent stages. It then decomposes workflow quality into per-node accuracy terms and per-edge dependency terms. Using a novel analytical formulation and SVD-based matrix completion, DAGGER estimates workflow quality from sparse observations without executing every candidate workflow. We evaluate DAGGER on three generative AI workflows across three datasets. DAGGER estimates workflow quality with a median error of 6.8 percentage points while reducing profiling cost by 10.2$\times$ relative to BenchPress, a state-of-the-art baseline, and selects workflow configurations competitive with state-of-the-art frameworks such as LLMSelector.
中文翻译
估计生成式AI工作流的质量具有挑战性，因为这些工作流包含多个阶段，错误可能在各阶段间传播，使得端到端性能不仅取决于单个模型的准确性，还取决于其输出对下游阶段的影响。每个阶段可选用多种候选模型。因此，通过部署并评估所有可能的工作流配置来估计端到端质量是极其昂贵的。现有方法面临成本与精度之间的权衡：低成本方法常产生不可靠的质量估计，而高精度方法需要部署和评估大量工作流配置。本文提出DAGGER，一种无需全面部署和性能分析即可估计生成式AI工作流质量的框架。DAGGER将工作流表示为图结构，节点对应阶段，边表示前一阶段输出对后续阶段的影响。它将工作流质量分解为节点级准确率项和边级依赖项。通过一种新颖的解析公式和基于SVD的矩阵补全技术，DAGGER仅从稀疏观测中估计工作流质量，而无需执行所有候选配置。我们在三个生成式AI工作流和三个数据集上评估DAGGER，结果显示其质量估计的中位误差仅为6.8个百分点，相比最先进的基线BenchPress，性能分析成本降低了10.2倍，并能选择出与LLMSelector等先进框架相媲美的工作流配置。

Validation Bias in Large Language Model Responses to Cancer Treatment Queries: A Factorial Cross-Sectional Study
大型语言模型在癌症治疗咨询中的验证偏差：一项因子横断面研究
Large language model (LLM) chatbots are increasingly used by patients seeking cancer information, but their safety when users present evidence-misaligned beliefs about treatment remains uncharacterized. We hypothesized that LLMs exhibit validation bias, affirming rather than correcting such beliefs, and evaluated this with a 2x2x2 factorial design crossing conversation format (single-turn vs multi-turn), treatment type (evidence-based vs alternative), and query valence (positive vs negative). Framing was evidence-aligned (supporting evidence-based treatments or questioning alternative therapies) or evidence-misaligned (promoting alternative treatments or questioning evidence-based therapies). We analyzed 1,272 conversations (6,360 interactions) across 14 LLMs from 6 vendors, scored by an LLM judge (GPT-5-mini) validated against human annotation on a 6-dimension safety rubric. The primary outcome was the unsafe response rate, defined as a Weak classification or any critical failure. Evidence-misaligned framing increased unsafe response rates compared with evidence-aligned framing (14.4% vs 5.1%; OR 3.14, 95% CI 2.07-4.78; P < .001), and the association persisted after accounting for clustering by LLM. Among 429 conversations classified as Strong under evidence-aligned framing, 22.6% were downgraded in their paired evidence-misaligned counterpart. Multi-turn conversations had lower unsafe response rates than single-turn conversations (5.2% vs 14.7%; P < .001). Unsafe response rates varied by vendor from 1.8% to 36.8%. LLMs exhibited significant validation bias in oncology, with unsafe responses nearly tripling under evidence-misaligned framing. Evaluations limited to neutral, fact-seeking queries may overestimate real-world safety, and safety testing of medical AI systems should include evidence-misaligned user framing.

DiaCap: Jointly Learning Who Speaks When for Audiovisual Dialogue Captioning
DiaCap：联合学习音频视觉对话中的说话人与时间
We present DiaCap, an audiovisual LLM for comprehensive dialogue captioning, which produces the transcript, timestamps, and a visual description of the speaker for every utterance in a video. Considering that utterance timing and audiovisual diarization are tightly coupled, DiaCap unifies the tasks in a single model, trained in two complementary stages. First, we train DiaCap on 225K curated clips, using synthetic captions that densely label every utterance in detail. Second, we further refine the model via reinforcement learning on 2.9K human-corrected clips, using fine-grained feedback that directly evaluates utterance timing, speaker grouping, and speaker binding. On DiaDemBench, which we extend with utterance-level timestamps and subtitle redaction, both DiaCap with off-the-shelf transcripts and its end-to-end variant DiaCap-E2E achieve strong results. Specifically, DiaCap performs on par with Gemini 4 Argon in audiovisual diarization while clearly outperforming it in timestamp accuracy (76.59 vs. 42.76 TIME). It also maintains strong diarization on multilingual and minute-long videos, and transfers to audiovisual question answering.

agentic-rl
BriConstrain: Geometrically Constrained Decoding for Autoregressive Brick Assembly Generation
BriConstrain：自回归积木组装生成中的几何约束解码
Language models can generate LEGO$^\circledR$ assemblies from text, but their builds often contain parts that collide or attach to nothing. The usual approach of rejecting and resampling invalid placements discards decoded tokens without guaranteeing a valid build. We present BriConstrain, a constrained decoding method that restricts a pretrained language model's next-token probabilities before sampling, steering it towards valid placements. Because the validity of a brick placement depends on the non-predetermined build state, it cannot be compiled into a fixed grammar and must be checked dynamically. However, checking the validity of a brick before sampling is challenging as the space of valid placements is combinatorially large and cannot be enumerated exhaustively. Our key idea is to narrow the search space by constraining how parts attach rather than which parts attach to each other. Concretely, given the model's predictions of the currently placed part, and the previously placed parent it wants to connect to, we constrain the model's choice of how they attach. This allows us to span the space of part attachments, using a precomputed geometric oracle, and to effectively filter out colliding options. With valid attachment candidates in hand, we bias the model's next-token distribution towards the remaining valid candidates. We evaluate our method on two LEGO$^\circledR$ formats and models: BrickNet, which annotates connectivity explicitly, and our proposed Implicit Connectivity LDraw Format (ICLF), which uses numeric values for placements and leaves connectivity implicit. Our method raises collision-free builds from 31% to 91% on BrickNet and from 15% to 65% on ICLF, and outperforms rejection sampling on both formats at about 60% of its decoding cost.
中文翻译
语言模型可以从文本生成乐高积木组装，但其构建中经常出现部件碰撞或未连接的情况。通常通过拒绝和重采样无效放置的方法会丢弃已解码的标记，且无法保证生成有效结构。我们提出BriConstrain，一种约束解码方法，在采样前限制预训练语言模型的下一个词概率，引导其趋向于有效的放置位置。由于积木放置的有效性依赖于非预设的构建状态，无法编译为固定语法，必须动态检查。然而，在采样前检查积木有效性具有挑战性，因为有效放置的空间是组合爆炸的，无法穷举。我们的核心思想是通过约束部件如何连接，而非连接哪些部件，来缩小搜索空间。具体而言，给定模型对当前放置部件及其希望连接的父部件的预测，我们约束它们之间的连接方式。这使我们能够利用预先计算的几何Oracle覆盖所有部件连接的可能性，并有效过滤掉碰撞选项。在获得有效连接候选后，我们偏向模型的下一个词分布以保留这些有效候选。我们在两种乐高格式和模型上评估该方法：BrickNet（显式标注连接性）和我们提出的隐式连接LDraw格式（ICLF，使用数值表示位置，连接性隐含）。该方法将无碰撞构建率从BrickNet的31%提升至91%，ICLF从15%提升至65%，且在约60%的解码成本下优于拒绝采样。
👤 Nadav Magar, Amir Hertz, Ariel Shamir, Or Patashnik 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

A Bias in Disguise: Tiny Gradient Edits Stop a Small Language Model's Thinking Loops and Complement a Frontier Model's Weight Edit
伪装的偏差：微小梯度编辑阻止小型语言模型的思维循环并补充前沿模型的权重编辑
Qwen3.5-0.8B rarely finishes reasoning in thinking mode: at a 4,096-token budget only 1 or 2 of 500 MATH-500 answers close their think block (two seeds), and accuracy is 15.4%. A recent demonstration had GPT-6 Astra edit 131,072 of the model's weights without gradients and reported MATH-500 rising from 7.9% to 48.1% at a 32,768-token budget. We provide the missing gradient baseline, then combine the two edits. Fine-tuning only the 140 to 139,750 weights with the largest gradient, on the model's own correct answers to MATH training problems, raises thinking-mode MATH-500 at 4k tokens to 39.0-45.5% after 2-9 minutes of training on one GPU; 50 training examples suffice, and 1,398 random positions give only 16.3%. The gradient concentrates the edit on the most nearly constant channel of each of the first three layers. Because the model has no bias terms, an edit confined to those channels acts as a constant vector added to the residual stream: adding three fixed vectors to the unedited model matches the accuracy of such an edit. Under the Astra protocol (32k tokens, presence penalty 1.5), the Astra edit scores 52.1% (finished and correct) in our reproduction and our full-strength edits reach 45.6-45.7%, but ours win at every budget up to 16k tokens (39.5% against 6.7% at 4k). The two edits are complementary. The Astra edit plus our 139,750-weight edit at half strength scores 62.2% at 32k, 10 points above the Astra edit alone (p = 3e-9), with half as many tokens; our half-strength edit alone scores 46.7%.
中文翻译
Qwen3.5-0.8B在思考模式下很少能完成推理：在4,096个token预算下，仅500个MATH-500问题中有1到2个关闭了思考块（两个种子），准确率为15.4%。最近一项演示显示，GPT-6 Astra在不使用梯度的情况下修改了模型的131,072个权重，报告在32,768个token预算下MATH-500准确率从7.9%提升至48.1%。我们提供了缺失的梯度基线，然后结合两种编辑方法。仅对模型自身正确答案上的140至139,750个梯度最大的权重进行微调，在单个GPU上训练2-9分钟后，4k token预算下的思考模式MATH-500准确率提升至39.0%-45.5%；仅需50个训练样本，而随机选择1,398个位置仅得16.3%。梯度集中于前三层中最接近恒定的通道。由于模型无偏置项，仅限于这些通道的编辑相当于向残差流添加一个常数向量：向未编辑模型添加三个固定向量即可达到类似编辑的准确率。在Astra协议（32k token，存在惩罚1.5）下，我们复现的Astra编辑得分为52.1%（完成且正确），而我们的全强度编辑达到45.6%-45.7%，但在每个预算（最高16k token）下均表现更优（4k时为39.5% vs 6.7%）。两种编辑具有互补性。Astra编辑与我们半强度的139,750权重编辑结合，在32k token下得分为62.2%，比Astra单独编辑高出10个百分点（p=3e-9），且仅需一半token；我们半强度编辑单独使用得分为46.7%。

Latent Merger: Latent-Space Composition of Hypernetwork-Generated LoRAs
潜在融合：超网络生成LoRA的潜在空间组合
Recent work proposes hypernetworks that convert a document into a low-rank parameter update (LoRA) in a single forward pass, enabling fast injection of new knowledge into a frozen LLM. However, storing separate LoRA adapters for each document can be unsustainable in streaming settings. In this paper, we study the problem of composing hypernetwork-generated LoRAs using two representative systems, Doc-to-LoRA and SHINE. We show that hypernetwork-generated LoRAs have a geometry sharply different from directly fine-tuned LoRAs. They exhibit greater spectral concentration and stronger cross-document alignment than next-token prediction and QA fine-tuned adapters, which are diffuse and nearly orthogonal across documents. Guided by this observation, we propose latent-space LoRA merging. We compose the intermediate hypernetwork latents and then decode the merged latent through the frozen LoRA-generation head. We show that latent-space merging techniques consistently improve over weight-space and factor-space baselines while preserving a single fixed-rank adapter. These results support latent-space composition as a promising mechanism for combining hypernetwork-generated LoRAs under a fixed output-rank budget.
中文翻译
近期工作提出超网络可在一次前向传播中将文档转换为低秩参数更新（LoRA），实现快速注入新知识到冻结的大语言模型中。然而，在流式场景中为每篇文档单独存储LoRA适配器可能不可持续。本文研究了使用两种代表性系统（Doc-to-LoRA和SHINE）对超网络生成的LoRA进行组合的问题。我们发现，超网络生成的LoRA在几何结构上与直接微调的LoRA显著不同：它们表现出更强的谱集中性和跨文档对齐性，而下一词预测和问答微调的适配器则呈弥散状且几乎正交。基于此观察，我们提出潜在空间LoRA融合。我们先组合中间超网络潜在表示，再通过冻结的LoRA生成头解码合并后的潜在表示。结果表明，潜在空间融合技术始终优于权重空间和因子空间基线，同时保持单一固定秩适配器。这些结果支持潜在空间组合作为在固定输出秩预算下融合超网络生成LoRA的一种有前景机制。
👤 Yanlai Yang, Minki Kang, Divyam Madaan, Sung Ju Hwang, Mengye Ren 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Can a Frontier LLM Repair a Quantized Network by Tool-Free Weight-Space Programming?
前沿大语言模型能否通过无工具权重空间编程修复量化网络？
We give four large language models (LLMs) of the Claude family the complete weights of a 20k-parameter CIFAR-10 CNN as text and ask them to produce a 4-bit network without tools, data or the ability to run inference. Standard round-to-nearest (RTN) quantization to 4 bit lowers the test accuracy from 81.4% to 60.4%. We design four evaluation protocols that differ in the input and task description given to the LLMs and analyze their responses. In the first two protocols the LLMs are given the float and the 4-bit RTN network and are told that the accuracy has dropped significantly. In the last two they receive only the float network and the task to quantize it, which tests whether they apply an optimization on their own or fall back to plain RTN. Our analysis shows that a frontier LLM can recover most of the lost accuracy. Given one answer per layer, Opus 5.5 raises the accuracy of the 4-bit network to 77.95% by balancing the rounding errors within each 3x3 kernel, reasoning over the integer weights without tools. This is 3.5 pp below the float network and 2.8 pp below AdaRound, which learns the rounding from calibration data. Given only the float network, the LLMs name error-cancelling corrections but do not apply them and fall back to plain RTN, at most with mildly clipped scales, which still lifts Fable 5.2's quantized network to 70.0%. We analyze in detail what the LLMs did and why it works. Code and prompts can be found at https://github.com/urbste/weight-space-programming-ptq
中文翻译
我们让四款Claude系列的大语言模型接收一个20k参数的CIFAR-10 CNN的完整权重作为文本，并要求它们在无工具、无数据、无法运行推理的情况下生成一个4比特网络。标准的最近舍入（RTN）量化至4比特将测试准确率从81.4%降至60.4%。我们设计了四种评估协议，其输入和任务描述各不相同，分析了模型的响应。前两个协议中，模型同时接收到浮点网络和4比特RTN网络，并被告知准确率显著下降。最后两个协议中，模型仅接收浮点网络和量化任务，测试其是否能自主应用优化而非退化为普通RTN。分析表明，前沿大语言模型可以恢复大部分损失的准确率。在每层仅给出一个回答的情况下，Opus 5.5通过平衡每个3x3卷积核内的舍入误差，将4比特网络的准确率提升至77.95%，无需工具地推理整数权重。这比浮点网络低3.5个百分点，比AdaRound（从校准数据学习舍入）低2.8个百分点。仅接收浮点网络时，模型虽能命名误差抵消修正，但并未应用，最终退化为普通RTN，最多仅轻微裁剪尺度，但仍使Fable 5.2的量化网络准确率提升至70.0%。我们详细分析了模型的行为及其原理。代码和提示可参见https://github.com/urbste/weight-space-programming-ptq

Can LLM Judges Catch Unsafe Clinical Actions? A Fault-Injection Study
大语言模型裁判能否识别不安全的临床操作？一项故障注入研究
As clinical AI agents begin to interact with electronic health records (EHRs), large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate their actions. But how reliably can these judges recognize clinically dangerous mistakes, especially when an agent appears to have completed its task successfully? We investigate this question using MedAgentBench by introducing controlled clinical errors into verified agent trajectories. The experiments include 416 faulty trajectories, eight error categories, and six open-weight LLM judges. By comparing each faulty trajectory with its correct counterpart, we measure whether judges genuinely recognize errors rather than simply flagging more actions as unsafe. The findings show that access to clinical evidence significantly improves judge reliability. When shown only the final answer, judges cannot distinguish errors that leave it unchanged, while flagging 56% of correct trajectories. With full trajectory information, the ability to distinguish unsafe actions from correct ones reaches a score of 0.75, and providing actual EHR responses improves discrimination by 0.39. Surprisingly, including the agent's reasoning does not improve overall performance and reduces detection of incorrectly reported clinical values by 0.18, which remain the hardest errors to detect. MedGemma performed worse than the general-purpose model and was the only judge to miss potentially dangerous medication overdoses. We also find that the benchmark's original grader accepts all 38 trajectories with an intentionally skipped laboratory check, while combining LLM judges with a rule-based checker informed by the full EHR record detects 97% of injected faults.
中文翻译
随着临床AI代理开始与电子健康记录（EHR）交互，大型语言模型（LLMs）被越来越多地用于评估其行为。但这些裁判在代理看似成功完成任务时，能否可靠地识别出临床上危险的错误？我们通过在MedAgentBench中引入受控的临床错误来研究这一问题。实验包含416条有缺陷的轨迹、八个错误类别以及六个开源权重的LLM裁判。通过将每条有缺陷的轨迹与其正确对应轨迹进行比较，我们衡量裁判是否真正识别错误，而非仅仅将更多动作标记为不安全。研究发现，访问临床证据显著提升了裁判的可靠性。当仅看到最终答案时，裁判无法区分那些结果不变的错误，且会将56%的正确轨迹标记为不安全；而提供完整轨迹信息后，区分不安全行为与正确行为的能力得分达到0.75，若提供实际EHR响应，辨别能力进一步提升0.39。令人意外的是，包含代理的推理过程并未提高整体性能，反而使错误报告的临床值检测率下降0.18，这类错误仍是最难检测的。MedGemma的表现不如通用模型，是唯一漏检潜在危险药物过量的裁判。我们还发现，基准原评分器接受了所有38条故意跳过实验室检查的轨迹，而结合LLM裁判与基于完整EHR记录的规则检查器可检测到97%注入的故障。
👤 Neda Ghafouri 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32991]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
On Potentiality and Actuality Of Foundation Models
基础模型的潜能与现实
Foundation models are built through large-scale pretraining, often followed by post-training, and are then adapted to specific tasks and behaviors. But, does the adaptation add truly new capabilities, or the base model already holds it? We decompose the update induced by different adaptations into a component shared across all inputs and a residual that varies with the input. We find that the shared component suffices for most of the adaptation gain and can be learned directly: a shift adapter that learns a constant vector per layer, identical for every input, reproduces the behavior of LoRA and full fine-tuning. We call this the potentiality principle of foundation models: the base model already contains the capacity needed for task-specific expertise. To adapt to a new task, the model just needs to be actualized, i.e., pushed in a certain direction via a constant, input-independent shift. We show the implications of this principle on parameter-efficient adaptation, steering, and convergence across models, and how it explains the neural thickets phenomenon.
中文翻译
基础模型通过大规模预训练构建，通常随后进行后训练，并适应特定任务和行为。但这种适应是否带来了真正的新能力，还是基础模型本身已具备这些能力？我们把不同适应带来的更新分解为一个对所有输入共享的成分和一个随输入变化的残差部分。研究发现，共享成分足以解释大部分适应收益，且可直接学习：一种每层学习恒定向量的“转移适配器”（shift adapter），对所有输入相同，能复现LoRA和全微调的行为。我们称之为基础模型的‘潜能原则’：基础模型已具备实现特定任务专长所需的能力。要适应新任务，只需将其‘现实化’，即通过恒定的、与输入无关的偏移推动模型朝某一方向发展。我们展示了该原则在参数高效适应、模型引导和收敛性方面的意义，并解释了神经丛林现象。
👤 Fabian Gröger, Shuo Wen, Maria Brbić 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

When Repair Accuracy Misleads: Training a VLM on Its Own Failures
修复准确率具有误导性：基于自身失败训练视觉-语言模型
Fine-tuning a vision-language model on its own failures is a direct way to repair it, but accuracy on held-out failures, the obvious measure of success, misleads. Many mined failures are not model errors: a human audit of 100 failures of Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct on questions generated from COCO and GQA found label errors in 37. And mined failures share wording regularities: question-only classifiers get both answers right on 73.2% of held-out failure pairs. We study both traps with verified failure-driven repair (VFR): typed minimal-pair failure mining, verification by three LLM judges that answer without seeing the stored labels, answer-first corrective targets, and LoRA fine-tuning with replay. VFR repairs 82.8% of held-out failures (base model: 10.0%), and on independently labeled, unconditioned pairs it raises overall accuracy from 87.4% to 92.4% through spatial relations, whose accuracy rises from 66.7% to 96.8% even where the wording points to the wrong answer. Yet on negation, of which it repairs 88.9% of held-out failures, its accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls from 88.7% to 79.7%: it breaks negations about absent objects, rare in its training set. Without verification, accuracy on unconditioned pairs falls to 80.8%, below the base model's 87.4%, through a negation shortcut our development benchmarks miss; without failure selection, it stays at 92.0% (one seed) while repair falls to 73.4%. Training also lowers two-image relation accuracy (74.3% to 49.6%; replay data alone: 48.4%); multi-image replay partly and unevenly reverses this. Paraphrasing the spatial questions keeps most of the gain (87.3% vs. the base model's 62.0%), though rewording lowers spatial repair on held-out failures from 92.9% to 76.8%. On What'sUp's independent photos of objects under others, caption choice stays perfect while accuracy on our yes/no pairs falls from 84.5% to 26.4%.
中文翻译
对视觉-语言模型在其自身失败上进行微调是一种直接的修复方式，但对保留失败集的准确率——这一显而易见的成功度量——具有误导性。许多挖掘出的失败并非模型错误：对Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct在COCO和GQA生成问题上的100个失败进行人工审计发现，其中37个存在标签错误。此外，挖掘出的失败具有语言规律性：仅基于问题的分类器在73.2%的保留失败对中能正确回答两个问题。我们通过验证失败驱动修复（VFR）研究这两种陷阱：类型化的最小差异失败挖掘、由三位LLM裁判在不查看存储标签的情况下作答的验证、先回答再纠正的目标设定，以及带重放的LoRA微调。VFR修复了82.8%的保留失败（基线模型仅为10.0%），在独立标注、无条件的样本上，整体准确率从87.4%提升至92.4%，空间关系准确率从66.7%升至96.8%，即使语言提示指向错误答案也有效。然而，在否定句方面，尽管修复了88.9%的保留失败，其在无条件样本上的准确率却从88.7%降至79.7%：它在处理训练集中罕见的“不存在物体”的否定时失效。若无验证，无条件样本准确率降至80.8%，低于基线模型的87.4%，这是开发基准未捕捉到的否定捷径；若无失败选择，准确率维持在92.0%（单次种子），但修复效果降至73.4%。训练还降低了双图像关系的准确率（74.3%降至49.6%；仅重放数据：48.4%）；多图像重放部分且不均衡地逆转此趋势。改写空间问题保留了大部分增益（87.3% vs 基线62.0%），但重述降低了在保留失败上的空间修复率，从92.9%降至76.8%。在What'sUp独立图片中，对象被其他对象遮挡时，标题选择保持完美，但在我们的是/否对上，准确率从84.5%降至26.4%。
👤 Ang Qi, Yue Wang 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

"I Do Intend to Deliver": Will LLMs Run a Marketplace Advance-Fee Scam?
我确实打算交付”：大语言模型会实施市场预付款诈骗吗？
Online fraud is traditionally limited by the cost of the human labor needed to talk to potential victims. We ask whether hosted large language models will now supply that labor. We prompted ten off-the-shelf models to play the seller in the "free item, just pay delivery" advance-fee scam common on buy-and-sell sites, using a plain system prompt with no jailbreak. Each model played 30 conversations against each of four buyer personas, from credulous to guarded, with the same model playing the buyer (1,200 conversations). We label each conversation by what happened to the buyer's money, and validate the labels against two human coders. 737 conversations (61%) ended with the buyer paying in advance. Eight of the ten models played in almost every conversation, and running both sides cost about 2.5 cents per payment. Only the guarded buyer, who wanted a findable identity, resisted, and the most willing sellers got past it by inventing names and phone numbers. Sellers that recognized the scam still took the money in 133 conversations, usually reasoning about their own honesty rather than the buyer's risk. Several models protected the buyer without refusing, by conceding payment on delivery, so refusal rates alone misstate safety. We release the benchmark, prompts, code and labels.
中文翻译
在线欺诈传统上受限于与潜在受害者交谈所需的人力成本。我们探讨托管的大语言模型是否会现在承担这部分人力。我们让十个现成模型扮演“免费物品，只需支付运费”预付款骗局中的卖家角色，使用普通系统提示，无越狱操作。每个模型与四种买家人格（从轻信到谨慎）各进行30轮对话，同一模型扮演买家（共1200轮对话）。我们根据买家资金的去向标注每轮对话，并通过两名人类编码员验证标签。61%的对话（737轮）以买家提前付款结束。十款模型中有八款几乎参与了所有对话，双方运行成本约为每次付款2.5美分。只有谨慎型买家（要求可查找的身份）表现出抵抗，最愿意的卖家通过虚构姓名和电话号码绕过了这一障碍。即使识别出骗局的卖家仍在133次对话中收取了钱款，通常以自身诚实为理由，而非考虑买家风险。多个模型在不拒绝的前提下保护了买家，例如承诺交付后付款，因此仅靠拒绝率无法准确反映安全性。我们发布了该基准、提示、代码和标签。
👤 Megan Squire 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

ShellBench: What Matters More for AI Agents, Models, or Harness for Everyday Tasks
ShellBench：对于日常任务，AI代理的模型还是工具更重要？
The use of AI agents among the general public has been increasing exponentially throughout 2026, though it is often hard for users to determine which configuration is most effective for their personal use. To help provide clarity here we introduce ShellBench, 100 day-to-day tasks based on more than 10 million user requests, each set in a realistic workspace with messy files and competing data sources, and graded for both correctness and safety. We evaluate 12 models across 5 agent harnesses at 3 reasoning levels for a total of 53,100 rollouts, 20% of which are on tasks designated for public release. We find that model choice impacts performance more than harness choice, but the three leading models differ by only 1.1 percentage points. GPT-6.1 Sol passed 61.4% of attempts at about one-sixth of GPT-6 Astra's estimated token cost per attempt, while GPT-6 Astra led at 62.5%. We also found that harness choice had little effect on either performance or safety with all harnesses scoring between 42.9 (Claude Code) and 45.2 (OpenClaw) for pass@1.
中文翻译
2026年，公众使用AI代理的数量呈指数级增长，但用户往往难以判断哪种配置最适合个人使用。为帮助澄清这一问题，我们推出了ShellBench，基于超过一千万用户请求设计的100项日常任务，每项任务均设置在包含杂乱文件和竞争数据源的真实工作环境中，并对正确性和安全性进行评分。我们评估了12个模型在5种代理工具下，三种推理层级的总表现，共53,100次运行，其中20%的任务面向公众发布。我们发现，模型选择对性能的影响大于工具选择，但前三个领先模型之间仅相差1.1个百分点。GPT-6.1 Sol在约六分之一的GPT-6 Astra每轮估算token成本下通过了61.4%的尝试，而GPT-6 Astra则以62.5%领先。我们还发现，工具选择对性能或安全性影响甚微，所有工具的pass@1得分介于42.9（Claude Code）到45.2（OpenClaw）之间。
👤 Vincent Koc, Aditya Washikar, Rishab Naveen Venkataraman, Onur Solmaz, Zhentong Fan et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

InstructReplay: Replaying Assistant Turns to Preserve General Behavior During Factual Fine-Tuning
InstructReplay：通过重播助手回复保留事实微调过程中的通用行为
Fine-tuning an instruction-tuned language model on new facts often erodes its general abilities. We find that standard fine-tuning lowers instruction-following, reasoning and coding benchmarks by 35 points on average, and that these losses track a collapse in answer length: fine-tuned models at least halve their responses, and the further a response drifts from the initial model's length, the more often it fails. We propose InstructReplay: the initial model answers a set of general instructions once, and during fine-tuning the model is kept close to the initial model on these answers. Across three model families and two domains, InstructReplay reduces the average decline on five general benchmarks from 28.1 to 2.4 points and keeps answer length close to the initial model's, while learning the new facts as well as standard fine-tuning. It balances learning and retention better than seven adaptation methods in all six settings. Controlled comparisons attribute the gain to replaying the model's own answers to general instructions rather than to the form of the loss.
中文翻译
在新事实上对指令微调的语言模型进行微调时，往往会削弱其通用能力。我们发现，标准微调平均使指令遵循、推理和编程基准下降35分，且这些损失与回答长度的崩溃密切相关：微调后的模型至少将响应长度减半，且响应偏离初始模型长度越远，失败频率越高。我们提出InstructReplay：初始模型先对一组通用指令进行一次回答，微调过程中则保持模型在这些回答上接近初始模型。在三个模型家族和两个领域中，InstructReplay将五个通用基准的平均下降从28.1分降至2.4分，同时保持回答长度接近初始模型，且学习新事实的能力与标准微调相当。在所有六个设置中，其学习与保留的平衡优于七种其他适应方法。受控对比实验表明，该优势源于重播模型自身对通用指令的回答，而非损失形式本身。

SOARS: System One Assisted Reranking for Agentic Search
SOARS：基于系统一的智能体搜索重排序框架
Deep search agents address complex information needs through iterative retrieval and reasoning, where the relevance of retrieved evidence influences downstream performance. Existing rerankers improve retrieval effectiveness but introduce substantial latency and cost when invoked throughout the search trajectory. We introduce SOARS, a training-free framework leveraging a System One model for fast, calibrated relevance estimation. At each search step, SOARS selects query-relevant passages from long documents and reranks candidates in a single call, jointly considering the original question and agent-generated sub-query. Across six LLM backbones on BrowseComp-Plus and WebShaper, SOARS consistently improves answer accuracy and evidence recall while reducing search calls, end-to-end latency, and inference cost. On BrowseComp-Plus, it improves GPT-5.4 Mini's accuracy from 73.9% to 84.7% while reducing search calls from 23 to 14, demonstrating the potential of System One reranking for more effective and efficient deep search.
中文翻译

Sample-Efficient In-Context Adaptation via Sleep
通过睡眠实现高效的上下文自适应
The ability to learn and generalize from limited experience, or sample efficiency, is a cornerstone of intelligence. How can we build language models that turn a few user interactions, a few snippets of unfamiliar code, or a handful of experimental results provided in context into knowledge that generalizes? Prior work has found that sleep, a process where the model performs $N$ recurrent forward passes to refine its representations of a chunk of context, can improve long-context reasoning (specifically, within SSM-attention hybrid models). We study whether this method also improves in-context adaptation on general, non-synthetic tasks for transformer language models. To this end, we pre-train language models up to 1.2B-parameters on up to 100B tokens with sleep mechanisms. Notably, longer sleep (i.e., using a larger number of recurrent forward passes $N$) enables the model to learn faster in-context on multiple few-shot algorithmic, question-answering tasks, while also achieving lower next-token prediction loss on out-of-distribution evaluations. On held-out code, models with $N=2$ and $N=4$ match the no-sleep baseline's loss using $2.10\times$ and $2.39\times$ fewer tokens in-context, respectively. More broadly, we view our work as a step toward building models that have better tradeoffs between in-context adaptation and decoding latency.
中文翻译
从有限经验中学习并泛化的能力，即样本效率，是智能的核心特征。我们如何构建语言模型，使其能够将少量用户交互、几段陌生代码或若干实验结果等上下文信息转化为可泛化的知识？先前研究表明，睡眠（sleep）——即模型对一段上下文执行N次递归前向传播以优化其表征——可提升长上下文推理能力（特别是SSM-attention混合模型）。我们研究该方法是否也能提升Transformer语言模型在一般非合成任务上的上下文自适应能力。为此，我们预训练了高达12亿参数的语言模型，最多使用1000亿token，并引入睡眠机制。值得注意的是，更长的睡眠（即更大的递归前向传播次数N）使模型在多个少样本算法、问答任务上更快实现上下文学习，同时在分布外评估中也实现了更低的下一个词预测损失。在保留代码上，N=2和N=4的模型分别仅需2.10倍和2.39倍于无睡眠基线的上下文token数即可达到相同损失。更广泛地说，我们认为这项工作是朝着构建在上下文自适应与解码延迟之间具有更好权衡的模型迈出的重要一步。

Gated Concept Adapters: Layer-Wise Concept Bottlenecks for Auditable Text Understanding
门控概念适配器：可审计文本理解的逐层概念瓶颈
Human-facing text understanding systems are used to infer affect, moderate harmful content, and support socially sensitive decisions. However, Transformer predictions are difficult to audit because they are produced from high-dimensional hidden states that do not provide a compact, inspectable interface for human analysis or intervention. We introduce Gated Concept Adapters (GCA), a layer-wise concept bottleneck framework that attaches gated, low-dimensional concept-producing readout adapters to selected outputs of pretrained Transformers. Token representations are mapped into concept activations, filtered by learned gates, pooled into layer-level summaries, and exposed to the prediction head as a concept-mediated interface. The dimensions can be inspected through usage statistics, signature n-grams, post-hoc concept names, and targeted concept interventions. We evaluate GCA on fine-grained emotion classification, toxicity detection, topic classification, and a controlled verb-cloze stress test. In the affective and safety-relevant classification settings, GCA learns comparatively low-redundancy and intervention-sensitive concept representations while retaining useful predictive performance. On toxicity detection, it remains close to an unconstrained ModernBERT baseline while enabling concept-level inspection, targeted intervention, and lightweight post-hoc control. On GoEmotions, the end-to-end concept model incurs a macro-F1 cost relative to unconstrained RoBERTa, while a task-initialized frozen variant substantially reduces this gap. On AG News, GCA remains competitive with recent concept-based baselines, and the Qwen verb-cloze experiment clarifies the limits of concept-mediated full-vocabulary prediction. Overall, GCA provides a neural concept interface for auditable text understanding, prioritizing transparency, diagnosis, and intervention over unconstrained accuracy maximization.

面向人类的文本理解系统用于推断情感、监管有害内容并支持社会敏感决策。然而，Transformer的预测难以审计，因其来自高维隐藏状态，缺乏紧凑且可检查的人类分析或干预接口。我们提出门控概念适配器（GCA），一种逐层概念瓶颈框架，将带有门控的低维概念生成读出适配器附加到预训练Transformer的选定输出上。将标记表示映射为概念激活，经由学习的门控过滤后，聚合为层级摘要，并作为概念中介接口提供给预测头。其维度可通过使用统计、签名n-gram、事后概念名称及针对性概念干预进行检查。我们在细粒度情绪分类、毒性检测、主题分类及受控动词填空压力测试中评估GCA。在情感与安全相关分类场景中，GCA学习到冗余较低且易于干预的概念表示，同时保持有用的预测性能。在毒性检测中，其性能接近无约束的ModernBERT基线，同时实现概念级检查、目标干预和轻量级事后控制。在GoEmotions上，端到端概念模型相对于无约束RoBERTa略有宏F1损失，而任务初始化冻结变体显著缩小了这一差距。在AG News上，GCA与近期基于概念的基线相当，Qwen动词填空实验揭示了概念中介全词汇预测的局限性。总体而言，GCA为可审计的文本理解提供了神经概念接口，优先考虑透明性、诊断与干预，而非无约束的准确性最大化。

STAR: Counterfactual Dual-World Rollouts and Spatial Target Adaptive Reconstruction for Visual On-Policy Distillation
STAR：反事实双世界轨迹与空间目标自适应重构用于视觉在线策略蒸馏
Vision-language models often rely on global context and language priors while overlooking answer-determining local details. Privileged visual on-policy self-distillation supervises full-image student rollouts using a frozen teacher that views high-resolution evidence crops. Yet our cross-scale diagnostics show that models retain over half of their full-image accuracy when local evidence is completely masked, indicating that single-image correctness does not guarantee reliance on local evidence, while the need for visual correction also varies across tokens with their spatial attention states. We propose STAR, a visual on-policy self-distillation framework combining independent counterfactual dual-world rollouts with spatial-attention-guided target reconstruction. Local image editing constructs paired worlds with identical questions and backgrounds but distinct answer-determining facts, where a shared student samples trajectories independently to expose contextual shortcuts under image-consistent prefixes. Within each world, the teacher contrasts real and mean-color crops to determine the visual correction direction, while the divergence between student image attention and a teacher-centroid spatial reference adaptively controls token-wise target log-probability reconstruction. Without auxiliary attention losses or test-time tool overhead, STAR improves Qwen3.5 across scales by up to 8.04 percentage points in average accuracy over initialization across six fine-grained benchmarks, reaches OPSD's final accuracy $4.7\times$ faster, and generalizes to out-of-domain reasoning tasks.

MAGPIE: Agentic Multi-Reference Image Generation via Executable Scene Construction
MAGPIE：通过可执行场景构建实现智能体多参考图像生成
Multi-reference image generation requires a model to preserve the appearance of selected subjects while arranging them according to an instruction. Despite recent progress in reference-based generation and compositional control, reliably organizing selected subjects into the intended scene remains challenging. Although existing methods provide various forms of compositional guidance, the planned relationships are not always explicitly instantiated for inspection and refinement before image synthesis. To address this limitation, we introduce MAGPIE, which represents the intended composition as a simplified 3D scene defined by an executable Blender program. A coding agent links each scene instance to its reference, checks the geometry and rendered view against the instruction, and revises the program to correct discrepancies. The final proxy rendering serves as a visual plan for the image generator. Using MAGPIE-Instruct-3K, we train the generator to follow this plan while drawing each subject's appearance from its reference. MAGPIE improves both Qwen and FLUX on DreamOmni2 and SconeEval. With FLUX, it improves overall success on DreamOmni2 by 28.21 percentage points over the original backbone and achieves the highest overall score among the models evaluated on SconeEval.

Factorized Context Attention: From Emulated to Native Grid Probing
因子化上下文注意力：从模拟到原生网格探测
Models read a book, an hour of video, or an image in one forward pass. As the context grows, this pass slows down, loses accuracy, and stops at the context window. Sparse attention is efficient, but keeps the other two limitations. Grid probing, a recent training-free method, recovers the accuracy and extends the reach by asking a frozen model the same question about short local and strided spans of the input, but every question repeats the whole computation, since each span is encoded again. Our insight is that grid probing is emulated attention, an attention pattern reproduced from outside the model through prompts, since each probe only restricts which part of the input the question can see. We introduce Factorized Context Attention (FCA), a training-free block-sparse attention that runs the same pattern natively, as the attention of the model itself. FCA encodes the input once into a KV cache, reads all spans as masked sequences in one batched forward, and uses a stair position schedule to keep every span inside the trained position range. Across long video understanding, long-context text inference and image grounding, FCA matches the accuracy and the reach of grid probing at a fraction of its cost. It gains about 10 points over the single pass on long video and 9 to 14 points on long novels, finds needles in videos of over a thousand frames and in texts four times the length of the window, and answers a later question about the same input in as little as a tenth of the time of grid probing. Native attention thus keeps what emulated attention gains without its cost, all with existing frozen language and vision-language models.
中文翻译
模型可以在一次前向传播中阅读一本书、一小时视频或一张图像。随着上下文增长，这种传播会变慢、精度下降，并在上下文窗口处停止。稀疏注意力效率高，但仍然存在另外两个限制。网格探测是一种最近的无训练方法，通过向冻结模型提出关于输入短局部和步进跨度的相同问题来恢复精度并扩展范围，但由于每个跨度都需要重新编码，因此每次问题都会重复整个计算过程。我们的洞察是，网格探测本质上是模拟注意力，即通过提示在模型外部重现的一种注意力模式，因为每个探测仅限制问题能看到输入的哪一部分。我们引入了因子化上下文注意力（FCA），一种无需训练的块稀疏注意力机制，能够以模型自身注意力的形式原生运行该模式。FCA将输入一次性编码为KV缓存，批量前向处理所有跨度作为掩码序列，并使用阶梯位置调度确保每个跨度保持在训练的位置范围内。在长视频理解、长上下文文本推理和图像定位任务中，FCA以极低的成本达到了与网格探测相当的精度和覆盖范围。它在长视频上比单次通过提升约10分，在长篇小说上提升9至14分，能在超过一千帧的视频中找到关键信息，在长度为窗口四倍的文本中进行问答，且对同一输入的后续问题回答时间仅为网格探测的十分之一。原生注意力因此保留了模拟注意力的优势，而无需其高昂代价，且仅使用现有的冻结语言和视觉-语言模型即可实现。
👤 Mohamed Eltahir, Manar Eyad, Yara Alzahrani, Fatimah Albesher, Duaa M. Tahir et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32316]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
ASTERISM: Do Multimodal LLMs Actually Look at All the Images?
ASTERISM：多模态大语言模型真的会看所有图像吗？
Multi-image benchmarks aim to test whether a multimodal large language model (MLLM) combines evidence across images, but many of their items can be answered from a single image. Across about 33k items from 19 public benchmarks, our audit and a two-stage single-image filter find that one image or the question alone settles 24.2% of them. On these items, MLLMs recover from one image a median 78% of the accuracy that all images add over the text alone, so these items reward answering from one image, which we call the single-image shortcut. MLLMs take this shortcut even on items where one image is not enough. On such items, most of their wrong answers repeat an answer they give from one image, at a rate well above chance. We build ASTERISM, to our knowledge the largest audited multi-image benchmark, from these 19 benchmarks by keeping their items that need more than one image. Its 18.7k items, 2.0k of them corrected or rewritten, share one format, one scorer and seven task categories. We also release ASTERISM-Train with 49k training items and ASTERISM-Lite, a hard subset whose items we manually verify one by one. On ASTERISM-Lite, the strongest proprietary MLLM scores 68%. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), now common in MLLM post-training, rewards the shortcut as well, because a correct answer read off one image earns as much reward as one that combines images. In line with this, existing RLVR objectives add little to the ASTERISM score. To stop rewarding the shortcut in training as ASTERISM does in evaluation, we propose cross-image credit assignment (CICA), an extension of RLVR that moves the credit toward the tokens that need more than one image. On three open-source MLLMs, CICA raises the ASTERISM score by 2.6 to 6.0 points, more than twice the gain of each general and vision-specific RL objective trained on the same data.
中文翻译
多图像基准测试旨在检验多模态大语言模型（MLLM）是否能跨图像整合证据，但其中许多题目仅凭单张图像即可解答。通过对19个公开基准中的约3.3万项进行审计和两阶段单图像过滤发现，仅凭一张图像或问题本身就能解决其中24.2%的题目。在这些题目上，MLLM仅从单张图像中就能获得比仅依赖文本高出78%的准确率，因此这类题目奖励从单张图像作答，我们称之为单图像捷径。即使在单张图像不足以回答的题目上，MLLM仍会采用此捷径。在这些题目上，它们大多数错误答案重复了从单张图像得出的答案，频率远高于随机水平。我们构建了ASTERISM，据我们所知目前最大的经过审计的多图像基准，从这19个基准中筛选出需要多张图像才能解答的题目。其包含1.87万项，其中2000项经过修正或重写，统一格式、评分器和七类任务。我们还发布了ASTERISM-Train（4.9万条训练数据）和ASTERISM-Lite（人工逐项验证的高难度子集）。在ASTERISM-Lite上，最强的专有MLLM得分仅为68%。当前常见的多模态大模型后训练方法——可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），同样奖励这一捷径，因为从单张图像读取正确答案所获奖励与融合多图答案相同。与此一致，现有RLVR目标对ASTERISM得分提升甚微。为在训练中阻止对捷径的奖励，如ASTERISM在评估中所做的那样，我们提出了跨图像信用分配（CICA），这是RLVR的扩展，将信用分配给需要多张图像才能处理的token。在三个开源MLLM上，CICA使ASTERISM得分提升2.6至6.0分，提升幅度超过在同一数据上训练的一般和视觉特定RL目标的两倍。
👤 Chanseul Cho, Dongwoo Kim, Noori Bae, Seungjae Moon, Seongsu Lee et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32199]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Steerable by Design: Conditional Pretraining for Robust Control of Language Models
按设计可调控：用于鲁棒语言模型控制的条件预训练
Our goal is to build language models that are steerable by design. Today's language models are pretrained with next-token prediction, which relegates alignment and control to the final 1% of training tokens or inference-time interventions. These models repurpose a single input channel for both instructions and data, leading to common failure modes where the model mistakenly treats data as instructions such as intentional jailbreaks and unintentional in-context alignment drift. To correct this, we propose a pretraining method and architecture that separates the two from the very start, producing Mancelle-3B. An encoder processes high-level semantic features of upcoming text with cross-attention that prevents the token stream from writing back into this control channel. During pretraining, the model learns to rely on these features to predict the next token, turning the channel into an effective handle for steering the model. Mancelle-3B steers more strongly than prompt- and activation-based methods on comparable open models while maintaining fluency, and it withstands adversarial black-box and soft-prompt counter-steering that defeats those baselines. We reproduce this against a more controlled baseline with Mancelle-400M. Our results show that robust control can be built into language models if we do so early and intentionally.
中文翻译
我们的目标是构建按设计即可调控的语言模型。当前的语言模型通常通过下一个词预测进行预训练，这使得对齐与控制被推迟到训练最后1%的tokens或推理时的干预。这些模型将单一输入通道同时用于指令和数据，导致常见故障模式，例如模型错误地将数据当作指令，引发有意的越狱攻击或无意的上下文对齐漂移。为解决此问题，我们提出一种预训练方法和架构，从一开始就分离这两个要素，从而生成Mancelle-3B。该编码器处理未来文本的高层语义特征，通过交叉注意力防止词流回写入该控制通道。在预训练过程中，模型学会依赖这些特征来预测下一个词，从而将该通道转变为有效的调控手段。Mancelle-3B在同等开放模型上表现出比基于提示和激活的方法更强的调控能力，同时保持流畅性，并能抵御对抗性的黑盒和软提示反调控，这些基线方法在此类攻击下失效。我们通过Mancelle-400M复现了这一结果，证明若早期且有意识地构建，鲁棒控制是可以融入语言模型的。

ThinkMorph-SSL: Scaling Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning with Pretext Tasks
ThinkMorph-SSL：通过预训练任务实现交错多模态推理的规模化
People reason with pictures as well as words. Interleaved multimodal reasoning gives models a similar way to solve problems by creating intermediate images and using them to guide further reasoning. Training models with this ability usually relies on task-specific demonstrations, which are difficult to collect. We introduce ThinkMorph-SSL, a two-stage training recipe that uses images themselves as a source of supervision. The first stage, pretext reasoning, converts visual pretext tasks, such as image colorization and denoising, into instruction-free interleaved traces. Models describe a corrupted or transformed image, generate a restored or canonical image, and interpret the result. The second stage uses automatically constructed demonstrations for question-driven instruction tuning. At the same instruction-tuning budget, models trained with pretext reasoning outperform those trained with instruction tuning alone. The full recipe improves over the base model by an average of 11.33 accuracy points across twelve benchmarks. These results show that supervision derived from images can build the visual skills needed for interleaved reasoning and improve learning from task-specific demonstrations.
中文翻译
人们既用图像也用文字进行推理。交错多模态推理为模型提供了类似的方法，通过创建中间图像并利用它们引导进一步推理来解决问题。训练具备此能力的模型通常依赖于特定任务的示范，而这些示范难以收集。我们提出了ThinkMorph-SSL，一种两阶段训练方案，利用图像本身作为监督来源。第一阶段“预训练推理”将视觉预训练任务（如图像着色和去噪）转化为无需指令的交错推理轨迹。模型描述被破坏或变换的图像，生成修复或标准图像，并解释结果。第二阶段使用自动生成的示范进行面向问题的指令微调。在相同的指令微调预算下，采用预训练推理训练的模型优于仅使用指令微调训练的模型。完整训练方案在十二个基准上平均提升11.33个百分点的准确率。这些结果表明，源自图像的监督可以构建实现交错推理所需的视觉技能，并改善从特定任务示范中学习的效果。
👤 Jiawei Gu, Linjie Li, Yiming Liu, Yunzhuo Hao, Huichen Wang et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

ARS-VLM: Visually Aligned Augmented Random Search for Vision-Language Models
ARS-VLM：用于视觉-语言模型的视觉对齐增强随机搜索
First-order reinforcement learning, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), is widely used to post-train Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for multimodal reasoning. However, it incurs heavy memory and system overhead from backpropagation, optimizer states, and a reference model. Forward-only zeroth-order (ZO) methods, such as Evolution Strategies (ES) and its top-k variant Augmented Random Search (ARS), avoid these overheads via reward-guided weight perturbations, yet remain unexplored for VLM reasoning. We find that directly applying standard ES or vanilla ARS to VLMs suffers from two fundamental failure modes: (i) patch-grid overfitting, fitting fragile grid-specific visual token artifacts while ignoring spatial invariants, and (ii) catastrophic forgetting, degrading general perception and out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks due to unbounded high-rank parameter and policy drift. We propose ARS-VLM, a visually aligned Augmented Random Search framework for forward-only VLM post-training. First, ARS-VLM extracts a contrastive visual basis from bilinearly paired cross-grid views, concentrating perturbations on shared visual signals while suppressing patch-shift artifacts. Second, ARS-VLM anchors shallow visual-fusion layers with a soft subspace prior while freeing deep reasoning layers, restraining policy drift without a reference model. Third, ARS-VLM scores perturbations on paired views to discard view-fragile directions during top-k selection. We evaluate ARS-VLM on Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B on multimodal reasoning tasks. On 8B, ARS-VLM reaches 61.0% validation accuracy in 20 steps versus 150 for GRPO (7.5x fewer optimization steps) and avoids the late OOD collapse of vanilla ARS and ES. Moreover, ARS-VLM achieves 75.41% single-view micro accuracy across seven OOD vision reasoning benchmarks, compared with 75.31% for full-parameter GRPO, using forward-only post-training.
中文翻译
一阶强化学习（如组相对策略优化GRPO）广泛用于后训练视觉-语言模型（VLMs）以实现多模态推理。然而，这种方法因反向传播、优化器状态和参考模型带来沉重的内存与系统开销。仅前向的零阶（ZO）方法（如进化策略ES及其top-k变体增强随机搜索ARS）通过奖励引导的权重扰动避免了这些开销，但在VLM推理中尚未被充分探索。我们发现，直接将标准ES或原始ARS应用于VLMs会遭遇两种根本性失败模式：(i) 图块-网格过拟合，模型拟合脆弱的网格特定视觉标记特征，而忽略空间不变性；(ii) 灾难性遗忘，由于无界高秩参数和策略漂移，导致泛化能力下降及分布外（OOD）任务性能退化。我们提出ARS-VLM，一种面向仅前向VLM后训练的视觉对齐增强随机搜索框架。首先，ARS-VLM从双线性配对的跨网格视图中提取对比视觉基底，集中扰动于共享视觉信号，同时抑制图块位移伪影。其次，ARS-VLM通过软子空间先验锚定浅层视觉融合层，同时释放深层推理层，无需参考模型即可抑制策略漂移。第三，ARS-VLM在成对视图上对扰动进行评分，剔除视图脆弱方向以进行top-k选择。我们在Qwen3-VL-4B和Qwen3-VL-8B上评估ARS-VLM在多模态推理任务上的表现。在8B模型上，ARS-VLM仅用20步即达到61.0%的验证准确率，而GRPO需150步（优化步骤减少7.5倍），且避免了原始ARS和ES的后期OOD崩溃。此外，ARS-VLM在七个OOD视觉推理基准上实现了75.41%的单视图微平均准确率，与全参数GRPO的75.31%相当，且仅使用前向后训练。
👤 Xiangwei Wang, Jianyuan Lin, Peng Wang, Saman Halgamuge 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Calibrated Reading of Latent Reasoning and Communication
校准的潜在推理与通信阅读
Latent reasoning and key-value communication store intermediate information in continuous representations, limiting direct inspection of model behavior. Existing activation readers trade the low cost of task-specific probes against the flexibility of language-model decoders. We propose the Calibrated Latent Reader, CLR, a compact transformer that predicts reasoning outcomes and message content through task-conditioned interactions between states and candidate answers. Source adapters support transfer across models, while conformal calibration provides coverage and false-alarm guarantees under exchangeability. On two CODI models, CLR improves accuracy over MLP and attention probes by 4-18 percentage points for final-answer prediction and reference-value membership. On CODI-GPT-2, it achieves accuracy comparable to a 1.7B LatentQA-based verbalizer using 437-648 times fewer inference FLOPs. On synthetic Qwen3-4B communications, comparing decoded facts with public commitments detects 93.1% of message substitutions at a nominal 5% false-positive rate.

潜在推理和键值通信将中间信息存储在连续表示中，限制了对模型行为的直接观察。现有的激活读取器在任务特定探针的低成本与语言模型解码器的灵活性之间进行权衡。我们提出了校准的潜在读取器（CLR），一种紧凑的Transformer模型，通过状态与候选答案之间的任务条件交互来预测推理结果和消息内容。源适配器支持跨模型迁移，而共形校准在可交换性条件下提供覆盖率和误报保证。在两个CODI模型上，CLR在最终答案预测和参考值归属方面比MLP和注意力探针提高了4-18个百分点的准确率。在CODI-GPT-2上，其准确率与使用437-648倍更少推理浮点运算的17亿参数基于LatentQA的表述器相当。在合成Qwen3-4B通信中，通过对比解码事实与公开承诺，在名义5%假阳性率下检测到93.1%的消息替换。
