Fix How It Acts, Not How It Thinks: Surgical Fine-Tuning of Frontier LLMs
修复其行为，而非思维：前沿大模型的精准微调
Open-weight LLMs are competent software engineers, but they sometimes fail at tasks they are otherwise able to do. Often it is not a failure of reasoning. It is a few small missteps over a long trajectory (repeating a step that has already failed, or declaring the task done while a requirement is still unmet) that make the difference. Correcting this calls for a surgical weight update that steers the model toward the right actions without interfering with its reasoning. Such updates, typically learned with LoRA adapters, are often too small to survive merging into a released checkpoint. In our BF16 release, the median weight change is only 1.5% of a single rounding step, and merging loses roughly two thirds of the update's norm. When the same adapter is merged into the mixed FP8/NVFP4 release of the same model, 93% of the update disappears. Instead of increasing the size of the update, we directly train on the surviving part. We introduce merge-aware fine-tuning. Standard LoRA rounds the merged weights to the storage format only at export, so the loss never sees the rounded weights. We instead round the sum of base weights and adapter update to the release format at every training step, keeping the vendor's quantization scales, and compute the loss on these weights. The forward pass matches the serving engine numerics, and the gradient passes straight through the rounding, so the model we train is the model we ship. The loss optimizes only the model's actions and keeps its reasoning close to the base model. Applied to Nemotron-3.5, the recipe improves agentic coding in both the BF16 and the NVFP4 release, by up to 8.4 points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, and we publicly release both models.
中文翻译
开放权重的大语言模型具备优秀的软件工程能力，但有时在本应完成的任务上失败。这种失败往往并非源于推理问题，而是长轨迹中的几个小失误（如重复已失败步骤，或在需求未满足时宣布任务完成）所致。纠正此类问题需要一种精准的权重更新，引导模型走向正确行为而不干扰其推理过程。这类更新通常通过LoRA适配器学习，但往往过小，难以在合并至发布检查点后保留。在我们的BF16发布版本中，权重变化的中位数仅为单次舍入步长的1.5%，合并后约三分之二的更新范数丢失；当同一适配器合并至同模型的混合FP8/NVFP4发布版本时，93%的更新消失。我们不增加更新大小，而是直接在幸存部分上训练。我们引入了融合感知微调：标准LoRA仅在导出时对合并权重进行舍入，因此损失从未看到舍入后的权重。我们则在每个训练步骤中将基础权重与适配器更新之和舍入至发布格式，保留厂商量化尺度，并在此基础上计算损失。前向传播匹配服务引擎数值，梯度直接穿过舍入操作，因此我们训练的模型即为交付的模型。损失仅优化模型的行为，保持其推理接近基线模型。应用于Nemotron-3.5时，该方法在BF16和NVFP4发布版本中均提升了代理式编程性能，Terminal-Bench 2.1上最高提升8.4分，并公开发布了两个模型。
👤 Sandeep Singh Sandha, Prann Bansal, Arvind Sundararajan 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Marketing-Bench: Evaluating Frontier Models and Agents on Real-World Marketing Capabilities
Marketing-Bench：评估前沿模型与智能体在真实世界营销能力上的表现
As frontier AI models and agents are deployed more broadly in the real world, marketing is emerging as one of their largest applications. Marketing teams use AI models to produce brand assets like website copy and social media posts, while consumers' agents autonomously discover products, research purchases, and navigate websites. However, evaluating marketing capability is difficult because unlike areas like software development, customer support, or legal review where ground truth is more objectively defined, marketing is subjective, and output quality is judged based on taste and brand. We introduce Marketing-Bench, the first open-source marketing benchmark, which pairs discriminative tasks (detecting brand anomalies) with generative tasks (remediation), while keeping answers verifiable. Marketing-Bench tests five key pillars: factuality, consistency, coherence, structure, and brand voice, all crucial elements for good brand content on the web. We find that frontier models are far from solving the benchmark: the average model corrects only about half of the introduced issues (53.1%), whereas a marketing expert corrects nearly all of them. Even the strongest, GPT-6 Astra, reaches only 74.8%, with Claude Opus and Fable, Gemini Argon, and GPT-6 Sol falling well short. These findings suggest that marketing capabilities are less mature than adoption implies, and that principled evaluation should precede further scaling of usage. Marketing-Bench is a first step toward "frontier marketing models" with superhuman marketing performance.
中文翻译
随着前沿AI模型与智能体在现实世界中更广泛部署，营销正成为其最重要的应用场景之一。营销团队利用AI模型生成品牌资产，如网站文案和社交媒体帖子；而消费者代理则自主发现产品、研究购买并导航网站。然而，评估营销能力具有挑战性，因为与软件开发、客户服务或法律审查等有明确客观真值的领域不同，营销具有主观性，输出质量取决于品味与品牌调性。我们提出Marketing-Bench，首个开源营销基准，结合判别任务（检测品牌异常）与生成任务（修复），同时确保答案可验证。Marketing-Bench测试五大核心维度：事实性、一致性、连贯性、结构和品牌声音，这些都是网络优质品牌内容的关键要素。我们发现，前沿模型远未解决该基准：平均模型仅修正了约一半引入的问题（53.1%），而营销专家几乎全部修正。即使最强的GPT-6 Astra也仅达到74.8%，Claude Opus、Fable、Gemini Argon和GPT-6 Sol均显著落后。这些发现表明，营销能力的实际成熟度低于其广泛应用所暗示的程度，且应在进一步扩大使用前进行严谨评估。Marketing-Bench是迈向‘前沿营销模型’——具备超人类营销表现——的第一步。

TableRouter: Query-Aware Evidence Construction for Long-Table Question Answering
TableRouter：面向长表问答的查询感知证据构建
LLMs can reason over tables, but when tables outgrow the context budget, retrieving relevant rows is only part of the solution. A retrieved subset may omit information required for an exact aggregate; even complete evidence may remain difficult for an LLM to use. We introduce TableRouter, a training-free framework that analyzes each query, then chooses between two routes: a computation route, which derives question-conditioned statistics from the full table, and a coverage route, which retrieves relevant rows and column summaries. The chosen context is passed to a frozen LLM in one call. Across three LLMs and three large-table benchmarks, TableRouter outperforms the strongest of six single-call baselines in all nine settings by 1.2-18.3 accuracy points, using 77-95% fewer estimated FLOPs than truncated-table prompting, with indexing amortized across questions. The gains extend to long tables from databases not used in earlier experiments, where TableRouter achieves 35.8% with Qwen2.5-7B, versus 16.4% for the strongest external baseline. A controlled experiment shows that explicitly providing computed statistics improves accuracy by up to 40.0 points, even when all answer-determining rows are available. These results distinguish evidence access from evidence use: long-table reasoning depends on how information is represented, not only on which rows are retrieved.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLM）能够对表格进行推理，但当表格超出上下文预算时，仅检索相关行只是部分解决方案。即使获取了完整的证据，LLM也可能难以有效利用这些信息。我们提出了TableRouter，一种无需训练的框架，它分析每个查询后选择两条路径之一：计算路径，从完整表格中推导出与问题相关的统计信息；覆盖路径，检索相关行和列摘要。所选上下文一次性传递给冻结的LLM。在三个LLM和三个大型表格基准测试中，TableRouter在所有九种设置下均优于六种单次调用基线，准确率提升1.2-18.3个百分点，且估计FLOPs比截断表格提示减少77%-95%，索引成本可跨问题分摊。该优势也扩展到先前实验未使用的数据库中的长表格，TableRouter在Qwen2.5-7B上达到35.8%准确率，而最强外部基线仅为16.4%。受控实验表明，显式提供计算出的统计信息可使准确率提升高达40.0分，即使所有决定答案的行均已可用。这些结果揭示了证据获取与证据使用之间的区别：长表推理依赖于信息的表示方式，而不仅仅是检索了哪些行。
👤 Anahita Bolourani, Xiaofeng Lin, Tung Sum Thomas Kwok, Guang Cheng 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Subliminal Learning Crosses Modalities
隐性学习跨越模态
Subliminal learning refers to a surprising phenomenon in language models wherein a prompt given to one model (e.g., preference for cats) can be subliminally distilled to another model via training on data that is ostensibly meaningless (e.g., number sequences). This paper shows that text-based subliminal learning transfers cross-modally, in particular, a topic/preference (e.g., animals) transferred through number sequences across vision-language models also changes how images are generated and represented. First, we show that a diffusion model using the student as its text encoder generates the student's animal more often than with the original model as its text encoder, but not its color or style. We then investigate the mechanism behind this phenomenon and which concepts transfer across modalities. A constant component of the student's change accounts for much of the shift of image representations toward the trait. Adding this component uniformly can change the generated animal, which depends on the whole prompt, but not concepts such as color or style, which are encoded in a single token. Finally, we introduce weight lens as an approach to identify a subliminal trait from the weights of a single trained model, and show that it identifies the trait of nearly every student, including traits that do not surface in generated images.
中文翻译
隐性学习是指语言模型中一种令人惊讶的现象：一个模型（如偏好猫）接收到的提示可通过看似无意义的数据（如数字序列）被隐性地蒸馏到另一个模型中。本文表明，基于文本的隐性学习可以跨模态迁移，特别是通过数字序列在视觉-语言模型之间传递主题/偏好（如动物），也会改变图像的生成和表征方式。首先，我们发现使用学生模型作为文本编码器的扩散模型生成学生模型的动物的概率高于使用原始模型作为文本编码器的情况，但不会影响颜色或风格。随后我们研究了这一现象背后的机制以及哪些概念可在模态间转移。学生模型变化中的恒定成分解释了图像表征向该特征偏移的大部分原因。统一添加该成分可改变生成的动物（取决于整个提示），但不会影响颜色或风格等由单个标记编码的概念。最后，我们引入权重透镜（weight lens）作为一种方法，从单一训练模型的权重中识别隐性特征，并证明其能识别几乎所有学生的特征，包括那些在生成图像中不显现的特征。
👤 Saraswathy Amjith, Kimia Hamidieh, Antonio Torralba, Yoon Kim 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Learning Compact State Abstractions for LLM Agents
学习大语言模型智能体的紧凑状态抽象
LLM agents commonly use the full interaction history as a proxy for state, retaining all past observations and actions regardless of whether they remain relevant. We instead ask what information an agent actually needs to carry forward. We define agent state as a compact abstraction of interaction history that preserves the competence of a fixed reference agent, with the minimal such abstraction as the ideal. Because matching the reference agent's behavior is unnecessarily restrictive and difficult for black-box policies, we define sufficiency through task performance rather than behavior matching. We represent state abstractions as deterministic recursive programs and search over them using an LLM-guided genetic algorithm. Across ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, the learned abstractions preserve near-reference task success while reducing mean policy input by up to 71% and 57%, respectively. The resulting programs are directly inspectable, providing an explicit account of what information is retained, updated, and discarded during interaction.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体通常将完整的交互历史作为状态的代理，保留所有过去的观察和动作，无论它们是否仍相关。我们提出的问题是：智能体实际上需要携带哪些信息？我们将智能体状态定义为交互历史的紧凑抽象，该抽象需保持固定参考智能体的能力，最小的此类抽象即为理想状态。由于与参考智能体行为完全匹配对黑箱策略而言过于严格且困难，我们通过任务表现而非行为匹配来定义充分性。我们将状态抽象表示为确定性的递归程序，并使用LLM引导的遗传算法进行搜索。在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld两个环境中，所学抽象在几乎保持参考任务成功率的同时，分别将平均策略输入减少了71%和57%。所得程序具有可直接检查性，明确揭示了交互过程中哪些信息被保留、更新和丢弃。

SwarmLeak: Does Coordination Improve Privacy Attacks on LLM Agents?
SwarmLeak：协作是否提升对大语言模型智能体的隐私攻击效果？
LLM agents increasingly collaborate in group chats while holding tool access to confidential records, which makes the shared chat an attack surface. It remains unclear whether several colluding attackers extract such information more effectively than one. We introduce SwarmLeak, a red-teaming dataset of 24 synthetic group-chat scenarios across healthcare, law, finance, and software engineering, where attackers pose as colleagues to make a victim agent post protected fields despite an explicit confidentiality policy. With GLM 5.2 attacking five victim models through seven conversational strategies, we compare one attacker with a swarm of four sharing a private channel, matching scenarios, strategies, and a 200,000-token budget that also covers swarm coordination. Swarms show no advantage: a single attacker matches or exceeds them on every victim, and the share of runs leaking any field differs between conditions by at most 14.3 percentage points, whereas changing the victim shifts it from 0% to 99.4%. On the two most vulnerable models, nearly every run leaks under both conditions, but single attackers expose more of the record at first disclosure (85.4% vs. 68.1% of protected fields on Nemotron 3.5 Lightning). Four of the five victims leaked despite the policy. GLM 5.2 retrieved protected data in 64.3% of individual-attack runs and leaked in 0.6%: retrieval does not imply disclosure, but in high-stakes settings even one leaked field can identify a patient or client. At equal budgets, a larger red team is not automatically a stronger one; the victim model matters far more.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体越来越多地在群聊中协作并访问敏感记录，这使得共享聊天成为攻击面。目前尚不清楚多个共谋攻击者是否比单个攻击者更有效地提取此类信息。我们提出了SwarmLeak，一个包含24个合成群聊场景的红队数据集，涵盖医疗、法律、金融和软件工程领域，攻击者伪装成同事，诱使目标智能体在明确保密政策下发布受保护字段。使用GLM 5.2通过七种对话策略攻击五个目标模型，我们比较了单个攻击者与四个共享私密通道的攻击者群体的表现，条件包括相同场景、策略和20万token预算，后者还包含群体协调成本。结果显示，群体并无优势：单个攻击者在所有目标上表现相当或更优，且泄露任意字段的比例差异不超过14.3个百分点；而更换目标模型则使泄露率从0%升至99.4%。在最脆弱的两个模型上，两种条件下几乎每次运行都会发生泄露，但单个攻击者首次披露时暴露的信息更多（Nemotron 3.5 Lightning上分别为85.4% vs 68.1%的受保护字段）。五名受害者中有四名在政策存在的情况下仍泄露数据。GLM 5.2在单独攻击中检索到受保护数据的比例为64.3%，但仅0.6%发生泄露：检索不等于披露，但在高风险场景中，哪怕一字段泄露也可能识别患者或客户。在相同预算下，更大的红队并不自动更强；目标模型的影响远大于团队规模。
👤 Asma Farajidizaji, Vyas Raina, Vatsal Raina, Ivaxi Sheth 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Byte Patches versus BPE at Matched Data: A Matched-Twin Comparison at 350M Parameters
字节块与BPE在匹配数据下的对比：3.5亿参数下的配对孪生比较
We compare a MEGABYTE-style byte-patch Transformer with a 64-byte sliding local decoder (local64) to a matched byte-level 8k BPE decoder (BPE-8k) under identical raw bytes, document order, and approximately matched bytes per optimizer step: 350M parameters and 10 billion training bytes. Under matched-span document sliding (identical scored byte ranges), BPE-8k leads with relative gaps of -5.2%, -4.9%, -9.3% and -6.1% in bits per byte (overall, text, code, math; paired bootstrap). Downstream, CharBench equal fine-tuning favors local64 (53.7% vs 40.0%), fact200 favors BPE-8k (16.5% vs 7.0%), and PIQA (raw and length-normalized) and ARC-Easy length-normalized favor BPE-8k after Holm correction at alpha = 0.05; remaining tasks are ties. Evaluation pitfalls that changed conclusions include context-length extrapolation past the BPE training context, bits per token reported as bits per byte, and inconsistent byte denominators. Unlike controlled tokenizer studies that vary the subword tokenizer under a fixed token budget, or FLOP-controlled byte-model scaling studies, we hold the raw bytes, their order and the optimizer recipe fixed and ask whether a fixed-patch byte model can replace the tokenizer at all. Limits: one size, one seed, one 8k vocabulary fixed before the runs, and a known difference in embedding initialization between the twins.
中文翻译
我们在相同的原始字节、文档顺序和近似每优化步字节数（3.5亿参数，100亿训练字节）下，将一种类似MEGABYTE的字节块Transformer（local64）与一个匹配的字节级8k BPE解码器（BPE-8k）进行比较。在匹配跨度文档滑动（相同评分字节范围）条件下，BPE-8k在比特/字节指标上领先，相对差距分别为-5.2%、-4.9%、-9.3%和-6.1%（总体、文本、代码、数学；配对bootstrap检验）。下游任务中，CharBench等微调任务更青睐local64（53.7% vs 40.0%），fact200更支持BPE-8k（16.5% vs 7.0%），PIQA（原始和长度归一化）及ARC-Easy长度归一化任务在Holm校正后（α=0.05）也支持BPE-8k；其余任务为平局。评估陷阱导致结论改变的因素包括：超出BPE训练上下文的上下文长度外推、将比特/词报告为比特/字节、以及不一致的字节分母。不同于控制分词器研究中在固定词元预算下改变子词分词器，或FLOP控制的字节模型缩放研究，我们固定原始字节、其顺序和优化器方案，探讨固定块大小的字节模型能否完全替代分词器。局限性：单一尺寸、单一种子、一个在运行前固定的8k词汇表，以及双胞胎模型间已知的嵌入初始化差异。

TACT-Bench: Testing Adaptive Conversational Threats against Tool-Use Agents
TACT-Bench：针对工具使用智能体的自适应对话威胁测试
The growing autonomy of AI agents in using external tools and making decisions has created an urgent need for comprehensive safety benchmarks. Existing benchmarks test compromised tool channels or adaptive multi-turn attacks, though they may not capture the unique capabilities of policy-governed tool-use agents, which must resist unauthorized requests without abandoning the legitimate work embedded in the same interaction. To fill this gap, we introduce TACT-Bench, a stateful benchmark built around scenario-specific stories, personas, manipulation tactics, and response-adaptive escalation. Each adversarial task targets an action explicitly prohibited by the policy provided to the assistant, while the policy remains hidden from the attacking user. TACT pairs each adversarial task with at least one benign counterpart, and its adversarial tasks retain authorized ground-truth actions when a compliant resolution remains available. We define these base tasks as TACT-Base. We additionally introduce TACT-TrustPrime, which places an independent benign task before an unrelated attack in the same conversation. We also propose Paired Selective Trustworthiness (PST), which combines explicit safe refusal with demonstrated capability on matched benign tasks, to analyze an agent's overcautiousness and practicality. Averaged across 6 different models as assistants, TACT-Base raises Attack-Induced Failure Rate (AFR) by 17.53% over four compatible attack baselines, and TACT-TrustPrime raises it by a further 17.01%. The two evaluated defense methods reduce AFR by only 8.35%--9.41% on average and do not consistently improve PST, underscoring the need for stronger policy-enforcement methods.
中文翻译
AI智能体在使用外部工具和做出决策方面的自主性日益增强，迫切需要全面的安全基准测试。现有基准主要测试被攻陷的工具通道或自适应多轮攻击，但可能无法捕捉到受策略控制的工具使用智能体的独特能力——这些智能体必须在不放弃同一交互中嵌入的合法任务的前提下，抵御未经授权的请求。为填补这一空白，我们提出了TACT-Bench，一个基于场景特定故事、人物设定、操纵策略和响应自适应升级的状态化基准。每个对抗性任务都针对助手政策明确禁止的动作，而攻击者无法获知该政策。TACT为每个对抗性任务至少配备一个良性对照任务，且当合规解决方案仍可用时，其对抗性任务保留授权的真实动作。我们将这些基础任务定义为TACT-Base。我们还引入了TACT-TrustPrime，即在同一对话中，在无关攻击前放置一个独立的良性任务。我们提出配对选择性可信度（PST），结合显式安全拒绝与在匹配良性任务上的表现能力，以分析智能体的过度谨慎性和实用性。在6种不同模型作为助手的情况下平均，TACT-Base相较于四个兼容攻击基线使攻击引发失败率（AFR）提升了17.53%，TACT-TrustPrime进一步提升了17.01%。两种评估的防御方法仅平均降低AFR 8.35%–9.41%，且未持续提升PST，凸显了更强策略执行方法的必要性。
👤 Ilia Hashemi Rad, Feijie Wu, Haoyu Wang, Jing Gao 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

CATWAY: Cost-sensitive agentic three-way robot decisions with calibrated language-model confidence
CATWAY：基于校准语言模型置信度的成本敏感型三路机器人决策
A language-model robot agent must decide whether to execute, question or refuse an instruction, and an unsafe execution costs far more than an unneeded question. No prior robot gate derives all its thresholds from error costs or acts on calibrated probabilities, and none was fitted in one domain and evaluated unchanged in another. We propose CATWAY, a cost-sensitive three-way decision gate: a loss matrix over four instruction conditions (clear, ambiguous, infeasible, unsafe) fixes execute, ask and refuse regions on the probability simplex, the question being the information-gathering step. Its probabilities fuse a ten-sample vote with graded scores of ambiguity, infeasibility and risk by logistic regression. Fitted on household instructions and frozen, CATWAY was tested on text-level decisions of three small models in-domain, in two new robot domains and on the external TRUST-NAV benchmark. At eleven generations per decision against one, it was significantly cheaper than a direct prompt, a cost-aware prompt and an unguarded agent in all 18 household comparisons (mean loss per item 0.28 against 0.57-0.83), in 9 of 18 in the new domains and in 10 of 18 on TRUST-NAV (Holm per set), and never significantly costlier. It executed none of the 142 unsafe test instructions. On a unanimous vote, the minimum-expected-loss rule reduces calibration to one loss-derived threshold, so for two of six model configurations a gate on the vote alone never executed a clear instruction, costing 0.49 per item on TRUST-NAV against CATWAY's 0.13 and 0.09; the fused estimate avoided this on every set.
中文翻译
语言模型驱动的机器人智能体必须决定是否执行、质疑或拒绝指令，而错误执行的成本远高于不必要的提问。此前的机器人门控机制既未全部基于错误成本推导阈值，也未在单一领域拟合并在另一领域不变地评估。我们提出CATWAY，一种成本敏感的三路决策门控机制：在四种指令状态（清晰、模糊、不可行、危险）上的损失矩阵确定概率单纯形上的执行、询问和拒绝区域，其中询问是信息收集步骤。其概率融合了十次样本投票与逻辑回归得出的模糊性、不可行性和风险分级评分。在家庭指令上拟合并冻结后，CATWAY在三个小型模型的文本级决策中进行了测试，涵盖原域、两个新机器人领域以及外部TRUST-NAV基准。在每项决策生成11次对比一次的情况下，它在所有18个家庭比较中显著更低成本（每项平均损失0.28，对比0.57–0.83），在新领域中18项中有9项，在TRUST-NAV中18项中有10项（Holm检验），且从未显著更昂贵。它成功执行了142条危险测试指令中的零条。在全体一致投票情况下，最小期望损失规则将校准简化为一个由损失推导出的阈值，因此在六种模型配置中的两种，仅基于投票的门控从未执行清晰指令，导致TRUST-NAV上每项损失0.49，而CATWAY仅为0.13和0.09；融合估计在所有设置中避免了这一问题。
👤 Erik Derner 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

E2SIM: Long-Horizon Simulation of Everyday Human Behavior with Evolving States
E2SIM：具有演化状态的日常人类行为长周期模拟
AI agents are increasingly expected to support users over long periods, across work, and daily life. This requires modeling not only who users are, but also how their goals, interests, activities, and interactions evolve over time. A challenge in longitudinal user simulation is maintaining consistency between these evolving states and the consequences of actions in a shared environment. We introduce E2SIM, a longitudinal user simulation framework that jointly models evolving actor states and a dynamic shared environment. E2SIM connects actor-state updates with shared-world changes through a feedback loop linking immediate actions, delayed outcomes, and actor-specific observations across work and life. This allows the consequences of earlier actions to shape later behavior, while preserving differences in what each actor knows. We further propose a suite of longitudinal trajectory diagnostics, measuring dynamic consistency, coherent event progression and collaboration, and non-degenerate trajectory development rather than static consistency alone. In one 60-day simulation, E2SIM produced more extracted event threads judged meaningful by our evaluator and fewer near-duplicate actions than four direct-generation baselines. The framework and evaluation methods provide a basis for studying longitudinal user simulation, with potential applications in training and evaluating AI agents that assist users over extended periods.
中文翻译
AI智能体正被期望在长时间跨度内支持用户，贯穿工作与日常生活。这要求不仅建模用户是谁，还需建模其目标、兴趣、活动和互动如何随时间演变。长期用户模拟的一个挑战在于保持这些演化状态与共享环境中行动后果之间的一致性。我们提出E2SIM，一种联合建模演化主体状态与动态共享环境的长期用户模拟框架。E2SIM通过反馈回路将主体状态更新与共享世界变化相连接，该回路关联即时行动、延迟结果以及跨工作与生活的情境观察。这使得早期行动的后果能够塑造后续行为，同时保留各主体所知信息的差异。我们进一步提出一系列长期轨迹诊断工具，衡量动态一致性、事件进展连贯性与协作性，以及非退化的轨迹发展，而非仅静态一致性。在一个60天的模拟中，E2SIM产生的被评估者认为有意义的事件线程数量多于四个直接生成基线，且近似重复动作更少。该框架与评估方法为研究长期用户模拟提供了基础，潜在应用于训练和评估长期协助用户的AI智能体。
👤 Ziwei Gong, Xiangyang Yin, Thai Quoc On, Lin Ai, Julia Hirschberg et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31582]

LatentLens: Rethinking Latent Interface in Multi-Agent Systems
LatentLens：重新思考多智能体系统中的潜在接口
Latent interfaces let agents in a multi-agent system communicate through continuous representations rather than decoded text. Prior work reports performance and efficiency gains, but neither separates latent communication from the agent harness nor examines its internal components, leaving its actual contribution unclear. We propose LatentLens, a framework that decouples the latent interface at two levels: externally from the agent harness, to measure its contribution to overall performance (RQ1), and internally into realignment compatibility, information accessibility, and propagation dynamics, to identify the benefits and limitations of each (RQ2). Across seven benchmarks with Qwen3 models, adding the latent interface to the same harness does not improve and can even substantially degrade accuracy. Internally, latent representations retain some domain information, but incompatible realignment can substantially degrade performance, and repeated latent steps can amplify representation-level failures. Thus, while latent representations may preserve information, they do not necessarily ensure effective communication, which also requires compatible realignment, information accessibility, and stable propagation.
中文翻译
潜在接口使多智能体系统中的智能体通过连续表示而非解码后的文本进行通信。先前研究报道了性能和效率的提升，但未将潜在通信与智能体框架分离，也未深入分析其内部组件，导致其实际贡献不明确。我们提出了LatentLens框架，从两个层面解耦潜在接口：外部上与智能体框架分离，以评估其对整体性能的贡献（RQ1）；内部上分解为对齐兼容性、信息可访问性和传播动态，以识别各部分的优势与局限（RQ2）。在七个基于Qwen3模型的基准测试中，将潜在接口加入相同框架并未提升性能，甚至可能显著降低准确率。内部分析显示，潜在表示保留部分领域信息，但不兼容的对齐可能导致性能严重下降，重复的潜在步骤会放大表示层面的失败。因此，尽管潜在表示可能保留信息，但并不保证有效通信，后者还需具备兼容的对齐、信息可访问性和稳定的传播机制。
👤 Ziqing Yang, Kewei Cheng, Mingjie Li, Yang Zhang 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

SwiftLoop: Selective Refinement for Adaptive Implicit Reasoning in Looped Transformers
SwiftLoop：用于循环Transformer中自适应隐式推理的选择性精炼
Looped Transformers advance implicit chain-of-thought reasoning through recurrent latent refinement. However, existing looped reasoners typically employ fixed-depth recurrence, updating all latent positions at every iteration and incurring redundancy when further refinement is unnecessary. We introduce \textsc{SwiftLoop}, an inference-time framework for selective recurrent refinement through position-wise freezing, enabling adaptive computation allocation across both recurrent depth and active width. SwiftLoop learns lightweight freezing policies through hindsight-guided supervision derived from two complementary criteria: K/V reusability at the internal representation level and functional impact at the output level. At inference time, these policies guide adaptive freezing based on observed hidden states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SwiftLoop achieves favorable accuracy--computation trade-offs over fixed-loop inference and adaptive baselines, with measured inference acceleration. Beyond implicit reasoning, we further demonstrate its applicability to recurrent prompt prefilling in general looped models.
中文翻译
循环Transformer通过递归潜在精炼推进隐式思维链推理。然而，现有的循环推理器通常采用固定深度的递归，在每次迭代中更新所有潜在位置，当进一步精炼不再必要时会造成冗余。我们提出了 extsc{SwiftLoop}，一种推理时的选择性递归精炼框架，通过位置级冻结实现计算资源的自适应分配，覆盖递归深度和活跃宽度。SwiftLoop通过来自两个互补标准的回溯引导监督学习轻量级冻结策略：内部表示层面的K/V可复用性与输出层面的功能影响。在推理时，这些策略根据观测到的隐藏状态指导自适应冻结。大量实验表明，SwiftLoop在固定循环推理和自适应基线之间实现了更优的准确率-计算权衡，并实现了可测量的推理加速。除隐式推理外，我们还展示了其在一般循环模型中的递归提示预填充中的适用性。
👤 Tianyi Zhao, Ke Wan, Chen Chen 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31427]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Learning What vs. Learning How: Decomposing Task and Interface Competence in Agentic Training
学习什么 vs. 学习如何：分解智能体训练中的任务与接口能力
LLM agents operate through harnesses that define system prompts, action formats, feedback loops, and resource constraints. A model may possess the knowledge needed to solve a task yet fail to express that knowledge through a particular harness. This raises a basic question: when a policy fails under a given harness, is it missing task knowledge or the competence to operate the interface? We distinguish two components of agentic competence: task competence, the knowledge and reasoning needed to solve a task, and interface competence, the ability to act on that knowledge through a given harness. Across four reasoning and agentic benchmarks, we show that harness-conditioned training yields vastly different outcomes depending on how these interact. Multi-harness distillation aggregates task knowledge from trajectories collected through different harnesses into a shared policy, but this knowledge is realised only when training preserves or improves competence on the inference-time interface. Otherwise, single-harness training can induce interference in interface competence, sometimes catastrophically collapsing performance on a held-out harness despite improvements in underlying task knowledge. To disentangle task and interface supervision, we introduce harness rehearsal: distillation on traces that carry minimal task knowledge while exercising the target interface. Cross-harness task supervision or rehearsal alone yields little gain, but their combination yields gains far larger than either: on BFCL V3 multi-turn with Qwen3-4B, they achieve 8.5% and 32.5% respectively, versus 58.8% together. Finally, on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is more robust than distillation to single-harness training: it does not substantially degrade performance on other harnesses, and interface competence acquired through multi-harness distillation persists through subsequent RL.
中文翻译
大语言模型智能体通过定义系统提示、动作格式、反馈循环和资源约束的“ harness”来运行。一个模型可能具备解决任务所需的知识，却无法通过特定 harness 表达该知识。这引发了一个基本问题：当策略在特定 harness 下失败时，是缺乏任务知识，还是不具备操作接口的能力？我们区分了智能体能力的两个组成部分：任务能力（解决任务所需的知识与推理）和接口能力（通过给定 harness 运用知识的能力）。在四个推理与智能体基准测试中，我们发现 harness 条件下的训练结果因两者交互方式而异。多 harness 蒸馏将不同 harness 收集轨迹中的任务知识聚合到共享策略中，但只有在训练过程中保持或提升推理时接口的能力时，这些知识才能被有效利用。否则，单 harness 训练可能导致接口能力干扰，有时甚至在保留 harness 上性能崩溃，尽管底层任务知识有所提升。为解耦任务与接口监督，我们引入 harness 重演：在仅携带少量任务知识但充分使用目标接口的轨迹上进行蒸馏。跨 harness 任务监督或重演单独作用效果有限，但二者结合可带来显著提升：在 BFCL V3 多轮任务中，Qwen3-4B 模型分别达到 8.5% 和 32.5% 的提升，联合使用则达 58.8%。最后，基于策略的强化学习（RL）相比蒸馏对单 harness 训练更具鲁棒性：不会显著降低其他 harness 上的表现，且通过多 harness 蒸馏获得的接口能力在后续 RL 中仍能持续保留。
👤 Chaeeun Lee, Hossein A. Rahmani, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Kexin Xu, Emine Yilmaz et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.30325]
agentic-rl
Verifiable On-Policy Distillation
可验证的在线策略蒸馏
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving language-model reasoning, but its supervision is inherently sparse: a sequence-level verifier reward is propagated only through sampled trajectories and provides no direct signal for alternative actions. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a complementary form of supervision by providing dense teacher feedback over the full next-token distribution, but this signal is not grounded in verified task outcomes. Simply co-optimizing the two objectives does not couple these signals: verifier feedback remains sparse and does not inform the dense teacher supervision. We introduce Verifiable On-Policy Distillation (VOPD), which transfers sparse verifier feedback into dense, verifier-grounded supervision. Our key observation is that the implicit-reward view of OPD places teacher supervision and verifier feedback in a common reward space while retaining a reward signal available for every next-token action. Under this view, VOPD projects verifier preference onto the teacher's implicit-reward direction and Rao--Blackwellizes the resulting signal over the full vocabulary, propagating verifier-grounded supervision to actions that were never sampled. The resulting objective can also be interpreted as adaptively extrapolating the distillation target according to teacher--verifier agreement. Across two teacher--student configurations and eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, VOPD consistently improves both final performance and learning efficiency over vanilla OPD and other on-policy distillation baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/juntongshi48/VOPD.
中文翻译
带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升语言模型推理能力的强大方法，但其监督信号本质上是稀疏的：序列级验证器奖励仅通过采样轨迹传播，无法为其他可能的动作提供直接信号。在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过在完整下一个词分布上提供密集的教师反馈，提供了互补的监督形式，但这种信号并未基于已验证的任务结果。简单地联合优化这两个目标并不能有效耦合这两种信号：验证器反馈依然稀疏，且无法指导密集的教师监督。我们提出了可验证的在线策略蒸馏（VOPD），将稀疏的验证器反馈转化为密集且基于验证结果的监督信号。我们的关键观察是，OPD的隐式奖励视角将教师监督与验证器反馈置于同一奖励空间中，同时保留了针对每个下一个词动作的奖励信号。在此视角下，VOPD将验证器偏好投影到教师的隐式奖励方向上，并对整个词汇表进行Rao--Blackwell化处理，从而将基于验证结果的监督传播至从未被采样的动作。该目标还可被解释为根据教师与验证器的一致性自适应外推蒸馏目标。在两种教师-学生配置和八个数学推理基准测试中，VOPD在最终性能和学习效率方面均持续优于原始OPD及其他在线策略蒸馏基线。代码已公开于 https://github.com/juntongshi48/VOPD。
👤 Juntong Shi, Stefano Ermon, Jure Leskovec, Minkai Xu 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv

[2611.33078]
FOL-Proof: Verifying LLM Text-to-Logic Translations via Deterministic Semantics
FOL-Proof：通过确定性语义验证LLM文本到逻辑的翻译
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to translate natural-language statements into first-order logic (FOL) to improve reasoning. However, LLMs are prone to errors, and so a semantically unfaithful FOL formula could corrupt downstream reasoning. We present FOL-Proof, a semantic firewall pipeline that validates LLM-generated FOL translations against independently derived linguistic interpretations. FOL-Proof operates by deterministically obtaining a pool of candidate translations via semantic analysis tools, such as the English Resource Grammar and Minimal Recursion Semantics; then applying an SMT solver to check each derived reading for logical equivalence to the LLM-generated formula. If the translation is faithful it is accepted; otherwise, it is rejected, or optionally repaired via repeated invocations to the LLM. The procedure is conservative: incomplete search, unsupported constructions, or inconclusive checks yield abstention rather than false rejection. We evaluated FOL-Proof on established benchmarks (ProofWriter, RuleTaker and PrOntoQA), and also on a new adversarial benchmark (LongFaithCheck). Our experiments show that FOL-Proof attains near perfect selective accuracy, while direct LLM judges and round-trip baselines show substantially worse performance.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于将自然语言陈述翻译为一阶逻辑（FOL），以提升推理能力。然而，LLMs容易出错，语义不忠实的FOL公式可能破坏下游推理。我们提出FOL-Proof，一种语义防火墙管道，通过独立推导的语言学解释验证LLM生成的FOL翻译。FOL-Proof通过语义分析工具（如英语资源语法和最小递归语义）确定性地获取一组候选翻译；然后使用SMT求解器检查每个推导出的解读是否与LLM生成的公式逻辑等价。若翻译忠实则接受，否则拒绝，或通过重复调用LLM选择性修复。该过程保守：搜索不完整、不支持的构造或结论不确定时，选择不判断而非错误拒绝。我们在现有基准（ProofWriter、RuleTaker和PrOntoQA）以及一个新的对抗性基准（LongFaithCheck）上评估了FOL-Proof。实验表明，FOL-Proof实现了近乎完美的选择性准确率，而直接LLM评判者和往返基线表现显著较差。
👤 Yisrael Clark, Guy Katz 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.33050]
Worth the Wait? How Response Time and Streaming Shape Judgments of Generative AI Answers and Chatbot Engagement
值得等待吗？响应时间和流式传输如何影响对生成式AI回答及聊天机器人参与度的判断
Answers from generative AI chatbots built on large language models (LLMs) now arrive at very different speeds: reasoning models deliberate before replying, while response caches can return a stored answer at once. Blind preference votes, which rank these models, are read as judgments of their text, and if delivery matters, faster answers are expected to gain. Research on the labor illusion and on how text appears on screen suggests instead that an answer which unfolds as it is written is credited over the same answer shown at once. We test these accounts with three sources of variation in natural use. First, compar:IA, a French government chatbot arena, answered repeated prompts, by a random draw, either with a fresh answer streamed as it was written or with a stored earlier answer from the same model, shown at once. Within the same prompt, a replay lowered an answer's chance of winning a decisive blind vote by 25.8 percentage points. The same stored text won 57% of its decisive votes when streamed and 29% when replayed, and voters called it less complete, more superficial and more often incorrect when replayed. Second, provider congestion slowed streamed answers. In 324,029 conversations, slower answers earned no detectable net credit in the vote, but a slower first exchange made visitors less likely to ask a follow-up question. Third, in 4,378,514 WildChat replies from everyday ChatGPT use, replies slowed by congestion made users less likely to continue. Delivery moved blind votes, in the direction opposite to the one the arena's designers expected, while waiting cost follow-up questions that a comparison between two answers cannot register when both are slow. The results bear on how AI services cache and stream answers, how they budget latency, and how preference leaderboards evaluate models.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的生成式AI聊天机器人提供的答案现在呈现极不相同的响应速度：推理模型会在回复前进行思考，而响应缓存可立即返回存储的答案。盲选偏好投票将这些模型视为对其文本的判断，若交付速度重要，更快的答案理应获得青睐。然而，关于劳动幻觉以及文本在屏幕上呈现方式的研究表明，逐步展开的回答比一次性展示的相同内容更受认可。我们通过三种自然使用场景的变化来检验这些观点。首先，法国政府聊天机器人竞技场compar:IA，通过随机抽样，对重复提示以两种方式回应：一种是逐字流式生成的新答案，另一种是同一模型先前存储的答案一次性展示。在同一提示下，回放使答案赢得决定性盲选的概率下降25.8个百分点。相同存储文本在流式播放时赢得57%的决定性投票，而在回放时仅赢得29%，且投票者认为其更不完整、更肤浅、更常被判定为错误。其次，服务提供方拥塞导致流式回答变慢。在324,029次对话中，较慢的回答未获得可检测的净优势，但较慢的首次交互使访客更少提出后续问题。第三，在4,378,514条日常ChatGPT使用中的WildChat回复中，因拥塞而变慢的回复使用户更少继续互动。交付方式改变了盲选结果，方向与竞技场设计者预期相反，而等待代价则影响了后续问题的提出，这种差异在两个回答都慢时无法通过比较捕捉。研究结果涉及AI服务如何缓存和流式传输答案、如何权衡延迟，以及偏好排行榜如何评估模型。
👤 Zihan Chen, Di Zhu, Lei Nico Zheng 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32997]
Modelling Billingual Learning Through LLM Training: A Critical-Period Exploration
通过LLM训练建模双语学习：关键期探索
Critical-period accounts of second-language (L2) acquisition suggest that the ability to learn a new language may depend on how easily previously acquired first-language (L1) knowledge can be modified. This exploratory computational study extends a neural language-model approach to critical period effects by examining internal representations rather than behavior. A small two-layer GPT-2 model was trained on English and then on German (a relatively related L2) or Finnish (a relatively distant L2) through either Sequential training or Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), which limits change in parameters important for English. Mean-pooled hidden states for 20 held-out probe sentences per language were compared with those of the English-only model using cosine similarity at the embedding layer and two transformer layers after one and two L2 epochs. English similarity remained high in the transformer layers but became negative in the embedding layer. L2 similarity increased in the transformer layers, and German and Finnish differed more in the second transformer layer than in the first. EWC reduced Fisher-weighted parameter drift by about 6% but changed transformer-layer similarity by less than .001. With one training run per condition, these findings are preliminary.
中文翻译
第二语言（L2）习得的关键期理论认为，学习新语言的能力可能取决于先前掌握的第一语言（L1）知识被修改的难易程度。本探索性计算研究通过考察内部表示而非行为，扩展了神经语言模型方法对关键期效应的理解。使用一个小规模的两层GPT-2模型，先在英语上训练，再在德语（相对相关的L2）或芬兰语（相对遥远的L2）上进行顺序训练或弹性权重巩固（EWC）训练，后者限制对英语重要参数的改变。对每种语言各20个保留探测句的均值池化隐藏状态，使用余弦相似度在嵌入层及两个Transformer层（分别经过一、二个L2训练周期后）进行比较。结果显示，英语相似度在Transformer层中保持较高，但在嵌入层变为负值；L2相似度在Transformer层中上升，德语与芬兰语在第二个Transformer层中的差异大于第一个层。EWC使Fisher加权参数漂移减少约6%，但对Transformer层相似度的影响小于0.001。由于每种条件仅一次训练运行，这些发现尚属初步。
👤 Uriel Benymon, Tristan Whipps, Armita Ghobadi 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32889]
Joint Detection of Constructive Speech, Hate Speech and Agreement in Online Conversations
在线对话中建设性言论、仇恨言论与同意的联合检测
Online hate speech is an ever-growing issue with negative impacts on both groups and individuals, potentially causing users to withdraw from online conversations and debate. With some prominent social media platforms reducing their moderation efforts, constructive speech has emerged as an alternative strategy for countering hate. Automated detection of constructive and hateful speech can aid monitoring and improve understanding of their prevalence and online dynamics. In this work, we collect and annotate a large dataset of English and Danish social media comments for hateful tone, constructive tone, and agreement with parent comments. We fine-tune several multilingual LLMs to classify along these three dimensions and compare context-enriched and non-context-enriched model architectures. The E5-large LLM performs best on both the context-enriched (82% average F1) and non-context-enriched (81.6% average F1) classification tasks, demonstrating the potential of multilingual LLMs for scalable detection of hateful and constructive speech across languages.
中文翻译
在线仇恨言论是一个日益严重的问题，对群体和个人均造成负面影响，可能导致用户退出在线对话与讨论。随着一些主流社交媒体平台减少内容审核投入，建设性言论作为一种替代策略应运而生。自动化检测建设性与仇恨言论有助于监控并增进对其流行程度与在线动态的理解。本文收集并标注了一个大规模的英丹麦社交媒体评论数据集，涵盖仇恨语气、建设性语气以及对父评论的同意程度。我们微调多个多语言LLM以在这三个维度上进行分类，并比较上下文增强与非上下文增强模型架构的表现。E5-large LLM在上下文增强任务（平均F1达82%）和非上下文增强任务（平均F1达81.6%）中表现最佳，展示了多语言LLM在跨语言仇恨与建设性言论可扩展检测中的潜力。

Is Prosody Encoded in the Neural Audio Codec Mimi?
韵律是否编码在神经音频编解码器 Mimi 中？
Neural audio codecs (NACs) have become a fundamental infrastructure for modern speech-language models, discretizing continuous speech signals into token sequences that enable large language models to process and generate audio. Most research evaluates codec quality at the decoding phase, assessing the naturalness of reconstructed speech. However, little research investigates what phonetic information is captured during encoding. In this paper, we investigated the potential correlates of prosody in the NAC Mimi, a codec used by conversational speech agents such as Moshi and Sesame and used in downstream tasks such as speech-to-speech translation (Hibiki).
中文翻译
神经音频编解码器（NACs）已成为现代语音-语言模型的基础架构，将连续语音信号离散化为标记序列，使大型语言模型能够处理和生成音频。大多数研究在解码阶段评估编解码器质量，关注重建语音的自然度。然而，很少有研究探讨编码过程中捕获了哪些语音信息。本文研究了用于对话式语音代理（如Moshi和Sesame）以及语音到语音翻译（Hibiki）等下游任务的编解码器Mimi中韵律的潜在关联。

[2611.32778]
Beyond Fixed-Context Likelihood: Zero-Shot AI-Generated Text Detection via Context Perturbation Discrepancy
超越固定上下文似然：基于上下文扰动差异的零样本AI生成文本检测
The same increase in token log probability from earlier context can be typical at one position and unusually large at another. We introduce Context Perturbation Discrepancy (CPD), a zero-shot detector that measures this gain relative to what a single pretrained language model expects. Keeping the passage fixed, CPD scores each observed token under its full prefix and under a short window of recent tokens. Under the full-prefix distribution, the expected gain equals the KL divergence from that distribution to the restricted-prefix distribution. CPD subtracts this expectation from each observed gain and scales the sum of the signed deviations by its analytical reference standard deviation. On unedited human and AI texts from GEN, DetectRL-X, and HART, subtracting the expected gain raises AUROC by 32.78-35.81 percentage points at the same variance scale. CPD outperforms seven zero-shot baselines, averaging 94.80% AUROC and 79.60% recall at a 5% false-positive rate. On original and edited HART passages, the same scores separate texts more clearly by draft origin than by the benchmark's final-expression labels (89.15% versus 65.50% AUROC). Code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-tyf/CPD.
中文翻译
相同上下文带来的词元对数概率增加，在不同位置可能表现为正常或异常大。我们提出了一种名为上下文扰动差异（CPD）的零样本检测方法，通过测量该增益相对于单个预训练语言模型预期值的偏离程度来实现检测。在保持段落不变的前提下，CPD分别计算每个观察到的词元在其完整前缀和近期短窗口前缀下的得分。在完整前缀分布下，期望增益等于该分布与受限前缀分布之间的KL散度。CPD从每个观测增益中减去这一期望值，并将其标准化偏差之和按解析参考标准差进行缩放。在GEN、DetectRL-X和HART的原始人类与AI文本上，减去期望增益使AUROC提升了32.78至35.81个百分点，且在相同方差尺度下表现优于七个零样本基线，平均达到94.80% AUROC和79.60%召回率（假阳性率为5%）。在原始与编辑后的HART段落上，该方法在区分文本来源方面比基准的最终表达标签更清晰（89.15% vs 65.50% AUROC）。代码已公开于https://github.com/BUPT-tyf/CPD。
👤 Yifan Tang, Jingran Li, Yihao Wang, Zhen Yang, Jianyi Liu et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32631]
Generating Schema-Aligned Synthetic Dialogues and Measuring Their Diversity
生成结构对齐的合成对话并测量其多样性
Training or evaluating a model that turns a conversation into a structured record requires paired data, meaning a transcript together with the exact record it should be converted into. Such pairs are expensive to annotate, and in sensitive domains they are also difficult to obtain at all. We therefore build them in the opposite order by sampling a schema-valid record first, then writing a dialogue from it, and optionally synthesizing speech from the dialogue, so the label exists before the text and we need no annotation step. Naively prompting a language model for such dialogues yields small variations of a single conversation, so we add a sampled variation configuration, personas, and a topic plan that orders the fields of the record as explicit mechanisms for variation. We measure the variation that reaches the output through embedding space, where the embedding variance spreads over about two and a half times as many independent directions with our full configuration compared to without (participation ratio 23.3 against 9.2). Two corpora of medical dialogue give values of the same order, with sample size, text length and language being additional factors there. We also show the positive effect of our method in a downstream test, where an 8B extraction model reaches 64.8% field accuracy when fine-tuned on dialogues from our method, against 57.5% from naive generation. We release the generation library and the generated corpus of German clinical dialogues with aligned records.
中文翻译
训练或评估将对话转化为结构化记录的模型需要成对数据，即一段对话及其应转换为的确切记录。这类配对数据标注成本高昂，在敏感领域甚至难以获取。因此，我们反向构建：先采样一个符合模式的有效记录，再据此撰写对话，可选地从对话中合成语音，使标签先于文本存在，无需标注步骤。直接提示语言模型生成此类对话会得到大量重复的单一对话变体，为此我们引入采样变化配置、角色设定和话题规划，作为显式的变化机制。我们通过嵌入空间测量输出层面的变化程度，发现使用完整配置时嵌入方差分布在约2.5倍更多的独立方向上，参与度比率从9.2提升至23.3。两个医学对话语料库也显示出类似量级的结果，样本量、文本长度和语言是额外影响因素。我们在下游测试中展示了该方法的积极效果：在我们的方法生成的对话上微调的8B提取模型，字段准确率达到64.8%，优于朴素生成方法的57.5%。我们发布了生成库及德语临床对话与对齐记录的生成语料库。
👤 Konrad Stolte, Toni Fetzer 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32630]
MargEmbed-Akan: Bridging Twi Questions and English Knowledge through Cross-Lingual Score Distillation
MargEmbed-Akan：通过跨语言分数蒸馏连接蒂维语问题与英语知识
Most information sought online by Twi (Akan) speakers is written in English, making cross-lingual retrieval essential. We evaluate eight systems: BM25, five open embedding models, and two proprietary models (gemini-embedding-001 and OpenAI text-embedding-3-large) on two Twi-English tasks. AfriQA contains 1,221 natively written Asante Twi questions that must retrieve their answering English Wikipedia passages. Bible translation retrieval requires a Twi passage to retrieve its English translation from 6,195 candidates. We introduce MargEmbed-Akan, a 560M-parameter open query encoder trained from AfriE5-Large-instruct using cross-lingual score distillation. The frozen base model scores each English training question against its answer passage and seven hard negatives. The student learns to reproduce these scores from both an NLLB-200 Twi translation that passes a round-trip translation check and the original English question, preserving English behaviour. During training, passages are encoded by the frozen base model; at evaluation, MargEmbed-Akan encodes questions and passages. On AfriQA, base AfriE5 and gemini-embedding-001 both achieve 66.6 nDCG@10; EmbeddingGemma 2, Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B, and OpenAI text-embedding-3-large achieve 56.6, 51.3, and 53.4, respectively. MargEmbed-Akan achieves 71.5, outperforming gemini-embedding-001 by 4.9 points, its base model by 5.0, and OpenAI text-embedding-3-large by 18.1. Its largest gain is on Twi typed without E and O (+7.5 over gemini-embedding-001). English retrieval does not differ significantly from the base model, although both proprietary models score 1.5-2.2 points higher. On Bible translation retrieval, MargEmbed-Akan and gemini-embedding-001 are statistically tied on standard Twi (97.2 vs. 96.5 nDCG@10); MargEmbed-Akan performs better on diacritic-free Twi (92.7 vs. 84.9). No AfriQA data was used for training or model selection.
中文翻译
大多数蒂维语（阿坎语）使用者在线寻求的信息以英语书写，因此跨语言检索至关重要。我们在两个蒂维语-英语任务上评估了八种系统：BM25、五种开源嵌入模型，以及两种专有模型（gemini-embedding-001 和 OpenAI text-embedding-3-large）。AfriQA 包含1,221条原生撰写的阿桑蒂蒂维语问题，需检索对应的英文维基百科段落作为答案。圣经翻译检索任务要求从6,195个候选中检索出蒂维语段落的英文翻译。我们提出 MargEmbed-Akan，一个5.6亿参数的开源查询编码器，基于 AfriE5-Large-instruct 使用跨语言分数蒸馏训练而成。冻结的基模型对每个英文训练问题与其答案段落及七个困难负例进行评分。学生模型学习从 NLLB-200 的蒂维语翻译（通过往返翻译检查）和原始英文问题中重现这些分数，以保持英文行为。训练期间，段落由冻结基模型编码；评估时，MargEmbed-Akan 编码问题和段落。在 AfriQA 上，基模型 AfriE5 和 gemini-embedding-001 均达到66.6 nDCG@10；EmbeddingGemma 2、Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B 和 OpenAI text-embedding-3-large 分别为56.6、51.3 和53.4。MargEmbed-Akan 达到71.5，优于 gemini-embedding-001 4.9分，基模型5.0分，OpenAI text-embedding-3-large 18.1分。其最大增益出现在未使用 E 和 O 的蒂维语输入上（较 gemini-embedding-001 提升7.5分）。英语检索表现与基模型无显著差异，尽管两种专有模型得分高出1.5–2.2分。在圣经翻译检索任务中，MargEmbed-Akan 与 gemini-embedding-001 在标准蒂维语上统计上持平（nDCG@10分别为97.2 vs. 96.5）；在无变音符号的蒂维语上表现更优（92.7 vs. 84.9）。训练和模型选择过程中未使用任何 AfriQA 数据。
👤 Prince N. Y. Annan, Ali A. A. Aziz, Stephen E. Moore 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32614]
When "Neutral" Means Irrelevant: A Construct-Validity Audit of NormAd-ETI
当“中立”意味着无关：NormAd-ETI 构念效度审计
NormAd-ETI scores a model's judgment of everyday actions as acceptable, unacceptable, or neither as the cultural context shrinks from a specific rule to a country name. We audit what that three-way score measures. It combines two decisions: whether a supplied norm applies, and whether the action conforms to it. Separating them shows that when only a country is shown, applicability falls to near chance while conformity stays well above it, because the prompt withholds the rule a Neutral label depends on. Because one answer carries both decisions, the score follows the steadier conformity and hides the weaker applicability, and no single prompt recovers the two separately. Dedicated forced-choice probes separate them for frontier models but not for small open models, where the applicability judgment still tracks the hidden acceptability label. Adding a written rationale reduces that conflation without removing it. We recommend reporting the two results separately and measuring the conflation gap.
中文翻译
NormAd-ETI 评估模型对日常行为是否可接受、不可接受或中立的判断，随着文化背景从具体规则缩小至国家名称而变化。我们审计该三分类评分所衡量的内容。它实际上结合了两个决策：给定规范是否适用，以及行为是否符合该规范。分离这两个维度显示，当仅提供国家名称时，适用性降至接近随机水平，而符合性仍远高于此，因为提示中隐去了“中立”标签所依赖的规则。由于一个答案同时承载两个判断，评分结果跟随更稳定的符合性，掩盖了较弱的适用性。没有任何单一提示能单独恢复这两个维度。针对前沿模型，专门设计的强制选择探针可实现分离，但对小型开源模型无效，其适用性判断仍追踪隐藏的可接受性标签。添加书面理由可减少这种混淆，但无法完全消除。我们建议分别报告两项结果，并测量混淆差距。
👤 Aarav Singh, Animesh Pathak, Christian Hoang, Navyansh Singh, Avantika Singh 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.32525]
What If Chinese Were Latinized? A Counterfactual Study of Input Representation, Tokenization, and Language Modeling
如果中文被拉丁化？关于输入表示、分词与语言建模的反事实研究
If Chinese had been Latinized, as the Latinxua Sin Wenz movement proposed, what would it have meant for Chinese NLP? Homophones and tone marking complicate the answer. We convert Chinese corpora to syllable-separated Pinyin and train superBPE tokenizers from scratch. The resulting Pinyin vocabularies not only exhibit expected homophone collisions, but also show approximately 10% higher fertility than characters. An alternative BPE configuration changes the relative fertility of the two representations. We further pretrain Llama-style models on Chinese characters and Pinyin. The Pinyin models perform worse on per-character perplexity, homophone disambiguation, and overall ZhoBLiMP minimal-pair accuracy. An exploratory phonological probe yields near-chance performance for both representations. The broader implication is that written input representations are not merely neutral encodings. Choices for representations affect language modeling and their downstream performances. Latinization never actually happened for Chinese, but other languages did, such as Hangul and Latin script replaced character in Korean and Vietnamese; the cost we measure is a guide to where modeling effort for those languages should concentrate.
中文翻译
如果中文曾如拉丁化新文字运动所提议那样被拉丁化，会对中文自然语言处理产生何种影响？同音字和声调标记使答案复杂化。我们将中文语料转换为音节分隔的拼音，并从头训练superBPE分词器。结果发现，拼音词汇不仅表现出预期的同音冲突，其相对词频也比汉字高出约10%。另一种BPE配置改变了两种表示形式的相对词频。我们进一步在中文字符和拼音上预训练类似Llama的模型。拼音模型在每字符困惑度、同音歧义消解以及整体ZhoBLiMP最小对准确率方面表现较差。探索性语音学探针显示两种表示形式的表现接近随机水平。更广泛的启示是，书面输入表示并非仅仅是中立编码。表示选择会影响语言建模及其下游性能。中文从未真正实现拉丁化，但其他语言如韩文和拉丁字母已取代越南语和韩语中的汉字；我们所测量的成本可为这些语言的建模努力提供聚焦方向。
👤 Zijie Zheng, Ej Zhou 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

ArEssays: Towards Robust Arabic Handwritten Text Understanding in Educational Settings

Automated understanding of handwritten Arabic student essays remains challenging due to script complexity and high variability in children's writing. We introduce ArEssays, a large-scale dataset of authentic essays from primary to secondary students across Egypt, with both verbatim transcriptions and linguistically corrected versions. 中文翻译

[2611.31771]
SPARQL-Planned Single-Hop Traversal over a Live Knowledge Graph for Multi-Hop Question Answering
基于SPARQL规划的实时知识图谱单跳遍历用于多跳问答
Semantic retrieval struggles with multi-hop question answering on both recall and precision: gold passages are buried among distractors that degrade the reader's answer, and retrieving them requires reasoning over the query rather than matching it. GraphRAG techniques expand semantic retrieval by connecting passages to graph nodes and performing one or more hops of expansion after initial retrieval. This improves recall, but multi-hop expansion becomes expensive and brings additional noise into the retrieved set. We break this tradeoff by \emph{interleaving} dense retrieval with knowledge-graph traversal for complex multi-hop questions. Dense retrieval locates the first relevant regions of the search space at good recall, while traversal of a pre-existing external KG (Wikidata) restores precision and improves recall by confirming retrieved passages against a reasoning path through the KG. To keep traversal both question-adaptive and reliable, we have an LLM plan it as code generation task for graph traversal in SPARQL format but \emph{parse rather than execute} the query. On full 1{,}000-question splits under a shared corpus and reader, and with a single configuration applied identically to every dataset, we reach \textbf{62.5 / 75.1} EM/F1 on HotpotQA, \textbf{73.5 / 81.0} on 2WikiMultiHopQA, and \textbf{37.1 / 50.0} on MuSiQue, ahead of reproduced HippoRAG2, T\textsuperscript{2}RAG, and IRCoT on all three benchmarks. A second, token-matched setting stays ahead of T\textsuperscript{2}RAG on HotpotQA and 2Wiki while spending roughly its per-query budget. Executing the generated SPARQL instead of parsing it costs up to 11.4 EM, mostly because raw queries return empty results, not because of syntax errors.
中文翻译
语义检索在多跳问答任务中面临召回率和精确率双重挑战：真实答案段落被干扰项淹没，影响阅读器的判断；而检索这些段落需要对查询进行推理而非简单匹配。GraphRAG技术通过将段落连接到图节点并在初始检索后执行一次或多次扩展来拓展语义检索，提升了召回率，但多跳扩展成本高且引入额外噪声。我们通过将密集检索与知识图谱遍历相融合，打破了这一权衡。密集检索在良好召回率下定位搜索空间中的首个相关区域，随后对预存在的外部知识图谱（Wikidata）进行遍历，通过图谱中的推理路径验证检索到的段落，从而恢复精确度并进一步提升召回率。为确保遍历既适应问题又可靠，我们让大语言模型以代码生成任务形式规划SPARQL格式的图谱遍历，但仅解析而不执行该查询。在共享语料库和阅读器、单一配置应用于所有数据集的情况下，我们在HotpotQA上达到62.5/75.1的EM/F1，在2WikiMultiHopQA上达到73.5/81.0，在MuSiQue上达到37.1/50.0，优于复现的HippoRAG2、T²RAG和IRCoT。在第二个按标记匹配的设置中，仍优于T²RAG在HotpotQA和2Wiki上的表现，且每查询开销大致相当。若执行生成的SPARQL而非仅解析它，EM得分最高下降11.4，主要原因是原始查询返回空结果，而非语法错误。
👤 Priyesh Vakharia, Masud Moshtaghi, Pratyay Banerjee, Nicholas Jedema, Amita Misra et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv

Tablassert: Declarative Extraction of Knowledge Graphs from Biomedical Tables
Tablassert：从生物医学表格中声明式提取知识图谱
Tables published in biomedical research papers, particularly supplemental tables, capture knowledge assertions that may not be in the main text of the manuscript for several reasons, including low statistical significance, space limitations, and narrative scope. These tables include data (e.g., association statistics, assay readouts, curated gene lists, and omics) encoding relationships between biological entities. Tablassert converts such tabular data (Excel, CSV, TSV, Parquet) into knowledge graphs ready for the NCATS Biomedical Data Translator (Fecho et al. 2025). A user declares in YAML how each table's data map to subject-predicate-object-qualifier statements; Tablassert resolves free text (e.g., ''lung cancer'') to standard identifiers, attaches provenance and statistics, and emits graph files conforming to Translator standards (D. Unni, Bruskiewich, and Moxon 2026; D. R. Unni et al. 2022).
中文翻译
发表在生物医学研究论文中的表格，尤其是补充表格，包含了可能未出现在正文中的知识断言，原因包括统计显著性较低、篇幅限制以及叙述范围的局限。这些表格包含数据（如关联统计、检测读数、整理的基因列表和组学数据），编码了生物实体之间的关系。Tablassert 将此类表格数据（Excel、CSV、TSV、Parquet）转换为可供 NCATS 生物医学数据翻译器（Fecho 等，2025）使用的知识图谱。用户通过 YAML 声明表格数据如何映射为“主体-谓词-客体-限定符”语句；Tablassert 将自由文本（例如“肺癌”）解析为标准标识符，附加来源信息和统计数据，并生成符合翻译器标准的图文件（D. Unni, Bruskiewich 和 Moxon，2026；D. R. Unni 等，2022）。

PazaBench: Large-Scale ASR Benchmark for Low-Resource Languages in Africa
PazaBench：非洲低资源语言的大规模自动语音识别基准
Low-resource languages remain underrepresented in ASR benchmarks, limiting the ability to reliably assess and compare model performance. We present a large-scale benchmark for automatic speech recognition (ASR) on low-resource languages in Africa. The benchmark harmonizes six public corpora into a unified, reproducible evaluation framework and evaluates 52 state-of-the-art ASR models spanning three model architectural paradigms across 39 African languages on diverse datasets. Models are evaluated using standard accuracy and efficiency metrics, word error rate (WER), character error rate (CER), and inverse real-time factor (RTFx). This benchmark introduces architecture-aware comparison that systematically analyzes both individual models and broader model families. In addition, we quantify linguistic variance across language families, revealing how these factors shape ASR performance across diverse African languages. We also provide a comprehensive accuracy and efficiency trade-off analysis directly relevant to deployment in low-resource settings. Together, the benchmark, evaluation pipeline, and results support transparent, reproducible, and deployment-aware evaluation of ASR models for low-resource languages.
中文翻译
低资源语言在 ASR 基准中仍被严重忽视，限制了对模型性能进行可靠评估和比较的能力。我们提出了一个针对非洲低资源语言的大规模自动语音识别（ASR）基准。该基准将六个公开语料库统一整合为一个可重现的评估框架，并在 39 种非洲语言的多样化数据集上评估了 52 个最先进的 ASR 模型，涵盖三种模型架构范式。模型采用标准的准确率和效率指标进行评估，包括词错误率（WER）、字符错误率（CER）和逆实时因子（RTFx）。该基准引入了架构感知比较，系统分析了单个模型和更广泛的模型家族。此外，我们量化了语言家族间的语言差异，揭示了这些因素如何影响不同非洲语言上的 ASR 性能。我们还提供了直接与低资源环境部署相关的全面准确率与效率权衡分析。总体而言，该基准、评估流程和结果支持对低资源语言 ASR 模型进行透明、可重现且面向部署的评估。

[2611.31569]
CLAIMGATE: Cautious On-Device Checking of Spoken Legal and Clinical Claims
CLAIMGATE：对口头法律与临床声明的谨慎本地化检查
In law and medicine, a misstated rule can shape a decision before anyone has time to check it. Checking such claims in real time helps only if the checker keeps pace with conversation, keeps sensitive audio on the device, and stays silent rather than mislead. We present CLAIMGATE, an on- device assistant that listens to a conversation, detects checkable claims, retrieves the relevant official rule, and labels each claim as supported, contradicted or not covered, with a citation. CLAIMGATE is designed to fail towards silence: its 4-bit 7B- parameter language model may cite only retrieved rules, and the verdict is derived in code from the claim and rule the model quotes. We evaluate it on traffic stops and medical visits using real official rule text (5,742 Florida and Texas statute subsections; CDC, FDA and MedlinePlus guidance), real transcripts, live speech and author-written gold-labelled claims. On 141 held-out traffic-stop claims, CLAIMGATE reaches 80.1% accuracy and cuts serious errors (a true claim called false, or the reverse) from 20 to 5 compared with the same model without retrieval; deriving the verdict in code alone reduces them from 13 to 5 at no cost to accuracy. The claim gate passes 95.7% of held-out claims and rejects all 30 everyday sentences, and feedback arrives a median 1.86 s after a speech window closes on an 8 GB consumer GPU. On 148 allergy claims, with only the claim gate to rebuild, CLAIMGATE reaches 87.8% accuracy with 5 serious errors, against 20 without retrieval.
中文翻译
在法律和医学领域，一个错误陈述的规则可能在任何人有时间核查之前就影响决策。实时核查只有在检查器能跟上对话节奏、将敏感音频保留在设备上且保持沉默而非误导时才有帮助。我们提出了CLAIMGATE，一种本地化助手，能够监听对话，检测可核查的声明，检索相关官方规则，并根据引用对每个声明标注为支持、矛盾或未覆盖。CLAIMGATE设计为倾向于沉默：其4比特7B参数语言模型仅能引用检索到的规则，判决结果由代码从模型引用的声明和规则中推导得出。我们在交通拦截和医疗访问场景中使用真实官方规则文本（5,742个佛罗里达州和德克萨斯州法规条文；CDC、FDA和MedlinePlus指南）、真实转录文本、实时语音以及作者编写的真实标注声明进行评估。在141个保留的交通拦截声明上，CLAIMGATE达到80.1%的准确率，并将严重错误（真实声明被误判为虚假，反之亦然）从20例减少到5例，相比无检索的同一模型；仅通过代码推导判决进一步将错误从13例降至5例，且不影响准确率。声明门通过了95.7%的保留声明，拒绝了全部30个日常句子，反馈延迟中位数为1.86秒（在8GB消费级GPU上）。在148个过敏声明上，仅依赖声明门重建时，CLAIMGATE达到87.8%准确率，仅有5个严重错误，而无检索时则有20个。
👤 Peter Forcha, Harshitha Kaje Kusumadhara, Audrey Moguem Souop, Shalom Iboh 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31532]
Can LLM-Simulated Respondents Pre-Test Survey Questions? Evidence From Preregistered Prospective Validation
大型语言模型模拟受访者能否预测试题？来自预先注册前瞻性验证的证据
Large language models (LLMs) can be conditioned to simulate behavioral responses, including survey responses, but it remains unclear whether and how these simulations can support downstream research tasks. Here, we evaluate whether simulated respondents can support pre-testing new candidate survey questions before fielding them, operationalized as prioritizing candidate survey items. Specifically, we rank candidate questions by their mean responses, which can reveal ceiling or floor effects; their variance, which indicates whether responses contain meaningful variation; and differences between subpopulations, which indicate whether questions capture distinctions between relevant groups within a population. We introduce a preregistered, prospective evaluation that commits model predictions before target human responses exist, ruling out memorization of those responses by design. Using two waves of a local survey (2025: N = 1,271; 2026: N = 1,161), we built simulated respondents from 2025 participants and preregistered GPT-4.1 predictions for 22 questions introduced in 2026. Simulations accurately ranked questions by mean responses (Spearman \r{ho} = 0.71) and anticipated which questions distinguished city from county residents (\r{ho} = 0.88), Black from White residents (\r{ho} = 0.68), and employed from unemployed residents (\r{ho} = 0.57), suggesting simulations can help prioritize questions before fielding. More broadly, our prospective, preregistered design provides a general framework for evaluating AI simulations without contamination from memorized target outcomes.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）可以被设定以模拟行为反应，包括调查回应，但尚不清楚这些模拟是否以及如何支持下游研究任务。本文评估了模拟受访者是否能在实际投放前支持新候选调查问题的预测试，具体操作为优先排序候选调查项目。我们通过平均响应值对候选问题进行排序，以揭示天花板效应或地板效应；通过方差判断响应是否包含有意义的变化；通过不同子群体间的差异判断问题是否捕捉到人群中相关群体之间的区别。我们引入了一种预先注册的前瞻性评估方法，在目标人类响应出现前即承诺模型预测，从而从设计上排除了对这些响应的记忆。利用两轮本地调查数据（2025年：N=1,271；2026年：N=1,161），我们基于2025年参与者构建了模拟受访者，并预先注册了GPT-4.1对2026年引入的22个问题的预测。模拟结果准确地按平均响应值对问题进行了排序（Spearman ρ = 0.71），并成功预测了哪些问题能区分城市与县居民（ρ = 0.88）、黑人与白人居民（ρ = 0.68）以及就业与失业居民（ρ = 0.57），表明模拟可在实际投放前帮助优先排序问题。更广泛地说，我们的前瞻性、预先注册设计提供了一个通用框架，用于评估AI模拟而不受目标结果记忆污染的影响。
👤 Sristi Bafna, Mac MacComas, Michael Bader, Kristina Gligorić 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31496]
Reading Between the Layers: Where Representation Quality Lives, and What Geometry Reveals
在层间阅读：表示质量所在之处，几何学揭示的真相
Representation quality in pretrained text foundation models is not concentrated at the final layer, yet practitioners routinely probe it there because layer selection without labels has remained an open problem. We present a large-scale empirical study of where layer quality lives across 25 models spanning four architectural families and four pretraining strategies, evaluated on 13 datasets across classification, pair classification, semantic textual similarity, and multiple-choice question answering. Peak probing layers depend systematically on pretraining strategy and architecture: masked encoders peak in late-middle layers, sequence-to-sequence models shift their peak with task type, contrastive sentence encoders concentrate signal at the final layer under CLS and last-token readouts but peak early under mean pooling, and decoder-only models exhibit distinct phase structure tied to depth. We distill these findings into the Stable Geometry Index (SGI), a closed-form, fully label-free selector with no fitted coefficients combining power-law fit quality, effective-rank capacity, spectral stability, and a weak depth prior. Across 383 evaluation groups SGI improves over the last-layer baseline and lifts Hit@3 from 0.39 to 0.52, recovering 40% of the gap to the oracle without using any labels, and exceeds a supervised ELMo-style layer mix on the matched subset despite using strictly less information. Our ablations also reverse a common assumption in the spectral-decay literature: power-law fit quality is a stronger predictor of layer quality than the exponent's proximity to one.
中文翻译
预训练文本基础模型中的表示质量并不集中于最后一层，但从业者仍习惯性地在该层进行探测，因为无标签情况下的层选择问题一直未解。我们对25个跨越四种架构家族和四种预训练策略的模型进行了大规模实证研究，评估了13个数据集上的分类、成对分类、语义文本相似度和多项选择问答任务。峰值探测层系统性地依赖于预训练策略和架构：掩码编码器在中后层达到峰值，序列到序列模型的峰值随任务类型变化，对比句编码器在CLS和最后token读出时信号集中在最后一层，但在均值池化下则早期达到峰值；解码器-only模型表现出与深度相关的独特相变结构。我们将这些发现提炼为稳定几何指数（SGI），一种闭式、完全无标签的层选择器，无需拟合系数，结合幂律拟合质量、有效秩容量、谱稳定性及弱深度先验。在383个评估组中，SGI优于最后一层基线，将Hit@3从0.39提升至0.52，恢复了与理想oracle之间40%的差距，且未使用任何标签；尽管仅使用更少信息，其表现仍超过监督式ELMo风格层混合方法。我们的消融实验也推翻了谱衰减文献中的常见假设：幂律拟合质量比指数接近1的强弱更能预测层质量。
👤 Arnesh Batra, Aniket Khandelwal, Arush Gumber, Krish Thukral, Kushagra Agrawal et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31495]
ModernELECTRA: Replaced-Token Detection with Boundary-Window Reconstruction for Compact Encoders that Classify and Embed
ModernELECTRA：边界窗口重构的替换词检测，用于紧凑编码器的分类与嵌入
ELECTRA-style replaced-token detection (RTD) is a sample-efficient objective for pre-training text encoders, and RTD-trained models such as DeBERTaV3 are strong on classification benchmarks. The same models, however, are known to be poor backbones for embedding tasks such as retrieval and semantic similarity: the binary "original vs. replaced" signal does not ask the final layer to retain the token identity and contextual content that dense representations rely on. We introduce ModernELECTRA, a pre-training recipe that keeps RTD as the main objective and adds a lightweight auxiliary task, boundary-window reconstruction. For every token, we sample a window of 3-12 tokens that strictly contains it and reconstruct the token's original identity only from the discriminator's hidden states at the two window boundaries. The task costs one small FFN at training time and is discarded afterwards, yet it forces every final-layer state to carry information about its neighbourhood. Combined with a frozen, separately pre-trained generator, a temperature curriculum for hard negatives and a 512-to-8192 length curriculum, we train two compact encoders: EdgeCoder-31M, an 8-layer English model, and EdgeCoder-Multilingual-61M, a 16-layer model covering 46 languages, each on about 1T tokens. They reach 86.0 and 85.1 on GLUE. The English model outperforms Ettin-Enc-32m (83.5) and MiniLM-L12 (84.6) on GLUE while matching them on MTEB English v2 (51.1), and the multilingual model outperforms the 2.3x larger mmBERT-small on GLUE and Multilingual MiniLM and mDistilBERT on XTREME over the languages it covers. We also describe FlashDeBERTa, our open-source fused implementation of DeBERTa's disentangled attention that stores no $n \times n$ score matrix: at 4096 tokens it is 10x faster and uses 93% less memory than the reference implementation, and it loads existing DeBERTa checkpoints unchanged.
中文翻译
ELECTRA风格的替换词检测（RTD）是一种高效的预训练文本编码器目标，RTD训练的模型如DeBERTaV3在分类基准上表现优异。然而，这类模型作为嵌入任务（如检索和语义相似度）的骨干时表现较差：二元的“原始 vs. 替换”信号并未要求最终层保留词身份和上下文内容，而这正是密集表示所依赖的。我们提出ModernELECTRA，一种保留RTD为主目标的预训练方案，并引入轻量级辅助任务——边界窗口重构。对于每个词，我们采样一个严格包含它的3-12词窗口，并仅从判别器在窗口两端隐藏状态中重建该词的原始身份。该任务在训练时仅增加一个小FFN开销，之后丢弃，却迫使每个最终层状态携带其邻域信息。结合冻结的独立预训练生成器、硬负样本的温度课程和512至8192长度的课程，我们训练了两个紧凑编码器：EdgeCoder-31M（8层英文模型）和EdgeCoder-Multilingual-61M（16层多语言模型，覆盖46种语言），每款模型均在约1T词上训练。它们在GLUE上分别达到86.0和85.1分。英文模型在GLUE上优于Ettin-Enc-32m（83.5）和MiniLM-L12（84.6），在MTEB English v2（51.1）上与之持平；多语言模型在GLUE上优于2.3倍大的mmBERT-small，在XTREME上超越Multilingual MiniLM和mDistilBERT在覆盖语言上的表现。我们还介绍了FlashDeBERTa，我们开源的DeBERTa解耦注意力融合实现，不存储n×n得分矩阵：在4096个词时，速度是参考实现的10倍，内存使用减少93%，且可直接加载现有DeBERTa检查点。
👤 Mykhailo Shtopko, Ihor Stepanov, Dmytro Vodianytskyi, Oleksandr Lukashov 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31276]
Lexical Tone and Word Order Matter in Translation: Prosodic Correspondence Across Languages
词汇声调与词序在翻译中至关重要：跨语言的韵律对应关系
Prosody conveys emphasis, emotion, and intent, yet its correspondence across translation remains underexplored, especially when languages differ in lexical tone and word order. To our knowledge, we present the first systematic word-level analysis of prosodic correspondence in translated speech spanning tonal and non-tonal Asian languages, with comparisons to European-language findings and explicit analyses of lexical tone and word reordering. Building on prior evidence of fine-grained prosody correspondence in European-language pairs, we investigate how these linguistic factors relate to word-level pitch and energy correspondence between English and five languages: Mandarin Chinese, Thai, and Vietnamese, which have lexical tone, and Japanese and Korean as additional comparisons. Correspondence is generally lower than previously reported European-language estimates. Adjusting for lexical tone increases pitch correspondence for English-Mandarin Chinese, English-Thai, and English-Vietnamese. However, English-Japanese and English-Korean also exhibit lower correspondence than the European-language estimates, indicating that tonal status alone does not explain the observed pattern. Greater word reordering is associated with weaker local correspondence even under semantic alignment, suggesting that semantic alignment does not fully account for differences in prosodic organization. These findings characterize the limits of fine-grained acoustic correspondence across translation and motivate prosody modeling that accounts for lexical pitch constraints and the structural context of translated content.
中文翻译
韵律传达强调、情感和意图，但其在翻译中的对应关系仍研究不足，尤其当语言在词汇声调和词序上存在差异时。据我们所知，这是首个系统性的逐词分析，探讨涵盖声调与非声调亚洲语言的翻译语音中韵律对应关系，同时与欧洲语言研究结果进行比较，并明确分析词汇声调与词序重排的影响。基于先前关于欧洲语言对中精细韵律对应关系的证据，我们研究了这些语言因素如何影响英语与五种语言之间的词级音高和能量对应关系：普通话、泰语和越南语具有词汇声调，日语和韩语作为额外对照。总体而言，对应程度低于此前报道的欧洲语言估计值。调整词汇声调后，英语-普通话、英语-泰语和英语-越南语的音高对应有所提高。然而，英语-日语和英语-韩语的对应也低于欧洲语言估计值，表明仅靠声调状态无法解释观察到的模式。更大的词序重排与较弱的局部对应相关，即使在语义对齐的情况下也是如此，说明语义对齐并不能完全解释韵律组织的差异。这些发现刻画了翻译中精细声学对应关系的局限性，并推动了考虑词汇音高约束和翻译内容结构背景的韵律建模。
👤 Haopeng Xie, Ismail Rasim Ulgen, Shengying Zhao, Berrak Sisman, Philipp Koehn 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31267]
ToneMamaba: Learning Contextualized Tonal Contours with Boundary-Reset State Space Models
ToneMamaba：基于边界重置状态空间模型学习情境化声调轮廓
Mandarin tone recognition in connected speech is challenging because contextual and durational variation reshapes F0 contours. We propose ToneMamba, a boundary-aware state space model that replaces static boundary embeddings with bidirectional state resets at syllable, word, and IPU levels. Fixed weights fuse multi-level representations, while shared-weight bidirectional scans use channel-wise directional fusion to reduce parameters. Dynamic syllable pooling forms tonal contours, and rhythm-conditioned gating incorporates durational information. ToneMamba achieves 97.9% and 74.8% accuracy on FCU-VOICE-360 read and MCDC-8 conversational speech, outperforming the baseline (ToneNet) by 0.9 and 15.9 percentage points, respectively. Ablations show that word-level resets are particularly effective, while rhythm conditioning provides greater gains in conversational speech, underscoring the role of durational variation in spontaneous speech.
中文翻译
连贯语流中的普通话声调识别具有挑战性，因为上下文和持续时间变化会重塑基频（F0）轮廓。我们提出 ToneMamba，一种边界感知的状态空间模型，用双向状态重置替代静态边界嵌入，分别在音节、词和IPU层级进行。固定权重融合多层级表示，共享权重的双向扫描采用通道级方向融合以减少参数量。动态音节池化形成声调轮廓，节奏条件门控融入持续时间信息。ToneMamba 在 FCU-VOICE-360 朗读语音和 MCDC-8 对话语音上的准确率分别达到 97.9% 和 74.8%，相较于基线模型（ToneNet）分别提升 0.9 和 15.9 个百分点。消融实验显示，词级重置尤为有效，而节奏条件在对话语音中带来更大增益，凸显了持续时间变化在自发性口语中的重要作用。
👤 Po-Yu Chiu, Yi-Fen Liu 📅 2026-10-21 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31253]
Instantiation of Human Values in Image Generation
图像生成中人类价值观的实例化
Text-to-image (T2I) models are increasingly embedded in everyday visual culture, yet little is known about how they translate abstract human values into visual scenes. We address this gap by introducing a framework, grounded in Schwartz's theory of basic human values, for studying value instantiation: the process by which ideals such as success, care, or freedom are visually realized. Using person-centered prompts from 70 positive and negative value items, we generate 28,000 images across four state-of-the-art T2I models. We identify visual prototypes, recurring combinations of subjects, actions, and settings, and use them to quantify how narrowly models instantiate these values and how this breadth compares with human descriptions of the same items. We then map these prototypes onto a taxonomy of social configurations spanning setting, sociality, mode of engagement, and social register, revealing what is systematically foregrounded or omitted. Our results show that T2I models produce far more concentrated representations than humans do when describing the same values. They default to a hyper-individualistic, adult-centric worldview, reproducing value-specific associations, such as achievement tied to formal institutional settings or hedonism rendered as affluent consumption, while marginalizing everyday labor, relational care, and non-adult perspectives. As a proof of concept, we show that these structural gaps can guide a steering method that diversifies outputs without modifying the original prompt.
中文翻译
文本到图像（T2I）模型日益融入日常视觉文化，但人们对它们如何将抽象的人类价值观转化为视觉场景仍知之甚少。我们通过引入一个基于施瓦茨基本人类价值观理论的框架，研究价值观实例化过程——即成功、关怀或自由等理想如何被视觉呈现。利用来自70个正负价值项目的以人物为中心的提示，我们在四种最先进的T2I模型上生成了28,000张图像。我们识别出视觉原型，即主体、行为和场景的反复组合，并用其量化模型在多大程度上狭隘地实例化这些价值观，以及这种广度与人类对相同项目的描述之间的差异。随后，我们将这些原型映射到涵盖场景、社会性、参与方式和社会关系维度的社会配置分类体系中，揭示哪些内容被系统性突出或忽略。结果显示，T2I模型在描述相同价值观时产生的表征远比人类集中。它们默认采用一种高度个人主义、以成人为中心的世界观，再现特定价值关联，如成就与正式制度环境相关联，享乐主义表现为富裕消费，同时边缘化日常劳动、关系性关怀和非成人视角。作为概念验证，我们展示了这些结构性差距可用于引导一种无需修改原始提示即可多样化输出的调控方法。

[2611.32939]
One Style Fits All? Cultural Values Embedded in Conversational AI via a People-Pleasing Lens
一种风格适合所有人？通过讨好倾向视角探究对话式AI中的文化价值观
Conversational AI systems trained on large-scale web corpora inevitably encode the cultural values and interactional norms embedded in their training data, yet our understanding of how deployed LLMs reflect or reinforce culture-specific social expectations remains limited. This study examined how supportive versus challenging chatbot interaction styles shape user experience and continuance intention, and whether people-pleasing tendency (PPT) moderates these effects across cultures. Taiwanese (N = 49) and Korean (N = 52) participants completed a collaborative tourism-planning task. Results showed that: (1) supportive chatbots consistently led to higher continuance intention, satisfaction, and trust; (2) PPT did not moderate these effects; and (3) cultural variation emerged only in perceived threat, where higher PPT was associated with greater baseline threat in the Taiwanese but not the Korean sample. These findings reveal how a general-purpose LLM style may differentially activate culturally situated social scripts, raising implications for culturally inclusive conversational AI design.

[2611.32864]
Fresh Keys, Stale Secrets: Numeric Leakage across Linked LLM Prompts
新鲜密钥，陈旧秘密：跨关联提示中的数值泄露
Small businesses repeatedly ask cloud language models about the same confidential prices, unit costs and margins. Numeric prompt disguises for such numbers are evaluated one prompt at a time, but a provider sees a firm's history, in which each fresh secret gives a new, independent view of the same numbers. Since real costs are not public, we measure on synthetic firms with known values how much an honest-but-curious provider recovers when it knows the disguise algorithm, holds sector-level price and margin statistics, and links a firm's queries. In one prompt, per-field scaling, a fixed-axis instantiation of Zhang et al.'s order-preserving substitution and Pr$\varepsilon\varepsilon$mpt's released sanitizer keep the attacker's margin-hit advantage over a prior-only guess between -0.6 and +4.4 percentage points. After 16 linked queries with fresh secrets, every disguise we test (Pr$\varepsilon\varepsilon$mpt at $\varepsilon\geq 0.5$) has a target recovered significantly more often: under the order-preserving substitution, margin recovery rises from 25.0% to 59.4%. The same attacker recovers 29.4 percentage points more margins with fresh maps than with one reused map. Theory explains the contrast: one reused secret bounds all queries by a single full-precision view of the fields shown, whereas fresh scaling keys are identifiable. Except for Pr$\varepsilon\varepsilon$mpt at $\varepsilon=0.5$, the gains persist when the attacker's prior comes from another population model and when values drift by up to 5% between queries; on our firms, whose prose cues are weak, reading the prose with a language model adds at most 2.5 points. Code and data: https://github.com/Jamli58/fresh-keys-stale-secrets

小型企业反复向云语言模型询问相同的机密价格、单位成本和利润率。针对这些数值的提示伪装方法通常逐个评估，但服务提供商可查看企业的历史记录，每次新的秘密都提供了对相同数值的独立新视角。由于真实成本不公开，我们在已知数值的合成企业上测量了一个诚实但好奇的服务提供商在知晓伪装算法、掌握行业级价格与利润率统计并链接企业查询的情况下，能恢复多少信息。在单个提示中，按字段缩放（per-field scaling）——即Zhang等人提出的保序替换与Pr$εε$mpt发布的净化器的固定轴实例化——使攻击者相对于仅依赖先验猜测的边际收益在-0.6至+4.4个百分点之间。经过16次带有新密钥的关联查询后，我们测试的所有伪装方法（Pr$εε$mpt在$ε\geq 0.5$时）均显著更频繁地恢复目标值：在保序替换下，利润率恢复率从25.0%升至59.4%。同一攻击者使用新映射比重复使用旧映射多恢复29.4个百分点的利润率。理论解释了这一差异：重复使用一个秘密会以单一全精度视图约束所有查询，而新鲜的缩放密钥则可被识别。除Pr$εε$mpt在$ε=0.5$外，当攻击者的先验来自另一群体模型或查询间数值漂移不超过5%时，增益依然存在；在我们的企业中，其文本线索较弱，仅靠语言模型阅读文本最多增加2.5分。代码与数据：https://github.com/Jamli58/fresh-keys-stale-secrets

[2611.32782]
Measuring Layer-wise Intrinsic Dimensionality of FFNs in LLMs via PCA
通过PCA测量LLM中FFN的逐层内在维度
Modern large language models (LLMs) keep the feed-forward network (FFN) intermediate width identical across every layer, a convention inherited from the original Transformer. Yet mechanistic work suggests layers at different depths do qualitatively different work, raising a natural question: are their capacity demands also non-uniform, and if so, can a fixed parameter budget be allocated more wisely than uniformly? To probe this, we apply principal component analysis (PCA) to the input of each FFN down-projection and measure, for every layer, its intrinsic dimensionality as the smallest number of principal components needed to reconstruct the activation up to a fixed cosine similarity. Scanning 58 open-weight LLMs spanning 14 families and 70M to 72B parameters, we find a recurring middle-heavy profile in which middle layers carry higher intrinsic dimensionality than edge layers; the profile is robust to the calibration corpus, even across English and Chinese, and is largely preserved through post-training fine-tuning. As a direct test that this signal is structurally meaningful, we use it to guide width pruning in a knowledge-distilled student under a matched parameter budget: the PCA-guided allocation outperforms the uniform-width convention by +2.3 points on Qwen3-8B and +2.5 on Qwen2.5-3B, while lower-dispersion teachers show smaller gaps (+0.9 on Gemma-3-4B, +0.5 on LLaMA-3-8B), and a budget-matched inverted control does worst on all four teachers. Across these four teachers, the size of the gap tracks how unevenly the teacher's intrinsic dimensionality is distributed across layers (a single-number summary we call $σ_{99}$), and the advantage persists through downstream supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/naver-ai/lidf.
中文翻译
现代大型语言模型（LLMs）在每一层中保持前馈网络（FFN）的中间宽度一致，这一惯例源自原始Transformer架构。然而机制研究指出，不同深度的层执行定性不同的任务，这引发了一个自然问题：它们的容量需求是否也不均匀？如果如此，能否比均匀分配更智慧地分配固定的参数预算？为此，我们对每个FFN下投影的输入应用主成分分析（PCA），并测量每层的内在维度，即重建激活至固定余弦相似度所需的最少主成分数量。通过对58个开源权重LLM（涵盖14个系列，参数量从70M到72B）的扫描，我们发现一种反复出现的‘中间偏重’模式：中间层的内在维度高于边缘层；该模式在不同校准语料库下均稳健存在，即使在英文和中文之间也基本保持不变，并且在后续微调过程中大部分得以保留。作为对该信号结构意义的直接验证，我们使用其指导知识蒸馏学生模型在相同参数预算下的宽度剪枝：基于PCA的分配方式在Qwen3-8B上优于均匀宽度惯例2.3分，在Qwen2.5-3B上提升2.5分；而低分散教师模型的差距较小（Gemma-3-4B为+0.9，LLaMA-3-8B为+0.5），且预算匹配的反向对照表现最差。在四个教师模型中，差距大小与教师内在维度在层间的分布不均衡程度（我们称之为$σ_{99}$的单数值总结）呈正相关，且优势在下游监督微调中持续存在。代码已公开于https://github.com/naver-ai/lidf。
