[2611.32050]
Do Clinician-Scored Examples Help LLMs Code ADOS-2 from Hour-Long Transcripts?
临床评分示例是否有助于大模型从一小时转录中编码ADOS-2？
Large language models can apply a written rubric to a transcript, making automated coding of the Autism Diagnostic Observation Schedule (ADOS-2) look within reach. A common way to align such a rater with clinicians is to show it expert-scored examples, but whether correct labels improve agreement is rarely tested. We test this for eight speech-based items of hour-long ADOS-2 Module 3 sessions from 48 children, with one local model and a pre-registered, placebo-controlled design. We compare few-shot scoring, an anchored comparison in which the model only judges a child better, similar or worse than scored anchors and a fixed rule gives the score, post-hoc calibration, protocol-based evidence routing and direct scoring of the full transcript. On 40 held-out children, agreement with clinicians was modest, with quadratic weighted kappa between 0.22 and 0.30. Replacing every example's label with a wrong one changed about a third of the few-shot predictions, more often towards the corrupted labels than chance, but did not lower agreement, 0.25 with correct and 0.23 with wrong labels. A larger second model did the same. The anchored comparison did not improve on few-shot scoring, its cross-child judgements were no more accurate than those implied by absolute scores, and routing did not help. Among the registered, uncalibrated arms, direct scoring of the full transcript agreed best at 0.30. Exploratory follow-up analyses found that the model's graded judgements ranked children better than its final scores, that existing batch calibration raised agreement to 0.46 on the same children, and that in controlled synthetic sessions scoring episodes separately did not beat calibrated whole-transcript scoring. Expert-scored examples do not by themselves calibrate model-based clinical raters, and claims of calibration or added pipeline complexity need corrupted-label controls and strong baselines.
中文翻译
大语言模型能够应用书面评分标准对转录内容进行分析，使自动化编码自闭症诊断观察量表（ADOS-2）看起来触手可及。一种常见的对齐方式是向模型展示专家评分示例，但正确标签是否能提高一致性却很少被验证。我们针对48名儿童的8个基于语音的ADOS-2模块3项目，采用一个本地模型和预先注册的安慰剂对照设计进行了测试。比较了少样本评分、锚定比较（模型仅判断某儿童优于、相似或劣于评分锚点，由固定规则给出分数）、事后校准、基于协议的证据路由以及完整转录的直接评分。在40个保留儿童样本上，与临床医生的一致性较低，加权二次kappa系数介于0.22至0.30之间。将每个示例的标签替换为错误标签后，约三分之一的少样本预测发生变化，且更倾向于偏向错误标签而非随机，但并未降低一致性（正确标签下为0.25，错误标签下为0.23）。更大的第二个模型也表现出类似结果。锚定比较未优于少样本评分，其跨儿童判断的准确性并不高于绝对评分所隐含的水平，而证据路由也未带来改善。在注册的未校准组中，直接评分完整转录的表现最佳，kappa达0.30。探索性后续分析发现，模型的分级判断比最终得分更能准确排序儿童；现有批量校准可将一致性提升至0.46；在受控合成会话中，分别评分片段无法超越校准后的整体转录评分。专家评分示例本身并不能校准基于模型的临床评分者，关于校准或流程复杂性的主张需包含带错误标签的对照组和强基线。
👤 Zeteng Lin, Yikang Liu, Bolin Zeng, Mengyuan Ding, Xihui Wu et al. 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31735]
BEARING: Who Is Talking to Whom? Grounding Acoustic Direction in Vision for Online Multi-Party Conversation
BEARING：谁在和谁说话？基于视觉的在线多方对话声源定位
Understanding a group conversation means deciding, from video and audio, who is speaking and to whom. Leading systems treat these tasks offline on single-channel clips and align models with a self-evaluation reward. We propose BEARING, a geometry-first alternative built on one measured signal: the direction from which a microphone array beside the camera hears a sound. A label-free calibration learned from the stream registers this direction into the camera image, and an online registry of faces, voices and directions needs no enrollment. The registered direction serves as (i) a visual prompt for a vision-language model, a marker on the speaker or an arrow toward an off-screen source; (ii) one input of a reliability-weighted fusion with visual active-speaker detection, for speakers in the same direction from the array; and (iii) a verifiable reward for alignment: the named speaker must sit where the sound comes from, the confidence must match that check, and always answering "group" earns nothing. Every decision is online, with the lookahead as an explicit parameter. On 9,498 addressee-labelled utterances from three AMI sites and egocentric EasyCom, the direction prompt raises speaker accuracy from 54% to 74%; addressee accuracy moves by about two points, because telling one addressee from the group depends on the words more than on where people are. For co-located speakers on AVDIAR, visual active-speaker detection holds 95% where direction alone falls to 59%. The verifiable reward beats self-evaluation under GRPO, reward-ranked fine-tuning and DPO; shrinking the lookahead from 5 s to 0.5 s costs 4.5 DER points on AMI, and learning the registration online costs a dip in the first two minutes before the system overtakes vision alone. Code and evaluation protocols will be released.
中文翻译
理解群体对话意味着从视频和音频中判断谁在讲话以及对谁讲话。现有主流系统在单通道片段上离线处理这些任务，并通过自我评估奖励对齐模型。我们提出BEARING，一种以几何为核心的替代方案，基于一个测量信号：麦克风阵列从摄像头旁接收到声音的方向。从流数据中学习的无标签校准将此方向注册到相机图像中，而在线的人脸、声音和方向注册表无需注册过程。注册方向用作：(i) 视觉语言模型的视觉提示，标记说话者或指向屏幕外的声源；(ii) 与视觉活跃说话者检测进行可靠性加权融合的输入，用于来自阵列相同方向的说话者；(iii) 可验证的奖励：被命名的说话者必须位于声音来源处，置信度必须与此检查一致，始终回答“群体”则无收益。所有决策均为在线进行，前瞻时间作为显式参数。在来自三个AMI站点和自拍视角EasyCom的9,498个有接收方标注的语句上，方向提示将说话人识别准确率从54%提升至74%；接收方准确率提升约两个点，因为区分个体接收者更多依赖于话语内容而非位置。在AVDIAR上的共处说话者场景中，视觉活跃说话者检测达到95%准确率，而仅靠方向则降至59%。可验证奖励在GRPO、奖励排序微调和DPO下均优于自我评估；将前瞻时间从5秒缩小至0.5秒在AMI上损失4.5 DER点，而在线学习注册则在前两分钟出现短暂下降，随后系统超越纯视觉表现。代码和评估协议将公开发布。
👤 Nader Lobandi, Lombe Chileshe, Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini, Mohammad H. Mahoor 📅 2026-11-24 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31465]
A dataset of clinically similar image pairs from citation-linked case reports for medical image retrieval
基于引用链接病例报告的临床相似图像对数据集，用于医学图像检索
Retrieving images of patients clinically similar to a given patient can support diagnosis, teaching and research. Training and evaluating retrieval systems requires knowing which images of different patients show the same finding, and existing datasets are small or narrow in scope. We construct PMC-PaIR, a dataset of 86,144 pairs of clinically similar images from 56,013 open-access PubMed Central case reports, which often document rare or challenging presentations. We develop an approach that follows citations, which often link case reports of similar patients. To determine what two linked cases share clinically, it prompts large language models to generate and answer hierarchical questions, from modality to diagnosis and findings, and to match images by caption using the answers. Experts find the pairs highly accurate, and this question-guided approach finds twice as many correct pairs as direct matching. Fine-tuning BiomedCLIP on PMC-PaIR improves retrieval in all modality, anatomy and diagnosis categories tested, for image-only queries and image queries with a short text naming the finding. We release the dataset and the automated pipeline, which can extend PMC-PaIR as new case reports are published.
中文翻译
检索与给定患者临床特征相似的图像，可支持诊断、教学和研究。训练和评估检索系统需要知道不同患者图像中是否表现出相同发现，而现有数据集规模小或范围狭窄。我们构建了PMC-PaIR数据集，包含来自56,013篇开放获取PubMed Central病例报告的86,144对临床相似图像，这些报告常记录罕见或复杂病例。我们提出一种跟随引用的方法，因为引用常链接相似患者的病例报告。为确定两个关联病例在临床上有何共性，我们利用大语言模型生成并回答分层问题，从成像模态到诊断和发现，并通过答案匹配图像的标题。专家评估认为这些配对高度准确，且该问题引导方法找到的正确配对数量是直接匹配方法的两倍。在PMC-PaIR上微调BiomedCLIP，显著提升了所有成像模态、解剖部位和诊断类别中的检索性能，适用于纯图像查询以及带有简短文本描述发现的图像查询。我们公开发布该数据集及自动化处理流程，未来可随着新病例报告的发表持续扩展PMC-PaIR。
👤 Guy Shmueli, Keno K. Bressem, Tom Hope 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31397]
CoRe: Jointly Learning Contextual Representations for Understanding and Generation
CoRe：联合学习用于理解和生成的上下文表示
We introduce CoRe, in which understanding and generation jointly shape one contextual representation for images and one for text, trained from scratch. Each representation is read for understanding, conditions the other modality, and is the space in which that modality is generated by a continuous flow from Gaussian noise. With 383M parameters trained on CC12M and no pretrained language model, text encoder, or VAE at inference, CoRe matches a same-data contrastive-only baseline in global understanding and reaches COCO-30K FID 10.10, comparable to much larger generators built on pretrained components. In controlled comparisons at equal data and architecture, CoRe generates better in both directions (text to image and image to text) than a representation shaped by understanding alone and then frozen, and follows prompts better than generating in a space that understanding does not shape. In the other direction, with an attentive segmentation probe, on monocular depth, and when read by a frozen language model, CoRe surpasses the contrastive-only baseline: generation adds information that stronger readouts reach. Because generation runs inside the understanding representation, frozen real-image probes read and steer intermediate states and the model ranks its own samples without extra training. Because both modalities use continuous flows, both also distill to a few steps. Our controls indicate why one representation suffices: understanding organizes the representation semantically, while generation makes it retain more information.
中文翻译
我们提出 CoRe，其理解与生成共同塑造图像和文本的单一上下文表示，并从零开始训练。每个表示既可用于理解，又作为另一模态的条件，同时也是该模态通过从高斯噪声连续流生成的空间。在 CC12M 数据集上训练，参数量为 383M，推理时不依赖预训练语言模型、文本编码器或 VAE，CoRe 在全局理解方面达到与仅对比学习基线相当的水平，并在 COCO-30K 上实现 FID 10.10，接近使用预训练组件的大规模生成器。在数据与架构相同的控制比较中，CoRe 在两个方向（文本到图像和图像到文本）的生成质量均优于仅由理解塑造并冻结的表示，且比在理解未塑造的空间中生成更符合提示。在反向方面，通过注意力分割探测器、单目深度估计以及由冻结语言模型读取时，CoRe 超越了仅对比学习基线：生成增加了更强读出所能捕捉的信息。由于生成在理解表示内部运行，冻结的真实图像探测器可以读取并引导中间状态，模型无需额外训练即可对自身样本进行排序。由于两种模态均使用连续流，两者均可压缩至少数步骤。我们的对照实验表明，单一表示足以满足需求：理解使表示具有语义组织，而生成使其保留更多信息。
👤 Hangke Sui, Chang Li, Yuyan Li, Mohammad Haris Baig, Daniel Ulbricht 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31350]
GHC:Expanding Residual Interfaces in Hyper-Connections
GHC：扩展超连接中的残差接口
Hyper-Connections expand residual capacity across parallel streams, yet restrict every branch to narrow read and write interfaces: each branch still reads a single stream mixture and writes scaled copies of one output vector to all streams. To lift this bottleneck, we propose Grouped Hyper-Connections (GHC). By partitioning each branch into parallel groups along attention heads or FFN channels, GHC supplies each group with a distinct stream mixture and aggregates their separate outputs into each stream with learned weights. Extensive experiments across dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models show that GHC consistently improves upon Identity Hyper-Connections (IHC) with negligible parameter and memory-access overhead, increasing training efficiency by 35% over Pre-Norm and 10% over IHC. Moreover, expanding residual interfaces overcomes the diminishing returns of adding streams, allowing wider residual states to yield further gains.
中文翻译
超连接通过并行流扩展残差容量，但限制每条分支只能使用狭窄的读写接口：每个分支仍只读取单一的流混合，并将一个输出向量的缩放副本写入所有流。为打破这一瓶颈，我们提出分组超连接（GHC）。通过沿注意力头或前馈网络通道将每个分支划分为并行组，GHC 为每组提供不同的流混合，并使用可学习权重将各组独立输出聚合到每条流中。在密集模型和专家混合（MoE）模型上的大量实验表明，GHC 在几乎不增加参数和内存访问开销的情况下，始终优于身份超连接（IHC），训练效率比 Pre-Norm 提高 35%，比 IHC 提高 10%。此外，扩展残差接口克服了增加流数带来的收益递减，使得更宽的残差状态能够带来进一步提升。
👤 Tianyu Li, Hongtao Zhang, Dongchen Han, Jingshu Liu, Bojun Wang et al. 📅 2026-11-23 🔗 arXiv
[2611.31277]
LLM Genetic Feature Engineering
LLM 遗传特征工程
Feature Engineering is the (traditionally manual) process of constructing derived variables from an existing dataset by combining, transforming, or otherwise manipulating the original data fields to reduce statistical noise and extract meaningful, domain-specific quantities of interest from the raw dataset. Recent work demonstrates promising progress toward the au tomation of this process through the integration of large language models (LLMs) and various metaheuristics to guide the search for useful features. We build on this approach by introducing a novel genetic algorithm that implements selection, crossover, and mutation at the individual feature level, rather than at the level of the entire engineered feature set. In doing so, we enable in-process statistical analysis of newly-generated features, thereby enabling the genetic algorithm to simultaneously integrate individual and group-level feature metrics into its selec tion process. We demonstrate this capability using state-of-the-art feature importance metrics, and show that this approach yields superior predictive performance while driving meaningful improvements in feature relevance, algorithm robustness, and overall model interpretability.
中文翻译
特征工程是传统上手动完成的过程，通过组合、转换或以其他方式操作原始数据字段，从现有数据集中构建衍生变量，以减少统计噪声，并从原始数据集中提取有意义的、领域特定的数量。近期研究展示了通过整合大语言模型（LLMs）与各种元启发式算法来引导搜索有用特征的自动化进程的前景。我们在此基础上引入一种新型遗传算法，该算法在个体特征层面实现选择、交叉和变异，而非在整个工程特征集层面。由此，我们实现了对新生成特征的实时统计分析，使遗传算法能够在选择过程中同时整合个体和群体级别的特征指标。我们使用最先进的特征重要性度量验证了这一能力，结果表明该方法在预测性能上表现更优，同时显著提升了特征相关性、算法鲁棒性和整体模型可解释性。
👤 Jonathan Thompson, Razvan Stefanescu, Manoj Varma Namburi, Liuxia Wang 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
